# luna-core: own runtime + SDK, leaving deepagents / LangChain / LangGraph — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build `luna-core` — an async agent runtime and public SDK (own loop, tools, event-log sessions, five provider adapters, policy, hooks, sandboxes, subagents, parallel and background agents, MCP, ACP, remote client) — move the Luna app onto it, and remove deepagents, LangChain and LangGraph entirely.

**Architecture:** A uv workspace with two packages: `packages/luna-core` (import `luna_core`, deps `httpx`, `httpx-sse`, `pyyaml`) and the app `luna-simple`. The core is built bottom-up with its own test suite while the app keeps running on deepagents; one cut-over phase moves the app onto the core, after which the frameworks are deleted from the dependency set. Sessions start from a clean slate (old LangGraph checkpoints are not migrated).

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.11+, asyncio, httpx + httpx-sse (provider adapters, no vendor SDKs), sqlite3 (event log), PyYAML, optional `mcp` and `agent-client-protocol` SDKs, Textual/Starlette (app), pytest + pytest-asyncio + `httpx.MockTransport`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-luna-core-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- Python `>=3.11`; ruff line length 100; docstrings per the repo's ruff `D` rules (tests exempt); `ruff check` **and** `ruff format --check` must pass (CI runs both).
- Core runtime dependencies are exactly `httpx>=0.27`, `httpx-sse>=0.4`, `pyyaml>=6`. Extras: `mcp` → `mcp>=1.12,<2` (v2 replaces the client API), `acp` → `agent-client-protocol>=0.4`, `lsp` → `multilspy>=0.0.15`, `websearch` → `ddgs>=8.0`.
- **No** `deepagents`, `langchain*`, `langgraph*`, `litellm`, `openai`, `anthropic`, `google-genai` imports anywhere in `packages/luna-core`. After Phase D none anywhere in the repo.
- Public API = names exported from `luna_core/__init__.py`; version `0.1.0`.
- All core I/O is async; blocking work (subprocess, file walking, git) runs via `asyncio.to_thread` or `asyncio.create_subprocess_exec`. Event-log writes are short synchronous SQLite transactions in WAL mode and are called directly (sub-millisecond).
- Built-in tool names and parameters stay as today: `ls(path)`, `read_file(file_path, offset, limit)`, `write_file(file_path, content)`, `edit_file(file_path, old_string, new_string, replace_all)`, `delete(file_path)`, `glob(pattern, path)`, `grep(pattern, path, glob, output_mode, max_count)`, `execute(command, timeout)`, `task(description, subagent_type, background, isolation)`, plus new `write_todos(todos)`, `task_status(handle)`, `task_cancel(handle)`. Agent paths are project-relative; a leading `/` means the project root (today's virtual mode), and anything resolving outside the root is a tool error.
- Wire event names keep today's SSE shape where it exists: `text_delta`, `usage_delta`, `tool_started`, `tool_finished`, `approval_needed`, `notice`, `turn_done`, `error`, `reload_requested`; new: `thinking_delta`, `todos`, `compacted`, `background_done`, `cancelled`.
- Approval decisions: `{"type": "approve"}`, `{"type": "edit", "args": {...}}`, `{"type": "reject", "message": str}`, plus optional `"always": "<rule>"` (today's format).
- Compaction triggers at 80% of the model context window; keeps the last 4 user turns verbatim.
- Background/parallel limits: `max_parallel=4` default.
- Tests: `uv run pytest -o addopts="" -q` from the repo root runs both suites; live provider tests are marked `@pytest.mark.live` and skipped without keys.
- Every task ends with the **whole** suite green and lint clean, and is committed with the `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` trailer.

## Review Focus

1. **Cancel mid tool call** (Esc in the TUI while `execute` runs): the subprocess is killed, the log records `Cancelled` and synthetic tool results, and the next turn starts cleanly → Task 19 test `test_cancel_kills_running_tool_and_patches_history`.
2. **Provider stream breaks after partial output** (network drop mid-answer): no blind retry that would duplicate text; the turn ends with a readable `TurnError` and the partial assistant message is kept → Task 6 test `test_no_retry_after_first_event`.
3. **Two parallel subagents editing the same file**: worktree isolation keeps them apart; without isolation, edits to one path are serialised → Task 23 tests `test_worktrees_keep_parallel_writers_apart`, `test_shared_workspace_serialises_writes_to_one_path`.
4. **Agent asks for a path outside the project** (`/Users/me/Desktop/x.txt`): explicit tool error, nothing written inside the repo → Task 13 test `test_outside_path_is_an_error_not_remapped`.
5. **Background session while the TUI closes**: the run continues in the server, its events are logged, and reopening shows the finished transcript and a completion notice → Task 24 test `test_background_session_survives_client_disconnect` (core `AgentManager`) and Task 30 test of the same name (server: the TUI's stream drops mid-turn).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** — `packages/luna-core/tests/test_package.py` (no `__init__.py` in this tests dir: module names stay distinct from the app's `tests` package):

```python
import re
from pathlib import Path

import luna_core

#: Frameworks and vendor SDKs the core must never import (spec: own adapters on httpx).
FORBIDDEN = (
    "deepagents",
    "langchain",
    "langgraph",
    "litellm",
    "openai",
    "anthropic",
    "google.genai",
    "google.generativeai",
)


def test_version():
    assert luna_core.__version__ == "0.1.0"


def test_core_never_imports_frameworks_or_vendor_sdks():
    root = Path(luna_core.__file__).parent
    names = "|".join(re.escape(m) for m in FORBIDDEN)
    pattern = re.compile(rf"^\s*(?:from|import)\s+(?:{names})\b", re.MULTILINE)
    offenders = [str(p) for p in root.rglob("*.py") if pattern.search(p.read_text("utf-8"))]
    assert offenders == []
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `packages/luna-core/tests/test_messages.py`:

```python
import base64

import pytest

from luna_core.messages import (
    Image,
    Message,
    Text,
    Thinking,
    ToolCall,
    ToolResult,
    image_from_file,
    message_from_dict,
    message_to_dict,
)


def test_user_message_with_text_and_image():
    img = Image("image/png", "AAAA")
    msg = Message.user("look", images=[img])
    assert msg.role == "user"
    assert msg.content == [Text("look"), img]
    assert msg.text == "look"


def test_assistant_text_and_tool_calls_properties():
    call = ToolCall("c1", "read_file", {"file_path": "a.py"})
    msg = Message.assistant([Thinking("hm", signature="sig"), Text("ok "), Text("go"), call])
    assert msg.text == "ok go"
    assert msg.tool_calls == [call]


def test_tool_results_message():
    res = ToolResult("c1", (Text("contents"),), name="read_file")
    msg = Message.tool_results([res])
    assert msg.role == "tool"
    assert msg.results == [res]
    assert res.text == "contents"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "msg",
    [
        Message.user("hi", images=[Image("image/jpeg", "Zm9v")]),
        Message.assistant(
            [
                Thinking("why", signature="s", provider="anthropic"),
                Text("answer"),
                ToolCall("c1", "grep", {"pattern": "x"}, extra={"thought_signature": "t"}),
            ]
        ),
        Message.tool_results([ToolResult("c1", (Text("out"),), is_error=True, name="grep")]),
    ],
)
def test_round_trip(msg):
    data = message_to_dict(msg)
    assert data["v"] == 1
    again = message_from_dict(data)
    assert again == msg
    assert again.content[-1].__class__ is msg.content[-1].__class__


def test_extra_survives_round_trip():
    call = ToolCall("c1", "grep", {}, extra={"thought_signature": "t"})
    again = message_from_dict(message_to_dict(Message.assistant([call])))
    assert again.tool_calls[0].extra == {"thought_signature": "t"}


def test_unknown_block_type_is_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown block type"):
        message_from_dict({"v": 1, "role": "user", "content": [{"type": "video"}]})


def test_image_from_file(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "shot.png"
    path.write_bytes(b"\x89PNG")
    img = image_from_file(path)
    assert img.media_type == "image/png"
    assert base64.b64decode(img.data) == b"\x89PNG"


def test_image_from_file_rejects_unknown_type(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "notes.txt"
    path.write_text("x")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not a supported image"):
        image_from_file(path)
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run** `uv run pytest -o addopts="" -q packages/luna-core/tests/test_messages.py` — Expected: FAIL (`ModuleNotFoundError: luna_core.messages`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/messages.py`:

```python
"""Conversation content: blocks, messages and their JSON form.

The system prompt is not a message: it is passed separately to providers.
Messages are what the event log stores and what adapters translate into each
provider's wire format.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Union

#: Bumped when the JSON shape of messages changes incompatibly.
SCHEMA_VERSION = 1

_IMAGE_TYPES = {
    ".png": "image/png",
    ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
    ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
    ".gif": "image/gif",
    ".webp": "image/webp",
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Text:
    """Plain text."""

    text: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Thinking:
    """Model reasoning.

    ``signature`` carries the provider's opaque data that must be sent back
    unchanged (Anthropic thinking signatures, OpenAI encrypted reasoning).
    ``provider`` records which adapter produced it, so another provider is
    never sent a signature it cannot verify.
    """

    text: str
    signature: str | None = None
    provider: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Image:
    """An inline image, base64-encoded."""

    media_type: str
    data: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolCall:
    """A tool invocation requested by the model.

    ``extra`` keeps provider-specific data that has to round-trip (for
    example Gemini's thought signature on a function call).
    """

    id: str
    name: str
    args: dict
    extra: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolResult:
    """The outcome of one tool call, returned to the model."""

    call_id: str
    content: tuple[Text | Image, ...]
    is_error: bool = False
    name: str = ""

    @property
    def text(self) -> str:
        """Concatenated text parts of the result."""
        return "".join(part.text for part in self.content if isinstance(part, Text))


Block = Union[Text, Thinking, Image, ToolCall, ToolResult]
Role = Literal["user", "assistant", "tool"]


@dataclass
class Message:
    """One conversation message."""

    role: Role
    content: list[Block]

    @classmethod
    def user(cls, text: str = "", images: list[Image] | tuple[Image, ...] = ()) -> Message:
        """Build a user message from text and optional images."""
        blocks: list[Block] = [Text(text)] if text else []
        blocks.extend(images)
        return cls("user", blocks)

    @classmethod
    def assistant(cls, blocks: list[Block]) -> Message:
        """Build an assistant message."""
        return cls("assistant", list(blocks))

    @classmethod
    def tool_results(cls, results: list[ToolResult]) -> Message:
        """Build the message carrying tool results back to the model."""
        return cls("tool", list(results))

    @property
    def text(self) -> str:
        """Concatenated text blocks (thinking excluded)."""
        return "".join(b.text for b in self.content if isinstance(b, Text))

    @property
    def tool_calls(self) -> list[ToolCall]:
        """Tool calls in this message, in order."""
        return [b for b in self.content if isinstance(b, ToolCall)]

    @property
    def results(self) -> list[ToolResult]:
        """Tool results in this message, in order."""
        return [b for b in self.content if isinstance(b, ToolResult)]


def block_to_dict(block: Block) -> dict:
    """Serialise one block to a JSON-compatible dict."""
    if isinstance(block, Text):
        return {"type": "text", "text": block.text}
    if isinstance(block, Thinking):
        return {
            "type": "thinking",
            "text": block.text,
            "signature": block.signature,
            "provider": block.provider,
        }
    if isinstance(block, Image):
        return {"type": "image", "media_type": block.media_type, "data": block.data}
    if isinstance(block, ToolCall):
        return {
            "type": "tool_call",
            "id": block.id,
            "name": block.name,
            "args": block.args,
            "extra": block.extra,
        }
    if isinstance(block, ToolResult):
        return {
            "type": "tool_result",
            "call_id": block.call_id,
            "content": [block_to_dict(p) for p in block.content],
            "is_error": block.is_error,
            "name": block.name,
        }
    raise TypeError(f"not a block: {block!r}")


def block_from_dict(data: dict) -> Block:
    """Rebuild a block from :func:`block_to_dict` output."""
    kind = data.get("type")
    if kind == "text":
        return Text(data["text"])
    if kind == "thinking":
        return Thinking(data["text"], data.get("signature"), data.get("provider"))
    if kind == "image":
        return Image(data["media_type"], data["data"])
    if kind == "tool_call":
        return ToolCall(data["id"], data["name"], dict(data["args"]), dict(data.get("extra") or {}))
    if kind == "tool_result":
        parts = tuple(block_from_dict(p) for p in data["content"])
        return ToolResult(data["call_id"], parts, bool(data.get("is_error")), data.get("name", ""))
    raise ValueError(f"unknown block type {kind!r}")


def message_to_dict(message: Message) -> dict:
    """Serialise a message (versioned)."""
    return {
        "v": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "role": message.role,
        "content": [block_to_dict(b) for b in message.content],
    }


def message_from_dict(data: dict) -> Message:
    """Rebuild a message from :func:`message_to_dict` output."""
    return Message(data["role"], [block_from_dict(b) for b in data["content"]])


def image_from_file(path: str | Path) -> Image:
    """Load an image file as an :class:`Image` block."""
    path = Path(path)
    media_type = _IMAGE_TYPES.get(path.suffix.lower())
    if media_type is None:
        raise ValueError(f"{path.name} is not a supported image (png, jpg, gif, webp)")
    return Image(media_type, base64.b64encode(path.read_bytes()).decode("ascii"))
```

from luna_core import catalog
from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.usage import Usage, cost


def test_usage_adds_and_totals():
    a = Usage(input=100, output=20, cache_read=50)
    b = Usage(input=10, output=5, reasoning=3)
    s = a + b
    assert s == Usage(input=110, output=25, cache_read=50, reasoning=3)
    assert s.total == 135
    assert Usage.from_dict(s.to_dict()) == s


def test_lookup_prefers_the_longest_matching_key():
    info = catalog.lookup("claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929")
    assert info.key == "claude-sonnet-4"
    assert info.context_window == 200_000
    assert info.vision is True


def test_lookup_unknown_model_gets_safe_defaults():
    info = catalog.lookup("totally-new-model")
    assert info.context_window == 128_000
    assert info.input_price is None


def test_overrides_win_over_packaged_entries():
    info = catalog.lookup("deepseek-v4-flash", {"deepseek-v4": {"window": 1_000_000, "input": 0.14, "output": 0.28}})
    assert (info.context_window, info.input_price, info.output_price) == (1_000_000, 0.14, 0.28)


def test_cost_counts_cached_input_at_the_cache_price():
    info = catalog.ModelInfo("m", 100_000, None, 1.0, 2.0, 0.1, 1.25, False, False)
    usage = Usage(input=1_000_000, output=1_000_000, cache_read=500_000)
    # 0.5M fresh * 1.0 + 0.5M cached * 0.1 + 1M out * 2.0
    assert cost(usage, info) == pytest.approx(0.5 + 0.05 + 2.0)


def test_cost_unknown_price_is_none():
    assert cost(Usage(input=10), catalog.lookup("totally-new-model")) is None


def test_provider_error_kinds():
    err = ProviderError("slow down", kind="rate_limit", status=429, retry_after=2.0)
    assert err.retryable is True
    assert ProviderError("bad key", kind="auth", status=401).retryable is False
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ProviderError("x", kind="weird")

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/usage.py`:

```python
"""Token usage and cost."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

from luna_core.catalog import ModelInfo


@dataclass
class Usage:
    """Tokens for one request, a turn or a whole session.

    ``input`` is the full prompt size including cached tokens; ``cache_read``
    and ``cache_write`` are the parts of it served from / written to the
    provider's prompt cache. ``reasoning`` is the part of ``output`` spent on
    hidden reasoning, when the provider reports it.
    """

    input: int = 0
    output: int = 0
    cache_read: int = 0
    cache_write: int = 0
    reasoning: int = 0

    def __add__(self, other: Usage) -> Usage:
        """Sum two usages field by field."""
        return Usage(
            self.input + other.input,
            self.output + other.output,
            self.cache_read + other.cache_read,
            self.cache_write + other.cache_write,
            self.reasoning + other.reasoning,
        )

    @property
    def total(self) -> int:
        """Input plus output tokens."""
        return self.input + self.output

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        """JSON form."""
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict) -> Usage:
        """Rebuild from :meth:`to_dict` output (unknown keys ignored)."""
        return cls(**{k: int(data.get(k, 0) or 0) for k in cls.__dataclass_fields__})


def cost(usage: Usage, info: ModelInfo) -> float | None:
    """USD cost of ``usage`` for ``info``'s prices, or ``None`` without prices."""
    if info.input_price is None or info.output_price is None:
        return None
    cache_read_price = info.cache_read_price if info.cache_read_price is not None else info.input_price
    cache_write_price = (
        info.cache_write_price if info.cache_write_price is not None else info.input_price
    )
    fresh = max(0, usage.input - usage.cache_read - usage.cache_write)
    total = (
        fresh * info.input_price
        + usage.cache_read * cache_read_price
        + usage.cache_write * cache_write_price
        + usage.output * info.output_price
    )
    return total / 1_000_000
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/catalog.py`:

```python
"""Model catalogue: context windows, prices and capabilities.

Keys are substrings of model ids; the longest key contained in the model id
wins, so ``claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929`` matches ``claude-sonnet-4``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import tomllib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import cache
from importlib import resources

DEFAULT_WINDOW = 128_000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelInfo:
    """What the core knows about a model. Prices are USD per million tokens."""

    key: str
    context_window: int
    max_output: int | None
    input_price: float | None
    output_price: float | None
    cache_read_price: float | None
    cache_write_price: float | None
    vision: bool
    reasoning: bool


@cache
def _packaged() -> dict[str, dict]:
    text = resources.files("luna_core").joinpath("data/models.toml").read_text("utf-8")
    return tomllib.loads(text).get("models", {})


def _info(key: str, entry: dict) -> ModelInfo:
    def num(name: str) -> float | None:
        value = entry.get(name)
        return float(value) if value is not None else None

    return ModelInfo(
        key=key,
        context_window=int(entry.get("window", DEFAULT_WINDOW)),
        max_output=int(entry["max_output"]) if "max_output" in entry else None,
        input_price=num("input"),
        output_price=num("output"),
        cache_read_price=num("cache_read"),
        cache_write_price=num("cache_write"),
        vision=bool(entry.get("vision", False)),
        reasoning=bool(entry.get("reasoning", False)),
    )


def _best(entries: dict[str, dict], model: str) -> tuple[str, dict] | None:
    needle = model.lower()
    hits = [(k, v) for k, v in entries.items() if k.lower() in needle]
    return max(hits, key=lambda kv: len(kv[0])) if hits else None


def lookup(model: str, overrides: dict | None = None) -> ModelInfo:
    """Return the best catalogue entry for ``model`` (overrides first)."""
    for source in (overrides or {}, _packaged()):
        hit = _best(source, model or "")
        if hit is not None:
            return _info(*hit)
    return ModelInfo(model or "", DEFAULT_WINDOW, None, None, None, None, None, False, False)
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/errors.py`:

```python
"""Exceptions raised by the core."""

from __future__ import annotations

KINDS = ("auth", "rate_limit", "context_length", "bad_request", "server", "network", "unknown")
_RETRYABLE = {"rate_limit", "server", "network"}


class LunaCoreError(Exception):
    """Base class for core errors."""


class ProviderError(LunaCoreError):
    """A model provider request failed.

    ``kind`` classifies the failure so callers can decide what to do:
    ``auth`` (bad or missing key), ``rate_limit``, ``context_length``,
    ``bad_request``, ``server``, ``network``, ``unknown``.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        message: str,
        kind: str = "unknown",
        status: int | None = None,
        retry_after: float | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if kind not in KINDS:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown provider error kind {kind!r}")
        super().__init__(message)
        self.kind = kind
        self.status = status
        self.retry_after = retry_after

    @property
    def retryable(self) -> bool:
        """True for transient failures worth retrying."""
        return self.kind in _RETRYABLE


class ToolError(LunaCoreError):
    """A tool failed in a way the model should see as an error result."""
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/events.py`:

```python
"""Events: what a provider streams, and what a turn emits to clients.

Turn events are the public protocol of the core: the REPL, the TUI, the
server's SSE stream, ACP and SDK users all consume them. ``to_wire`` gives
their JSON form (documented in ``docs/protocol.md``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Union

from luna_core.usage import Usage

NOTICE_LEVELS = ("dim", "info", "ok", "warn", "error")


# --- model stream events ---------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TextDelta:
    """A piece of answer text (also a turn event)."""

    text: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ThinkingDelta:
    """A piece of reasoning; ``signature`` arrives on the last piece (also a turn event)."""

    text: str
    signature: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolCallStart:
    """The model started a tool call at position ``index``."""

    index: int
    id: str
    name: str
    extra: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolCallDelta:
    """More JSON argument text for the tool call at ``index``."""

    index: int
    args_json: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class UsageReport:
    """Token usage reported by the provider for this request."""

    usage: Usage


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StopEvent:
    """The request finished: ``end``, ``tool_use``, ``max_tokens`` or ``refusal``."""

    reason: str


ModelEvent = Union[TextDelta, ThinkingDelta, ToolCallStart, ToolCallDelta, UsageReport, StopEvent]


# --- turn events -----------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ApprovalRequest:
    """A tool call waiting for a human decision."""

    call_id: str
    name: str
    args: dict
    description: str = ""
    suggested_rule: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TurnStarted:
    """A turn began."""

    turn_id: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolStarted:
    """A tool call started running."""

    call_id: str
    name: str
    args: dict
    preview: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolFinished:
    """A tool call finished."""

    call_id: str
    name: str
    ok: bool
    detail: str
    duration: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NeedsApproval:
    """The turn is waiting for ``Session.decide`` on this request."""

    request: ApprovalRequest


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TodosUpdated:
    """The agent's plan (``write_todos``) changed."""

    todos: list[dict]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Notice:
    """A short status line for the user."""

    level: str
    text: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """Validate the level."""
        if self.level not in NOTICE_LEVELS:
            raise ValueError(f"notice level must be one of {NOTICE_LEVELS}, got {self.level!r}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class UsageUpdate:
    """Usage of the request that just finished."""

    usage: Usage


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Compacted:
    """Old history was summarised; ``removed`` messages left the model context."""

    summary: str
    removed: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BackgroundDone:
    """A background task finished."""

    handle: str
    ok: bool
    summary: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReloadRequested:
    """A tool asked for the agent to be rebuilt (new MCP server, skill...)."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TurnDone:
    """The turn finished normally."""

    usage: Usage
    cost: float | None
    stop_reason: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TurnError:
    """The turn failed; the log stays consistent."""

    message: str
    kind: str = "error"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Cancelled:
    """The turn was cancelled by the user."""


TurnEvent = Union[
    TurnStarted,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolStarted,
    ToolFinished,
    NeedsApproval,
    TodosUpdated,
    Notice,
    UsageUpdate,
    Compacted,
    BackgroundDone,
    ReloadRequested,
    TurnDone,
    TurnError,
    Cancelled,
]


def to_wire(event: object) -> dict:
    """JSON form of a turn event (the SSE / SDK protocol)."""
    if isinstance(event, TurnStarted):
        return {"event": "turn_started", "turn_id": event.turn_id}
    if isinstance(event, TextDelta):
        return {"event": "text_delta", "text": event.text}
    if isinstance(event, ThinkingDelta):
        return {"event": "thinking_delta", "text": event.text}
    if isinstance(event, ToolStarted):
        return {
            "event": "tool_started",
            "call_id": event.call_id,
            "name": event.name,
            "args": event.args,
            "args_preview": event.preview,
        }
    if isinstance(event, ToolFinished):
        return {
            "event": "tool_finished",
            "call_id": event.call_id,
            "name": event.name,
            "ok": event.ok,
            "detail": event.detail,
            "duration": event.duration,
        }
    if isinstance(event, NeedsApproval):
        r = event.request
        return {
            "event": "approval_needed",
            "value": {
                "action_requests": [
                    {
                        "call_id": r.call_id,
                        "name": r.name,
                        "args": r.args,
                        "description": r.description,
                        "suggested_rule": r.suggested_rule,
                    }
                ]
            },
        }
    if isinstance(event, TodosUpdated):
        return {"event": "todos", "todos": event.todos}
    if isinstance(event, Notice):
        return {"event": "notice", "level": event.level, "text": event.text}
    if isinstance(event, UsageUpdate):
        return {"event": "usage_delta", "usage": event.usage.to_dict()}
    if isinstance(event, Compacted):
        return {"event": "compacted", "summary": event.summary, "removed": event.removed}
    if isinstance(event, BackgroundDone):
        return {"event": "background_done", "handle": event.handle, "ok": event.ok, "summary": event.summary}
    if isinstance(event, ReloadRequested):
        return {"event": "reload_requested"}
    if isinstance(event, TurnDone):
        return {
            "event": "turn_done",
            "usage": event.usage.to_dict(),
            "cost": event.cost,
            "stop_reason": event.stop_reason,
        }
    if isinstance(event, TurnError):
        return {"event": "error", "message": event.message, "kind": event.kind}
    if isinstance(event, Cancelled):
        return {"event": "cancelled"}
    raise TypeError(f"not a turn event: {event!r}")
```

### Task 5: Tools base — `@tool`, schemas, `ToolContext`

**Files:**

- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_tools_base.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `ToolSpec(name, description, parameters)` (frozen) — what providers see.
  - `Tool(name, description, parameters, fn, mutating=False, timeout=None)` with `async def invoke(ctx, args) -> ToolOutput` and `.spec`.
  - `ToolOutput(content: tuple[Text|Image, ...], is_error=False, detail="")`; `ToolOutput.text(s)`, `ToolOutput.error(s)`.
  - `ToolContext` (dataclass): `workspace`, `sandbox`, `session_id`, `call_id`, `emit` (callable for notices/todos), `state` (dict shared per session), `cancel` (`asyncio.Event`), `services` (dict for app-provided objects, e.g. background manager).
  - `@tool(name=None, mutating=False, timeout=None)` decorator → `Tool`; schema from type hints (`str int float bool`, `list[X]`, `dict`, `Literal[...]`, `X | None`), parameter descriptions from a Google-style `Args:` docstring section, required = no default. The first parameter named `ctx` is injected, never in the schema.
  - `args_preview(args) -> str` (first non-empty string arg, 40 chars) — same rule as today's `luna/core/turn_events.args_preview`.

import pytest

from luna_core.messages import Text
from luna_core.tools.base import Tool, ToolContext, ToolOutput, args_preview, tool


@tool()
def greet(name: str, times: int = 1, mood: Literal["calm", "loud"] = "calm") -> str:
    """Say hello.

    Args:
        name: who to greet.
        times: how many times.
        mood: tone of voice.
    """
    return " ".join([f"hi {name}"] * times)


@tool(name="ctx_echo", mutating=True)
async def echo(ctx: ToolContext, items: list[str], note: str | None = None) -> ToolOutput:
    """Echo items back."""
    return ToolOutput.text(f"{ctx.session_id}:{','.join(items)}:{note}")


def test_schema_from_hints_and_docstring():

    assert spec.name == "greet"
    assert spec.description == "Say hello."
    props = spec.parameters["properties"]
    assert props["name"] == {"type": "string", "description": "who to greet."}
    assert props["times"] == {"type": "integer", "description": "how many times.", "default": 1}
    assert props["mood"]["enum"] == ["calm", "loud"]

    assert spec.parameters["additionalProperties"] is False


def test_ctx_is_injected_and_hidden_from_schema():

    assert props["items"] == {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}
    assert props["note"] == {"type": "string"}
    assert echo.mutating is True and echo.name == "ctx_echo"

    out = await greet.invoke(ctx, {"name": "Luna", "times": 2})
    assert out.content == (Text("hi Luna hi Luna"),) and out.is_error is False
    out = await echo.invoke(ctx, {"items": ["a", "b"]})

async def test_bad_arguments_become_an_error_result():

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/tools/base.py`:

```python
"""The tool API: specs for providers, invocation with validation and timeouts."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import inspect
import re
import types
import typing
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Literal, Union, get_args, get_origin

from luna_core.messages import Image, Text


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolSpec:
    """What a provider sees: name, description, JSON Schema of the arguments."""

    name: str
    description: str
    parameters: dict


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolOutput:
    """A tool's result: content for the model, a short detail for the UI."""

    content: tuple[Text | Image, ...]
    is_error: bool = False
    detail: str = ""

    @classmethod
    def text(cls, text: str, detail: str = "") -> ToolOutput:
        """A successful text result."""
        return cls((Text(text),), False, detail)

    @classmethod
    def error(cls, text: str) -> ToolOutput:
        """An error result (the model sees it; the turn continues)."""
        return cls((Text(text),), True, text.splitlines()[0][:120] if text else "")


@dataclass
class ToolContext:
    """Everything a tool may use while it runs.

    ``services`` carries app- or runtime-provided objects by name (for
    example ``"background"``, ``"subagents"``, ``"policy"``); tools look them
    up instead of importing global singletons.
    """

    workspace: Any = None
    sandbox: Any = None
    session_id: str = ""
    call_id: str = ""
    emit: Callable[[object], None] = lambda event: None
    state: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    cancel: asyncio.Event = field(default_factory=asyncio.Event)
    services: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


ToolFn = Callable[..., Any]


class Tool:
    """A callable tool with a JSON-Schema spec.

    Built with :func:`tool` from a typed function, or directly with
    ``raw=True`` and ``fn(ctx, args) -> ToolOutput`` for dynamic tools such
    as MCP ones.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        name: str,
        description: str,
        parameters: dict,
        fn: ToolFn,
        *,
        mutating: bool = False,
        timeout: float | None = None,
        raw: bool = False,
        wants_ctx: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.description = description
        self.parameters = parameters
        self.mutating = mutating
        self.timeout = timeout
        self._fn = fn
        self._raw = raw
        self._wants_ctx = wants_ctx

    @property
    def spec(self) -> ToolSpec:
        """The provider-facing description of this tool."""
        return ToolSpec(self.name, self.description, self.parameters)

    async def invoke(self, ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> ToolOutput:
        """Run the tool; every failure becomes an error result, never an exception."""
        try:
            coro = self._call(ctx, args)
            result = await asyncio.wait_for(coro, self.timeout) if self.timeout else await coro
        except TimeoutError:
            return ToolOutput.error(f"{self.name} timed out after {self.timeout:g}s")
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            raise
        except _ArgError as exc:
            return ToolOutput.error(f"invalid arguments for {self.name}: {exc}")
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - tool failures are results for the model
            return ToolOutput.error(f"{self.name} failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
        return _to_output(result)

    async def _call(self, ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> Any:
        if self._raw:
            return await _maybe_await(self._fn(ctx, args))
        kwargs = _bind(self.parameters, args)
        if self._wants_ctx:
            return await _maybe_await(self._fn(ctx, **kwargs))
        return await _maybe_await(self._fn(**kwargs))


class _ArgError(Exception):
    pass


async def _maybe_await(value: Any) -> Any:
    return await value if inspect.isawaitable(value) else value


def _to_output(result: Any) -> ToolOutput:
    if isinstance(result, ToolOutput):
        return result
    if isinstance(result, str):
        return ToolOutput.text(result)
    if isinstance(result, (Text, Image)):
        return ToolOutput((result,))
    return ToolOutput.text(str(result))


def _bind(schema: dict, args: dict) -> dict:
    props = schema.get("properties", {})
    unknown = set(args) - set(props)
    if unknown:
        raise _ArgError(f"unknown argument(s): {', '.join(sorted(unknown))}")
    missing = [name for name in schema.get("required", []) if name not in args]
    if missing:
        raise _ArgError(f"missing required argument(s): {', '.join(missing)}")
    for name, value in args.items():
        _check(name, props[name], value)
    return dict(args)


_JSON_TYPES = {
    "string": str,
    "integer": int,
    "number": (int, float),
    "boolean": bool,
    "array": list,
    "object": dict,
}


def _check(name: str, prop: dict, value: Any) -> None:
    if value is None and prop.get("nullable", True):
        return
    expected = prop.get("type")
    if expected is None:
        return
    py = _JSON_TYPES[expected]
    if expected in ("integer", "number") and isinstance(value, bool):
        raise _ArgError(f"{name} must be {expected}")
    if not isinstance(value, py):
        raise _ArgError(f"{name} must be {expected}")
    if "enum" in prop and value not in prop["enum"]:
        raise _ArgError(f"{name} must be one of {prop['enum']}")


_PRIMITIVES = {str: "string", int: "integer", float: "number", bool: "boolean"}


def _schema_for(annotation: Any) -> dict:
    origin = get_origin(annotation)
    if annotation in _PRIMITIVES:
        return {"type": _PRIMITIVES[annotation]}
    if annotation in (dict, typing.Dict) or origin is dict:  # noqa: UP006
        return {"type": "object"}
    if origin in (list, typing.List):  # noqa: UP006
        (item,) = get_args(annotation) or (str,)
        return {"type": "array", "items": _schema_for(item)}
    if origin is Literal:
        values = list(get_args(annotation))
        return {"type": _PRIMITIVES[type(values[0])], "enum": values}
    if origin in (Union, types.UnionType):
        inner = [a for a in get_args(annotation) if a is not type(None)]
        if len(inner) == 1:
            return _schema_for(inner[0])
    raise TypeError(f"unsupported parameter annotation: {annotation!r}")


_ARGS_SECTION = re.compile(r"^\s*Args:\s*$", re.MULTILINE)
_ARG_LINE = re.compile(r"^\s{2,}(\w+):\s*(.+)$")


def _parse_doc(doc: str) -> tuple[str, dict[str, str]]:
    doc = inspect.cleandoc(doc or "")
    match = _ARGS_SECTION.search(doc)
    summary = (doc[: match.start()] if match else doc).strip()
    params: dict[str, str] = {}
    if match:
        current = None
        for line in doc[match.end() :].splitlines():
            arg = _ARG_LINE.match(line)
            if arg:
                current = arg.group(1)
                params[current] = arg.group(2).strip()
            elif current and line.strip() and line.startswith(" " * 6):
                params[current] += " " + line.strip()
            elif line.strip() and not line.startswith(" "):
                break
    return summary, params


def tool(
    name: str | None = None, *, mutating: bool = False, timeout: float | None = None
) -> Callable[[ToolFn], Tool]:
    """Turn a typed function into a :class:`Tool`.

    The first parameter, if named ``ctx``, receives the :class:`ToolContext`.
    The summary of the docstring becomes the description; an ``Args:``
    section documents parameters.
    """

    def deco(fn: ToolFn) -> Tool:
        sig = inspect.signature(fn)
        hints = typing.get_type_hints(fn)
        summary, docs = _parse_doc(fn.__doc__ or "")
        props: dict[str, dict] = {}
        required: list[str] = []
        wants_ctx = False
        for pname, param in sig.parameters.items():
            if pname == "ctx":
                wants_ctx = True
                continue
            prop = _schema_for(hints.get(pname, str))
            if pname in docs:
                prop["description"] = docs[pname]
            if param.default is inspect.Parameter.empty:
                required.append(pname)
            elif param.default is not None:
                prop["default"] = param.default
            props[pname] = prop
        schema = {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": props,
            "required": required,
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }
        return Tool(
            name or fn.__name__,
            summary,
            schema,
            fn,
            mutating=mutating,
            timeout=timeout,
            wants_ctx=wants_ctx,
        )

    return deco


def args_preview(args: dict, limit: int = 40) -> str:
    """Return the first non-empty string argument, whitespace-collapsed and truncated."""
    for value in args.values():
        if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
            text = " ".join(value.split())
            return text if len(text) <= limit else text[: limit - 1] + "…"
    return ""


AsyncToolFn = Callable[[ToolContext, dict], Awaitable[ToolOutput]]
```

Note on `_check`: a `None` value for an optional parameter is accepted; the `nullable` flag defaults to true because the schema generator drops `None` from `X | None` (providers reject `"type": ["string","null"]` inconsistently).

## Phase B — Providers

All adapters are tested against **recorded wire fixtures** served by `httpx.MockTransport`. A fixture is the exact SSE body a provider returns (copied from the provider's API reference / a real capture) plus assertions on the request JSON we send. When implementing, capture one real response per scenario with a live key (`pytest -m live --record` is not required — paste the body from `curl -N`) and update the fixture if the provider's current format differs; record any difference as a Ruling.

Shared test helper — create once in this phase as `packages/luna-core/tests/sse.py` (imported by the adapter tests):

```python
"""Helpers to fake provider HTTP endpoints in adapter tests."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import httpx


def sse_body(events: list[tuple[str | None, object]]) -> bytes:
    """Encode ``(event_name, data)`` pairs as an SSE stream body."""
    out = []
    for name, data in events:
        if name:
            out.append(f"event: {name}")
        payload = data if isinstance(data, str) else json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)
        out.append(f"data: {payload}")
        out.append("")
    return ("\n".join(out) + "\n").encode()


class Recorder:
    """A MockTransport handler that records requests and replays responses in order."""

    def __init__(self, *responses: httpx.Response) -> None:
        self.responses = list(responses)
        self.requests: list[httpx.Request] = []

    def __call__(self, request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        self.requests.append(request)
        return self.responses.pop(0)

    @property
    def last_json(self) -> dict:
        return json.loads(self.requests[-1].content)

    def client(self) -> httpx.AsyncClient:
        return httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(self))


def stream_response(events: list[tuple[str | None, object]], status: int = 200) -> httpx.Response:
    return httpx.Response(status, headers={"content-type": "text/event-stream"}, content=sse_body(events))


def json_response(status: int, body: dict, headers: dict | None = None) -> httpx.Response:
    return httpx.Response(status, json=body, headers=headers or {})
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: messages (Task 2), usage/errors (Task 3), model events (Task 4), `ToolSpec` (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `ModelRequest(model, system, messages, tools=[], max_tokens=None, temperature=None, reasoning=None, cache=True)`
  - `Provider` protocol: attribute `name: str`, method `stream(request) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]`
  - `RetryPolicy(attempts=3, base_delay=1.0, max_delay=30.0)`; `with_retries(open_stream, policy, sleep=asyncio.sleep)`
  - `error_from_response(status, body, headers) -> ProviderError`
  - `post_sse(client, url, *, headers, json) -> AsyncIterator[tuple[str, str]]`
  - `default_client() -> httpx.AsyncClient` (timeouts connect 15 s, read 600 s, write 60 s)
  - `StreamAccumulator(provider_name)` with `feed(event)`, `.message() -> Message`, `.usage: Usage`, `.stop_reason: str`
  - `FakeProvider(script)` with `.requests: list[ModelRequest]`; script items are `Message`s (assistant replies) or explicit event lists; helpers `FakeProvider.text(s)`, `FakeProvider.call(name, args, call_id=None)`.

```python
import httpx
import pytest

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.events import (
    StopEvent,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolCallDelta,
    ToolCallStart,
    UsageReport,
)
from luna_core.messages import Message, Text, Thinking, ToolCall
from luna_core.providers.base import (
    ModelRequest,
    RetryPolicy,
    StreamAccumulator,
    error_from_response,
    post_sse,
    with_retries,
)
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider
from luna_core.usage import Usage
from sse import Recorder, json_response, stream_response


def test_accumulator_keeps_block_order_and_parses_args():
    acc = StreamAccumulator("anthropic")
    for ev in [
        ThinkingDelta("let me "),
        ThinkingDelta("see", signature="sig1"),
        TextDelta("Reading "),
        TextDelta("files."),
        ToolCallStart(0, "c1", "read_file"),
        ToolCallDelta(0, '{"file_'),
        ToolCallDelta(0, 'path": "a.py"}'),
        ToolCallStart(1, "c2", "ls", extra={"k": "v"}),
        ToolCallDelta(1, "{}"),
        UsageReport(Usage(input=10, output=3)),
        StopEvent("tool_use"),
    ]:
        acc.feed(ev)
    assert acc.message() == Message.assistant(
        [
            Thinking("let me see", signature="sig1", provider="anthropic"),
            Text("Reading files."),
            ToolCall("c1", "read_file", {"file_path": "a.py"}),
            ToolCall("c2", "ls", {}, extra={"k": "v"}),
        ]
    )
    assert acc.usage == Usage(input=10, output=3)
    assert acc.stop_reason == "tool_use"


def test_accumulator_signature_without_text_creates_a_thinking_block():
    acc = StreamAccumulator("openai_responses")
    acc.feed(ThinkingDelta("", signature="enc"))
    acc.feed(TextDelta("hi"))
    assert acc.message().content == [Thinking("", signature="enc", provider="openai_responses"), Text("hi")]


def test_invalid_tool_json_is_kept_for_the_tool_to_report():
    acc = StreamAccumulator("x")
    acc.feed(ToolCallStart(0, "c1", "grep"))
    acc.feed(ToolCallDelta(0, '{"pattern": '))
    assert acc.message().tool_calls[0].args == {"__invalid_json__": '{"pattern": '}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("status", "body", "kind"),
    [
        (401, '{"error": {"message": "bad key"}}', "auth"),
        (403, "forbidden", "auth"),
        (429, '{"error": {"message": "slow"}}', "rate_limit"),
        (400, '{"error": {"message": "prompt is too long: 300000 tokens > 200000"}}', "context_length"),
        (400, '{"error": {"message": "maximum context length is 128000"}}', "context_length"),
        (400, '{"error": {"message": "bad field"}}', "bad_request"),
        (500, "oops", "server"),
        (529, "overloaded", "server"),
    ],
)
def test_error_classification(status, body, kind):
    err = error_from_response(status, body, {"retry-after": "3"} if status == 429 else {})
    assert err.kind == kind
    assert err.status == status
    if status == 429:
        assert err.retry_after == 3.0
    if body.startswith("{"):
        assert "error" not in str(err)  # the inner message, not the raw JSON


async def test_retries_transient_errors_before_any_event():
    calls = {"n": 0}

    async def opener():
        calls["n"] += 1
        if calls["n"] < 3:
            raise ProviderError("busy", "server", 503)
        yield TextDelta("ok")

    slept = []

    async def fake_sleep(s):
        slept.append(s)

    events = [ev async for ev in with_retries(opener, RetryPolicy(attempts=3, base_delay=1), fake_sleep)]
    assert events == [TextDelta("ok")]
    assert slept == [1, 2]


async def test_no_retry_after_first_event():
    async def opener():
        yield TextDelta("partial")
        raise ProviderError("connection reset", "network")

    got = []
    with pytest.raises(ProviderError, match="connection reset"):
        async for ev in with_retries(opener, RetryPolicy(attempts=5), lambda s: None):
            got.append(ev)
    assert got == [TextDelta("partial")]


async def test_auth_errors_are_not_retried():
    calls = {"n": 0}

    async def opener():
        calls["n"] += 1
        raise ProviderError("bad key", "auth", 401)
        yield  # pragma: no cover

    with pytest.raises(ProviderError):
        async for _ in with_retries(opener, RetryPolicy(attempts=3), lambda s: None):
            pass
    assert calls["n"] == 1


async def test_post_sse_yields_events_and_maps_http_errors():
    rec = Recorder(
        stream_response([("message", {"a": 1}), (None, "[DONE]")]),
        json_response(401, {"error": {"message": "invalid x-api-key"}}),
    )
    client = rec.client()
    got = [pair async for pair in post_sse(client, "https://x/v1", headers={}, json={"q": 1})]
    assert got == [("message", '{"a": 1}'), ("message", "[DONE]")]
    with pytest.raises(ProviderError) as exc:
        [pair async for pair in post_sse(client, "https://x/v1", headers={}, json={})]
    assert exc.value.kind == "auth" and "invalid x-api-key" in str(exc.value)


async def test_post_sse_maps_network_errors():
    def boom(request):
        raise httpx.ConnectError("refused", request=request)

    client = httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(boom))
    with pytest.raises(ProviderError) as exc:
        [pair async for pair in post_sse(client, "https://x", headers={}, json={})]
    assert exc.value.kind == "network"


async def test_fake_provider_replays_messages_and_records_requests():
    fake = FakeProvider([FakeProvider.call("ls", {"path": "."}, call_id="c1"), FakeProvider.text("done")])
    req = ModelRequest(model="m", system="s", messages=[Message.user("hi")])
    first = [ev async for ev in fake.stream(req)]
    assert ToolCallStart(0, "c1", "ls") in first and first[-1] == StopEvent("tool_use")
    second = [ev async for ev in fake.stream(req)]
    assert second[0] == TextDelta("done") and second[-1] == StopEvent("end")
    assert len(fake.requests) == 2
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="script exhausted"):
        [ev async for ev in fake.stream(req)]

"""Shared provider machinery: request type, retries, SSE transport, accumulation."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Protocol

import httpx
from httpx_sse import aconnect_sse

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.events import (
    ModelEvent,
    StopEvent,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolCallDelta,
    ToolCallStart,
    UsageReport,
)
from luna_core.messages import Block, Message, Text, Thinking, ToolCall
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolSpec
from luna_core.usage import Usage


@dataclass
class ModelRequest:
    """One model call."""

    model: str
    system: str
    messages: list[Message]
    tools: list[ToolSpec] = field(default_factory=list)
    max_tokens: int | None = None
    temperature: float | None = None
    reasoning: str | None = None  # None | "low" | "medium" | "high"
    cache: bool = True


class Provider(Protocol):
    """A model provider adapter."""

    name: str

    def stream(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        """Stream the model's response as :data:`ModelEvent`s."""
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetryPolicy:
    """Retry transient failures that happen before any output arrived."""

    attempts: int = 3
    base_delay: float = 1.0
    max_delay: float = 30.0


async def with_retries(
    open_stream: Callable[[], AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]],
    policy: RetryPolicy,
    sleep: Callable[[float], object] = asyncio.sleep,
) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
    """Yield from ``open_stream()``, retrying retryable errors before the first event.

    Once any event was yielded the error propagates: retrying then would
    duplicate text the caller has already shown.
    """
    attempt = 0
    while True:
        started = False
        try:
            async for event in open_stream():
                started = True
                yield event
            return
        except ProviderError as exc:
            if started or not exc.retryable or attempt + 1 >= policy.attempts:
                raise
            delay = exc.retry_after or min(policy.max_delay, policy.base_delay * 2**attempt)
            attempt += 1
            result = sleep(delay)
            if asyncio.iscoroutine(result):
                await result


_CONTEXT_MARKERS = (
    "context length",
    "context window",
    "prompt is too long",
    "too many tokens",
    "maximum context",
    "context_length_exceeded",
    "input is too long",
)


def _error_message(body: str) -> str:
    try:
        data = json.loads(body)
    except ValueError:
        return body.strip()[:500] or "no response body"
    err = data.get("error", data) if isinstance(data, dict) else data
    if isinstance(err, dict):
        return str(err.get("message") or err.get("msg") or err)[:500]
    if isinstance(data, dict) and "message" in data:
        return str(data["message"])[:500]
    return str(err)[:500]


def error_from_response(status: int, body: str, headers: Mapping[str, str]) -> ProviderError:
    """Classify a failed HTTP response."""
    message = _error_message(body)
    lowered = message.lower()
    retry_after = None
    raw = headers.get("retry-after") if headers else None
    if raw:
        try:
            retry_after = float(raw)
        except ValueError:
            retry_after = None
    if status in (401, 403):
        kind = "auth"
    elif status == 429:
        kind = "rate_limit"
    elif status in (400, 413, 422) and any(m in lowered for m in _CONTEXT_MARKERS):
        kind = "context_length"
    elif 400 <= status < 500:
        kind = "bad_request"
    elif status >= 500:
        kind = "server"
    else:
        kind = "unknown"
    return ProviderError(f"HTTP {status}: {message}", kind, status, retry_after)


def default_client() -> httpx.AsyncClient:
    """An HTTP client tuned for long model streams."""
    return httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(connect=15.0, read=600.0, write=60.0, pool=15.0))


async def post_sse(
    client: httpx.AsyncClient, url: str, *, headers: Mapping[str, str], json: dict
) -> AsyncIterator[tuple[str, str]]:
    """POST ``json`` and yield ``(event, data)`` from the SSE response."""
    try:
        async with aconnect_sse(client, "POST", url, headers=dict(headers), json=json) as source:
            response = source.response
            if response.status_code >= 400:
                body = (await response.aread()).decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
                raise error_from_response(response.status_code, body, response.headers)
            async for sse in source.aiter_sse():
                yield sse.event, sse.data
    except httpx.TimeoutException as exc:
        raise ProviderError(f"request timed out: {exc}", "network") from exc
    except httpx.TransportError as exc:
        raise ProviderError(f"network error: {exc}", "network") from exc


class StreamAccumulator:
    """Assembles model events into the assistant :class:`Message`, in order."""

    def __init__(self, provider_name: str) -> None:
        self.provider_name = provider_name
        self.usage = Usage()
        self.stop_reason = "end"
        self._segments: list[list] = []  # [kind, payload...]
        self._calls: dict[int, list] = {}

    def feed(self, event: ModelEvent) -> None:
        """Consume one event."""
        if isinstance(event, TextDelta):
            if self._segments and self._segments[-1][0] == "text":
                self._segments[-1][1].append(event.text)
            else:
                self._segments.append(["text", [event.text]])
        elif isinstance(event, ThinkingDelta):
            last = self._segments[-1] if self._segments else None
            if last is not None and last[0] == "thinking" and last[2] is None:
                last[1].append(event.text)
                last[2] = event.signature
            else:
                self._segments.append(["thinking", [event.text], event.signature])
        elif isinstance(event, ToolCallStart):
            call = ["call", event.id, event.name, [], dict(event.extra)]
            self._calls[event.index] = call
            self._segments.append(call)
        elif isinstance(event, ToolCallDelta):
            self._calls[event.index][3].append(event.args_json)
        elif isinstance(event, UsageReport):
            self.usage = self.usage + event.usage
        elif isinstance(event, StopEvent):
            self.stop_reason = event.reason

    def message(self) -> Message:
        """The assistant message so far."""
        blocks: list[Block] = []
        for seg in self._segments:
            if seg[0] == "text":
                blocks.append(Text("".join(seg[1])))
            elif seg[0] == "thinking":
                blocks.append(Thinking("".join(seg[1]), seg[2], self.provider_name))
            else:
                raw = "".join(seg[3]).strip() or "{}"
                try:
                    args = json.loads(raw)
                    if not isinstance(args, dict):
                        args = {"__invalid_json__": raw}
                except ValueError:
                    args = {"__invalid_json__": raw}
                blocks.append(ToolCall(seg[1], seg[2], args, seg[4]))
        return Message.assistant(blocks)
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/fake.py`:

```python
"""A scripted provider for tests and SDK examples."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

from luna_core.events import (
    ModelEvent,
    StopEvent,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolCallDelta,
    ToolCallStart,
    UsageReport,
)
from luna_core.messages import Message, Text, Thinking, ToolCall
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest
from luna_core.usage import Usage


class FakeProvider:
    """Replays a script: each ``stream`` call consumes the next item.

    An item is either an assistant :class:`Message` (converted to events) or
    an explicit list of :data:`ModelEvent`. Requests are recorded.
    """

    name = "fake"

    def __init__(self, script: list) -> None:
        self.script = list(script)
        self.requests: list[ModelRequest] = []

    @staticmethod
    def text(text: str) -> Message:
        """An assistant reply with plain text."""
        return Message.assistant([Text(text)])

    @staticmethod
    def call(name: str, args: dict, call_id: str | None = None) -> Message:
        """An assistant reply calling one tool."""
        return Message.assistant([ToolCall(call_id or f"call_{name}", name, args)])

    async def stream(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        """Yield the next scripted reply."""
        self.requests.append(request)
        assert self.script, "FakeProvider script exhausted"
        item = self.script.pop(0)
        events = item if isinstance(item, list) else _events_for(item)
        for event in events:
            if isinstance(event, BaseException):
                raise event
            yield event


def _events_for(message: Message) -> list[ModelEvent]:
    events: list[ModelEvent] = []
    index = 0
    for block in message.content:
        if isinstance(block, Text):
            events.append(TextDelta(block.text))
        elif isinstance(block, Thinking):
            events.append(ThinkingDelta(block.text, block.signature))
        elif isinstance(block, ToolCall):
            events.append(ToolCallStart(index, block.id, block.name, dict(block.extra)))
            events.append(ToolCallDelta(index, json.dumps(block.args)))
            index += 1
    events.append(UsageReport(Usage(input=10, output=5)))
    events.append(StopEvent("tool_use" if message.tool_calls else "end"))
    return events
```

**Files:**

- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_openai_chat.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6.
- Produces: `OpenAIChat(*, api_key, base_url="https://api.openai.com/v1", name="openai_chat", client=None, headers=None, retry=RetryPolicy(), send_reasoning=False, reasoning_param=False, max_tokens_field="max_tokens")` implementing `Provider`; `build_payload(request) -> dict` (public for tests).

Wire facts (OpenAI Chat Completions, also DeepSeek/Groq/OpenRouter/Ollama/…): `POST {base_url}/chat/completions`, `Authorization: Bearer <key>`, body `{"model", "messages", "stream": true, "stream_options": {"include_usage": true}, "tools": [{"type": "function", "function": {name, description, parameters}}]}`; stream chunks `choices[0].delta.{content, reasoning_content | reasoning, tool_calls[{index, id, function{name, arguments}}]}`, `finish_reason` (`stop|tool_calls|length|content_filter`), a final chunk with `usage {prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens, completion_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens}` (DeepSeek: `prompt_cache_hit_tokens`), then `data: [DONE]`.

```python
import pytest

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, Thinking, ToolCall, ToolResult
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, RetryPolicy, StreamAccumulator
from luna_core.providers.openai_chat import OpenAIChat
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolSpec
from luna_core.usage import Usage
from sse import Recorder, json_response, stream_response

GREP = ToolSpec("grep", "Search.", {"type": "object", "properties": {"pattern": {"type": "string"}}})


def chunk(delta=None, finish=None, usage=None):
    body = {"choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": delta or {}, "finish_reason": finish}]}
    if usage is not None:
        body = {"choices": [], "usage": usage}
    return (None, body)


async def run(provider, request):
    acc = StreamAccumulator(provider.name)
    async for ev in provider.stream(request):
        acc.feed(ev)
    return acc


def req(*messages, tools=(GREP,), **kw):
    return ModelRequest(model="gpt-x", system="be brief", messages=list(messages), tools=list(tools), **kw)


async def test_text_answer_with_usage():
    rec = Recorder(
        stream_response(
            [
                chunk({"role": "assistant", "content": "Hel"}),
                chunk({"content": "lo"}),
                chunk(finish="stop"),
                chunk(usage={"prompt_tokens": 12, "completion_tokens": 3, "prompt_tokens_details": {"cached_tokens": 8}}),
                (None, "[DONE]"),
            ]
        )
    )
    p = OpenAIChat(api_key="k", client=rec.client())
    acc = await run(p, req(Message.user("hi")))
    assert acc.message().content == [Text("Hello")]
    assert acc.usage == Usage(input=12, output=3, cache_read=8)
    assert acc.stop_reason == "end"
    sent = rec.last_json
    assert rec.requests[0].url.path == "/v1/chat/completions"
    assert rec.requests[0].headers["authorization"] == "Bearer k"
    assert sent["messages"][0] == {"role": "system", "content": "be brief"}
    assert sent["messages"][1] == {"role": "user", "content": "hi"}
    assert sent["stream"] is True and sent["stream_options"] == {"include_usage": True}
    assert sent["tools"] == [{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "grep", "description": "Search.", "parameters": GREP.parameters}}]


async def test_parallel_tool_calls_stream_in_pieces():
    rec = Recorder(
        stream_response(
            [
                chunk({"tool_calls": [{"index": 0, "id": "call_a", "type": "function", "function": {"name": "grep", "arguments": ""}}]}),
                chunk({"tool_calls": [{"index": 0, "function": {"arguments": '{"pattern"'}}]}),
                chunk({"tool_calls": [{"index": 0, "function": {"arguments": ': "x"}'}}]}),
                chunk({"tool_calls": [{"index": 1, "id": "call_b", "type": "function", "function": {"name": "grep", "arguments": '{"pattern": "y"}'}}]}),
                chunk(finish="tool_calls"),
                (None, "[DONE]"),
            ]
        )
    )
    acc = await run(OpenAIChat(api_key="k", client=rec.client()), req(Message.user("find")))
    assert acc.message().tool_calls == [ToolCall("call_a", "grep", {"pattern": "x"}), ToolCall("call_b", "grep", {"pattern": "y"})]
    assert acc.stop_reason == "tool_use"


async def test_deepseek_reasoning_content_and_cache_hits():
    rec = Recorder(
        stream_response(
            [
                chunk({"reasoning_content": "think"}),
                chunk({"content": "ok"}),
                chunk(finish="stop"),
                chunk(usage={"prompt_tokens": 100, "completion_tokens": 9, "prompt_cache_hit_tokens": 64, "completion_tokens_details": {"reasoning_tokens": 4}}),
                (None, "[DONE]"),
            ]
        )
    )
    p = OpenAIChat(api_key="k", base_url="https://api.deepseek.com", name="deepseek", client=rec.client(), send_reasoning=True)
    acc = await run(p, req(Message.user("q")))
    assert acc.message().content == [Thinking("think", None, "deepseek"), Text("ok")]
    assert acc.usage == Usage(input=100, output=9, cache_read=64, reasoning=4)
    assert rec.requests[0].url.path == "/chat/completions"


def test_history_conversion_tool_round_trip_and_images():
    p = OpenAIChat(api_key="k", name="deepseek", send_reasoning=True)
    history = [
        Message.user("look", images=[Image("image/png", "AAA")]),
        Message.assistant([Thinking("plan", None, "deepseek"), Text("checking"), ToolCall("c1", "grep", {"pattern": "x"})]),
        Message.tool_results([ToolResult("c1", (Text("3 hits"), Image("image/png", "BBB")), name="grep")]),
        Message.assistant([Thinking("other provider", "sig", "anthropic"), Text("done")]),
    ]
    msgs = p.build_payload(req(*history))["messages"]
    assert msgs[1] == {
        "role": "user",
        "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "look"}, {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": "data:image/png;base64,AAA"}}],
    }
    assert msgs[2] == {
        "role": "assistant",
        "content": "checking",
        "reasoning_content": "plan",
        "tool_calls": [{"id": "c1", "type": "function", "function": {"name": "grep", "arguments": '{"pattern": "x"}'}}],
    }
    assert msgs[3] == {"role": "tool", "tool_call_id": "c1", "content": "3 hits"}
    # images from a tool result travel in a follow-up user message (tool messages are text-only)
    assert msgs[4]["role"] == "user" and msgs[4]["content"][-1]["type"] == "image_url"
    assert msgs[5] == {"role": "assistant", "content": "done"}  # foreign thinking is never sent


def test_reasoning_effort_and_max_tokens_field():
    p = OpenAIChat(api_key="k", reasoning_param=True, max_tokens_field="max_completion_tokens")
    body = p.build_payload(req(Message.user("hi"), max_tokens=500, reasoning="high", temperature=0.2))
    assert body["max_completion_tokens"] == 500 and "max_tokens" not in body
    assert body["reasoning_effort"] == "high" and body["temperature"] == 0.2


async def test_http_error_and_retry():
    rec = Recorder(
        json_response(503, {"error": {"message": "busy"}}),
        stream_response([chunk({"content": "ok"}), chunk(finish="stop"), (None, "[DONE]")]),
    )
    p = OpenAIChat(api_key="k", client=rec.client(), retry=RetryPolicy(attempts=2, base_delay=0))
    acc = await run(p, req(Message.user("hi")))
    assert acc.message().text == "ok" and len(rec.requests) == 2
    rec = Recorder(json_response(401, {"error": {"message": "Incorrect API key"}}))
    with pytest.raises(ProviderError) as exc:
        await run(OpenAIChat(api_key="bad", client=rec.client()), req(Message.user("hi")))
    assert exc.value.kind == "auth"


async def test_error_object_inside_the_stream():
    rec = Recorder(stream_response([(None, {"error": {"message": "model overloaded", "code": 503}})]))
    with pytest.raises(ProviderError, match="model overloaded"):
        await run(OpenAIChat(api_key="k", client=rec.client(), retry=RetryPolicy(attempts=1)), req(Message.user("hi")))
```

```python
"""OpenAI Chat Completions adapter (also every OpenAI-compatible endpoint)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

import httpx

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.events import (
    ModelEvent,
    StopEvent,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolCallDelta,
    ToolCallStart,
    UsageReport,
)
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, Thinking
from luna_core.providers.base import (
    ModelRequest,
    RetryPolicy,
    default_client,
    post_sse,
    with_retries,
)
from luna_core.usage import Usage

_FINISH = {"stop": "end", "tool_calls": "tool_use", "function_call": "tool_use", "length": "max_tokens", "content_filter": "refusal"}


def _image_part(image: Image) -> dict:
    return {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": f"data:{image.media_type};base64,{image.data}"}}


def parse_usage(raw: dict) -> Usage:
    """Normalise OpenAI-style usage (incl. DeepSeek cache fields)."""
    prompt_details = raw.get("prompt_tokens_details") or {}
    completion_details = raw.get("completion_tokens_details") or {}
    cached = prompt_details.get("cached_tokens") or raw.get("prompt_cache_hit_tokens") or 0
    return Usage(
        input=int(raw.get("prompt_tokens") or 0),
        output=int(raw.get("completion_tokens") or 0),
        cache_read=int(cached),
        reasoning=int(completion_details.get("reasoning_tokens") or 0),
    )


class OpenAIChat:
    """Streams ``POST {base_url}/chat/completions``.

    ``send_reasoning`` returns earlier reasoning as ``reasoning_content``
    (DeepSeek thinking mode needs it inside a tool loop); ``reasoning_param``
    sends ``reasoning_effort`` for reasoning models.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        api_key: str | None,
        base_url: str = "https://api.openai.com/v1",
        name: str = "openai_chat",
        client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,
        headers: dict | None = None,
        retry: RetryPolicy | None = None,
        send_reasoning: bool = False,
        reasoning_param: bool = False,
        max_tokens_field: str = "max_tokens",
    ) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.client = client or default_client()
        self.headers = dict(headers or {})
        self.retry = retry or RetryPolicy()
        self.send_reasoning = send_reasoning
        self.reasoning_param = reasoning_param
        self.max_tokens_field = max_tokens_field

    def build_payload(self, request: ModelRequest) -> dict:
        """The JSON body for ``request``."""
        messages: list[dict] = []
        if request.system:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": request.system})
        for message in request.messages:
            messages.extend(self._convert(message))
        body: dict = {
            "model": request.model,
            "messages": messages,
            "stream": True,
            "stream_options": {"include_usage": True},
        }
        if request.tools:
            body["tools"] = [
                {"type": "function", "function": {"name": t.name, "description": t.description, "parameters": t.parameters}}
                for t in request.tools
            ]
        if request.max_tokens:
            body[self.max_tokens_field] = request.max_tokens
        if request.temperature is not None:
            body["temperature"] = request.temperature
        if request.reasoning and self.reasoning_param:
            body["reasoning_effort"] = request.reasoning
        return body

    def _convert(self, message: Message) -> list[dict]:
        if message.role == "user":
            images = [b for b in message.content if isinstance(b, Image)]
            if not images:
                return [{"role": "user", "content": message.text}]
            parts: list[dict] = [{"type": "text", "text": message.text}] if message.text else []
            parts.extend(_image_part(i) for i in images)
            return [{"role": "user", "content": parts}]
        if message.role == "assistant":
            out: dict = {"role": "assistant", "content": message.text or None}
            thinking = [b for b in message.content if isinstance(b, Thinking) and b.provider == self.name]
            if self.send_reasoning and thinking:
                out["reasoning_content"] = "".join(t.text for t in thinking)
            if message.tool_calls:
                out["tool_calls"] = [
                    {
                        "id": c.id,
                        "type": "function",
                        "function": {"name": c.name, "arguments": json.dumps(c.args, ensure_ascii=False)},
                    }
                    for c in message.tool_calls
                ]
            if out["content"] is None and "tool_calls" not in out:
                out["content"] = ""
            return [out]
        converted: list[dict] = []
        images: list[Image] = []
        for result in message.results:
            converted.append({"role": "tool", "tool_call_id": result.call_id, "content": result.text or "(no output)"})
            images.extend(p for p in result.content if isinstance(p, Image))
        if images:
            parts = [{"type": "text", "text": "Images returned by the tools above:"}]
            parts.extend(_image_part(i) for i in images)
            converted.append({"role": "user", "content": parts})
        return converted

    async def stream(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        """Stream one completion (retrying transient failures before output)."""
        async for event in with_retries(lambda: self._once(request), self.retry):
            yield event

    async def _once(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        headers = {"Accept": "text/event-stream", **self.headers}
        if self.api_key:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.api_key}"
        started: set[int] = set()
        stop = "end"
        url = f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions"
        async for _event, data in post_sse(self.client, url, headers=headers, json=self.build_payload(request)):
            if data.strip() == "[DONE]":
                break
            chunk = json.loads(data)
            if "error" in chunk:
                err = chunk["error"]
                message = err.get("message") if isinstance(err, dict) else str(err)
                raise ProviderError(f"stream error: {message}", "server")
            if chunk.get("usage"):
                yield UsageReport(parse_usage(chunk["usage"]))
            for choice in chunk.get("choices") or []:
                delta = choice.get("delta") or {}
                reasoning = delta.get("reasoning_content") or delta.get("reasoning")
                if reasoning:
                    yield ThinkingDelta(reasoning)
                if delta.get("content"):
                    yield TextDelta(delta["content"])
                for call in delta.get("tool_calls") or []:
                    index = int(call.get("index", 0))
                    fn = call.get("function") or {}
                    if index not in started:
                        started.add(index)
                        yield ToolCallStart(index, call.get("id") or f"call_{index}", fn.get("name", ""))
                    if fn.get("arguments"):
                        yield ToolCallDelta(index, fn["arguments"])
                if choice.get("finish_reason"):
                    stop = _FINISH.get(choice["finish_reason"], "end")
        yield StopEvent(stop)
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `AnthropicMessages(*, api_key, base_url="https://api.anthropic.com", name="anthropic", client=None, retry=None, default_max_tokens=16000, thinking_budgets={"low": 2048, "medium": 8192, "high": 24000})`; `build_payload(request)`.

Cache breakpoints (max 4): system block, last tool, last user content block. Messages alternate user/assistant; tool results are `tool_result` blocks inside a **user** message; images `{"type":"image","source":{"type":"base64","media_type","data"}}`; assistant blocks `thinking{thinking, signature}`, `text`, `tool_use{id,name,input}`. Stream events: `message_start{message{usage{input_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens, cache_creation_input_tokens}}}`, `content_block_start{index, content_block{type: text|thinking|tool_use|redacted_thinking, id, name}}`, `content_block_delta{index, delta{type: text_delta{text} | thinking_delta{thinking} | signature_delta{signature} | input_json_delta{partial_json}}}`, `content_block_stop`, `message_delta{delta{stop_reason}, usage{output_tokens}}`, `message_stop`, `ping`, `error{error{type,message}}`.
