- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/anthropic.py`:

```python
"""Anthropic Messages API adapter: prompt caching, thinking blocks, tool use."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

import httpx

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.events import (
    ModelEvent,
    StopEvent,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolCallDelta,
    ToolCallStart,
    UsageReport,
)
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, Thinking, ToolCall, ToolResult
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, RetryPolicy, default_client, post_sse, with_retries
from luna_core.usage import Usage

_CACHE = {"type": "ephemeral"}
_STOP = {"end_turn": "end", "stop_sequence": "end", "tool_use": "tool_use", "max_tokens": "max_tokens", "refusal": "refusal", "pause_turn": "end"}
_ERROR_KINDS = {"overloaded_error": "server", "api_error": "server", "rate_limit_error": "rate_limit", "authentication_error": "auth", "permission_error": "auth", "invalid_request_error": "bad_request"}


def _image(image: Image) -> dict:
    return {"type": "image", "source": {"type": "base64", "media_type": image.media_type, "data": image.data}}


class AnthropicMessages:
    """Streams ``POST {base_url}/v1/messages``."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        api_key: str | None,
        base_url: str = "https://api.anthropic.com",
        name: str = "anthropic",
        client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,
        retry: RetryPolicy | None = None,
        default_max_tokens: int = 16000,
        thinking_budgets: dict | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.client = client or default_client()
        self.retry = retry or RetryPolicy()
        self.default_max_tokens = default_max_tokens
        self.thinking_budgets = thinking_budgets or {"low": 2048, "medium": 8192, "high": 24000}

    def build_payload(self, request: ModelRequest) -> dict:
        """The JSON body for ``request``."""
        messages: list[dict] = []
        for message in request.messages:
            role, blocks = self._convert(message)
            if not blocks:
                continue
            if messages and messages[-1]["role"] == role:
                messages[-1]["content"].extend(blocks)
            else:
                messages.append({"role": role, "content": blocks})
        budget = self.thinking_budgets.get(request.reasoning or "", 0)
        max_tokens = request.max_tokens or self.default_max_tokens
        if budget and max_tokens <= budget:
            max_tokens = budget + 4096
        body: dict = {"model": request.model, "max_tokens": max_tokens, "messages": messages, "stream": True}
        if request.system:
            system = {"type": "text", "text": request.system}
            if request.cache:
                system["cache_control"] = _CACHE
            body["system"] = [system]
        if request.tools:
            tools = [{"name": t.name, "description": t.description, "input_schema": t.parameters} for t in request.tools]
            if request.cache:
                tools[-1]["cache_control"] = _CACHE
            body["tools"] = tools
        if budget:
            body["thinking"] = {"type": "enabled", "budget_tokens": budget}
        elif request.temperature is not None:
            body["temperature"] = request.temperature
        if request.cache and messages:
            last = messages[-1]
            if last["role"] == "user" and last["content"]:
                last["content"][-1] = {**last["content"][-1], "cache_control": _CACHE}
        return body

    def _convert(self, message: Message) -> tuple[str, list[dict]]:
        if message.role == "user":
            blocks: list[dict] = []
            for b in message.content:
                if isinstance(b, Text) and b.text:
                    blocks.append({"type": "text", "text": b.text})
                elif isinstance(b, Image):
                    blocks.append(_image(b))
            return "user", blocks
        if message.role == "assistant":
            blocks = []
            for b in message.content:
                if isinstance(b, Thinking):
                    if b.provider != self.name or not b.signature:
                        continue
                    if b.text:
                        blocks.append({"type": "thinking", "thinking": b.text, "signature": b.signature})
                    else:  # redacted thinking: the signature holds the encrypted data
                        blocks.append({"type": "redacted_thinking", "data": b.signature})
                elif isinstance(b, Text) and b.text:
                    blocks.append({"type": "text", "text": b.text})
                elif isinstance(b, ToolCall):
                    blocks.append({"type": "tool_use", "id": b.id, "name": b.name, "input": b.args})
            return "assistant", blocks
        blocks = []
        for r in message.content:
            if isinstance(r, ToolResult):
                parts = [{"type": "text", "text": p.text} if isinstance(p, Text) else _image(p) for p in r.content]
                blocks.append({"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": r.call_id, "content": parts or [{"type": "text", "text": "(no output)"}], "is_error": r.is_error})
        return "user", blocks

    async def stream(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        """Stream one message (retrying transient failures before output)."""
        async for event in with_retries(lambda: self._once(request), self.retry):
            yield event

    async def _once(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        headers = {"anthropic-version": "2023-06-01", "accept": "text/event-stream"}
        if self.api_key:
            headers["x-api-key"] = self.api_key
        kinds: dict[int, str] = {}
        stop = "end"
        input_usage = Usage()
        url = f"{self.base_url}/v1/messages"
        async for _name, data in post_sse(self.client, url, headers=headers, json=self.build_payload(request)):
            event = json.loads(data)
            kind = event.get("type")
            if kind == "message_start":
                u = (event.get("message") or {}).get("usage") or {}
                cache_read = int(u.get("cache_read_input_tokens") or 0)
                cache_write = int(u.get("cache_creation_input_tokens") or 0)
                input_usage = Usage(input=int(u.get("input_tokens") or 0) + cache_read + cache_write, cache_read=cache_read, cache_write=cache_write)
            elif kind == "content_block_start":
                index = int(event["index"])
                block = event.get("content_block") or {}
                kinds[index] = block.get("type", "")
                if kinds[index] == "tool_use":
                    yield ToolCallStart(index, block["id"], block["name"])
                elif kinds[index] == "redacted_thinking":
                    yield ThinkingDelta("", signature=block.get("data"))
            elif kind == "content_block_delta":
                index = int(event["index"])
                delta = event.get("delta") or {}
                dtype = delta.get("type")
                if dtype == "text_delta":
                    yield TextDelta(delta.get("text", ""))
                elif dtype == "thinking_delta":
                    yield ThinkingDelta(delta.get("thinking", ""))
                elif dtype == "signature_delta":
                    yield ThinkingDelta("", signature=delta.get("signature"))
                elif dtype == "input_json_delta":
                    yield ToolCallDelta(index, delta.get("partial_json", ""))
            elif kind == "message_delta":
                stop = _STOP.get((event.get("delta") or {}).get("stop_reason") or "", stop)
                out = int((event.get("usage") or {}).get("output_tokens") or 0)
                yield UsageReport(Usage(input_usage.input, out, input_usage.cache_read, input_usage.cache_write))
            elif kind == "error":
                err = event.get("error") or {}
                raise ProviderError(f"stream error: {err.get('message', err)}", _ERROR_KINDS.get(err.get("type", ""), "unknown"))
        yield StopEvent(stop)
```

The accumulator joins a `signature_delta` (text `""`, signature set) onto the open thinking segment — that is why `StreamAccumulator.feed` appends to the last thinking segment while its signature is still `None`.

### Task 9: OpenAI Responses adapter

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/openai_responses.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_openai_responses.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `OpenAIResponses(*, api_key, base_url="https://api.openai.com/v1", name="openai_responses", client=None, retry=None)`; `build_payload(request)`.

Wire facts: `POST {base}/responses`, Bearer auth; body `{model, instructions, input: [...items], tools: [{type: "function", name, description, parameters}], stream: true, store: false, include: ["reasoning.encrypted_content"], reasoning?: {effort, summary: "auto"}, max_output_tokens?}`. Input items: user `{"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "input_text", "text"}, {"type": "input_image", "image_url": "data:..."}]}`; assistant text `{"type": "message", "role": "assistant", "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text"}]}`; `{"type": "function_call", "call_id", "name", "arguments"}`; `{"type": "function_call_output", "call_id", "output"}`; reasoning `{"type": "reasoning", "id", "encrypted_content", "summary": [{"type": "summary_text", "text"}]}`. Stream events (each SSE `data` has a `type`): `response.output_item.added {output_index, item}`, `response.output_text.delta {output_index, delta}`, `response.function_call_arguments.delta {output_index, delta}`, `response.reasoning_summary_text.delta {delta}`, `response.output_item.done {output_index, item}`, `response.completed {response{status, incomplete_details, usage{input_tokens, output_tokens, input_tokens_details.cached_tokens, output_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens}}}`, `response.incomplete` (same shape), `response.failed {response{error{message}}}`, `error {message}`.

A reasoning item's opaque state travels in `Thinking.signature` as JSON `{"id": ..., "encrypted_content": ...}` so it round-trips with `store: false`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/openai_responses.py`:

```python
"""OpenAI Responses API adapter (stateless: ``store`` false, encrypted reasoning)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

import httpx

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.events import (
    ModelEvent,
    StopEvent,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolCallDelta,
    ToolCallStart,
    UsageReport,
)
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, Thinking, ToolCall
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, RetryPolicy, default_client, post_sse, with_retries
from luna_core.usage import Usage


def _usage(raw: dict) -> Usage:
    return Usage(
        input=int(raw.get("input_tokens") or 0),
        output=int(raw.get("output_tokens") or 0),
        cache_read=int((raw.get("input_tokens_details") or {}).get("cached_tokens") or 0),
        reasoning=int((raw.get("output_tokens_details") or {}).get("reasoning_tokens") or 0),
    )


class OpenAIResponses:
    """Streams ``POST {base_url}/responses``."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        api_key: str | None,
        base_url: str = "https://api.openai.com/v1",
        name: str = "openai_responses",
        client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,
        retry: RetryPolicy | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.client = client or default_client()
        self.retry = retry or RetryPolicy()

    def build_payload(self, request: ModelRequest) -> dict:
        """The JSON body for ``request``."""
        items: list[dict] = []
        for message in request.messages:
            items.extend(self._convert(message))
        body: dict = {
            "model": request.model,
            "input": items,
            "stream": True,
            "store": False,
            "include": ["reasoning.encrypted_content"],
        }
        if request.system:
            body["instructions"] = request.system
        if request.tools:
            body["tools"] = [
                {"type": "function", "name": t.name, "description": t.description, "parameters": t.parameters}
                for t in request.tools
            ]
        if request.reasoning:
            body["reasoning"] = {"effort": request.reasoning, "summary": "auto"}
        if request.max_tokens:
            body["max_output_tokens"] = request.max_tokens
        if request.temperature is not None and not request.reasoning:
            body["temperature"] = request.temperature
        return body

    def _convert(self, message: Message) -> list[dict]:
        if message.role == "user":
            parts: list[dict] = []
            for b in message.content:
                if isinstance(b, Text) and b.text:
                    parts.append({"type": "input_text", "text": b.text})
                elif isinstance(b, Image):
                    parts.append({"type": "input_image", "image_url": f"data:{b.media_type};base64,{b.data}"})
            return [{"role": "user", "content": parts}]
        if message.role == "assistant":
            out: list[dict] = []
            for b in message.content:
                if isinstance(b, Thinking) and b.provider == self.name and b.signature:
                    state = json.loads(b.signature)
                    summary = [{"type": "summary_text", "text": b.text}] if b.text else []
                    out.append({"type": "reasoning", "id": state["id"], "encrypted_content": state["encrypted_content"], "summary": summary})
                elif isinstance(b, Text) and b.text:
                    out.append({"type": "message", "role": "assistant", "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text": b.text}]})
                elif isinstance(b, ToolCall):
                    out.append({"type": "function_call", "call_id": b.id, "name": b.name, "arguments": json.dumps(b.args, ensure_ascii=False)})
            return out
        out = []
        images: list[dict] = []
        for r in message.results:
            out.append({"type": "function_call_output", "call_id": r.call_id, "output": r.text or "(no output)"})
            images.extend(
                {"type": "input_image", "image_url": f"data:{p.media_type};base64,{p.data}"}
                for p in r.content
                if isinstance(p, Image)
            )
        if images:
            out.append({"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Images returned by the tools above:"}, *images]})
        return out

    async def stream(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        """Stream one response (retrying transient failures before output)."""
        async for event in with_retries(lambda: self._once(request), self.retry):
            yield event

    async def _once(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        headers = {"Accept": "text/event-stream"}
        if self.api_key:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.api_key}"
        saw_call = False
        stop = "end"
        url = f"{self.base_url}/responses"
        async for _name, data in post_sse(self.client, url, headers=headers, json=self.build_payload(request)):
            event = json.loads(data)
            kind = event.get("type", "")
            if kind == "response.output_item.added":
                item = event.get("item") or {}
                if item.get("type") == "function_call":
                    saw_call = True
                    yield ToolCallStart(int(event["output_index"]), item["call_id"], item["name"])
            elif kind == "response.output_text.delta":
                yield TextDelta(event.get("delta", ""))
            elif kind == "response.reasoning_summary_text.delta":
                yield ThinkingDelta(event.get("delta", ""))
            elif kind == "response.function_call_arguments.delta":
                yield ToolCallDelta(int(event["output_index"]), event.get("delta", ""))
            elif kind == "response.output_item.done":
                item = event.get("item") or {}
                if item.get("type") == "reasoning" and item.get("encrypted_content"):
                    state = {"id": item["id"], "encrypted_content": item["encrypted_content"]}
                    yield ThinkingDelta("", signature=json.dumps(state))
            elif kind in ("response.completed", "response.incomplete"):
                response = event.get("response") or {}
                yield UsageReport(_usage(response.get("usage") or {}))
                reason = (response.get("incomplete_details") or {}).get("reason")
                if response.get("status") == "incomplete":
                    stop = "max_tokens" if reason == "max_output_tokens" else "refusal"
            elif kind == "response.failed":
                err = (event.get("response") or {}).get("error") or {}
                raise ProviderError(f"response failed: {err.get('message', err)}", "server")
            elif kind == "error":
                raise ProviderError(f"stream error: {event.get('message', event)}", "server")
        if stop == "end" and saw_call:
            stop = "tool_use"
        yield StopEvent(stop)
```

Note: the accumulator puts the signature on the open reasoning segment because `output_item.done` for the reasoning item arrives right after its summary deltas and before any text.

### Task 10: Google Gemini native adapter

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/google.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_google.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `GoogleGenAI(*, api_key, base_url="https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta", name="google", client=None, retry=None, thinking_budgets={"low": 1024, "medium": 8192, "high": 24576})`; `build_payload(request)`; `sanitize_schema(schema) -> dict`.

Wire facts (`generateContent` API — stable and supported; Google also ships the newer Interactions API, which this adapter does **not** use; if at implementation time `generateContent` is deprecated for the default models, record a Ruling and port to `/interactions` with the same tests' intent): `POST {base}/models/{model}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse`, header `x-goog-api-key`; body `{systemInstruction: {parts: [{text}]}, contents: [{role: "user"|"model", parts}], tools: [{functionDeclarations: [{name, description, parameters}]}], generationConfig: {maxOutputTokens?, temperature?, thinkingConfig?: {thinkingBudget, includeThoughts: true}}}`. Parts: `{text}`, `{text, thought: true}`, `{inlineData: {mimeType, data}}`, `{functionCall: {id?, name, args}}`, `{functionResponse: {id?, name, response: {result}}}`; any part may carry `thoughtSignature`, which must be returned on the same part. Each SSE `data` is a `GenerateContentResponse`: `candidates[0].content.parts`, `candidates[0].finishReason` (`STOP|MAX_TOKENS|SAFETY|...`), `usageMetadata {promptTokenCount, candidatesTokenCount, cachedContentTokenCount, thoughtsTokenCount}` (cumulative; the last one wins). Function args arrive whole (never as partial JSON). Gemini's schema subset rejects `additionalProperties`, `$schema`, `default`, and `title` — `sanitize_schema` strips them recursively.

Where signatures go: a `thoughtSignature` on a `functionCall` part goes into `ToolCall.extra["thought_signature"]`; one on a text part goes into a `Thinking("", signature, "google")` block placed right after that text, and is re-attached to the preceding text part when sending history.

from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, StreamAccumulator
from luna_core.providers.google import GoogleGenAI, sanitize_schema
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolSpec
from luna_core.usage import Usage
from sse import Recorder, stream_response

SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object",
    "properties": {"path": {"type": "string", "default": ".", "title": "Path"}},
    "required": [],
    "additionalProperties": False,
}
LS = ToolSpec("ls", "List.", SCHEMA)


def chunk(parts=None, finish=None, usage=None):
    body: dict = {"candidates": [{"content": {"role": "model", "parts": parts or []}}]}
    if finish:
        body["candidates"][0]["finishReason"] = finish
    if usage:
        body["usageMetadata"] = usage
    return (None, body)


async def run(p, request):
    acc = StreamAccumulator(p.name)
    async for e in p.stream(request):
        acc.feed(e)
    return acc


def req(*messages, **kw):

    rec = Recorder(
        stream_response(
            [
                chunk([{"text": "consider", "thought": True}]),
                chunk([{"text": "Listing"}]),
                chunk([{"functionCall": {"name": "ls", "args": {"path": "."}}, "thoughtSignature": "TS1"}]),
                chunk(finish="STOP", usage={"promptTokenCount": 20, "candidatesTokenCount": 6, "cachedContentTokenCount": 10, "thoughtsTokenCount": 3}),

    )

    msg = acc.message()

    assert msg.content[1] == Text("Listing")

    assert call.id.startswith("call_")
    assert acc.stop_reason == "tool_use"
    assert acc.usage == Usage(input=20, output=9, cache_read=10, reasoning=3)
    r = rec.requests[0]

    assert r.url.params["alt"] == "sse" and r.headers["x-goog-api-key"] == "k"
    body = rec.last_json

    assert body["generationConfig"]["thinkingConfig"] == {"thinkingBudget": 8192, "includeThoughts": True}
    assert body["tools"][0]["functionDeclarations"][0]["parameters"] == sanitize_schema(SCHEMA)

            Message.assistant(

async def test_text_signature_becomes_a_thinking_block():

    )

    assert (await run(p, req(Message.user("x")))).stop_reason == "max_tokens"
    assert (await run(p, req(Message.user("x")))).stop_reason == "refusal"

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/google.py`:

```python
"""Google Gemini adapter (native ``streamGenerateContent``)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import uuid
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

import httpx

from luna_core.events import (
    ModelEvent,
    StopEvent,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolCallDelta,
    ToolCallStart,
    UsageReport,
)
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, Thinking, ToolCall
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, RetryPolicy, default_client, post_sse, with_retries
from luna_core.usage import Usage

_DROP = {"additionalProperties", "$schema", "default", "title", "examples"}
_FINISH = {"STOP": "end", "MAX_TOKENS": "max_tokens", "SAFETY": "refusal", "RECITATION": "refusal", "PROHIBITED_CONTENT": "refusal", "BLOCKLIST": "refusal"}


def sanitize_schema(schema: object) -> object:
    """Drop JSON-Schema keys Gemini's schema subset rejects, recursively."""
    if isinstance(schema, dict):
        return {k: sanitize_schema(v) for k, v in schema.items() if k not in _DROP}
    if isinstance(schema, list):
        return [sanitize_schema(v) for v in schema]
    return schema


class GoogleGenAI:
    """Streams ``POST {base_url}/models/{model}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse``."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        api_key: str | None,
        base_url: str = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta",
        name: str = "google",
        client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,
        retry: RetryPolicy | None = None,
        thinking_budgets: dict | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.client = client or default_client()
        self.retry = retry or RetryPolicy()
        self.thinking_budgets = thinking_budgets or {"low": 1024, "medium": 8192, "high": 24576}

    def build_payload(self, request: ModelRequest) -> dict:
        """The JSON body for ``request``."""
        contents: list[dict] = []
        for message in request.messages:
            role, parts = self._convert(message)
            if not parts:
                continue
            if contents and contents[-1]["role"] == role:
                contents[-1]["parts"].extend(parts)
            else:
                contents.append({"role": role, "parts": parts})
        body: dict = {"contents": contents}
        if request.system:
            body["systemInstruction"] = {"parts": [{"text": request.system}]}
        if request.tools:
            body["tools"] = [
                {
                    "functionDeclarations": [
                        {"name": t.name, "description": t.description, "parameters": sanitize_schema(t.parameters)}
                        for t in request.tools
                    ]
                }
            ]
        config: dict = {}
        if request.max_tokens:
            config["maxOutputTokens"] = request.max_tokens
        if request.temperature is not None:
            config["temperature"] = request.temperature
        budget = self.thinking_budgets.get(request.reasoning or "")
        if budget:
            config["thinkingConfig"] = {"thinkingBudget": budget, "includeThoughts": True}
        if config:
            body["generationConfig"] = config
        return body

    def _convert(self, message: Message) -> tuple[str, list[dict]]:
        if message.role == "user":
            parts: list[dict] = []
            for b in message.content:
                if isinstance(b, Text) and b.text:
                    parts.append({"text": b.text})
                elif isinstance(b, Image):
                    parts.append({"inlineData": {"mimeType": b.media_type, "data": b.data}})
            return "user", parts
        if message.role == "assistant":
            parts = []
            for b in message.content:
                if isinstance(b, Thinking):
                    # Thought summaries are never sent back; a signature-only block
                    # belongs to the text part right before it.
                    if b.provider == self.name and b.signature and not b.text and parts and "text" in parts[-1]:
                        parts[-1]["thoughtSignature"] = b.signature
                elif isinstance(b, Text) and b.text:
                    parts.append({"text": b.text})
                elif isinstance(b, ToolCall):
                    part: dict = {"functionCall": {"name": b.name, "args": b.args}}
                    if b.extra.get("thought_signature"):
                        part["thoughtSignature"] = b.extra["thought_signature"]
                    parts.append(part)
            return "model", parts
        parts = []
        for r in message.results:
            parts.append({"functionResponse": {"name": r.name, "response": {"result": r.text or "(no output)"}}})
            parts.extend(
                {"inlineData": {"mimeType": p.media_type, "data": p.data}} for p in r.content if isinstance(p, Image)
            )
        return "user", parts

    async def stream(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        """Stream one generation (retrying transient failures before output)."""
        async for event in with_retries(lambda: self._once(request), self.retry):
            yield event

    async def _once(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        headers = {"x-goog-api-key": self.api_key or ""}
        url = f"{self.base_url}/models/{request.model}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse"
        index = 0
        stop = "end"
        usage: Usage | None = None
        async for _name, data in post_sse(self.client, url, headers=headers, json=self.build_payload(request)):
            chunk = json.loads(data)
            if chunk.get("usageMetadata"):
                u = chunk["usageMetadata"]
                thoughts = int(u.get("thoughtsTokenCount") or 0)
                usage = Usage(
                    input=int(u.get("promptTokenCount") or 0),
                    output=int(u.get("candidatesTokenCount") or 0) + thoughts,
                    cache_read=int(u.get("cachedContentTokenCount") or 0),
                    reasoning=thoughts,
                )
            for candidate in chunk.get("candidates") or []:
                for part in (candidate.get("content") or {}).get("parts") or []:
                    signature = part.get("thoughtSignature")
                    if "functionCall" in part:
                        fc = part["functionCall"]
                        extra = {"thought_signature": signature} if signature else {}
                        call_id = fc.get("id") or f"call_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
                        yield ToolCallStart(index, call_id, fc.get("name", ""), extra)
                        yield ToolCallDelta(index, json.dumps(fc.get("args") or {}))
                        index += 1
                        stop = "tool_use"
                    elif part.get("thought"):
                        yield ThinkingDelta(part.get("text", ""))
                    elif "text" in part:
                        if part["text"]:
                            yield TextDelta(part["text"])
                        if signature:
                            yield ThinkingDelta("", signature=signature)
                finish = candidate.get("finishReason")
                if finish and stop != "tool_use":
                    stop = _FINISH.get(finish, "end")
        if usage is not None:
            yield UsageReport(usage)
        yield StopEvent(stop)
```

Note: the thought text from `ThinkingDelta("consider")` has no signature, so a later signature-only `ThinkingDelta` after text starts a new segment (the accumulator only appends to the *last* segment, which is text by then). That is what keeps a text signature attached to its text.

### Task 11: GigaChat adapter

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/gigachat.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_gigachat.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `GigaChat(*, credentials, scope="GIGACHAT_API_PERS", base_url="https://gigachat.devices.sberbank.ru/api/v1", auth_url="https://ngw.devices.sberbank.ru:9443/api/v2/oauth", name="gigachat", client=None, retry=None, ca_bundle=None, verify=True, clock=time.time)`; `build_payload(request)`; `async token() -> str`.

Wire facts (developers.sber.ru, checked 2026-10-08): OAuth `POST {auth_url}`, headers `Authorization: Basic <credentials>`, `RqUID: <uuid4>`, `Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded`, body `scope=<scope>`; response `{access_token, expires_at}` — `expires_at` is a Unix timestamp, documented in seconds but returned in **milliseconds** by the live service, so treat values above `10**12` as ms. Token lives 30 minutes; refresh 60 s before expiry and once on a 401. Chat `POST {base_url}/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer <token>`; body `{model, messages, functions: [{name, description, parameters}], stream: true, max_tokens?, temperature?}`. The model calls **one** function per reply: `message.function_call {name, arguments}` where `arguments` is a JSON **object**, plus `functions_state_id`; `finish_reason: "function_call"`. Every assistant `function_call` in history must be followed by a `{"role": "function", "name", "content": "<json string>"}` message; the assistant message must carry its `functions_state_id` back. Stream chunks are OpenAI-shaped: `choices[0].delta.{content, function_call, functions_state_id}`, final usage `{prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, precached_prompt_tokens}`, then `[DONE]`. Newer accounts also use `https://api.giga.chat/v1` as `base_url` — it is a constructor argument, the preset keeps the classic host. Images need a separate file upload and are not supported by this adapter: image blocks become the text `[image omitted]` (Ruling recorded here so the executor does not have to make it). Sber's TLS chain uses the Russian Ministry of Digital Development root CA: `ca_bundle` points to a PEM with it; `verify=False` exists for development and logs nothing secret.

Mapping choices: `ToolCall.extra["functions_state_id"]` stores the state id; the call id is generated locally (`call_<hex>`) since GigaChat has none; tool results are matched back by order.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `packages/luna-core/tests/test_gigachat.py`:

```python
import json

import httpx
import pytest

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, ToolCall, ToolResult
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, RetryPolicy, StreamAccumulator
from luna_core.providers.gigachat import GigaChat
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolSpec
from luna_core.usage import Usage
from sse import json_response, stream_response

LS = ToolSpec("ls", "List.", {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}}})


class Router:
    """Routes OAuth and chat requests to separate response queues."""

    def __init__(self, tokens, chats):
        self.tokens = list(tokens)
        self.chats = list(chats)
        self.oauth = []
        self.chat = []

    def __call__(self, request):
        if request.url.path.endswith("/oauth"):
            self.oauth.append(request)
            return self.tokens.pop(0)
        self.chat.append(request)
        return self.chats.pop(0)

    def client(self):
        return httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(self))


def token(value, expires_ms):
    return json_response(200, {"access_token": value, "expires_at": expires_ms})


def chunk(delta=None, finish=None, usage=None):
    body = {"choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": delta or {}, "finish_reason": finish}]}
    if usage:
        body["usage"] = usage
    return (None, body)


async def run(p, request):
    acc = StreamAccumulator(p.name)
    async for e in p.stream(request):
        acc.feed(e)
    return acc


def req(*messages):
    return ModelRequest(model="GigaChat-2-Max", system="sys", messages=list(messages), tools=[LS])


async def test_oauth_then_function_call():
    router = Router(
        [token("T1", 1_900_000_000_000)],
        [
            stream_response(
                [
                    chunk({"role": "assistant", "content": ""}),
                    chunk({"function_call": {"name": "ls", "arguments": {"path": "."}}, "functions_state_id": "fs-1"}, finish="function_call"),
                    chunk(usage={"prompt_tokens": 30, "completion_tokens": 8, "precached_prompt_tokens": 12}),
                    (None, "[DONE]"),
                ]
            )
        ],
    )
    p = GigaChat(credentials="BASE64KEY", client=router.client(), clock=lambda: 1_700_000_000)
    acc = await run(p, req(Message.user("hi")))
    call = acc.message().tool_calls[0]
    assert (call.name, call.args, call.extra) == ("ls", {"path": "."}, {"functions_state_id": "fs-1"})
    assert acc.stop_reason == "tool_use"
    assert acc.usage == Usage(input=30, output=8, cache_read=12)
    oauth = router.oauth[0]
    assert oauth.headers["authorization"] == "Basic BASE64KEY" and len(oauth.headers["rquid"]) == 36
    assert oauth.content == b"scope=GIGACHAT_API_PERS"
    chat = router.chat[0]
    assert chat.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer T1"
    body = json.loads(chat.content)
    assert body["functions"] == [{"name": "ls", "description": "List.", "parameters": LS.parameters}]
    assert body["messages"][0] == {"role": "system", "content": "sys"}


async def test_token_is_cached_and_refreshed_near_expiry():
    now = {"t": 1_700_000_000}
    router = Router(
        [token("T1", (1_700_000_000 + 1800) * 1000), token("T2", (1_700_000_000 + 3600) * 1000)],
        [stream_response([chunk({"content": "a"}), (None, "[DONE]")]) for _ in range(3)],
    )
    p = GigaChat(credentials="K", client=router.client(), clock=lambda: now["t"])
    await run(p, req(Message.user("1")))
    await run(p, req(Message.user("2")))
    assert len(router.oauth) == 1
    now["t"] += 1790  # within 60 s of expiry
    await run(p, req(Message.user("3")))
    assert len(router.oauth) == 2
    assert router.chat[-1].headers["authorization"] == "Bearer T2"


async def test_401_refreshes_the_token_once():
    router = Router(
        [token("OLD", 1_900_000_000_000), token("NEW", 1_900_000_000_000)],
        [json_response(401, {"message": "Token has expired"}), stream_response([chunk({"content": "ok"}), (None, "[DONE]")])],
    )
    p = GigaChat(credentials="K", client=router.client(), clock=lambda: 1_700_000_000)
    acc = await run(p, req(Message.user("hi")))
    assert acc.message().text == "ok"
    assert [r.headers["authorization"] for r in router.chat] == ["Bearer OLD", "Bearer NEW"]


async def test_bad_credentials_are_an_auth_error():
    router = Router([json_response(401, {"message": "Authorization error: header is incorrect"})], [])
    p = GigaChat(credentials="bad", client=router.client(), retry=RetryPolicy(attempts=1))
    with pytest.raises(ProviderError) as exc:
        await run(p, req(Message.user("hi")))
    assert exc.value.kind == "auth" and "header is incorrect" in str(exc.value)


def test_history_function_messages_and_images():
    p = GigaChat(credentials="K")
    msgs = p.build_payload(
        req(
            Message.user("look", images=[Image("image/png", "AAA")]),
            Message.assistant([Text(""), ToolCall("call_1", "ls", {"path": "."}, extra={"functions_state_id": "fs-1"})]),
            Message.tool_results([ToolResult("call_1", (Text("a.py"),), name="ls")]),
        )
    )["messages"]
    assert msgs[1] == {"role": "user", "content": "look\n[image omitted]"}
    assert msgs[2] == {
        "role": "assistant",
        "content": "",
        "function_call": {"name": "ls", "arguments": {"path": "."}},
        "functions_state_id": "fs-1",
    }
    assert msgs[3] == {"role": "function", "name": "ls", "content": json.dumps({"result": "a.py"}, ensure_ascii=False)}


def test_several_calls_in_one_assistant_message_are_split():
    p = GigaChat(credentials="K")
    msgs = p.build_payload(
        req(
            Message.user("x"),
            Message.assistant([ToolCall("c1", "ls", {"path": "a"}), ToolCall("c2", "ls", {"path": "b"})]),
            Message.tool_results([ToolResult("c1", (Text("A"),), name="ls"), ToolResult("c2", (Text("B"),), name="ls")]),
        )
    )["messages"]
    assert [m["role"] for m in msgs[1:]] == ["user", "assistant", "function", "assistant", "function"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/gigachat.py`:

```python
"""GigaChat adapter: OAuth token exchange, one-function-per-reply calling."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import time
import uuid
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable

import httpx

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.events import ModelEvent, StopEvent, TextDelta, ToolCallDelta, ToolCallStart, UsageReport
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, ToolCall
from luna_core.providers.base import (
    ModelRequest,
    RetryPolicy,
    error_from_response,
    post_sse,
    with_retries,
)
from luna_core.usage import Usage

_FINISH = {"stop": "end", "function_call": "tool_use", "length": "max_tokens", "blacklist": "refusal"}
_REFRESH_MARGIN = 60.0


class GigaChat:
    """Streams GigaChat chat completions, managing the OAuth access token."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        credentials: str | None,
        scope: str = "GIGACHAT_API_PERS",
        base_url: str = "https://gigachat.devices.sberbank.ru/api/v1",
        auth_url: str = "https://ngw.devices.sberbank.ru:9443/api/v2/oauth",
        name: str = "gigachat",
        client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,
        retry: RetryPolicy | None = None,
        ca_bundle: str | None = None,
        verify: bool = True,
        clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time,
    ) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.credentials = credentials
        self.scope = scope
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.auth_url = auth_url
        self.retry = retry or RetryPolicy()
        self.clock = clock
        if client is None:
            client = httpx.AsyncClient(
                verify=ca_bundle or verify,
                timeout=httpx.Timeout(connect=15.0, read=600.0, write=60.0, pool=15.0),
            )
        self.client = client
        self._token: str | None = None
        self._expires = 0.0
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()

    async def token(self, *, force: bool = False) -> str:
        """A valid access token, fetched or refreshed as needed."""
        async with self._lock:
            if not force and self._token and self.clock() < self._expires - _REFRESH_MARGIN:
                return self._token
            if not self.credentials:
                raise ProviderError("GigaChat credentials are not set", "auth")
            headers = {
                "Authorization": f"Basic {self.credentials}",
                "RqUID": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
                "Accept": "application/json",
            }
            try:
                response = await self.client.post(self.auth_url, headers=headers, content=f"scope={self.scope}")
            except httpx.TransportError as exc:
                raise ProviderError(f"GigaChat OAuth network error: {exc}", "network") from exc
            if response.status_code >= 400:
                raise error_from_response(response.status_code, response.text, response.headers)
            data = response.json()
            expires = float(data.get("expires_at") or 0)
            self._expires = expires / 1000 if expires > 10**12 else expires
            self._token = data["access_token"]
            return self._token

    def build_payload(self, request: ModelRequest) -> dict:
        """The JSON body for ``request``."""
        messages: list[dict] = []
        if request.system:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": request.system})
        pending: dict[str, str] = {}
        for message in request.messages:
            if message.role == "user":
                text = message.text
                if any(isinstance(b, Image) for b in message.content):
                    text = f"{text}\n[image omitted]" if text else "[image omitted]"
                messages.append({"role": "user", "content": text})
            elif message.role == "assistant":
                calls = message.tool_calls
                if not calls:
                    messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": message.text})
                    continue
                # GigaChat takes one function call per assistant message; several
                # calls are replayed as call/result pairs in order.
                for i, call in enumerate(calls):
                    out: dict = {
                        "role": "assistant",
                        "content": message.text if i == 0 else "",
                        "function_call": {"name": call.name, "arguments": call.args},
                    }
                    if call.extra.get("functions_state_id"):
                        out["functions_state_id"] = call.extra["functions_state_id"]
                    messages.append(out)
                    pending[call.id] = call.name
                    messages.append({"__placeholder__": call.id})
            else:
                for result in message.results:
                    for i, m in enumerate(messages):
                        if m.get("__placeholder__") == result.call_id:
                            messages[i] = {
                                "role": "function",
                                "name": result.name or pending.get(result.call_id, ""),
                                "content": json.dumps({"result": result.text or "(no output)"}, ensure_ascii=False),
                            }
        messages = [m for m in messages if "__placeholder__" not in m]
        body: dict = {"model": request.model, "messages": messages, "stream": True}
        if request.tools:
            body["functions"] = [{"name": t.name, "description": t.description, "parameters": t.parameters} for t in request.tools]
        if request.max_tokens:
            body["max_tokens"] = request.max_tokens
        if request.temperature is not None:
            body["temperature"] = request.temperature
        return body

    async def stream(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        """Stream one completion (retrying transient failures before output)."""
        async for event in with_retries(lambda: self._once(request), self.retry):
            yield event

    async def _once(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        try:
            async for event in self._attempt(request, await self.token()):
                yield event
        except ProviderError as exc:
            if exc.kind != "auth" or exc.status != 401 or not self._token:
                raise
            # Expired or revoked token: one forced refresh, then a single retry.
            async for event in self._attempt(request, await self.token(force=True)):
                yield event

    async def _attempt(self, request: ModelRequest, token: str) -> AsyncIterator[ModelEvent]:
        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "Accept": "text/event-stream"}
        stop = "end"
        started = False
        url = f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions"
        async for _name, data in post_sse(self.client, url, headers=headers, json=self.build_payload(request)):
            if data.strip() == "[DONE]":
                break
            chunk = json.loads(data)
            if chunk.get("usage"):
                u = chunk["usage"]
                yield UsageReport(
                    Usage(
                        input=int(u.get("prompt_tokens") or 0),
                        output=int(u.get("completion_tokens") or 0),
                        cache_read=int(u.get("precached_prompt_tokens") or 0),
                    )
                )
            for choice in chunk.get("choices") or []:
                delta = choice.get("delta") or {}
                if delta.get("content"):
                    yield TextDelta(delta["content"])
                fc = delta.get("function_call")
                if fc and not started:
                    started = True
                    extra = {"functions_state_id": delta["functions_state_id"]} if delta.get("functions_state_id") else {}
                    yield ToolCallStart(0, f"call_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}", fc.get("name", ""), extra)
                    args = fc.get("arguments")
                    yield ToolCallDelta(0, args if isinstance(args, str) else json.dumps(args or {}, ensure_ascii=False))
                if choice.get("finish_reason"):
                    stop = _FINISH.get(choice["finish_reason"], "end")
        yield StopEvent(stop)
```

Caveat for the 401 retry: it happens before any event is yielded (the 401 comes from `post_sse` before streaming), so no duplicated text is possible.

  - `PRESETS: dict[str, Preset]` — `anthropic, openai, openai_chat, deepseek, google, gigachat, yandex, ollama, mistral, xai, groq, fireworks, together, openrouter, perplexity, cerebras`
  - `resolve(provider: str, *, model: str | None = None, api_key: str | None = None, base_url: str | None = None, options: dict | None = None, custom: dict[str, Preset] | None = None, env: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ) -> tuple[Provider, str]` — returns the adapter and the model id; raises `LunaCoreError` for an unknown provider or a missing key (message names the env var).

import pytest

from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.providers import PRESETS, Preset, resolve
from luna_core.providers.anthropic import AnthropicMessages
from luna_core.providers.gigachat import GigaChat
from luna_core.providers.google import GoogleGenAI
from luna_core.providers.openai_chat import OpenAIChat
from luna_core.providers.openai_responses import OpenAIResponses


def test_every_preset_resolves_with_its_key():
    for key, preset in PRESETS.items():
        env = {preset.env_var: "x"} if preset.env_var else {}
        if key == "yandex":
            env["YANDEX_FOLDER_ID"] = "b1g"
        provider, model = resolve(key, env=env)
        assert provider.name == key
        assert model


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("key", "cls"),
    [
        ("anthropic", AnthropicMessages),
        ("openai", OpenAIResponses),
        ("deepseek", OpenAIChat),
        ("google", GoogleGenAI),
        ("gigachat", GigaChat),
    ],
)
def test_preset_api_classes(key, cls):
    provider, _ = resolve(key, env={PRESETS[key].env_var: "x"})
    assert isinstance(provider, cls)


def test_missing_key_names_the_env_var():
    with pytest.raises(LunaCoreError, match="DEEPSEEK_API_KEY"):
        resolve("deepseek", env={})


def test_explicit_key_and_model_win():
    provider, model = resolve("deepseek", model="deepseek-reasoner", api_key="k", env={})
    assert model == "deepseek-reasoner" and provider.api_key == "k"
    assert provider.send_reasoning is True and provider.base_url == "https://api.deepseek.com"


def test_unknown_provider_lists_choices():
    with pytest.raises(LunaCoreError, match="anthropic"):
        resolve("nope", env={})


def test_yandex_model_uri_and_folder_header():
    provider, model = resolve("yandex", env={"YANDEX_API_KEY": "k", "YANDEX_FOLDER_ID": "b1g"})
    assert model == "gpt://b1g/yandexgpt/latest"
    assert provider.headers["OpenAI-Project"] == "b1g"
    with pytest.raises(LunaCoreError, match="YANDEX_FOLDER_ID"):
        resolve("yandex", env={"YANDEX_API_KEY": "k"})


def test_ollama_needs_no_key():
    provider, _ = resolve("ollama", env={})
    assert provider.api_key is None


def test_custom_provider_cannot_shadow_builtin():
    custom = {
        "anthropic": Preset("anthropic", "openai_chat", "m", None, "https://evil.example/v1"),
        "local": Preset("local", "openai_chat", "llama", None, "http://localhost:8080/v1"),
    }
    provider, _ = resolve("anthropic", custom=custom, env={"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "x"})
    assert isinstance(provider, AnthropicMessages)
    provider, model = resolve("local", custom=custom, env={})
    assert provider.base_url == "http://localhost:8080/v1" and model == "llama"


def test_gigachat_options_from_env():

        "gigachat",
        env={"GIGACHAT_CREDENTIALS": "c", "GIGACHAT_SCOPE": "GIGACHAT_API_CORP"},
    )
    assert provider.scope == "GIGACHAT_API_CORP"

`packages/luna-core/tests/test_provider_contract.py` — the same scenario through every adapter: a plain answer and a tool round trip must produce the same normalised `Message`s:

```python
import json

import pytest

from luna_core.messages import Message, Text, ToolCall, ToolResult
from luna_core.providers.anthropic import AnthropicMessages
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, StreamAccumulator
from luna_core.providers.gigachat import GigaChat
from luna_core.providers.google import GoogleGenAI
from luna_core.providers.openai_chat import OpenAIChat
from luna_core.providers.openai_responses import OpenAIResponses
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolSpec
from sse import Recorder, json_response, stream_response

LS = ToolSpec("ls", "List.", {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}}, "required": []})


def openai_chat_tool():
    return [
        (None, {"choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": {"tool_calls": [{"index": 0, "id": "c1", "function": {"name": "ls", "arguments": '{"path": "."}'}}]}, "finish_reason": "tool_calls"}]}),
        (None, "[DONE]"),
    ]


def anthropic_tool():
    return [
        ("content_block_start", {"type": "content_block_start", "index": 0, "content_block": {"type": "tool_use", "id": "c1", "name": "ls"}}),
        ("content_block_delta", {"type": "content_block_delta", "index": 0, "delta": {"type": "input_json_delta", "partial_json": '{"path": "."}'}}),
        ("message_delta", {"type": "message_delta", "delta": {"stop_reason": "tool_use"}, "usage": {"output_tokens": 1}}),
    ]


def responses_tool():
    return [
        (None, {"type": "response.output_item.added", "output_index": 0, "item": {"type": "function_call", "call_id": "c1", "name": "ls"}}),
        (None, {"type": "response.function_call_arguments.delta", "output_index": 0, "delta": '{"path": "."}'}),
        (None, {"type": "response.completed", "response": {"status": "completed", "usage": {}}}),
    ]


def google_tool():
    return [(None, {"candidates": [{"content": {"parts": [{"functionCall": {"id": "c1", "name": "ls", "args": {"path": "."}}}]}, "finishReason": "STOP"}]})]


def gigachat_tool():
    return [(None, {"choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": {"function_call": {"name": "ls", "arguments": {"path": "."}}}, "finish_reason": "function_call"}]}), (None, "[DONE]")]


CASES = [
    ("openai_chat", lambda c: OpenAIChat(api_key="k", client=c), openai_chat_tool),
    ("anthropic", lambda c: AnthropicMessages(api_key="k", client=c), anthropic_tool),
    ("openai_responses", lambda c: OpenAIResponses(api_key="k", client=c), responses_tool),
    ("google", lambda c: GoogleGenAI(api_key="k", client=c), google_tool),
    ("gigachat", lambda c: GigaChat(credentials="k", client=c, clock=lambda: 0), gigachat_tool),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "make", "script"), CASES, ids=[c[0] for c in CASES])
async def test_tool_call_normalises_identically(name, make, script):
    responses = [stream_response(script())]
    if name == "gigachat":
        responses.insert(0, json_response(200, {"access_token": "t", "expires_at": 10**13}))
    rec = Recorder(*responses)
    provider = make(rec.client())
    acc = StreamAccumulator(provider.name)
    history = [Message.user("list files")]
    async for ev in provider.stream(ModelRequest(model="m", system="s", messages=history, tools=[LS])):
        acc.feed(ev)
    msg = acc.message()
    assert [(c.name, c.args) for c in msg.tool_calls] == [("ls", {"path": "."})]
    assert acc.stop_reason == "tool_use"
    # The produced history converts back without error and keeps the call/result pairing.
    call = msg.tool_calls[0]
    follow = history + [msg, Message.tool_results([ToolResult(call.id, (Text("a.py"),), name="ls")])]
    payload = provider.build_payload(ModelRequest(model="m", system="s", messages=follow, tools=[LS]))
    assert "a.py" in json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
    assert ToolCall  # imported for readers: the normalised type under test
```

import os

from luna_core.messages import Message
from luna_core.providers import PRESETS, resolve
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, StreamAccumulator
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolSpec

pytestmark = pytest.mark.live
ECHO = ToolSpec("echo", "Echo the text back.", {"type": "object", "properties": {"text": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["text"]})
LIVE = ["anthropic", "openai", "deepseek", "google", "gigachat", "yandex", "openrouter"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("key", LIVE)
async def test_live_tool_call(key):
    preset = PRESETS[key]
    if preset.env_var and not os.environ.get(preset.env_var):
        pytest.skip(f"{preset.env_var} not set")
    provider, model = resolve(key)
    acc = StreamAccumulator(provider.name)
    request = ModelRequest(
        model=model,
        system="Always call the echo tool with the user's text. Do not answer in prose.",
        messages=[Message.user("привет")],
        tools=[ECHO],
        max_tokens=256,
    )
    async for ev in provider.stream(request):
        acc.feed(ev)
    assert acc.message().tool_calls, acc.message()
    assert acc.usage.input > 0

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/providers/__init__.py`:

```python
"""Model providers: adapters for the wire APIs and the registry of presets."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, Provider, RetryPolicy, StreamAccumulator
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider

APIS = ("openai_chat", "openai_responses", "anthropic", "google", "gigachat")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Preset:
    """A named provider: which wire API, default model, key variable, endpoint."""

    key: str
    api: str
    default_model: str
    env_var: str | None
    base_url: str | None = None
    options: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


def _p(key: str, api: str, model: str, env: str | None, url: str | None = None, **options: object) -> Preset:
    return Preset(key, api, model, env, url, dict(options))


PRESETS: dict[str, Preset] = {
    p.key: p
    for p in (
        _p("anthropic", "anthropic", "claude-sonnet-4-5", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"),
        _p("openai", "openai_responses", "gpt-5", "OPENAI_API_KEY"),
        _p("openai_chat", "openai_chat", "gpt-4.1", "OPENAI_API_KEY", max_tokens_field="max_completion_tokens"),
        _p("deepseek", "openai_chat", "deepseek-chat", "DEEPSEEK_API_KEY", "https://api.deepseek.com", send_reasoning=True),
        _p("google", "google", "gemini-2.5-pro", "GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
        _p("gigachat", "gigachat", "GigaChat-2-Max", "GIGACHAT_CREDENTIALS"),
        _p("yandex", "openai_chat", "yandexgpt/latest", "YANDEX_API_KEY", "https://llm.api.cloud.yandex.net/v1"),
        _p("ollama", "openai_chat", "qwen2.5-coder", None, "http://localhost:11434/v1"),
        _p("mistral", "openai_chat", "mistral-large-latest", "MISTRAL_API_KEY", "https://api.mistral.ai/v1"),
        _p("xai", "openai_chat", "grok-4", "XAI_API_KEY", "https://api.x.ai/v1"),
        _p("groq", "openai_chat", "openai/gpt-oss-120b", "GROQ_API_KEY", "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1"),
        _p("fireworks", "openai_chat", "accounts/fireworks/models/qwen3p5-397b-a17b", "FIREWORKS_API_KEY", "https://api.fireworks.ai/inference/v1"),
        _p("together", "openai_chat", "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct", "TOGETHER_API_KEY", "https://api.together.xyz/v1"),
        _p("openrouter", "openai_chat", "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6", "OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"),
        _p("perplexity", "openai_chat", "sonar", "PPLX_API_KEY", "https://api.perplexity.ai"),
        _p("cerebras", "openai_chat", "gpt-oss-120b", "CEREBRAS_API_KEY", "https://api.cerebras.ai/v1"),
    )
}


def build(api: str, **kwargs: object) -> Provider:
    """Instantiate the adapter for ``api`` (imports lazily)."""
    if api == "openai_chat":
        from luna_core.providers.openai_chat import OpenAIChat

        return OpenAIChat(**kwargs)
    if api == "openai_responses":
        from luna_core.providers.openai_responses import OpenAIResponses

        return OpenAIResponses(**kwargs)
    if api == "anthropic":
        from luna_core.providers.anthropic import AnthropicMessages

        return AnthropicMessages(**kwargs)
    if api == "google":
        from luna_core.providers.google import GoogleGenAI

        return GoogleGenAI(**kwargs)
    if api == "gigachat":
        from luna_core.providers.gigachat import GigaChat

        return GigaChat(**kwargs)
    raise LunaCoreError(f"unknown provider API {api!r}; expected one of {', '.join(APIS)}")


def resolve(
    provider: str,
    *,
    model: str | None = None,
    api_key: str | None = None,
    base_url: str | None = None,
    options: dict | None = None,
    custom: dict[str, Preset] | None = None,
    env: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ,
) -> tuple[Provider, str]:
    """Build the adapter for a provider key and return it with the model id.

    Built-in presets always win over ``custom`` entries with the same key,
    so a stray config entry can never redirect ``anthropic`` elsewhere.
    """
    registry = {**(custom or {}), **PRESETS}
    preset = registry.get(provider)
    if preset is None:
        raise LunaCoreError(f"Unknown provider {provider!r}. Choose one of: {', '.join(sorted(registry))}.")
    key = api_key or (env.get(preset.env_var) if preset.env_var else None)
    if preset.env_var and not key:
        raise LunaCoreError(f"No API key for {provider}: set {preset.env_var} or pass api_key.")
    model_id = model or preset.default_model
    kwargs: dict = {"name": preset.key, **preset.options, **(options or {})}
    url = base_url or preset.base_url
    if url:
        kwargs["base_url"] = url
    if preset.api == "gigachat":
        kwargs["credentials"] = key
        if env.get("GIGACHAT_SCOPE"):
            kwargs.setdefault("scope", env["GIGACHAT_SCOPE"])
        if env.get("GIGACHAT_CA_BUNDLE"):
            kwargs.setdefault("ca_bundle", env["GIGACHAT_CA_BUNDLE"])
    else:
        kwargs["api_key"] = key
    if preset.key == "yandex":
        folder = env.get("YANDEX_FOLDER_ID")
        if not folder:
            raise LunaCoreError("Yandex AI Studio needs YANDEX_FOLDER_ID (the cloud folder id).")
        if not model_id.startswith("gpt://"):
            model_id = f"gpt://{folder}/{model_id}"
        kwargs["headers"] = {"OpenAI-Project": folder, **kwargs.get("headers", {})}
    return build(preset.api, **kwargs), model_id


__all__ = [
    "APIS",
    "PRESETS",
    "FakeProvider",
    "ModelRequest",
    "Preset",
    "Provider",
    "RetryPolicy",
    "StreamAccumulator",
    "build",
    "resolve",
]
```

## Phase C — Runtime

### Task 13: Workspace and sandboxes

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/workspace.py`, `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/sandbox.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_workspace.py`, `packages/luna-core/tests/test_sandbox.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ToolError` (Task 3).
- Produces:
  - `OutsideWorkspace(ToolError)`
  - `Workspace(root)`: `.root: Path` (resolved), `.resolve(path: str) -> Path`, `.rel(path: str | Path) -> str` (POSIX, `"."` for the root), `.ignored(rel: str) -> bool`, `IGNORED_DIRS`
  - `RunResult(exit_code: int, output: str, timed_out: bool = False, cancelled: bool = False)`
  - `Sandbox` protocol: `async run(command, *, cwd: Path, timeout: float, env: dict | None = None, cancel: asyncio.Event | None = None) -> RunResult`; `async close()`
  - `LocalSandbox(max_output=200_000)`
  - `DockerSandbox(root, *, image="python:3.12-slim", network="none", docker="docker", extra_args=())`: `async start()`, `.container_id`, `run`, `close`

Path rule (spec "workspace" + Review Focus 4): relative paths are project-relative; `""`, `"."` and `"/"` mean the root. An absolute path is accepted when it is under the real root. Any other absolute path `/<first>/...` is treated as project-relative (today's virtual mode, `/src/a.py`) **only if** `<first>` exists in the project or does **not** exist on the host's filesystem root; otherwise (`/Users/...`, `/tmp/...`, `/etc/...`, `C:\...`) it is an `OutsideWorkspace` error. `..` and symlinks that leave the root are errors too. This is what stopped the stray `Users/` directory the old virtual mode created in the repo.

import os
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from luna_core.workspace import OutsideWorkspace, Workspace


@pytest.fixture
def ws(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "src").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "src" / "a.py").write_text("x = 1\n")
    return Workspace(tmp_path)


def test_relative_and_root_forms(ws):
    assert ws.resolve("src/a.py") == ws.root / "src" / "a.py"
    assert ws.resolve("") == ws.root and ws.resolve(".") == ws.root and ws.resolve("/") == ws.root
    assert ws.rel(ws.root) == "." and ws.rel(ws.root / "src" / "a.py") == "src/a.py"


def test_virtual_root_paths_map_into_the_project(ws):
    assert ws.resolve("/src/a.py") == ws.root / "src" / "a.py"
    assert ws.resolve("/brand-new-dir/x.md") == ws.root / "brand-new-dir" / "x.md"


def test_real_absolute_path_inside_the_root_is_accepted(ws):
    assert ws.resolve(str(ws.root / "src" / "a.py")) == ws.root / "src" / "a.py"


def test_outside_path_is_an_error_not_remapped(ws):
    host_dir = next(p for p in ("/Users", "/home", "/tmp", "/etc") if Path(p).is_dir())
    with pytest.raises(OutsideWorkspace, match="outside the project"):
        ws.resolve(f"{host_dir}/me/Desktop/x.txt")
    assert not (ws.root / host_dir.lstrip("/")).exists()


def test_dotdot_escape_is_an_error(ws):
    with pytest.raises(OutsideWorkspace):
        ws.resolve("src/../../etc/passwd")


def test_symlink_escape_is_an_error(ws, tmp_path_factory):
    outside = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("outside")
    os.symlink(outside, ws.root / "link")
    with pytest.raises(OutsideWorkspace):
        ws.resolve("link/secret.txt")


def test_ignored_dirs(ws):
    assert ws.ignored(".git/config") and ws.ignored("node_modules/x/y.js") and ws.ignored(".luna/undo/a")
    assert not ws.ignored("src/a.py")

import asyncio

import sys
import time

import pytest

from luna_core.sandbox import DockerSandbox, LocalSandbox


async def test_local_runs_and_merges_output(tmp_path):
    r = await LocalSandbox().run("echo out; echo err 1>&2; exit 3", cwd=tmp_path, timeout=10)
    assert r.exit_code == 3 and "out" in r.output and "err" in r.output and not r.timed_out


async def test_local_timeout_kills_the_process_group(tmp_path):
    marker = tmp_path / "late"
    started = time.monotonic()
    r = await LocalSandbox().run(f"(sleep 3; touch {marker}) & sleep 30", cwd=tmp_path, timeout=0.5)
    assert r.timed_out and time.monotonic() - started < 5
    await asyncio.sleep(3.5)
    assert not marker.exists()  # the background child died with the group


async def test_local_cancel_event_stops_the_command(tmp_path):
    cancel = asyncio.Event()
    task = asyncio.create_task(LocalSandbox().run("sleep 30", cwd=tmp_path, timeout=60, cancel=cancel))
    await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
    cancel.set()
    r = await asyncio.wait_for(task, 5)
    assert r.cancelled


async def test_local_output_is_capped(tmp_path):
    r = await LocalSandbox(max_output=1000).run(
        f"{sys.executable} -c \"print('x' * 50000)\"", cwd=tmp_path, timeout=10
    )
    assert len(r.output) < 1200 and "truncated" in r.output

async def test_docker_sandbox_lifecycle(tmp_path, fake_docker):

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/workspace.py`:

```python
"""Path confinement: every agent path resolves inside the project root."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath

from luna_core.errors import ToolError

IGNORED_DIRS = frozenset(
    {".git", ".hg", ".svn", "node_modules", ".venv", "venv", "__pycache__", ".mypy_cache", ".pytest_cache", ".ruff_cache", ".luna", "dist", "build", ".tox"}
)


class OutsideWorkspace(ToolError):
    """The agent addressed a path outside the project."""


class Workspace:
    """The project root and the rules for resolving agent paths into it."""

    def __init__(self, root: str | os.PathLike) -> None:
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()

    def resolve(self, path: str) -> Path:
        """Return the absolute path for an agent path, or raise :class:`OutsideWorkspace`."""
        raw = str(path or "").strip()
        if raw in ("", ".", "/"):
            return self.root
        candidate = Path(raw).expanduser()
        if candidate.is_absolute():
            if not self._under(candidate.resolve()):
                parts = PurePosixPath(raw.replace("\\", "/")).parts
                first = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else ""
                on_host = bool(first) and Path("/", first).exists()
                in_project = bool(first) and (self.root / first).exists()
                if (on_host and not in_project) or (len(raw) > 1 and raw[1] == ":"):
                    raise OutsideWorkspace(f"outside the project: {raw} (project root is {self.root})")
                candidate = self.root.joinpath(*parts[1:])
        else:
            candidate = self.root / candidate
        resolved = candidate.resolve()
        if not self._under(resolved):
            raise OutsideWorkspace(f"outside the project: {raw} (project root is {self.root})")
        return resolved

    def rel(self, path: str | Path) -> str:
        """Project-relative POSIX form of an absolute path inside the root."""
        p = Path(path)
        if not p.is_absolute():
            p = self.resolve(str(path))
        rel = p.resolve().relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
        return rel or "."

    @staticmethod
    def ignored(rel: str) -> bool:
        """True for paths inside VCS, dependency, cache or Luna state directories."""
        return any(part in IGNORED_DIRS for part in PurePosixPath(rel).parts)

    def _under(self, path: Path) -> bool:
        try:
            path.relative_to(self.root)
        except ValueError:
            return False
        return True
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/sandbox.py`:

```python
"""Where shell commands run: on the host or in a per-session Docker container."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import os
import signal
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunResult:
    """Outcome of one command."""

    exit_code: int
    output: str
    timed_out: bool = False
    cancelled: bool = False


class Sandbox(Protocol):
    """Runs shell commands for the ``execute`` tool."""

    async def run(
        self,
        command: str,
        *,
        cwd: Path,
        timeout: float,
        env: dict | None = None,
        cancel: asyncio.Event | None = None,
    ) -> RunResult:
        """Run ``command`` and return its merged stdout/stderr."""
        ...

    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Release resources (containers)."""
        ...


def _cap(data: bytes, limit: int) -> str:
    text = data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    if len(text) <= limit:
        return text
    head, tail = text[: limit // 2], text[-limit // 2 :]
    return f"{head}\n… [{len(text) - limit} characters truncated] …\n{tail}"


async def _run_process(
    argv_or_cmd,
    *,
    shell: bool,
    cwd: Path | None,
    timeout: float,
    env: dict | None,
    cancel: asyncio.Event | None,
    limit: int,
) -> RunResult:
    kwargs = dict(
        stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=asyncio.subprocess.STDOUT,
        start_new_session=True,
        cwd=str(cwd) if cwd else None,
        env=env,
    )
    if shell:
        proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_shell(argv_or_cmd, **kwargs)
    else:
        proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(*argv_or_cmd, **kwargs)
    reader = asyncio.ensure_future(proc.stdout.read())
    cancel_wait = asyncio.ensure_future(cancel.wait()) if cancel else None
    waiters = {reader} | ({cancel_wait} if cancel_wait else set())
    timed_out = cancelled = False
    try:
        done, _ = await asyncio.wait(waiters, timeout=timeout, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)
        if reader not in done:
            cancelled = cancel_wait is not None and cancel_wait in done
            timed_out = not cancelled
            _kill_group(proc)
    except asyncio.CancelledError:
        _kill_group(proc)
        reader.cancel()
        raise
    finally:
        if cancel_wait:
            cancel_wait.cancel()
    if timed_out or cancelled:
        try:
            data = await asyncio.wait_for(reader, 2)
        except (TimeoutError, asyncio.CancelledError):
            data = b""
    else:
        data = reader.result()
    with contextlib.suppress(TimeoutError):
        await asyncio.wait_for(proc.wait(), 5)
    code = proc.returncode if proc.returncode is not None else -1
    output = _cap(data, limit)
    if timed_out:
        output += f"\n[timed out after {timeout:g}s]"
    if cancelled:
        output += "\n[cancelled]"
    return RunResult(code, output, timed_out, cancelled)


def _kill_group(proc: asyncio.subprocess.Process) -> None:
    with contextlib.suppress(ProcessLookupError, PermissionError, OSError):
        os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    with contextlib.suppress(ProcessLookupError):
        proc.kill()


class LocalSandbox:
    """Runs commands on the host in their own process group (killed on timeout/cancel)."""

    def __init__(self, max_output: int = 200_000) -> None:
        self.max_output = max_output

    async def run(self, command: str, *, cwd: Path, timeout: float, env: dict | None = None, cancel: asyncio.Event | None = None) -> RunResult:
        """Run ``command`` with ``sh`` in ``cwd``."""
        full_env = {**os.environ, **(env or {})}
        return await _run_process(command, shell=True, cwd=cwd, timeout=timeout, env=full_env, cancel=cancel, limit=self.max_output)

    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Nothing to release."""


class DockerSandbox:
    """One container per session; the project is mounted at ``/workspace``."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        root: str | os.PathLike,
        *,
        image: str = "python:3.12-slim",
        network: str = "none",
        docker: str = "docker",
        extra_args: tuple[str, ...] = (),
        max_output: int = 200_000,
    ) -> None:
        if network not in ("none", "bridge"):
            raise ValueError("network must be 'none' or 'bridge'")
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()
        self.image = image
        self.network = network
        self.docker = docker
        self.extra_args = tuple(extra_args)
        self.max_output = max_output
        self.container_id: str | None = None
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()

    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Start the container (idempotent)."""
        async with self._lock:
            if self.container_id:
                return
            argv = [self.docker, "run", "-d", "--rm", "--network", self.network, "-v", f"{self.root}:/workspace", "-w", "/workspace", *self.extra_args, self.image, "sleep", "infinity"]
            result = await _run_process(argv, shell=False, cwd=None, timeout=120, env=None, cancel=None, limit=10_000)
            if result.exit_code != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"docker run failed: {result.output.strip()}")
            self.container_id = result.output.strip().splitlines()[-1]

    async def run(self, command: str, *, cwd: Path, timeout: float, env: dict | None = None, cancel: asyncio.Event | None = None) -> RunResult:
        """Run ``command`` inside the container, in the matching ``/workspace`` subdirectory."""
        await self.start()
        try:
            rel = Path(cwd).resolve().relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
        except ValueError:
            rel = "."
        workdir = "/workspace" if rel == "." else f"/workspace/{rel}"
        env_args = [arg for k, v in (env or {}).items() for arg in ("-e", f"{k}={v}")]
        argv = [self.docker, "exec", "-w", workdir, *env_args, self.container_id, "sh", "-lc", command]
        return await _run_process(argv, shell=False, cwd=None, timeout=timeout, env=None, cancel=cancel, limit=self.max_output)

    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Remove the container."""
        if self.container_id:
            cid, self.container_id = self.container_id, None
            await _run_process([self.docker, "rm", "-f", cid], shell=False, cwd=None, timeout=60, env=None, cancel=None, limit=10_000)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        """Readable form for notices."""
        return f"DockerSandbox(image={self.image!r}, network={self.network!r}, container={self.container_id!r})"

```

The fake `docker` in the test runs `exec` commands on the host, which is why the second log line shows the exact `exec` argv and the output is real.

### Task 14: File tools

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/tools/files.py`, `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/tools/__init__.py` (exports)
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_file_tools.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `tool`, `Tool`, `ToolContext`, `ToolOutput` (Task 5); `Workspace` (Task 13); `Image` (Task 2).
- Produces: `ls`, `read_file`, `write_file`, `edit_file`, `delete`, `glob`, `grep` (each a `Tool`); `FILE_TOOLS: list[Tool]`; `luna_core.tools.builtin_tools() -> list[Tool]` (extended by Tasks 15 and 22).
- Session state keys used: `ctx.state["seen"]: set[str]` — project-relative paths read or written this session (write-guard for overwriting existing files).

Behaviour (kept from deepagents so prompts and skills keep working, with shorter descriptions):
- `read_file(file_path, offset=0, limit=100)`: numbered lines `cat -n` style (`{n:>6}\t{line}`), lines over 2 000 chars clipped, a 40 000-character budget per call, and a trailing hint `… N more lines; read again with offset=X`. PNG/JPEG/GIF/WEBP files return an `Image` block. Binary files → error.
- `write_file(file_path, content)`: creates parents; overwriting an **existing** file requires that it was read earlier in the session (`seen`), else error "read it first".
- `edit_file(file_path, old_string, new_string, replace_all=False)`: `old_string` must occur exactly once unless `replace_all`; errors name the count; `old_string == new_string` → error.
- `delete(file_path)`: removes a file or a directory tree; the root itself cannot be deleted.
- `glob(pattern, path=".")`: matches sorted newest first, ignored dirs skipped, max 200.
- `grep(pattern, path=".", glob=None, output_mode="files_with_matches", max_count=None)`: `output_mode` ∈ `files_with_matches | content | count`; ripgrep when on PATH, Python `re` fallback; max 200 result lines.
