`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/tools/files.py`:

```python
"""Filesystem tools confined to the workspace."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import base64
import fnmatch
import re
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from luna_core.messages import Image
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolContext, ToolOutput, tool
from luna_core.workspace import IGNORED_DIRS

_RG = shutil.which("rg")
READ_LIMIT = 100
READ_BUDGET = 40_000
LINE_CLIP = 2_000
MAX_RESULTS = 200
_IMAGE_TYPES = {".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg", ".jpeg": "image/jpeg", ".gif": "image/gif", ".webp": "image/webp"}


def _seen(ctx: ToolContext) -> set[str]:
    return ctx.state.setdefault("seen", set())


@tool()
async def ls(ctx: ToolContext, path: str = ".") -> ToolOutput:
    """List a directory (directories end with /).

    Args:
        path: Directory relative to the project root.
    """
    target = ctx.workspace.resolve(path)
    if not target.is_dir():
        return ToolOutput.error(f"not a directory: {path}")

    def run() -> list[str]:
        rows = []
        for child in sorted(target.iterdir(), key=lambda p: (not p.is_dir(), p.name.lower())):
            rel = ctx.workspace.rel(child)
            rows.append(rel + "/" if child.is_dir() else rel)
        return rows

    rows = await asyncio.to_thread(run)
    return ToolOutput.text("\n".join(rows) or "(empty directory)", f"{len(rows)} entries")


@tool()
async def read_file(ctx: ToolContext, file_path: str, offset: int = 0, limit: int = READ_LIMIT) -> ToolOutput:
    """Read a text file with line numbers, or an image.

    Args:
        file_path: File relative to the project root.
        offset: First line to read (0-based).
        limit: Maximum number of lines.
    """
    target = ctx.workspace.resolve(file_path)
    rel = ctx.workspace.rel(target)
    if not target.is_file():
        return ToolOutput.error(f"file not found: {rel}")
    media = _IMAGE_TYPES.get(target.suffix.lower())
    data = await asyncio.to_thread(target.read_bytes)
    if media:
        _seen(ctx).add(rel)
        return ToolOutput((Image(media, base64.b64encode(data).decode()),), False, f"image {rel}")
    if b"\x00" in data[:8192]:
        return ToolOutput.error(f"{rel} is a binary file")
    lines = data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()
    start = max(0, offset)
    out: list[str] = []
    used = 0
    end = start
    for number in range(start, min(len(lines), start + max(1, limit))):
        line = lines[number]
        if len(line) > LINE_CLIP:
            line = line[:LINE_CLIP] + " … [line clipped]"
        row = f"{number + 1:>6}\t{line}"
        if used + len(row) > READ_BUDGET and out:
            break
        out.append(row)
        used += len(row) + 1
        end = number + 1
    _seen(ctx).add(rel)
    if not lines:
        return ToolOutput.text("(empty file)", rel)
    if end < len(lines):
        out.append(f"… {len(lines) - end} more lines; read again with offset={end}")
    return ToolOutput.text("\n".join(out), f"{rel} {start + 1}-{end}")


@tool(mutating=True)
async def write_file(ctx: ToolContext, file_path: str, content: str) -> ToolOutput:
    """Create a file, or overwrite one you have already read.

    Args:
        file_path: File relative to the project root.
        content: Full new content.
    """
    target = ctx.workspace.resolve(file_path)
    rel = ctx.workspace.rel(target)
    if target.is_dir():
        return ToolOutput.error(f"{rel} is a directory")
    if target.exists() and rel not in _seen(ctx):
        return ToolOutput.error(f"{rel} already exists — read it first, then edit_file or write_file")

    def run() -> None:
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")

    await asyncio.to_thread(run)
    _seen(ctx).add(rel)
    return ToolOutput.text(f"wrote {rel} ({len(content.splitlines())} lines)", f"wrote {rel}")


@tool(mutating=True)
async def edit_file(ctx: ToolContext, file_path: str, old_string: str, new_string: str, replace_all: bool = False) -> ToolOutput:
    """Replace exact text in a file.

    Args:
        file_path: File relative to the project root.
        old_string: Exact text to replace; must be unique unless replace_all.
        new_string: Replacement text.
        replace_all: Replace every occurrence.
    """
    target = ctx.workspace.resolve(file_path)
    rel = ctx.workspace.rel(target)
    if not target.is_file():
        return ToolOutput.error(f"file not found: {rel}")
    if old_string == new_string:
        return ToolOutput.error("old_string and new_string are identical")
    text = await asyncio.to_thread(target.read_text, encoding="utf-8")
    count = text.count(old_string) if old_string else 0
    if count == 0:
        return ToolOutput.error(f"old_string not found in {rel}")
    if count > 1 and not replace_all:
        return ToolOutput.error(f"old_string occurs {count} times in {rel}; add context to make it unique or set replace_all")
    updated = text.replace(old_string, new_string) if replace_all else text.replace(old_string, new_string, 1)
    await asyncio.to_thread(target.write_text, updated, encoding="utf-8")
    _seen(ctx).add(rel)
    n = count if replace_all else 1
    return ToolOutput.text(f"edited {rel} ({n} replacement{'s' if n > 1 else ''})", f"edited {rel}")


@tool(mutating=True)
async def delete(ctx: ToolContext, file_path: str) -> ToolOutput:
    """Delete a file or directory.

    Args:
        file_path: Path relative to the project root.
    """
    target = ctx.workspace.resolve(file_path)
    if target == ctx.workspace.root:
        return ToolOutput.error("refusing to delete the project root")
    rel = ctx.workspace.rel(target)
    if not target.exists():
        return ToolOutput.error(f"not found: {rel}")
    if target.is_dir() and not target.is_symlink():
        await asyncio.to_thread(shutil.rmtree, target)
    else:
        await asyncio.to_thread(target.unlink)
    _seen(ctx).discard(rel)
    return ToolOutput.text(f"deleted {rel}", f"deleted {rel}")


@tool()
async def glob(ctx: ToolContext, pattern: str, path: str = ".") -> ToolOutput:
    """Find files by glob pattern, newest first.

    Args:
        pattern: Glob such as "**/*.py".
        path: Directory to search from.
    """
    base = ctx.workspace.resolve(path)

    def run() -> list[str]:
        hits = []
        for p in base.glob(pattern):
            rel = ctx.workspace.rel(p)
            if p.is_file() and not ctx.workspace.ignored(rel):
                hits.append((p.stat().st_mtime, rel))
        hits.sort(key=lambda t: (-t[0], t[1]))
        return [rel for _, rel in hits]

    hits = await asyncio.to_thread(run)
    more = f"\n… {len(hits) - MAX_RESULTS} more" if len(hits) > MAX_RESULTS else ""
    return ToolOutput.text("\n".join(hits[:MAX_RESULTS]) + more if hits else "no matches", f"{len(hits)} files")


@tool()
async def grep(
    ctx: ToolContext,
    pattern: str,
    path: str = ".",
    glob: str | None = None,
    output_mode: Literal["files_with_matches", "content", "count"] = "files_with_matches",
    max_count: int | None = None,
) -> ToolOutput:
    """Search file contents with a regular expression.

    Args:
        pattern: Regular expression.
        path: File or directory to search.
        glob: Only files matching this glob (e.g. "*.py").
        output_mode: files_with_matches, content (path:line:text) or count.
        max_count: Maximum matches per file.
    """
    try:
        regex = re.compile(pattern)
    except re.error as exc:
        return ToolOutput.error(f"invalid regex: {exc}")
    base = ctx.workspace.resolve(path)
    if _RG:
        lines = await _rg(ctx, pattern, base, glob, output_mode, max_count)
    else:
        lines = await asyncio.to_thread(_py_grep, ctx, regex, base, glob, output_mode, max_count)
    more = f"\n… {len(lines) - MAX_RESULTS} more" if len(lines) > MAX_RESULTS else ""
    return ToolOutput.text("\n".join(lines[:MAX_RESULTS]) + more if lines else "no matches", f"{len(lines)} results")


async def _rg(ctx: ToolContext, pattern: str, base: Path, glob_: str | None, mode: str, max_count: int | None) -> list[str]:
    argv = [_RG, "--no-heading", "--with-filename", "--color", "never", "--hidden"]
    argv += {"files_with_matches": ["-l"], "content": ["-n"], "count": ["-c"]}[mode]
    for d in sorted(IGNORED_DIRS):
        argv += ["-g", f"!{d}/"]
    if glob_:
        argv += ["-g", glob_]
    if max_count:
        argv += ["-m", str(max_count)]
    argv += ["-e", pattern, str(base)]
    proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(*argv, stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE, stderr=asyncio.subprocess.DEVNULL)
    out, _ = await proc.communicate()
    rows = []
    for line in out.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
        head, sep, rest = line.partition(":")
        rel = ctx.workspace.rel(Path(head)) if Path(head).is_absolute() else head
        rows.append(f"{rel}{sep}{rest}")
    return sorted(rows, key=_row_key)


def _row_key(row: str) -> tuple:
    parts = row.split(":")
    return (parts[0], int(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 2 and parts[1].isdigit() else 0)


def _py_grep(ctx: ToolContext, regex: re.Pattern, base: Path, glob_: str | None, mode: str, max_count: int | None) -> list[str]:
    files = [base] if base.is_file() else sorted(p for p in base.rglob("*") if p.is_file())
    rows: list[str] = []
    for p in files:
        rel = ctx.workspace.rel(p)
        if ctx.workspace.ignored(rel) or (glob_ and not fnmatch.fnmatch(p.name, glob_)):
            continue
        try:
            data = p.read_bytes()
        except OSError:
            continue
        if b"\x00" in data[:8192]:
            continue
        hits = 0
        for number, line in enumerate(data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines(), 1):
            if regex.search(line):
                hits += 1
                if mode == "content":
                    rows.append(f"{rel}:{number}:{line}")
                if max_count and hits >= max_count:
                    break
        if hits and mode == "files_with_matches":
            rows.append(rel)
        elif hits and mode == "count":
            rows.append(f"{rel}:{hits}")
    return rows


FILE_TOOLS = [ls, read_file, write_file, edit_file, delete, glob, grep]
```

```python
import asyncio

from luna_core.events import TodosUpdated
from luna_core.sandbox import LocalSandbox
from luna_core.tools import builtin_tools
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolContext
from luna_core.tools.shell import execute
from luna_core.tools.todos import write_todos
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace


def make_ctx(tmp_path, events=None):
    return ToolContext(workspace=Workspace(tmp_path), sandbox=LocalSandbox(), emit=(events.append if events is not None else lambda e: None))


async def test_execute_reports_exit_code(tmp_path):
    ctx = make_ctx(tmp_path)
    ok = await execute.invoke(ctx, {"command": "echo hi"})
    assert not ok.is_error and ok.content[0].text.strip().endswith("[exit code 0]") and "hi" in ok.content[0].text
    bad = await execute.invoke(ctx, {"command": "echo no >&2; exit 4"})
    assert bad.is_error and "[exit code 4]" in bad.content[0].text and "no" in bad.content[0].text


async def test_execute_timeout_is_capped_and_reported(tmp_path):
    out = await execute.invoke(make_ctx(tmp_path), {"command": "sleep 5", "timeout": 1})
    assert out.is_error and "timed out after 1s" in out.content[0].text
    assert execute.parameters["properties"]["timeout"]["default"] == 120


async def test_execute_honours_the_cancel_event(tmp_path):
    ctx = make_ctx(tmp_path)
    task = asyncio.create_task(execute.invoke(ctx, {"command": "sleep 30"}))
    await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
    ctx.cancel.set()
    out = await asyncio.wait_for(task, 5)
    assert out.is_error and "cancelled" in out.content[0].text


async def test_write_todos_validates_stores_and_emits(tmp_path):
    events = []
    ctx = make_ctx(tmp_path, events)
    todos = [{"content": "read code", "status": "completed"}, {"content": "fix bug", "status": "in_progress"}]
    out = await write_todos.invoke(ctx, {"todos": todos})
    assert not out.is_error and ctx.state["todos"] == todos
    assert events == [TodosUpdated(todos)]
    bad = await write_todos.invoke(ctx, {"todos": [{"content": "x", "status": "maybe"}]})
    assert bad.is_error and "status" in bad.content[0].text


def test_builtin_tool_names():
    assert [t.name for t in builtin_tools()] == ["ls", "read_file", "write_file", "edit_file", "delete", "glob", "grep", "execute", "write_todos"]
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/tools/shell.py`:

```python
"""The ``execute`` tool: run a shell command in the session sandbox."""

from __future__ import annotations

from luna_core.messages import Text
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolContext, ToolOutput, tool

DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 120
MAX_TIMEOUT = 600


@tool(mutating=True)
async def execute(ctx: ToolContext, command: str, timeout: int = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT) -> ToolOutput:
    """Run a shell command in the project directory.

    Args:
        command: Shell command line.
        timeout: Seconds before the command is killed (max 600).
    """
    seconds = max(1, min(int(timeout or DEFAULT_TIMEOUT), MAX_TIMEOUT))
    result = await ctx.sandbox.run(command, cwd=ctx.workspace.root, timeout=seconds, cancel=ctx.cancel)
    text = f"{result.output.rstrip()}\n[exit code {result.exit_code}]".lstrip()
    failed = result.exit_code != 0 or result.timed_out or result.cancelled
    first = command.strip().splitlines()[0][:60] if command.strip() else ""
    if failed:
        return ToolOutput((Text(text),), True, f"exit {result.exit_code}: {first}")
    return ToolOutput.text(text, f"ran {first}")
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/tools/todos.py`:

```python
"""``write_todos``: the agent's visible plan."""

from __future__ import annotations

from luna_core.events import TodosUpdated
from luna_core.tools.base import Tool, ToolContext, ToolOutput

STATUSES = ("pending", "in_progress", "completed")
_SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object",
    "properties": {
        "todos": {
            "type": "array",
            "description": "The full, updated plan.",
            "items": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {"content": {"type": "string"}, "status": {"type": "string", "enum": list(STATUSES)}},
                "required": ["content", "status"],
            },
        }
    },
    "required": ["todos"],
    "additionalProperties": False,
}


async def _write_todos(ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> ToolOutput:
    todos = args.get("todos")
    if not isinstance(todos, list):
        return ToolOutput.error("todos must be a list")
    clean = []
    for item in todos:
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or not str(item.get("content", "")).strip():
            return ToolOutput.error("each todo needs non-empty content")
        if item.get("status") not in STATUSES:
            return ToolOutput.error(f"status must be one of {', '.join(STATUSES)}")
        clean.append({"content": str(item["content"]).strip(), "status": item["status"]})
    ctx.state["todos"] = clean
    ctx.emit(TodosUpdated(clean))
    done = sum(1 for t in clean if t["status"] == "completed")
    return ToolOutput.text(f"plan updated: {done}/{len(clean)} done", f"todos {done}/{len(clean)}")


write_todos = Tool(
    "write_todos",
    "Keep a short plan for multi-step work; send the whole list each time.",
    _SCHEMA,
    _write_todos,
    raw=True,
)
```

```python
def builtin_tools() -> list[Tool]:
    """Fresh list of the built-in tools (``task`` tools are added by the agent)."""
    from luna_core.tools.shell import execute
    from luna_core.tools.todos import write_todos

    return [*FILE_TOOLS, execute, write_todos]
```

### Task 16: Policy (rules, approvals, plan mode, anchors) and undo journal

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/policy.py`, `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/undo.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_policy.py`, `packages/luna-core/tests/test_undo.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Tool` (Task 5), `ApprovalRequest` (Task 4), `Workspace` (Task 13).
- Produces (`policy.py`):
  - `Rules(allow=[], deny=[], ask=[])` with `.match(tool, args) -> "deny" | "ask" | "allow" | None` (deny wins, then ask, then allow); `Rules.load(paths: list[Path]) -> Rules` (TOML `[permissions]` or bare tables); `append_rule(path, rule, kind="allow")`; `suggest_rule(tool, args) -> str`; groups `write`, `fs`; `*` wildcard; rule subjects are project-relative for file tools and the command line for `execute`.
  - `Decision(type, args=None, message="", always=None)`: `Decision.approve()`, `.edit(args)`, `.reject(message)`, `.from_dict(d)`, `.to_dict()`.
  - `Verdict(action: "allow" | "deny" | "ask", message="", request: ApprovalRequest | None = None)`
  - `AnchorTracker(root)`: `remember(rel)`, `check(rel) -> bool`, `forget(rel)`, `forget_under(rel)`
  - `Policy(rules=None, *, approval_tools=frozenset({"write_file", "edit_file", "delete", "execute"}), yolo=False, plan_mode=False, workspace=None, persist_rule=None, anchors=None)`:
    - `.plan_mode` (bool attribute or callable), `.check(tool: Tool, args) -> Verdict`, `.after(name, args, ok)`, `.remember_always(rule)` (adds to in-memory allow list and calls `persist_rule(rule)` when given), `.child(workspace=None)` (shares rules; shares anchors only for the same workspace — used by subagents).
- Produces (`undo.py`):
  - `UndoJournal(root, session_id)`: `begin_turn(turn_id)`, `before_write(turn_id, rel)`, `undo() -> UndoResult | None`, `redo() -> UndoResult | None`, `depth -> int`, `redo_depth -> int`, `diff() -> str`, `is_git: bool`
  - `UndoResult(turn_id: str, note: str)`
  - `gc(root, keep_days=7, keep_max=20, keep=None)`

Undo design (replaces `luna/turn/undo.py`; the conversation half moves to `Session.rewind`, Task 17): in a git repo a turn begins with a shadow-tree commit of the worktree (temp index, `.luna/undo` excluded, `add.ignoreErrors=false` pinned — keep the existing docstring's reasoning) stored under `refs/luna/undo/<session>`; outside git, `before_write` stores each file's pre-image once per turn. `undo()` reverts the newest turn's files and pushes a redo record (post-tree or post-images); `redo()` re-applies it; `begin_turn` clears redo. Records live in `<root>/.luna/undo/<session>/journal.json` as `{"turns": [...], "redo": [...]}`.

```python
from luna_core.policy import AnchorTracker, Decision, Policy, Rules, append_rule, suggest_rule
from luna_core.tools.files import edit_file, read_file, write_file
from luna_core.tools.shell import execute
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace


def test_rules_deny_wins_then_ask_then_allow():
    rules = Rules(allow=["write:src/*", "execute:git *"], deny=["write_file:/.env"], ask=["execute:git push*"])
    assert rules.match("write_file", {"file_path": ".env"}) == "deny"
    assert rules.match("write_file", {"file_path": "/src/a.py"}) == "allow"
    assert rules.match("execute", {"command": "git push origin"}) == "ask"
    assert rules.match("execute", {"command": "git status"}) == "allow"
    assert rules.match("read_file", {"file_path": "x"}) is None


def test_rules_load_and_append(tmp_path):
    user = tmp_path / "config.toml"
    user.write_text('[permissions]\nallow = ["read_file:*"]\n')
    proj = tmp_path / "permissions.toml"
    proj.write_text('deny = ["execute:rm *"]\n')
    rules = Rules.load([user, proj, tmp_path / "missing.toml"])
    assert rules.allow == ["read_file:*"] and rules.deny == ["execute:rm *"]
    append_rule(proj, "execute:pytest *")
    again = Rules.load([proj])
    assert again.allow == ["execute:pytest *"] and again.deny == ["execute:rm *"]


def test_suggest_rule():
    assert suggest_rule("execute", {"command": "pytest -q tests"}) == "execute:pytest *"
    assert suggest_rule("edit_file", {"file_path": "/src/a.py"}) == "edit_file:src/a.py"


def test_decision_round_trip():
    for d in (Decision.approve(), Decision.edit({"command": "ls"}), Decision.reject("no"), Decision("approve", always="execute:ls *")):
        assert Decision.from_dict(d.to_dict()) == d


def make_policy(tmp_path, **kw):
    return Policy(Rules(deny=["execute:rm *"]), workspace=Workspace(tmp_path), **kw)


def test_policy_verdicts(tmp_path):
    p = make_policy(tmp_path)
    assert p.check(read_file, {"file_path": "a"}).action == "allow"
    ask = p.check(execute, {"command": "pytest"})
    assert ask.action == "ask" and ask.request.suggested_rule == "execute:pytest *"
    deny = p.check(execute, {"command": "rm -rf /"})
    assert deny.action == "deny" and "permission rule" in deny.message
    assert make_policy(tmp_path, yolo=True).check(execute, {"command": "pytest"}).action == "allow"
    assert make_policy(tmp_path, yolo=True).check(execute, {"command": "rm x"}).action == "deny"


def test_plan_mode_refuses_mutations(tmp_path):
    flag = {"on": True}
    p = make_policy(tmp_path, plan_mode=lambda: flag["on"])
    v = p.check(write_file, {"file_path": "a", "content": ""})
    assert v.action == "deny" and "plan mode" in v.message
    assert p.check(read_file, {"file_path": "a"}).action == "allow"
    flag["on"] = False
    assert p.check(write_file, {"file_path": "a", "content": ""}).action == "ask"


def test_always_rule_is_remembered_and_persisted(tmp_path):
    saved = []
    p = make_policy(tmp_path, persist_rule=saved.append)
    p.remember_always("execute:pytest *")
    assert p.check(execute, {"command": "pytest -q"}).action == "allow" and saved == ["execute:pytest *"]
    assert p.child().check(execute, {"command": "pytest -q"}).action == "allow"
    assert p.child().anchors is p.anchors
    (tmp_path / "wt").mkdir()
    other = Workspace(tmp_path / "wt")
    assert p.child(other).anchors is not p.anchors and p.child(other).workspace is other


def test_stale_anchor_blocks_edits(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "a.py"
    f.write_text("x = 1\n")
    p = make_policy(tmp_path, yolo=True)
    p.after("read_file", {"file_path": "a.py"}, ok=True)
    assert p.check(edit_file, {"file_path": "a.py", "old_string": "1", "new_string": "2"}).action == "allow"
    f.write_text("x = 99\n")  # hand edit
    v = p.check(edit_file, {"file_path": "/a.py", "old_string": "1", "new_string": "2"})
    assert v.action == "deny" and "changed on disk" in v.message
    p.after("read_file", {"file_path": "a.py"}, ok=True)
    assert p.check(edit_file, {"file_path": "a.py", "old_string": "9", "new_string": "2"}).action == "allow"


def test_anchor_forget_under(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "d").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "d" / "x").write_text("1")
    t = AnchorTracker(tmp_path)
    t.remember("d/x")
    (tmp_path / "d" / "x").write_text("2")
    assert not t.check("d/x")
    t.forget_under("d")
    assert t.check("d/x")
```

```python
import subprocess

import pytest

from luna_core.undo import UndoJournal, gc


def git(root, *args):
    subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=root, check=True, capture_output=True)


@pytest.fixture
def repo(tmp_path):
    git(tmp_path, "init", "-q")
    git(tmp_path, "config", "user.email", "t@t")
    git(tmp_path, "config", "user.name", "t")
    (tmp_path / "a.txt").write_text("one\n")
    git(tmp_path, "add", ".")
    git(tmp_path, "commit", "-qm", "init")
    return tmp_path


def test_git_undo_redo_restores_and_removes_created_files(repo):
    j = UndoJournal(repo, "s1")
    assert j.is_git and j.depth == 0
    j.begin_turn("t1")
    (repo / "a.txt").write_text("two\n")
    (repo / "new.txt").write_text("created\n")
    assert j.depth == 1
    res = j.undo()
    assert res.turn_id == "t1" and (repo / "a.txt").read_text() == "one\n" and not (repo / "new.txt").exists()
    assert j.depth == 0 and j.redo_depth == 1
    assert j.redo().turn_id == "t1"
    assert (repo / "a.txt").read_text() == "two\n" and (repo / "new.txt").read_text() == "created\n"
    assert j.undo() is not None and j.undo() is None


def test_git_redo_is_cleared_by_a_new_turn_and_index_is_untouched(repo):
    j = UndoJournal(repo, "s1")
    j.begin_turn("t1")
    (repo / "a.txt").write_text("two\n")
    j.undo()
    j.begin_turn("t2")
    assert j.redo_depth == 0
    status = subprocess.run(["git", "diff", "--cached", "--name-only"], cwd=repo, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    assert status == ""


def test_journal_without_git(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "a.txt").write_text("one\n")
    j = UndoJournal(tmp_path, "s1")
    assert not j.is_git
    j.begin_turn("t1")
    j.before_write("t1", "a.txt")
    (tmp_path / "a.txt").write_text("two\n")
    j.before_write("t1", "a.txt")  # second write in the same turn keeps the first pre-image
    (tmp_path / "a.txt").write_text("three\n")
    j.before_write("t1", "b.txt")
    (tmp_path / "b.txt").write_text("new\n")
    assert "-one" in j.diff() and "+three" in j.diff()
    res = j.undo()
    assert res.turn_id == "t1" and (tmp_path / "a.txt").read_text() == "one\n" and not (tmp_path / "b.txt").exists()
    j.redo()
    assert (tmp_path / "a.txt").read_text() == "three\n" and (tmp_path / "b.txt").read_text() == "new\n"


def test_turn_without_changes_still_counts(tmp_path):
    j = UndoJournal(tmp_path, "s1")
    j.begin_turn("t1")
    assert j.depth == 1 and j.undo().note == "no file changes in this turn"


def test_gc_keeps_the_named_session(tmp_path):
    for sid in ("a", "b", "c"):
        UndoJournal(tmp_path, sid).begin_turn("t")
    gc(tmp_path, keep_max=1, keep="a")
    left = sorted(p.name for p in (tmp_path / ".luna" / "undo").iterdir())
    assert "a" in left and len(left) == 2
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/policy.py`:

```python
"""Permission rules, approvals, plan mode and stale-edit anchors."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import posixpath
import tomllib
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from fnmatch import fnmatch
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath

from luna_core.events import ApprovalRequest
from luna_core.tools.base import Tool, args_preview
from luna_core.workspace import OutsideWorkspace, Workspace

MUTATING_FILE_TOOLS = frozenset({"write_file", "edit_file", "delete"})
DEFAULT_APPROVAL = frozenset({"write_file", "edit_file", "delete", "execute"})
_GROUPS = {
    "write": MUTATING_FILE_TOOLS,
    "fs": frozenset({"write_file", "edit_file", "delete", "read_file", "ls", "glob", "grep"}),
}


def _relpath(raw: str) -> str:
    return PurePosixPath(str(raw)).as_posix().lstrip("/") if raw else ""


def _subject(tool: str, args: dict) -> str:
    if tool == "execute":
        return str(args.get("command", ""))
    return _relpath(args.get("file_path", args.get("path", "")))


def _tool_matches(rule_tool: str, tool: str) -> bool:
    return rule_tool in ("*", tool) or tool in _GROUPS.get(rule_tool, ())


@dataclass
class Rules:
    """Allow / ask / deny rules of the form ``<tool or group>:<fnmatch pattern>``."""

    allow: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    deny: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    ask: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def _hit(self, rules: list[str], tool: str, subject: str) -> bool:
        for rule in rules:
            rtool, _, pattern = rule.partition(":")
            if not _tool_matches(rtool, tool):
                continue
            if tool != "execute":
                pattern = _relpath(pattern)
            if fnmatch(subject, pattern):
                return True
        return False

    def match(self, tool: str, args: dict) -> str | None:
        """``"deny"``, ``"ask"``, ``"allow"`` or ``None`` (deny wins)."""
        subject = _subject(tool, args)
        for kind in ("deny", "ask", "allow"):
            if self._hit(getattr(self, kind), tool, subject):
                return kind
        return None

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, paths: list[Path]) -> Rules:
        """Merge rule files in order (missing or broken files are skipped)."""
        rules = cls()
        for path in paths:
            data = _read(Path(path))
            table = data.get("permissions", data)
            if not isinstance(table, dict):
                continue
            for kind in ("allow", "deny", "ask"):
                values = table.get(kind, [])
                if isinstance(values, list):
                    dest = getattr(rules, kind)
                    dest += [r for r in values if isinstance(r, str) and r not in dest]
        return rules


def _read(path: Path) -> dict:
    try:
        with path.open("rb") as fh:
            return tomllib.load(fh)
    except (OSError, tomllib.TOMLDecodeError):
        return {}


def append_rule(path: Path, rule: str, kind: str = "allow") -> Path:
    """Add ``rule`` to ``kind`` in a ``[permissions]`` TOML file, keeping the rest."""
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    data = _read(path)
    table = data.get("permissions", data) if data else {}
    lists = {k: list(table.get(k, [])) for k in ("allow", "deny", "ask")}
    if rule not in lists[kind]:
        lists[kind].append(rule)
    lines = ["[permissions]"]
    for k, values in lists.items():
        if values:
            lines += [f"{k} = [", *(f"    {_toml_str(r)}," for r in values), "]"]
    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def _toml_str(value: str) -> str:
    return '"' + value.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"') + '"'


def suggest_rule(tool: str, args: dict) -> str:
    """A rule that would allow this call next time."""
    if tool == "execute":
        first = str(args.get("command", "")).split()
        return f"execute:{first[0]} *" if first else "execute:*"
    return f"{tool}:{_subject(tool, args)}"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decision:
    """A human answer to an approval request."""

    type: str
    args: dict | None = None
    message: str = ""
    always: str | None = None

    @classmethod
    def approve(cls, always: str | None = None) -> Decision:
        """Run the call as proposed."""
        return cls("approve", always=always)

    @classmethod
    def edit(cls, args: dict) -> Decision:
        """Run the call with edited arguments."""
        return cls("edit", args=dict(args))

    @classmethod
    def reject(cls, message: str = "") -> Decision:
        """Do not run the call; the model sees ``message``."""
        return cls("reject", message=message)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict) -> Decision:
        """Parse the wire form (``{"type": ..., "args"|"message"|"always": ...}``)."""
        kind = data.get("type")
        if kind not in ("approve", "edit", "reject"):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown decision type {kind!r}")
        return cls(kind, data.get("args"), data.get("message", ""), data.get("always"))

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        """Wire form."""
        out: dict = {"type": self.type}
        if self.args is not None:
            out["args"] = self.args
        if self.message:
            out["message"] = self.message
        if self.always:
            out["always"] = self.always
        return out


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Verdict:
    """What the policy says about one tool call."""

    action: str
    message: str = ""
    request: ApprovalRequest | None = None


class AnchorTracker:
    """Last-seen content hash per path; edits on changed files are refused."""

    def __init__(self, root: str | Path) -> None:
        self.root = Path(root)
        self._anchors: dict[str, str] = {}

    @staticmethod
    def _key(rel: str) -> str:
        return posixpath.normpath(rel.lstrip("/"))

    def _hash(self, key: str) -> str | None:
        try:
            return hashlib.sha256((self.root / key).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        except OSError:
            return None

    def remember(self, rel: str) -> None:
        """Record the file's current content as seen."""
        key = self._key(rel)
        digest = self._hash(key)
        if digest is not None:
            self._anchors[key] = digest

    def check(self, rel: str) -> bool:
        """True when there is no anchor or the file still matches it."""
        key = self._key(rel)
        anchored = self._anchors.get(key)
        return anchored is None or self._hash(key) == anchored

    def forget(self, rel: str) -> None:
        """Drop one anchor."""
        self._anchors.pop(self._key(rel), None)

    def forget_under(self, rel: str) -> None:
        """Drop an anchor and every anchor below it."""
        prefix = self._key(rel)
        self._anchors.pop(prefix, None)
        for key in [k for k in self._anchors if k.startswith(prefix + "/")]:
            del self._anchors[key]


class Policy:
    """Decides, per tool call, whether to run it, refuse it or ask a human."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        rules: Rules | None = None,
        *,
        approval_tools: frozenset[str] = DEFAULT_APPROVAL,
        yolo: bool = False,
        plan_mode: bool | Callable[[], bool] = False,
        workspace: Workspace | None = None,
        persist_rule: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
        anchors: AnchorTracker | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.rules = rules or Rules()
        self.approval_tools = frozenset(approval_tools)
        self.yolo = yolo
        self.plan_mode = plan_mode
        self.workspace = workspace
        self.persist_rule = persist_rule
        self.anchors = anchors or (AnchorTracker(workspace.root) if workspace else None)

    def _planning(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.plan_mode()) if callable(self.plan_mode) else bool(self.plan_mode)

    def _rel(self, args: dict) -> str:
        raw = args.get("file_path") or args.get("path") or ""
        if not raw or self.workspace is None:
            return _relpath(raw)
        try:
            return self.workspace.rel(self.workspace.resolve(raw))
        except OutsideWorkspace:
            return _relpath(raw)

    def check(self, tool: Tool, args: dict) -> Verdict:
        """Verdict for calling ``tool`` with ``args``."""
        name = tool.name
        match = self.rules.match(name, args)
        if match == "deny":
            return Verdict("deny", f"blocked by a Luna permission rule ({name})")
        mutating = tool.mutating or name in self.approval_tools
        if mutating and self._planning():
            return Verdict("deny", f"plan mode is on — refusing to {name}. Run /plan off to make changes.")
        rel = self._rel(args)
        if rel and name in MUTATING_FILE_TOOLS and self.anchors and not self.anchors.check(rel):
            return Verdict(
                "deny",
                f"{rel} has changed on disk since Luna last saw it (hand edit, another process, or a formatter) — read it again before retrying",
            )
        if match == "allow" or (self.yolo and match != "ask"):
            return Verdict("allow")
        if match == "ask" or name in self.approval_tools:
            request = ApprovalRequest("", name, dict(args), args_preview(args, 80), suggest_rule(name, args))
            return Verdict("ask", request=request)
        return Verdict("allow")

    def after(self, name: str, args: dict, ok: bool) -> None:
        """Update anchors after a tool ran."""
        if self.anchors is None:
            return
        rel = self._rel(args)
        if not rel or name not in {"read_file", *MUTATING_FILE_TOOLS}:
            return
        if name == "delete":
            if ok:
                self.anchors.forget_under(rel)
        elif name == "read_file" and not ok:
            self.anchors.forget(rel)
        elif ok:
            self.anchors.remember(rel)

    def remember_always(self, rule: str) -> None:
        """Allow ``rule`` for the rest of the session (and persist it if configured)."""
        if rule not in self.rules.allow:
            self.rules.allow.append(rule)
        if self.persist_rule:
            self.persist_rule(rule)

    def child(self, workspace: Workspace | None = None) -> Policy:
        """A policy for a subagent: same rules and plan mode.

        Anchors are shared when the child works in the same workspace and
        fresh when it works elsewhere (a git worktree).
        """
        same = workspace is None or (self.workspace is not None and workspace.root == self.workspace.root)
        return Policy(
            self.rules,
            approval_tools=self.approval_tools,
            yolo=self.yolo,
            plan_mode=self.plan_mode,
            workspace=self.workspace if workspace is None else workspace,
            persist_rule=self.persist_rule,
            anchors=self.anchors if same else None,
        )
```

Tools outside `approval_tools` run without asking unless a rule says `ask` — including extension tools declared `mutating=True` (mutating still matters for plan mode). The app passes `approval_tools = DEFAULT_APPROVAL | EXTENSION_APPROVALS` (Task 28) so its extension tools keep asking.

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/undo.py`:

```python
"""Per-turn file snapshots for /undo and /redo (git shadow trees or a file journal)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import difflib
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

# Luna state that is never part of a snapshot (subagent worktrees live under .luna too).
_EXCLUDES = (":(exclude).luna/undo", ":(exclude).luna/worktrees")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class UndoResult:
    """What an undo or redo did."""

    turn_id: str
    note: str


def _git(root: Path, args: list[str], env: dict | None = None) -> str | None:
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30, env=env)
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None
    return proc.stdout.strip() if proc.returncode == 0 else None


def _is_git(root: Path) -> bool:
    return _git(root, ["rev-parse", "--is-inside-work-tree"]) == "true"


def _snapshot_tree(root: Path) -> str | None:
    """Write the worktree to a tree object without touching the real index.

    ``git add`` exits 1 with an "ignored paths" warning when ``.luna`` is
    gitignored, although the temp index is built correctly; exit 128 means a
    real failure and must not produce an empty tree (an undo would then delete
    every file). ``add.ignoreErrors`` is pinned off so exit 1 cannot hide a
    genuine indexing error.
    """
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        env = {**os.environ, "GIT_INDEX_FILE": str(Path(tmp) / "index")}
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run(
                ["git", "-c", "add.ignoreErrors=false", "add", "-A", "--", ".", *_EXCLUDES],
                cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, env=env,
            )
        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
            return None
        if proc.returncode not in (0, 1):
            return None
        return _git(root, ["write-tree"], env=env)


class UndoJournal:
    """File history of one session, one entry per turn."""

    def __init__(self, root: str | Path, session_id: str) -> None:
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()
        self.session_id = session_id or "default"
        self.dir = self.root / ".luna" / "undo" / self.session_id
        self.is_git = _is_git(self.root)

    # -- storage ---------------------------------------------------------
    def _load(self) -> dict:
        try:
            data = json.loads((self.dir / "journal.json").read_text())
            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {"turns": [], "redo": []}
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return {"turns": [], "redo": []}

    def _save(self, data: dict) -> None:
        self.dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        tmp = self.dir / "journal.json.tmp"
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(data))
        tmp.replace(self.dir / "journal.json")

    @property
    def depth(self) -> int:
        """Number of turns that can be undone."""
        return len(self._load()["turns"])

    @property
    def redo_depth(self) -> int:
        """Number of undone turns that can be redone."""
        return len(self._load()["redo"])

    # -- recording -------------------------------------------------------
    def begin_turn(self, turn_id: str) -> None:
        """Record the state before a turn; clears the redo stack."""
        data = self._load()
        record: dict = {"turn_id": turn_id, "ts": time.time()}
        if self.is_git:
            tree = _snapshot_tree(self.root)
            if tree:
                record["pre_tree"] = tree
                commit = _git(self.root, ["commit-tree", tree, "-m", f"luna turn {turn_id}"])
                if commit:
                    _git(self.root, ["update-ref", f"refs/luna/undo/{self.session_id}", commit])
        else:
            record["files"] = {}
        data["turns"].append(record)
        data["redo"] = []
        self._save(data)

    def before_write(self, turn_id: str, rel: str) -> None:
        """Keep ``rel``'s pre-image for this turn (file-journal mode only)."""
        if self.is_git:
            return
        data = self._load()
        turn = next((t for t in reversed(data["turns"]) if t["turn_id"] == turn_id), None)
        if turn is None or rel in turn.setdefault("files", {}):
            return
        turn["files"][rel] = self._read(rel)
        self._save(data)

    def _read(self, rel: str) -> dict:
        target = self.root / rel
        if target.is_dir():
            return {"kind": "dir"}
        if not target.is_file():
            return {"kind": "absent"}
        try:
            return {"kind": "text", "text": target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")}
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return {"kind": "binary"}

    def _write(self, rel: str, image: dict) -> bool:
        target = self.root / rel
        kind = image.get("kind")
        if kind == "text":
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_text(image["text"], encoding="utf-8")
            return True
        if kind == "absent":
            if target.is_dir():
                shutil.rmtree(target, ignore_errors=True)
            elif target.exists():
                target.unlink()
            return True
        return False  # binary or directory pre-images cannot be restored

    # -- undo / redo -----------------------------------------------------
    def undo(self) -> UndoResult | None:
        """Revert the newest turn's files."""
        data = self._load()
        if not data["turns"]:
            return None
        turn = data["turns"].pop()
        if self.is_git:
            post = _snapshot_tree(self.root)
            pre = turn.get("pre_tree")
            changed = bool(pre and post and pre != post)
            if changed:
                self._restore_tree(pre, post)
            data["redo"].append({"turn_id": turn["turn_id"], "pre_tree": pre, "post_tree": post})
        else:
            files = turn.get("files", {})
            post_images = {rel: self._read(rel) for rel in files}
            skipped = [rel for rel, image in files.items() if not self._write(rel, image)]
            changed = bool(files)
            data["redo"].append({"turn_id": turn["turn_id"], "files": files, "post": post_images, "skipped": skipped})
        self._save(data)
        return UndoResult(turn["turn_id"], "files restored" if changed else "no file changes in this turn")

    def redo(self) -> UndoResult | None:
        """Re-apply the most recently undone turn's files."""
        data = self._load()
        if not data["redo"]:
            return None
        record = data["redo"].pop()
        if self.is_git:
            if record.get("post_tree") and record.get("pre_tree"):
                self._restore_tree(record["post_tree"], record["pre_tree"])
            data["turns"].append({"turn_id": record["turn_id"], "pre_tree": record.get("pre_tree"), "ts": time.time()})
        else:
            for rel, image in record.get("post", {}).items():
                self._write(rel, image)
            data["turns"].append({"turn_id": record["turn_id"], "files": record.get("files", {}), "ts": time.time()})
        self._save(data)
        return UndoResult(record["turn_id"], "files re-applied")

    def _restore_tree(self, target: str, current: str) -> None:
        _git(self.root, ["restore", "--source", target, "--worktree", "--", ".", *_EXCLUDES])
        # restore never deletes files that exist only in ``current``.
        added = _git(self.root, ["diff", "--name-only", "--diff-filter=A", target, current, "--", ".", *_EXCLUDES]) or ""
        for rel in added.splitlines():
            path = self.root / rel
            if path.is_file():
                with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                    path.unlink()

    def diff(self) -> str:
        """Unified diff of everything this session changed."""
        data = self._load()
        if not data["turns"]:
            return ""
        if self.is_git:
            first = next((t.get("pre_tree") for t in data["turns"] if t.get("pre_tree")), None)
            now = _snapshot_tree(self.root)
            return (_git(self.root, ["diff", first, now, "--", ".", *_EXCLUDES]) or "") if first and now else ""
        earliest: dict[str, dict] = {}
        for turn in data["turns"]:
            for rel, image in turn.get("files", {}).items():
                earliest.setdefault(rel, image)
        chunks = []
        for rel, image in earliest.items():
            if image.get("kind") not in ("text", "absent"):
                chunks.append(f"# {rel}: binary or unreadable — changed, no diff")
                continue
            now = self._read(rel)
            before = image.get("text", "").splitlines()
            after = now.get("text", "").splitlines()
            text = "\n".join(difflib.unified_diff(before, after, f"a/{rel}", f"b/{rel}", lineterm=""))
            if text:
                chunks.append(text)
        return "\n\n".join(chunks)


def gc(root: str | Path, *, keep_days: int = 7, keep_max: int = 20, keep: str | None = None) -> None:
    """Remove old per-session journals (and their shadow refs)."""
    base = Path(root) / ".luna" / "undo"
    if not base.is_dir():
        return
    cutoff = time.time() - keep_days * 86400

    def mtime(d: Path) -> float:
        try:
            return (d / "journal.json").stat().st_mtime
        except OSError:
            return d.stat().st_mtime

    dated = sorted(((d, mtime(d)) for d in base.iterdir() if d.is_dir() and d.name != keep), key=lambda t: t[1])
    survivors = [d for d, m in dated if m >= cutoff]
    doomed = [d for d, m in dated if m < cutoff]
    if len(survivors) > keep_max:
        doomed += survivors[: len(survivors) - keep_max]
    for d in doomed:
        shutil.rmtree(d, ignore_errors=True)
        _git(Path(root), ["update-ref", "-d", f"refs/luna/undo/{d.name}"])
```

`test_gc_keeps_the_named_session`: `a`, `b`, `c` all fresh; `keep="a"` is excluded from the candidates, `keep_max=1` keeps the newest of `b`/`c` → two directories left. (The sessions are created within the same second; if the mtime ties make the "newest" ambiguous the assertion still holds because it only counts.)

### Task 17: Event-log sessions

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/session.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_session.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: messages (Task 2), `Usage` (Task 3), `Decision` (Task 16), `UndoJournal`/`UndoResult` (Task 16), `LunaCoreError`.
- Produces:
  - `EventLog(path)`: `create(workdir, *, title="", parent_id=None, fork_seq=None, session_id=None, meta=None) -> str`, `append(session_id, type, payload) -> int`, `records(session_id, after=0) -> list[Record]`, `info(session_id) -> SessionInfo | None`, `list(workdir=None, limit=100) -> list[SessionInfo]`, `set_title(session_id, title)`, `set_meta(session_id, **values)`, `delete(session_id) -> bool`, `prune(older_than_days, workdir=None) -> int`, `close()`
  - `Record(seq, ts, type, payload)`, `SessionInfo(id, workdir, title, created, updated, parent_id, fork_seq, meta)`, `Entry(seq, turn_id, message)`
  - `Session.create(log, workdir, *, title="", undo=True, meta=None)`, `Session.open(log, session_id, *, undo=True)`; attributes `id`, `log`, `workdir`, `state: dict`, `undo_journal: UndoJournal | None`
  - `Session.append(type, payload) -> int`, `.records()`, `.entries()`, `.messages()`, `.turn_ids()`, `.usage() -> Usage`, `.cost() -> float`, `.todos() -> list[dict]`, `.title`, `.has_user_messages`
  - approvals: `.expect_decision(call_id) -> asyncio.Future`, `.decide(call_id, decision: Decision | dict) -> None` (raises `LunaCoreError` for an unknown call), `.pending_approvals -> list[str]`
  - queue: `.queue(text)`, `.drain_queue() -> list[str]`
  - turn control: `.busy`, `.cancel_turn() -> bool`, `._begin_turn() -> asyncio.Event`, `._end_turn()`
  - children and listeners: `.adopt(child)`, `.release(child)` (approvals of adopted subagent sessions are answered through the parent's `decide`), `.subscribe(fn) -> unsubscribe`, `.notify(event)`; `EventLog.children(session_id)`; `cost()` includes child sessions whose `meta.kind` is `subagent` or `background`
  - history: `.undo() -> UndoResult | None`, `.redo() -> UndoResult | None`, `.undo_depth`, `.rewind(to_seq) -> int`, `.fork(at_seq=None) -> Session`, `.export(fmt="md" | "html" | "json") -> str`
  - `patch_dangling(messages) -> list[Message]`
- Log event types (payload keys): `turn_started{turn_id}`, `user{message}`, `assistant{message, usage}`, `tool_results{message}`, `turn_done{turn_id, usage, cost, stop_reason}`, `turn_error{turn_id, message, kind}`, `cancelled{turn_id}`, `compacted{summary, upto_seq, removed}`, `rewound{turn_id}`, `restored{turn_id}`, `todos{todos}`, `approval{call_id, decision}`, `background_done{handle, ok, summary}`.

Event-log writes are short synchronous SQLite transactions in WAL mode (sub-millisecond); they are called directly from the loop, not via `asyncio.to_thread` — this refines the Global Constraint for sqlite (recorded in the overview).

```python
import asyncio
import json
import subprocess

import pytest

from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.messages import Message, Text, ToolCall, ToolResult, message_to_dict
from luna_core.policy import Decision
from luna_core.session import EventLog, Session, patch_dangling
from luna_core.usage import Usage


@pytest.fixture
def log(tmp_path):
    lg = EventLog(tmp_path / "sessions.db")
    yield lg
    lg.close()


def turn(session, turn_id, user, reply, usage=Usage(10, 2)):
    session.append("turn_started", {"turn_id": turn_id})
    session.append("user", {"message": message_to_dict(Message.user(user))})
    session.append("assistant", {"message": message_to_dict(Message.assistant([Text(reply)])), "usage": usage.to_dict()})
    session.append("turn_done", {"turn_id": turn_id, "usage": usage.to_dict(), "cost": 0.01, "stop_reason": "end"})


def test_log_create_append_list(log, tmp_path):
    sid = log.create(str(tmp_path), title="hello")
    assert log.append(sid, "user", {"x": 1}) == 1 and log.append(sid, "user", {"x": 2}) == 2
    assert [r.payload["x"] for r in log.records(sid)] == [1, 2]
    assert [r.seq for r in log.records(sid, after=1)] == [2]
    assert log.list(str(tmp_path))[0].title == "hello"
    assert log.delete(sid) and log.records(sid) == [] and log.info(sid) is None


def test_session_messages_usage_cost_title(log, tmp_path):
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    turn(s, "t1", "first question", "answer one")
    turn(s, "t2", "second", "answer two")
    assert [m.text for m in s.messages()] == ["first question", "answer one", "second", "answer two"]
    assert s.usage() == Usage(20, 4) and s.cost() == pytest.approx(0.02)
    assert s.title == "first question" and s.turn_ids() == ["t1", "t2"]
    reopened = Session.open(log, s.id, undo=False)
    assert [m.text for m in reopened.messages()] == [m.text for m in s.messages()]


def test_compaction_replaces_old_messages_with_a_summary(log, tmp_path):
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    turn(s, "t1", "a", "b")
    upto = s.records()[-1].seq
    turn(s, "t2", "c", "d")
    s.append("compacted", {"summary": "talked about a", "upto_seq": upto, "removed": 2})
    msgs = s.messages()
    assert "talked about a" in msgs[0].text and msgs[0].role == "user"
    assert [m.text for m in msgs[1:]] == ["c", "d"]
    assert len(s.records()) > 8  # the full history stays in the log


def test_patch_dangling_adds_cancelled_results():
    history = [
        Message.user("go"),
        Message.assistant([ToolCall("c1", "ls", {}), ToolCall("c2", "grep", {"pattern": "x"})]),
        Message.tool_results([ToolResult("c1", (Text("a"),), name="ls")]),
        Message.assistant([ToolCall("c3", "ls", {})]),
    ]
    patched = patch_dangling(history)
    assert [r.call_id for r in patched[2].results] == ["c1", "c2"] and patched[2].results[1].is_error
    assert patched[4].role == "tool" and patched[4].results[0].call_id == "c3"


def test_undo_redo_rewinds_conversation_without_git(log, tmp_path):
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    turn(s, "t1", "a", "b")
    turn(s, "t2", "c", "d")
    assert s.undo_depth == 2
    res = s.undo()
    assert res.turn_id == "t2" and [m.text for m in s.messages()] == ["a", "b"] and s.undo_depth == 1
    assert s.redo().turn_id == "t2" and [m.text for m in s.messages()][-1] == "d"
    s.undo()
    turn(s, "t3", "e", "f")
    assert s.redo() is None  # a new turn clears redo
    assert [m.text for m in s.messages()] == ["a", "b", "e", "f"]


def test_undo_also_reverts_files(log, tmp_path):
    subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=tmp_path, check=True)
    (tmp_path / "f.txt").write_text("one")
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path))
    s.append("turn_started", {"turn_id": "t1"})
    s.undo_journal.begin_turn("t1")
    s.append("user", {"message": message_to_dict(Message.user("edit"))})
    (tmp_path / "f.txt").write_text("two")
    res = s.undo()
    assert res.turn_id == "t1" and (tmp_path / "f.txt").read_text() == "one" and s.messages() == []


def test_rewind_to_seq(log, tmp_path):
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    turn(s, "t1", "a", "b")
    mark = s.records()[-1].seq
    turn(s, "t2", "c", "d")
    turn(s, "t3", "e", "f")
    assert s.rewind(mark) == 2 and [m.text for m in s.messages()] == ["a", "b"]


def test_fork_copies_history_up_to_seq(log, tmp_path):
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    turn(s, "t1", "a", "b")
    mark = s.records()[-1].seq
    turn(s, "t2", "c", "d")
    child = s.fork(mark)
    assert [m.text for m in child.messages()] == ["a", "b"]
    info = log.info(child.id)
    assert info.parent_id == s.id and info.fork_seq == mark


async def test_approvals_and_queue(log, tmp_path):
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    fut = s.expect_decision("c1")
    assert s.pending_approvals == ["c1"]
    s.decide("c1", {"type": "reject", "message": "no"})
    assert (await fut) == Decision.reject("no") and s.pending_approvals == []
    with pytest.raises(LunaCoreError):
        s.decide("missing", Decision.approve())
    s.queue("also check tests")
    assert s.drain_queue() == ["also check tests"] and s.drain_queue() == []


async def test_child_approvals_route_through_the_parent_and_cost_adds_up(log, tmp_path):
    parent = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    child = Session(log, log.create(str(tmp_path), parent_id=parent.id, meta={"kind": "subagent"}), workdir=str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    parent.adopt(child)
    fut = child.expect_decision("k1")
    assert parent.pending_approvals == ["k1"]
    parent.decide("k1", Decision.approve())
    assert (await fut) == Decision.approve()
    turn(child, "c1", "sub", "done")
    turn(parent, "p1", "main", "ok")
    assert parent.cost() == pytest.approx(0.02)
    seen = []
    unsubscribe = parent.subscribe(seen.append)
    parent.notify("ping")
    unsubscribe()
    parent.notify("pong")
    assert seen == ["ping"]


async def test_turn_flags(log, tmp_path):
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    assert not s.cancel_turn()
    cancel = s._begin_turn()
    assert s.busy and s.cancel_turn() and cancel.is_set()
    s._end_turn()
    assert not s.busy


def test_export_formats(log, tmp_path):
    s = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    turn(s, "t1", "hello <world>", "hi")
    md = s.export("md")
    assert "## You" in md and "hello <world>" in md and "## Luna" in md
    assert "&lt;world&gt;" in s.export("html")
    assert json.loads(s.export("json"))["records"][0]["type"] == "turn_started"


def test_prune(log, tmp_path):
    old = log.create(str(tmp_path))
    log._conn.execute("UPDATE sessions SET updated = 0 WHERE id = ?", (old,))
    log.create(str(tmp_path))
    assert log.prune(older_than_days=30) == 1 and log.info(old) is None
```

```python
"""Sessions stored as an append-only event log in SQLite."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import html
import json
import sqlite3
import threading
import time
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.messages import Message, Text, ToolResult, message_from_dict
from luna_core.policy import Decision
from luna_core.undo import UndoJournal, UndoResult
from luna_core.usage import Usage

SCHEMA_VERSION = 1
_MESSAGE_TYPES = ("user", "assistant", "tool_results")
_DDL = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions(
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    workdir TEXT NOT NULL,
    title TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    created REAL NOT NULL,
    updated REAL NOT NULL,
    parent_id TEXT,
    fork_seq INTEGER,
    meta TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(
    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    seq INTEGER NOT NULL,
    ts REAL NOT NULL,
    type TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL,
    v INTEGER NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (session_id, seq)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS sessions_by_workdir ON sessions(workdir, updated);
"""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Record:
    """One stored event."""

    seq: int
    ts: float
    type: str
    payload: dict


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SessionInfo:
    """A session row."""

    id: str
    workdir: str
    title: str
    created: float
    updated: float
    parent_id: str | None
    fork_seq: int | None
    meta: dict


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Entry:
    """A message with its position and turn."""

    seq: int
    turn_id: str | None
    message: Message


class EventLog:
    """The SQLite store shared by all sessions of a process."""

    def __init__(self, path: str | Path) -> None:
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False, isolation_level=None, timeout=30)
        self._conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        self._conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
        self._conn.executescript(_DDL)
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def create(
        self,
        workdir: str,
        *,
        title: str = "",
        parent_id: str | None = None,
        fork_seq: int | None = None,
        session_id: str | None = None,
        meta: dict | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Create a session and return its id."""
        sid = session_id or uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]
        now = time.time()
        with self._lock:
            self._conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO sessions(id, workdir, title, created, updated, parent_id, fork_seq, meta) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                (sid, workdir, title, now, now, parent_id, fork_seq, json.dumps(meta or {})),
            )
        return sid

    def append(self, session_id: str, type_: str, payload: dict) -> int:
        """Append one event; returns its sequence number."""
        now = time.time()
        with self._lock:
            self._conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            try:
                row = self._conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(seq), 0) FROM events WHERE session_id = ?", (session_id,)).fetchone()
                seq = int(row[0]) + 1
                self._conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO events(session_id, seq, ts, type, payload, v) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                    (session_id, seq, now, type_, json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False), SCHEMA_VERSION),
                )
                self._conn.execute("UPDATE sessions SET updated = ? WHERE id = ?", (now, session_id))
                self._conn.execute("COMMIT")
            except BaseException:
                self._conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
                raise
        return seq

    def records(self, session_id: str, after: int = 0) -> list[Record]:
        """Events of a session after ``after``, in order."""
        with self._lock:
            rows = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT seq, ts, type, payload FROM events WHERE session_id = ? AND seq > ? ORDER BY seq", (session_id, after)
            ).fetchall()
        return [Record(seq, ts, t, json.loads(p)) for seq, ts, t, p in rows]

    def _info(self, row: tuple) -> SessionInfo:
        sid, workdir, title, created, updated, parent, fork_seq, meta = row
        return SessionInfo(sid, workdir, title, created, updated, parent, fork_seq, json.loads(meta or "{}"))

    def info(self, session_id: str) -> SessionInfo | None:
        """The session row, or ``None``."""
        with self._lock:
            row = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT id, workdir, title, created, updated, parent_id, fork_seq, meta FROM sessions WHERE id = ?", (session_id,)
            ).fetchone()
        return self._info(row) if row else None

    def list(self, workdir: str | None = None, limit: int = 100) -> list[SessionInfo]:
        """Sessions, most recently updated first."""
        sql = "SELECT id, workdir, title, created, updated, parent_id, fork_seq, meta FROM sessions"
        args: tuple = ()
        if workdir is not None:
            sql += " WHERE workdir = ?"
            args = (workdir,)
        sql += " ORDER BY updated DESC LIMIT ?"
        with self._lock:
            rows = self._conn.execute(sql, (*args, limit)).fetchall()
        return [self._info(r) for r in rows]

    def children(self, session_id: str) -> list[SessionInfo]:
        """Sessions whose parent is ``session_id`` (forks, subagents, background tasks)."""
        with self._lock:
            rows = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT id, workdir, title, created, updated, parent_id, fork_seq, meta FROM sessions WHERE parent_id = ? ORDER BY created",
                (session_id,),
            ).fetchall()
        return [self._info(r) for r in rows]

    def set_title(self, session_id: str, title: str) -> None:
        """Rename a session."""
        with self._lock:
            self._conn.execute("UPDATE sessions SET title = ? WHERE id = ?", (title, session_id))

    def set_meta(self, session_id: str, **values: object) -> None:
        """Merge ``values`` into the session's metadata."""
        info = self.info(session_id)
        if info is None:
            return
        meta = {**info.meta, **values}
        with self._lock:
            self._conn.execute("UPDATE sessions SET meta = ? WHERE id = ?", (json.dumps(meta), session_id))

    def delete(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
        """Delete a session and its events."""
        with self._lock:
            cur = self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id = ?", (session_id,))
            self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM events WHERE session_id = ?", (session_id,))
        return cur.rowcount > 0

    def prune(self, older_than_days: float, workdir: str | None = None) -> int:
        """Delete sessions not updated for ``older_than_days``; returns how many."""
        cutoff = time.time() - older_than_days * 86400
        old = [i.id for i in self.list(workdir, limit=1_000_000) if i.updated < cutoff]
        for sid in old:
            self.delete(sid)
        return len(old)

    def close(self) -> None:
        """Close the database."""
        with self._lock:
            self._conn.close()


def patch_dangling(messages: list[Message]) -> list[Message]:
    """Give every tool call a result (synthetic "cancelled" ones where missing)."""
    out: list[Message] = []
    i = 0
    while i < len(messages):
        message = messages[i]
        out.append(message)
        i += 1
        calls = message.tool_calls if message.role == "assistant" else []
        if not calls:
            continue
        follow = messages[i] if i < len(messages) and messages[i].role == "tool" else None
        have = {r.call_id for r in follow.results} if follow else set()
        missing = [
            ToolResult(c.id, (Text("cancelled: the call did not finish"),), True, c.name) for c in calls if c.id not in have
        ]
        if follow is not None:
            i += 1
            out.append(Message.tool_results([*follow.results, *missing]) if missing else follow)
        elif missing:
            out.append(Message.tool_results(missing))
    return out


class Session:
    """A conversation backed by the event log."""

    def __init__(self, log: EventLog, session_id: str, *, workdir: str, undo: bool = True) -> None:
        self.log = log
        self.id = session_id
        self.workdir = workdir
        self.state: dict = {}
        self.undo_journal = UndoJournal(workdir, session_id) if undo else None
        self._queue: list[str] = []
        self._decisions: dict[str, asyncio.Future] = {}
        self._cancel: asyncio.Event | None = None
        self._children: list[Session] = []
        self._listeners: list = []

    @classmethod
    def create(cls, log: EventLog, workdir: str, *, title: str = "", undo: bool = True, meta: dict | None = None) -> Session:
        """A new session in ``workdir``."""
        return cls(log, log.create(workdir, title=title, meta=meta), workdir=workdir, undo=undo)

    @classmethod
    def open(cls, log: EventLog, session_id: str, *, undo: bool = True) -> Session:
        """An existing session."""
        info = log.info(session_id)
        if info is None:
            raise LunaCoreError(f"no session {session_id!r}")
        return cls(log, session_id, workdir=info.workdir, undo=undo)

    # -- log ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def append(self, type_: str, payload: dict) -> int:
        """Append an event to this session."""
        seq = self.log.append(self.id, type_, payload)
        if type_ == "user" and not self.log.info(self.id).title:
            text = message_from_dict(payload["message"]).text.strip().splitlines()
            if text:
                self.log.set_title(self.id, text[0][:80])
        return seq

    def records(self) -> list[Record]:
        """All events."""
        return self.log.records(self.id)

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        """Session title (first user line unless renamed)."""
        info = self.log.info(self.id)
        return info.title if info else ""

    def _scan(self) -> tuple[list[Entry], str | None, int, set[str], list[str]]:
        entries: list[Entry] = []
        summary: str | None = None
        cut = 0
        hidden: set[str] = set()
        redo: list[str] = []
        turn: str | None = None
        for rec in self.records():
            kind = rec.type
            if kind == "turn_started":
                turn = rec.payload["turn_id"]
                redo = []
            elif kind in _MESSAGE_TYPES:
                entries.append(Entry(rec.seq, turn, message_from_dict(rec.payload["message"])))
            elif kind == "compacted":
                summary, cut = rec.payload["summary"], int(rec.payload["upto_seq"])
            elif kind == "rewound":
                hidden.add(rec.payload["turn_id"])
                redo.append(rec.payload["turn_id"])
            elif kind == "restored":
                hidden.discard(rec.payload["turn_id"])
                if redo and redo[-1] == rec.payload["turn_id"]:
                    redo.pop()
        return entries, summary, cut, hidden, redo

    def entries(self) -> list[Entry]:
        """Visible messages (after compaction and rewinds), with positions."""
        entries, _summary, cut, hidden, _redo = self._scan()
        return [e for e in entries if e.seq > cut and e.turn_id not in hidden]

    def messages(self) -> list[Message]:
        """The model context: summary (if compacted) + visible messages, dangling calls patched."""
        _entries, summary, _cut, _hidden, _redo = self._scan()
        out = [Message.user(f"<summary of the earlier conversation>\n{summary}\n</summary>")] if summary else []
        out += [e.message for e in self.entries()]
        return patch_dangling(out)

    @property
    def has_user_messages(self) -> bool:
        """True once any user message was logged."""
        return any(r.type == "user" for r in self.records())

    def turn_ids(self) -> list[str]:
        """Visible turns that have messages, oldest first."""
        seen: list[str] = []
        for e in self.entries():
            if e.turn_id and e.turn_id not in seen:
                seen.append(e.turn_id)
        return seen

    def usage(self) -> Usage:
        """Tokens used by every model request in this session."""
        total = Usage()
        for rec in self.records():
            if rec.type == "assistant" and rec.payload.get("usage"):
                total = total + Usage.from_dict(rec.payload["usage"])
        return total

    def cost(self) -> float:
        """USD spent by this session and its subagents (turns with a known price only)."""
        own = sum(float(r.payload.get("cost") or 0) for r in self.records() if r.type == "turn_done")
        for info in self.log.children(self.id):
            if info.meta.get("kind") in ("subagent", "background"):
                own += Session(self.log, info.id, workdir=info.workdir, undo=False).cost()
        return own

    # -- children and listeners --------------------------------------------
    def adopt(self, child: Session) -> None:
        """Route approvals of ``child`` (a subagent session) through this session."""
        self._children.append(child)

    def release(self, child: Session) -> None:
        """Stop routing ``child``'s approvals."""
        if child in self._children:
            self._children.remove(child)

    def subscribe(self, fn) -> object:
        """Call ``fn(event)`` for out-of-turn events (background completions); returns an unsubscribe callable."""
        self._listeners.append(fn)
        return lambda: self._listeners.remove(fn) if fn in self._listeners else None

    def notify(self, event: object) -> None:
        """Deliver an out-of-turn event to every listener (listener errors are ignored)."""
        for fn in list(self._listeners):
            try:
                fn(event)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a broken listener must not break the agent
                pass

    def todos(self) -> list[dict]:
        """The latest plan written with ``write_todos``."""
        for rec in reversed(self.records()):
            if rec.type == "todos":
                return rec.payload["todos"]
        return []

    # -- approvals ---------------------------------------------------------
    def expect_decision(self, call_id: str) -> asyncio.Future:
        """A future resolved by :meth:`decide` for ``call_id``."""
        fut = asyncio.get_running_loop().create_future()
        self._decisions[call_id] = fut
        return fut

    def decide(self, call_id: str, decision: Decision | dict) -> None:
        """Answer a pending approval."""
        fut = self._decisions.pop(call_id, None)
        if fut is None:
            for child in self._children:
                if call_id in child.pending_approvals:
                    child.decide(call_id, decision)
                    return
            raise LunaCoreError(f"no approval is pending for call {call_id!r}")
        if not fut.done():
            fut.set_result(decision if isinstance(decision, Decision) else Decision.from_dict(decision))

    @property
    def pending_approvals(self) -> list[str]:
        """Call ids waiting for a decision (including adopted subagent sessions)."""
        own = [cid for cid, fut in self._decisions.items() if not fut.done()]
        return own + [cid for child in self._children for cid in child.pending_approvals]

    # -- queue and turn control -------------------------------------------
    def queue(self, text: str) -> None:
        """Deliver ``text`` as a user message at the next step boundary."""
        self._queue.append(text)

    def drain_queue(self) -> list[str]:
        """Take all queued texts."""
        items, self._queue = self._queue, []
        return items

    @property
    def busy(self) -> bool:
        """True while a turn runs."""
        return self._cancel is not None

    def _begin_turn(self) -> asyncio.Event:
        if self._cancel is not None:
            raise LunaCoreError("a turn is already running in this session")
        self._cancel = asyncio.Event()
        return self._cancel

    def _end_turn(self) -> None:
        self._cancel = None
        for fut in self._decisions.values():
            if not fut.done():
                fut.cancel()
        self._decisions.clear()

    def cancel_turn(self) -> bool:
        """Ask the running turn to stop; False when idle."""
        if self._cancel is None:
            return False
        self._cancel.set()
        return True

    # -- history -----------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def undo_depth(self) -> int:
        """How many turns /undo can revert."""
        return len(self.turn_ids())

    def undo(self) -> UndoResult | None:
        """Revert the last turn: files (when journaled) and conversation."""
        turns = self.turn_ids()
        if not turns:
            return None
        last = turns[-1]
        note = "conversation rewound"
        if self.undo_journal is not None:
            res = self.undo_journal.undo()
            if res is not None:
                note = f"{res.note}; conversation rewound"
        self.append("rewound", {"turn_id": last})
        return UndoResult(last, note)

    def redo(self) -> UndoResult | None:
        """Re-apply the most recently undone turn."""
        *_rest, redo = self._scan()
        if not redo:
            return None
        turn_id = redo[-1]
        if self.undo_journal is not None:
            self.undo_journal.redo()
        self.append("restored", {"turn_id": turn_id})
        return UndoResult(turn_id, "turn restored")

    def rewind(self, to_seq: int) -> int:
        """Undo every visible turn that started after ``to_seq``; returns how many."""
        started = {r.payload["turn_id"]: r.seq for r in self.records() if r.type == "turn_started"}
        count = 0
        for turn_id in reversed(self.turn_ids()):
            if started.get(turn_id, 0) <= to_seq:
                break
            self.undo()
            count += 1
        return count

    def fork(self, at_seq: int | None = None) -> Session:
        """A new session with this one's history up to ``at_seq``."""
        records = [r for r in self.records() if at_seq is None or r.seq <= at_seq]
        child_id = self.log.create(self.workdir, title=self.title, parent_id=self.id, fork_seq=at_seq)
        for rec in records:
            self.log.append(child_id, rec.type, rec.payload)
        return Session(self.log, child_id, workdir=self.workdir, undo=self.undo_journal is not None)

    def export(self, fmt: str = "md") -> str:
        """The transcript as Markdown, HTML or JSON."""
        if fmt == "json":
            return json.dumps(
                {"id": self.id, "title": self.title, "records": [r.__dict__ for r in self.records()]}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2
            )
        parts: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        for message in self.messages():
            if message.role == "user":
                parts.append(("You", message.text))
            elif message.role == "assistant":
                lines = [message.text] if message.text else []
                lines += [f"→ {c.name}({json.dumps(c.args, ensure_ascii=False)[:200]})" for c in message.tool_calls]
                parts.append(("Luna", "\n".join(lines)))
            else:
                for r in message.results:
                    parts.append((f"{r.name or 'tool'}{' (error)' if r.is_error else ''}", r.text[:2000]))
        if fmt == "md":
            body = "\n\n".join(f"## {who}\n\n{text}" for who, text in parts)
            return f"# {self.title or self.id}\n\n{body}\n"
        if fmt == "html":
            body = "\n".join(f"<section><h2>{html.escape(who)}</h2><pre>{html.escape(text)}</pre></section>" for who, text in parts)
            return f'<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>{html.escape(self.title)}</title>\n{body}\n'
        raise ValueError(f"unknown export format {fmt!r}")


__all__ = ["Entry", "EventLog", "Record", "Session", "SessionInfo", "patch_dangling"]
```

### Task 18: Hooks

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/hooks.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_hooks.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `POINTS = ("session_start", "pre_tool", "post_tool", "turn_end", "pre_compact")`
  - `HookResult(block=False, message="", args=None, context="", notices=())`
  - `HookFn = Callable[[dict], Awaitable[HookResult | None]]`
  - `Hooks()`: `.add(point, fn, *, match="*", timeout=60.0)`, `async .run(point, payload, *, tool=None) -> HookResult`, `.extend(other)`, `len(hooks)`
  - `CommandHook(command, *, cwd, timeout=60.0)` — awaitable hook running a shell command
  - `load_command_hooks(entries: list[dict], cwd) -> Hooks` — entries from config `[[hooks]]` (`event`, `command`, optional `match`, `timeout`)
- Semantics: hooks run in registration order. `pre_tool` hooks see the args already modified by earlier hooks (`payload["args"]` is replaced) and the merged result carries the final `args`. The first `block` stops the chain. `context` strings are joined with blank lines; `notices` accumulate. An exception or timeout in a hook becomes a notice (`hook <name> failed: …`) and never blocks. Command hooks receive the JSON payload on stdin; exit 0 → `context = stdout`; exit 2 → `block`, `message = stderr` (or stdout); other exit codes → notice with stderr.
