```python
import asyncio
import json
import sys

from luna_core.hooks import CommandHook, HookResult, Hooks, load_command_hooks


async def test_chain_modifies_args_and_first_block_wins():
    hooks = Hooks()
    seen = []

    async def add_flag(payload):
        seen.append(dict(payload["args"]))
        return HookResult(args={**payload["args"], "timeout": 5})

    async def blocker(payload):
        seen.append(dict(payload["args"]))
        return HookResult(block=True, message="no rm")

    async def never(payload):
        raise AssertionError("not reached")

    hooks.add("pre_tool", add_flag, match="execute")
    hooks.add("pre_tool", blocker, match="exec*")
    hooks.add("pre_tool", never)
    res = await hooks.run("pre_tool", {"args": {"command": "rm x"}}, tool="execute")
    assert res.block and res.message == "no rm" and seen[1] == {"command": "rm x", "timeout": 5}


async def test_match_filters_by_tool_and_context_joins():
    hooks = Hooks()
    hooks.add("post_tool", lambda p: _ret(HookResult(context="lint ok")), match="edit_file")
    hooks.add("post_tool", lambda p: _ret(HookResult(context="types ok")), match="*")
    assert (await hooks.run("post_tool", {}, tool="read_file")).context == "types ok"
    assert (await hooks.run("post_tool", {}, tool="edit_file")).context == "lint ok\n\ntypes ok"


async def _ret(value):
    return value


async def test_failures_and_timeouts_become_notices():
    hooks = Hooks()

    async def boom(payload):
        raise RuntimeError("bad")

    async def slow(payload):
        await asyncio.sleep(10)

    hooks.add("turn_end", boom)
    hooks.add("turn_end", slow, timeout=0.1)
    res = await hooks.run("turn_end", {})
    assert not res.block and len(res.notices) == 2 and "bad" in res.notices[0] and "timed out" in res.notices[1]


async def test_command_hook_exit_codes(tmp_path):
    script = tmp_path / "hook.py"
    script.write_text(
        "import json, sys\n"
        "p = json.load(sys.stdin)\n"
        "cmd = p['args'].get('command', '')\n"
        "if 'rm' in cmd:\n    print('dangerous', file=sys.stderr); sys.exit(2)\n"
        "if 'warn' in cmd:\n    print('meh', file=sys.stderr); sys.exit(1)\n"
        "print('checked ' + p['tool'])\n"
    )
    hook = CommandHook(f"{sys.executable} {script}", cwd=tmp_path)
    ok = await hook({"tool": "execute", "args": {"command": "ls"}})
    assert ok.context.strip() == "checked execute" and not ok.block
    blocked = await hook({"tool": "execute", "args": {"command": "rm -rf x"}})
    assert blocked.block and blocked.message.strip() == "dangerous"
    warned = await hook({"tool": "execute", "args": {"command": "warn"}})
    assert not warned.block and "meh" in warned.notices[0]


async def test_load_command_hooks_from_config(tmp_path):
    hooks = load_command_hooks(
        [
            {"event": "pre_tool", "match": "execute", "command": "exit 2", "timeout": 5},
            {"event": "nonsense", "command": "true"},
        ],
        tmp_path,
    )
    assert len(hooks) == 1
    assert (await hooks.run("pre_tool", {"args": {}}, tool="execute")).block
    assert not (await hooks.run("pre_tool", {"args": {}}, tool="ls")).block


def test_payload_is_json_serialisable_contract():
    assert json.dumps({"event": "pre_tool", "tool": "x", "args": {}, "session_id": "s"})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/hooks.py`:

```python
"""Hook points around the loop: Python callables and user command hooks."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from fnmatch import fnmatch
from pathlib import Path

POINTS = ("session_start", "pre_tool", "post_tool", "turn_end", "pre_compact")


@dataclass
class HookResult:
    """What a hook asks for."""

    block: bool = False
    message: str = ""
    args: dict | None = None
    context: str = ""
    notices: tuple[str, ...] | list[str] = field(default_factory=tuple)


HookFn = Callable[[dict], Awaitable[HookResult | None]]


@dataclass
class _Entry:
    fn: HookFn
    match: str
    timeout: float


class Hooks:
    """An ordered registry of hooks per point."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._hooks: dict[str, list[_Entry]] = {p: [] for p in POINTS}

    def add(self, point: str, fn: HookFn, *, match: str = "*", timeout: float = 60.0) -> None:
        """Register ``fn`` at ``point`` (``match`` filters tool names for tool points)."""
        if point not in POINTS:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown hook point {point!r}; expected one of {POINTS}")
        self._hooks[point].append(_Entry(fn, match, timeout))

    def extend(self, other: Hooks) -> None:
        """Append every hook of ``other``."""
        for point, entries in other._hooks.items():
            self._hooks[point].extend(entries)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        """Number of registered hooks."""
        return sum(len(v) for v in self._hooks.values())

    async def run(self, point: str, payload: dict, *, tool: str | None = None) -> HookResult:
        """Run the hooks for ``point`` and merge their results."""
        merged = HookResult(notices=[])
        payload = {**payload, "event": point}
        contexts: list[str] = []
        for entry in self._hooks[point]:
            if tool is not None and not fnmatch(tool, entry.match):
                continue
            name = getattr(entry.fn, "__name__", None) or repr(entry.fn)
            try:
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(entry.fn(payload), entry.timeout)
            except TimeoutError:
                merged.notices.append(f"hook {name} timed out after {entry.timeout:g}s")
                continue
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a hook must never break a turn
                merged.notices.append(f"hook {name} failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
                continue
            if result is None:
                continue
            merged.notices.extend(result.notices)
            if result.context:
                contexts.append(result.context)
            if result.args is not None:
                payload["args"] = result.args
                merged.args = result.args
            if result.block:
                merged.block = True
                merged.message = result.message
                break
        merged.context = "\n\n".join(contexts)
        merged.notices = tuple(merged.notices)
        return merged


class CommandHook:
    """A user hook: a shell command fed the JSON payload on stdin."""

    def __init__(self, command: str, *, cwd: str | Path, timeout: float = 60.0) -> None:
        self.command = command
        self.cwd = str(cwd)
        self.timeout = timeout
        self.__name__ = command.split()[0] if command.split() else "command"

    async def __call__(self, payload: dict) -> HookResult:
        """Run the command; exit 0 continues, 2 blocks, anything else warns."""
        proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_shell(
            self.command,
            cwd=self.cwd,
            stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
            stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
            start_new_session=True,
        )
        try:
            out, err = await asyncio.wait_for(proc.communicate(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False).encode()), self.timeout)
        except TimeoutError:
            proc.kill()
            await proc.wait()
            return HookResult(notices=(f"hook `{self.command}` timed out after {self.timeout:g}s",))
        stdout = out.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        stderr = err.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        if proc.returncode == 0:
            return HookResult(context=stdout.strip())
        if proc.returncode == 2:
            return HookResult(block=True, message=(stderr or stdout).strip() or f"blocked by hook `{self.command}`")
        return HookResult(notices=(f"hook `{self.command}` exited {proc.returncode}: {stderr.strip()[:300]}",))


def load_command_hooks(entries: list[dict], cwd: str | Path) -> Hooks:
    """Build :class:`Hooks` from config ``[[hooks]]`` tables (invalid entries are skipped)."""
    hooks = Hooks()
    for entry in entries or []:
        point = entry.get("event")
        command = entry.get("command")
        if point not in POINTS or not isinstance(command, str) or not command.strip():
            continue
        timeout = float(entry.get("timeout", 60))
        hooks.add(point, CommandHook(command, cwd=cwd, timeout=timeout), match=str(entry.get("match", "*")), timeout=timeout + 5)
    return hooks
```

---

### Task 19: Agent loop

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/loop.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_loop.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 2–18.
- Produces:
  - `PathLocks()`: `hold(keys: list[str])` async context manager (keys acquired sorted; shared by an agent and its subagents)
  - `Agent(provider, model, *, tools=None, system="", policy=None, workspace=None, sandbox=None, hooks=None, max_steps=50, max_tokens=None, temperature=None, reasoning=None, max_cost=None, compactor=None, services=None, tool_output_budget=30_000, locks=None, model_overrides=None, name="luna")`
    - attributes `provider, model, tools: dict[str, Tool], policy, workspace, sandbox, hooks, locks, services, name`
    - `.system_prompt(session) -> str` (`system` may be a string or `Callable[[Session], str]`)
    - `.add_tool(tool)`, `.remove_tool(name)`, `.model_info -> ModelInfo`
    - `async .run(session, text: str = "", *, images=()) -> AsyncIterator[TurnEvent]`
    - `.run_sync(session, text="", *, images=(), approve=None) -> list[TurnEvent]` (`approve: Callable[[ApprovalRequest], Decision]`; without it approvals are rejected with "no approval handler")
    - `.request(session, messages=None) -> ModelRequest` (used by compaction and `/btw`)
  - `readable_error(exc: ProviderError, provider_name: str) -> str`
  - `Compactor` protocol consumed: `async maybe_compact(agent, session) -> AsyncIterator[TurnEvent]` (Task 21 implements it)
  - `services` passed to tools: `{"agent": self, "session": session, "turn_id": ..., **self.services}`

Loop algorithm (one turn):
1. `turn_started` logged → `TurnStarted`; undo journal `begin_turn` (in a thread).
2. On the session's first user message, `session_start` hooks run; their `context` is appended to the user message as a text block; notices become `Notice("info")`.
3. Per step: check cancel → drain `session.queue()` into user messages → `compactor.maybe_compact` → `max_steps` / `max_cost` guards → build `ModelRequest` from `session.messages()` → stream (cancellable) → log `assistant` with usage → `UsageUpdate`.
4. No tool calls → `turn_end` hooks; a `block` result feeds its message back as a user message and the loop continues; otherwise `turn_done` → `TurnDone(usage, cost, stop_reason)`.
5. Tool calls → per call in order: unknown tool / invalid JSON → error result; policy verdict (`deny` → error; `ask` → `NeedsApproval` and wait for `session.decide` or cancel; `always` remembered; `edit` replaces args; `reject` → error "rejected by the user: …"); `pre_tool` hooks (block → error; args replaced). Approved calls then run **concurrently**, each under `PathLocks` keys (`<root>:<rel>` for file mutations, `<root>:shell` for `execute`, none for reads); file mutations call `undo_journal.before_write` first; `post_tool` hooks may append context or turn the result into an error; results over `tool_output_budget` characters are saved to `.luna/tool-output/<call_id>.txt` and replaced by head + tail + path; `policy.after` updates anchors. Results are logged in call order as one `tool_results` message.
6. Cancel (`session.cancel_turn()`): the in-flight stream is abandoned (partial text kept, partial tool calls dropped), tools see `ctx.cancel` (the sandbox kills the process group), tools still running after 2 s are task-cancelled, unfinished calls get "cancelled by the user" results, `cancelled` is logged → `Cancelled`. Cancelling the consumer task (`task.cancel()`) does the same bookkeeping and re-raises `CancelledError`.
7. `ProviderError` → partial text kept, `turn_error` logged → `TurnError(readable_error(...), kind)`.

```python
    events = []
    async for ev in agent.run(session, text):
        events.append(ev)
        if isinstance(ev, NeedsApproval) and decide:
            session.decide(ev.request.call_id, decide(ev.request))
    return events
```

```python
"""The agent loop: model ↔ tools, approvals, hooks, cancellation."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import dataclasses
import os
import time
import uuid
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol

from luna_core import catalog
from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.events import (
    ApprovalRequest,
    Cancelled,
    NeedsApproval,
    Notice,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    ToolFinished,
    ToolStarted,
    TurnDone,
    TurnError,
    TurnStarted,
    UsageUpdate,
)
from luna_core.hooks import Hooks
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, ToolCall, ToolResult, message_to_dict
from luna_core.policy import MUTATING_FILE_TOOLS, Decision, Policy
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, Provider, StreamAccumulator
from luna_core.sandbox import LocalSandbox, Sandbox
from luna_core.session import Session
from luna_core.tools import builtin_tools
from luna_core.tools.base import Tool, ToolContext, ToolOutput, args_preview
from luna_core.usage import Usage, cost
from luna_core.workspace import OutsideWorkspace, Workspace

_DONE = object()
_GRACE = 2.0


class Compactor(Protocol):
    """Shrinks the context when it gets too large (see ``luna_core.compaction``)."""

    def maybe_compact(self, agent: Agent, session: Session) -> AsyncIterator[object]:
        """Yield turn events (``Compacted``, notices) if it compacted."""
        ...


class _Cancelled(Exception):
    pass


class PathLocks:
    """Named asyncio locks; mutating tools hold one per path."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._locks: dict[str, asyncio.Lock] = {}

    def locked(self, key: str) -> bool:
        """True while ``key`` is held."""
        lock = self._locks.get(key)
        return bool(lock and lock.locked())

    @contextlib.asynccontextmanager
    async def hold(self, keys: list[str]):
        """Hold every key (sorted, so two holders never deadlock)."""
        acquired: list[asyncio.Lock] = []
        try:
            for key in sorted(set(keys)):
                lock = self._locks.setdefault(key, asyncio.Lock())
                await lock.acquire()
                acquired.append(lock)
            yield
        finally:
            for lock in reversed(acquired):
                lock.release()


def readable_error(exc: ProviderError, provider_name: str) -> str:
    """A message for the user, with the fix where there is one."""
    if exc.kind == "auth":
        return f"{provider_name} rejected the API key ({exc}). Set it with `luna config set-key {provider_name}` or the provider's env var."
    if exc.kind == "context_length":
        return f"the conversation no longer fits the model's context window ({exc}). Run /compact or start a new session."
    if exc.kind == "rate_limit":
        return f"{provider_name} is rate limiting requests ({exc}). Wait a moment and retry."
    if exc.kind == "network":
        return f"network problem talking to {provider_name}: {exc}"
    return f"{provider_name}: {exc}"


async def _cancellable(stream: AsyncIterator, cancel: asyncio.Event) -> AsyncIterator:
    iterator = stream.__aiter__()
    waiter = asyncio.ensure_future(cancel.wait())
    try:
        while True:
            step = asyncio.ensure_future(iterator.__anext__())
            done, _ = await asyncio.wait({step, waiter}, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)
            if step not in done:
                step.cancel()
                with contextlib.suppress(BaseException):
                    await step
                raise _Cancelled
            exc = step.exception()
            if isinstance(exc, StopAsyncIteration):
                return
            if exc is not None:
                raise exc
            yield step.result()
    finally:
        waiter.cancel()
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            await iterator.aclose()


class Agent:
    """A configured agent; ``run`` executes one turn in a session."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider: Provider,
        model: str,
        *,
        tools: list[Tool] | None = None,
        system: str | Callable[[Session], str] = "",
        policy: Policy | None = None,
        workspace: Workspace | None = None,
        sandbox: Sandbox | None = None,
        hooks: Hooks | None = None,
        max_steps: int = 50,
        max_tokens: int | None = None,
        temperature: float | None = None,
        reasoning: str | None = None,
        max_cost: float | None = None,
        compactor: Compactor | None = None,
        services: dict | None = None,
        tool_output_budget: int = 30_000,
        locks: PathLocks | None = None,
        model_overrides: dict | None = None,
        name: str = "luna",
    ) -> None:
        self.provider = provider
        self.model = model
        self.workspace = workspace or Workspace(os.getcwd())
        self.tools: dict[str, Tool] = {t.name: t for t in (tools if tools is not None else builtin_tools())}
        self.system = system
        self.policy = policy or Policy(workspace=self.workspace)
        self.sandbox = sandbox or LocalSandbox()
        self.hooks = hooks or Hooks()
        self.max_steps = max_steps
        self.max_tokens = max_tokens
        self.temperature = temperature
        self.reasoning = reasoning
        self.max_cost = max_cost
        self.compactor = compactor
        self.services = dict(services or {})
        self.tool_output_budget = tool_output_budget
        self.locks = locks or PathLocks()
        self.model_overrides = model_overrides
        self.name = name

    # -- configuration -----------------------------------------------------
    def add_tool(self, tool: Tool) -> None:
        """Add or replace a tool."""
        self.tools[tool.name] = tool

    def remove_tool(self, name: str) -> None:
        """Remove a tool if present."""
        self.tools.pop(name, None)

    @property
    def model_info(self) -> catalog.ModelInfo:
        """Catalogue entry for the model."""
        return catalog.lookup(self.model, self.model_overrides)

    def system_prompt(self, session: Session) -> str:
        """The system prompt for ``session``."""
        return self.system(session) if callable(self.system) else self.system

    def request(self, session: Session, messages: list[Message] | None = None) -> ModelRequest:
        """The model request for the session's current context."""
        return ModelRequest(
            model=self.model,
            system=self.system_prompt(session),
            messages=session.messages() if messages is None else messages,
            tools=[t.spec for t in self.tools.values()],
            max_tokens=self.max_tokens,
            temperature=self.temperature,
            reasoning=self.reasoning,
        )

    # -- running -----------------------------------------------------------
    async def run(self, session: Session, text: str = "", *, images: tuple[Image, ...] | list[Image] = ()) -> AsyncIterator[object]:
        """Run one turn; yields turn events."""
        cancel = session._begin_turn()
        turn_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
        try:
            async for event in self._turn(session, turn_id, text, list(images), cancel):
                yield event
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            if not any(r.type == "cancelled" and r.payload.get("turn_id") == turn_id for r in session.records()[-3:]):
                session.append("cancelled", {"turn_id": turn_id})
            raise
        finally:
            session._end_turn()

    def run_sync(
        self,
        session: Session,
        text: str = "",
        *,
        images: tuple[Image, ...] = (),
        approve: Callable[[ApprovalRequest], Decision] | None = None,
    ) -> list[object]:
        """Blocking wrapper around :meth:`run` for non-async callers."""

        async def main() -> list[object]:
            events = []
            async for event in self.run(session, text, images=images):
                events.append(event)
                if isinstance(event, NeedsApproval):
                    decision = approve(event.request) if approve else Decision.reject("no approval handler")
                    session.decide(event.request.call_id, decision)
            return events

        return asyncio.run(main())

    async def _turn(self, session: Session, turn_id: str, text: str, images: list[Image], cancel: asyncio.Event) -> AsyncIterator[object]:
        first = not session.has_user_messages
        session.append("turn_started", {"turn_id": turn_id})
        yield TurnStarted(turn_id)
        if session.undo_journal is not None:
            await asyncio.to_thread(session.undo_journal.begin_turn, turn_id)
        blocks: list = [Text(text)] if text else []
        blocks += images
        if first:
            outcome = await self.hooks.run("session_start", {"session_id": session.id, "workdir": session.workdir})
            for note in outcome.notices:
                yield Notice("info", note)
            if outcome.context:
                blocks.append(Text(f"\n\n{outcome.context}"))
        session.append("user", {"message": message_to_dict(Message("user", blocks))})
        total = Usage()
        steps = 0
        stop_reason = "end"
        while True:
            if cancel.is_set():
                yield self._record_cancel(session, turn_id)
                return
            for queued in session.drain_queue():
                session.append("user", {"message": message_to_dict(Message.user(queued))})
            if self.compactor is not None:
                async for event in self.compactor.maybe_compact(self, session):
                    yield event
            if steps >= self.max_steps:
                yield Notice("warn", f"stopped after max_steps={self.max_steps} model calls")
                stop_reason = "max_steps"
                break
            if self.max_cost is not None and session.cost() + (cost(total, self.model_info) or 0) >= self.max_cost:
                message = f"budget reached: max_cost=${self.max_cost:g}"
                session.append("turn_error", {"turn_id": turn_id, "message": message, "kind": "budget"})
                yield TurnError(message, "budget")
                return
            steps += 1
            acc = StreamAccumulator(self.provider.name)
            try:
                async for event in _cancellable(self.provider.stream(self.request(session)), cancel):
                    acc.feed(event)
                    if isinstance(event, TextDelta):
                        yield event
                    elif isinstance(event, ThinkingDelta) and event.text:
                        yield ThinkingDelta(event.text)
            except _Cancelled:
                self._record_partial(session, acc)
                yield self._record_cancel(session, turn_id)
                return
            except ProviderError as exc:
                self._record_partial(session, acc)
                message = readable_error(exc, self.provider.name)
                session.append("turn_error", {"turn_id": turn_id, "message": message, "kind": exc.kind})
                yield TurnError(message, exc.kind)
                return
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                self._record_partial(session, acc)
                raise
            message = acc.message()
            session.append("assistant", {"message": message_to_dict(message), "usage": acc.usage.to_dict()})
            total = total + acc.usage
            yield UsageUpdate(acc.usage)
            if not message.tool_calls:
                stop_reason = acc.stop_reason
                if stop_reason == "max_tokens":
                    yield Notice("warn", "the answer was cut off at max_tokens")
                outcome = await self.hooks.run("turn_end", {"session_id": session.id, "turn_id": turn_id, "text": message.text})
                for note in outcome.notices:
                    yield Notice("info", note)
                if outcome.block and outcome.message and steps < self.max_steps:
                    session.append("user", {"message": message_to_dict(Message.user(outcome.message))})
                    continue
                break
            results: list[ToolResult] = []
            async for event in self._run_tools(session, turn_id, message.tool_calls, cancel, results):
                yield event
            session.append("tool_results", {"message": message_to_dict(Message.tool_results(results))})
            if cancel.is_set():
                yield self._record_cancel(session, turn_id)
                return
        price = cost(total, self.model_info)
        session.append("turn_done", {"turn_id": turn_id, "usage": total.to_dict(), "cost": price, "stop_reason": stop_reason})
        yield TurnDone(total, price, stop_reason)

    def _record_partial(self, session: Session, acc: StreamAccumulator) -> None:
        partial = acc.message()
        kept = [b for b in partial.content if not isinstance(b, ToolCall)]
        if any(isinstance(b, Text) and b.text for b in kept):
            session.append("assistant", {"message": message_to_dict(Message.assistant(kept)), "usage": acc.usage.to_dict()})

    def _record_cancel(self, session: Session, turn_id: str) -> Cancelled:
        session.append("cancelled", {"turn_id": turn_id})
        return Cancelled()

    # -- tools -------------------------------------------------------------
    async def _run_tools(
        self, session: Session, turn_id: str, calls: list[ToolCall], cancel: asyncio.Event, out: list[ToolResult]
    ) -> AsyncIterator[object]:
        results: dict[str, ToolResult] = {}
        approved: list[tuple[ToolCall, Tool, dict]] = []

        def refuse(call: ToolCall, text: str) -> ToolFinished:
            results[call.id] = ToolResult(call.id, (Text(text),), True, call.name)
            return ToolFinished(call.id, call.name, False, text.splitlines()[0][:120], 0.0)

        for call in calls:
            if cancel.is_set():
                break
            tool = self.tools.get(call.name)
            if tool is None:
                yield refuse(call, f"unknown tool {call.name!r}; available: {', '.join(sorted(self.tools))}")
                continue
            args = dict(call.args)
            if "__invalid_json__" in args:
                yield refuse(call, f"the arguments for {call.name} were not valid JSON: {args['__invalid_json__'][:200]}")
                continue
            verdict = self.policy.check(tool, args)
            if verdict.action == "deny":
                yield refuse(call, verdict.message)
                continue
            if verdict.action == "ask":
                request = dataclasses.replace(verdict.request, call_id=call.id)
                future = session.expect_decision(call.id)
                yield NeedsApproval(request)
                decision = await self._await_decision(future, cancel)
                if decision is None:
                    break
                session.append("approval", {"call_id": call.id, "decision": decision.to_dict()})
                if decision.always:
                    self.policy.remember_always(decision.always)
                if decision.type == "reject":
                    yield refuse(call, "rejected by the user" + (f": {decision.message}" if decision.message else ""))
                    continue
                if decision.type == "edit" and decision.args is not None:
                    args = dict(decision.args)
            pre = await self.hooks.run("pre_tool", {"session_id": session.id, "tool": call.name, "args": args}, tool=call.name)
            for note in pre.notices:
                yield Notice("info", note)
            if pre.block:
                yield refuse(call, pre.message or f"blocked by a hook ({call.name})")
                continue
            if pre.args is not None:
                args = dict(pre.args)
            approved.append((call, tool, args))

        queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()
        tasks = [asyncio.create_task(self._execute(session, turn_id, call, tool, args, queue, cancel, results)) for call, tool, args in approved]
        watchdog = asyncio.create_task(self._watchdog(cancel, tasks)) if tasks else None
        try:
            finished = 0
            while finished < len(tasks):
                event = await queue.get()
                if event is _DONE:
                    finished += 1
                else:
                    yield event
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            cancel.set()
            for task in tasks:
                task.cancel()
            await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
            self._fill_cancelled(calls, results, out)
            session.append("tool_results", {"message": message_to_dict(Message.tool_results(out))})
            raise
        finally:
            if watchdog:
                watchdog.cancel()
        self._fill_cancelled(calls, results, out)

    @staticmethod
    def _fill_cancelled(calls: list[ToolCall], results: dict[str, ToolResult], out: list[ToolResult]) -> None:
        out.clear()
        for call in calls:
            out.append(results.get(call.id) or ToolResult(call.id, (Text("cancelled by the user"),), True, call.name))

    @staticmethod
    async def _await_decision(future: asyncio.Future, cancel: asyncio.Event) -> Decision | None:
        waiter = asyncio.ensure_future(cancel.wait())
        try:
            done, _ = await asyncio.wait({future, waiter}, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)
        finally:
            waiter.cancel()
        if future in done and not future.cancelled():
            return future.result()
        future.cancel()
        return None

    @staticmethod
    async def _watchdog(cancel: asyncio.Event, tasks: list[asyncio.Task]) -> None:
        await cancel.wait()
        await asyncio.sleep(_GRACE)
        for task in tasks:
            task.cancel()

    def _lock_keys(self, tool: Tool, args: dict) -> list[str]:
        root = str(self.workspace.root)
        if tool.name == "execute":
            return [f"{root}:shell"]
        if not tool.mutating:
            return []
        raw = args.get("file_path") or args.get("path") or ""
        try:
            rel = self.workspace.rel(self.workspace.resolve(raw)) if raw else "*"
        except OutsideWorkspace:
            rel = str(raw)
        return [f"{root}:{rel}"]

    async def _execute(
        self,
        session: Session,
        turn_id: str,
        call: ToolCall,
        tool: Tool,
        args: dict,
        queue: asyncio.Queue,
        cancel: asyncio.Event,
        results: dict[str, ToolResult],
    ) -> None:
        started = time.monotonic()
        queue.put_nowait(ToolStarted(call.id, call.name, args, args_preview(args)))
        ctx = ToolContext(
            workspace=self.workspace,
            sandbox=self.sandbox,
            session_id=session.id,
            call_id=call.id,
            emit=queue.put_nowait,
            state=session.state,
            cancel=cancel,
            services={**self.services, "agent": self, "session": session, "turn_id": turn_id},
        )
        try:
            async with self.locks.hold(self._lock_keys(tool, args)):
                # A subagent working in its parent's workspace records into the
                # parent's journal and turn (services set by the subagent runner).
                journal = self.services.get("undo_journal") or session.undo_journal
                journal_turn = self.services.get("undo_turn_id") or turn_id
                if tool.name in MUTATING_FILE_TOOLS and journal is not None:
                    raw = args.get("file_path") or args.get("path") or ""
                    with contextlib.suppress(OutsideWorkspace, OSError, ValueError):
                        rel = self.workspace.rel(self.workspace.resolve(raw))
                        await asyncio.to_thread(journal.before_write, journal_turn, rel)
                output = await tool.invoke(ctx, args)
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            output = ToolOutput.error("cancelled by the user")
        try:
            post = await self.hooks.run(
                "post_tool",
                {"session_id": session.id, "tool": call.name, "args": args, "ok": not output.is_error, "output": "".join(p.text for p in output.content if isinstance(p, Text))[:20_000]},
                tool=call.name,
            )
            for note in post.notices:
                queue.put_nowait(Notice("info", note))
            if post.context or post.block:
                extra = Text(f"\n\n{post.message or post.context}")
                output = ToolOutput((*output.content, extra), output.is_error or post.block, output.detail)
            output = self._clip(output, call.id)
            self.policy.after(call.name, args, not output.is_error)
            if call.name == "write_todos" and not output.is_error:
                session.append("todos", {"todos": session.state.get("todos", [])})
        finally:
            results[call.id] = ToolResult(call.id, output.content, output.is_error, call.name)
            detail = output.detail or (output.content[0].text.splitlines()[0][:120] if output.content and isinstance(output.content[0], Text) and output.content[0].text else "")
            queue.put_nowait(ToolFinished(call.id, call.name, not output.is_error, detail, time.monotonic() - started))
            queue.put_nowait(_DONE)

    def _clip(self, output: ToolOutput, call_id: str) -> ToolOutput:
        text = "".join(p.text for p in output.content if isinstance(p, Text))
        if len(text) <= self.tool_output_budget:
            return output
        target = self.workspace.root / ".luna" / "tool-output" / f"{call_id}.txt"
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        half = self.tool_output_budget // 2 - 200
        rel = Path(".luna", "tool-output", f"{call_id}.txt").as_posix()
        clipped = f"{text[:half]}\n\n… [{len(text) - 2 * half} characters omitted; full output saved to {rel} — read it with read_file] …\n\n{text[-half:]}"
        images = tuple(p for p in output.content if isinstance(p, Image))
        return ToolOutput((Text(clipped), *images), output.is_error, output.detail)


__all__ = ["Agent", "Compactor", "PathLocks", "readable_error"]
```

Notes for the implementer:
- `ToolContext.emit` is `queue.put_nowait`, so tool-emitted events (`TodosUpdated`, `Notice`, subagent progress) interleave with `ToolStarted`/`ToolFinished` in the stream.
- In `run`, the `except asyncio.CancelledError` branch only adds `cancelled` when `_turn` has not already logged it (a consumer cancel during tools logs the tool results inside `_run_tools`).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/context.py`, `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/memory.py`, `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/skills.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_context.py`, `packages/luna-core/tests/test_skills_memory.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`memory.py`): `KINDS = ("project", "conventions", "decisions", "failures")`, `memory_files(root) -> list[str]`, `append_note(root, kind, topic, note) -> Path`, `remember` tool (mutating; writes under `ctx.workspace.root`).
- Produces (`skills.py`): `SkillInfo(name, description, path: Path, scope)`, `SkillError(LunaCoreError)`, `parse_frontmatter(text) -> tuple[dict, str]` (YAML via `yaml.safe_load`), `discover(dirs: list[tuple[str, Path]]) -> list[SkillInfo]` (later dirs override earlier names), `load_body(name, dirs) -> str`, `save(name, description, body, dest_dir) -> Path` (frontmatter written with `yaml.safe_dump`), `remove(name, dirs) -> bool`, `install(source, dest_dir, *, name=None, run=subprocess.run, resolve=None) -> str`, `skill_tool(dirs_fn) -> Tool` (`skill(name)` returns the body), `save_skill_tool(dest_fn) -> Tool` (mutating).
- Produces (`context.py`): `DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT`, `build_system_prompt(base, *, root, skills=(), memory=None, extra="", now=None) -> str`, `expand_mentions(text, workspace) -> tuple[str, list[Image]]`, `PinnedFiles`, `render_pinned(pinned, root) -> str`, `MAX_ATTACH = 100_000`.

Prompt layout (stable text first, so provider prompt caches hit): base prompt → `# Environment` (working directory, platform, today's date) → `# Project instructions (AGENTS.md)` → `# Memory` (each `.luna/memory/*.md`, 20 000 chars max each) → `# Skills` index (`- name: description`, with "load one with the `skill` tool") → `extra`.

```python
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from luna_core import skills
from luna_core.memory import append_note, memory_files, remember
from luna_core.skills import SkillError
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolContext
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace


def write_skill(root: Path, name: str, desc: str, body: str = "do it") -> Path:
    d = root / name
    d.mkdir(parents=True)
    (d / "SKILL.md").write_text(f"---\nname: {name}\ndescription: {desc}\n---\n\n{body}\n")
    return d


def test_frontmatter_is_real_yaml():
    meta, body = skills.parse_frontmatter('---\nname: x\ndescription: "a: b # not a comment"\n---\nBody\n')
    assert meta == {"name": "x", "description": "a: b # not a comment"} and body.strip() == "Body"
    assert skills.parse_frontmatter("no frontmatter") == ({}, "no frontmatter")


def test_discover_project_overrides_user(tmp_path):
    user, proj = tmp_path / "user", tmp_path / "proj"
    write_skill(user, "deploy", "user deploy")
    write_skill(user, "lint", "lint things")
    write_skill(proj, "deploy", "project deploy")
    found = {s.name: s for s in skills.discover([("user", user), ("project", proj), ("project", tmp_path / "missing")])}
    assert found["deploy"].description == "project deploy" and found["deploy"].scope == "project"
    assert found["lint"].scope == "user"
    assert skills.load_body("deploy", [("user", user), ("project", proj)]).strip() == "do it"


def test_save_round_trips_tricky_descriptions(tmp_path):
    path = skills.save("fix-ci", 'Fix CI: "ruff" + tests # always', "1. run ruff", tmp_path)
    meta, body = skills.parse_frontmatter(path.read_text())
    assert meta["description"] == 'Fix CI: "ruff" + tests # always' and body.strip() == "1. run ruff"
    with pytest.raises(SkillError):
        skills.save("bad name!", "x", "y", tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(SkillError):
        skills.save("ok", "two\nlines", "y", tmp_path)


def test_install_from_a_local_git_repo(tmp_path):
    repo = tmp_path / "repo"
    write_skill(repo / "skills", "pdf", "read pdfs")
    subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=repo, check=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t", "add", "."], cwd=repo, check=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t", "commit", "-qm", "x"], cwd=repo, check=True)
    dest = tmp_path / "installed"
    msg = skills.install(str(repo / "skills" / "pdf"), dest)
    assert "installed skill 'pdf'" in msg and (dest / "pdf" / "SKILL.md").is_file()
    assert skills.remove("pdf", [("user", dest)]) and not (dest / "pdf").exists()


async def test_skill_and_save_skill_tools(tmp_path):
    write_skill(tmp_path / "s", "deploy", "ship it", "step one")
    tool = skills.skill_tool(lambda: [("project", tmp_path / "s")])
    out = await tool.invoke(ToolContext(), {"name": "deploy"})
    assert "step one" in out.content[0].text
    missing = await tool.invoke(ToolContext(), {"name": "nope"})
    assert missing.is_error and "deploy" in missing.content[0].text
    saver = skills.save_skill_tool(lambda project: tmp_path / ("p" if project else "u"))
    assert saver.mutating
    res = await saver.invoke(ToolContext(), {"name": "x", "description": "d", "body": "b"})
    assert not res.is_error and (tmp_path / "p" / "x" / "SKILL.md").is_file()


async def test_memory_notes_and_remember_tool(tmp_path):
    path = append_note(tmp_path, "failures", "pip", "pip install breaks the venv")
    assert "## " in path.read_text() and "pip install breaks" in path.read_text()
    assert memory_files(tmp_path) == [".luna/memory/failures.md"]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        append_note(tmp_path, "random", "x", "y")
    out = await remember.invoke(ToolContext(workspace=Workspace(tmp_path)), {"kind": "decisions", "topic": "db", "note": "sqlite"})
    assert not out.is_error and (tmp_path / ".luna" / "memory" / "decisions.md").is_file() and remember.mutating
```

```python
import base64
import datetime

from luna_core.context import PinnedFiles, build_system_prompt, expand_mentions, render_pinned
from luna_core.memory import append_note
from luna_core.messages import Image
from luna_core.skills import SkillInfo
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace


def test_prompt_sections_in_cache_friendly_order(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "AGENTS.md").write_text("Use uv.")
    append_note(tmp_path, "conventions", "style", "black is banned")
    prompt = build_system_prompt(
        "BASE",
        root=tmp_path,
        skills=[SkillInfo("deploy", "ship it", tmp_path, "project")],
        extra="EXTRA",
        now=datetime.date(2026, 10, 8),
    )
    order = [prompt.index(s) for s in ("BASE", "# Environment", "Use uv.", "black is banned", "- deploy: ship it", "EXTRA")]
    assert order == sorted(order)
    assert str(tmp_path) in prompt and "2026-10-08" in prompt and "`skill` tool" in prompt


def test_prompt_without_optional_parts(tmp_path):
    prompt = build_system_prompt("BASE", root=tmp_path)
    assert "AGENTS.md" not in prompt and "# Skills" not in prompt and "# Memory" not in prompt


def test_mentions_attach_files_dirs_and_images(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "a.py").write_text("x = 1\n")
    (tmp_path / "d").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "d" / "f.txt").write_text("")
    png = base64.b64decode("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mNkYPhfDwAChwGA60e6kgAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==")
    (tmp_path / "shot.png").write_bytes(png)
    text, images = expand_mentions("look @a.py @d @shot.png @missing.txt @../etc/passwd", Workspace(tmp_path))
    assert "<attached: a.py>\nx = 1" in text and "<dir d>\nf.txt" in text
    assert "(@missing.txt: not found)" in text and "(@../etc/passwd: outside the project)" in text
    assert images == [Image("image/png", base64.b64encode(png).decode())] and "<image: shot.png>" in text


def test_pinned_files_render_fresh(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "a.py").write_text("v1")
    pinned = PinnedFiles()
    pinned.add("a.py", "gone.py")
    (tmp_path / "a.py").write_text("v2")
    out = render_pinned(pinned, tmp_path)
    assert "v2" in out and "gone.py" not in out
    pinned.drop("a.py")
    assert pinned.paths == ["gone.py"]
```

```python
"""Durable notes in ``.luna/memory/<kind>.md`` and the ``remember`` tool."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from luna_core.tools.base import ToolContext, ToolOutput, tool

KINDS = ("project", "conventions", "decisions", "failures")


def _dir(root: str | Path) -> Path:
    return Path(root) / ".luna" / "memory"


def memory_files(root: str | Path) -> list[str]:
    """Existing memory files as sorted root-relative POSIX paths."""
    d = _dir(root)
    if not d.is_dir():
        return []
    return sorted(p.relative_to(Path(root)).as_posix() for p in d.glob("*.md"))


def append_note(root: str | Path, kind: str, topic: str, note: str) -> Path:
    """Append a dated section to ``.luna/memory/<kind>.md``."""
    if kind not in KINDS:
        raise ValueError(f"kind must be one of {KINDS}, got {kind!r}")
    path = _dir(root) / f"{kind}.md"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        fh.write(f"\n## {date.today().isoformat()} — {topic}\n\n{note}\n")
    return path


@tool(mutating=True)
async def remember(ctx: ToolContext, kind: Literal["project", "conventions", "decisions", "failures"], topic: str, note: str) -> ToolOutput:
    """Save a durable note for future sessions (failures = dead ends not to retry).

    Args:
        kind: project, conventions, decisions or failures.
        topic: Short heading.
        note: The note itself.
    """
    path = append_note(ctx.workspace.root, kind, topic, note)
    rel = path.relative_to(ctx.workspace.root).as_posix()
    return ToolOutput.text(f"noted in {rel}; it is loaded into context from the next session (or /reload)", f"noted {kind}")
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/skills.py`:

```python
"""Agent Skills: SKILL.md folders with YAML frontmatter."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.tools.base import Tool, ToolContext, ToolOutput

_NAME = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}")
_LINE_BREAKS = "\n\r\x0b\x0c\x1c\x1d\x1e\x85  "


class SkillError(LunaCoreError):
    """A skill could not be read, saved or installed."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SkillInfo:
    """One installed skill."""

    name: str
    description: str
    path: Path
    scope: str


def parse_frontmatter(text: str) -> tuple[dict, str]:
    """Split ``---`` YAML frontmatter from the body (``({}, text)`` without one)."""
    if not text.startswith("---"):
        return {}, text
    parts = text.split("\n---", 1)
    if len(parts) != 2:
        return {}, text
    try:
        meta = yaml.safe_load(parts[0][3:]) or {}
    except yaml.YAMLError:
        return {}, text
    body = parts[1].split("\n", 1)[1] if "\n" in parts[1] else ""
    return (meta if isinstance(meta, dict) else {}), body


def discover(dirs: list[tuple[str, Path]]) -> list[SkillInfo]:
    """Skills in ``(scope, dir)`` order; a later directory overrides a name."""
    found: dict[str, SkillInfo] = {}
    for scope, root in dirs:
        root = Path(root)
        if not root.is_dir():
            continue
        for child in sorted(root.iterdir()):
            md = child / "SKILL.md"
            if not md.is_file():
                continue
            meta, _ = parse_frontmatter(md.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
            name, desc = meta.get("name"), meta.get("description")
            if isinstance(name, str) and isinstance(desc, str):
                found[child.name] = SkillInfo(child.name, desc.strip(), child, scope)
    return sorted(found.values(), key=lambda s: s.name)


def load_body(name: str, dirs: list[tuple[str, Path]]) -> str:
    """The instructions of skill ``name``."""
    for info in discover(dirs):
        if info.name == name:
            return parse_frontmatter((info.path / "SKILL.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))[1]
    raise SkillError(f"no skill named {name!r}")


def _validate(name: str, description: str) -> None:
    if not _NAME.fullmatch(name):
        raise SkillError(f"{name!r} is not a valid skill name (letters, digits, - and _, max 64)")
    if not description.strip():
        raise SkillError("description must not be empty")
    if any(ch in description for ch in _LINE_BREAKS):
        raise SkillError("description must be a single line")


def save(name: str, description: str, body: str, dest_dir: Path) -> Path:
    """Write ``dest_dir/<name>/SKILL.md`` and return its path."""
    _validate(name, description)
    target = Path(dest_dir) / name / "SKILL.md"
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    front = yaml.safe_dump({"name": name, "description": description}, allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False, width=10_000)
    target.write_text(f"---\n{front}---\n\n{body.rstrip()}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return target


def remove(name: str, dirs: list[tuple[str, Path]]) -> bool:
    """Delete skill ``name`` from the first directory that has it."""
    for _scope, root in dirs:
        target = Path(root) / name
        if (target / "SKILL.md").is_file():
            shutil.rmtree(target)
            return True
    return False


def _source(source: str, resolve: Callable[[str], dict] | None) -> tuple[str, str | None, str]:
    src = source.rstrip("/")
    local = Path(src).expanduser()
    if "://" in src or src.startswith(("/", "~", ".")):
        if local.exists():
            probe = local if local.is_dir() else local.parent
            root = probe
            while root != root.parent and not (root / ".git").exists():
                root = root.parent
            if (root / ".git").exists():
                sub = probe.relative_to(root).as_posix()
                return str(root), (None if sub == "." else sub), probe.name
        return src, None, Path(src).name
    parts = src.split("/")
    if len(parts) == 2:
        return f"https://github.com/{src}.git", None, parts[1]
    if len(parts) >= 3:
        return f"https://github.com/{parts[0]}/{parts[1]}.git", "/".join(parts[2:]), parts[-1]
    if resolve is None:
        raise SkillError(f"unknown skill source {source!r}")
    spec = resolve(src)
    return f"https://github.com/{spec['repo']}.git", spec.get("path"), Path(spec.get("path") or src).name


def install(source: str, dest_dir: Path, *, name: str | None = None, run: Callable = subprocess.run, resolve: Callable[[str], dict] | None = None) -> str:
    """Clone ``source`` and copy the skill into ``dest_dir``; returns a status line."""
    clone, subdir, default = _source(source, resolve)
    skill_name = name or default
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="luna-skill-"))
    try:
        try:
            run(["git", "clone", "--depth", "1", clone, str(tmp / "repo")], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
            raise SkillError("git is required to install skills") from exc
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
            raise SkillError(f"git clone failed: {(exc.stderr or str(exc)).strip()}") from exc
        src = tmp / "repo" / (subdir or "")
        md = src / "SKILL.md"
        if not md.is_file():
            raise SkillError(f"no SKILL.md at {source}")
        meta, _ = parse_frontmatter(md.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if not isinstance(meta.get("name"), str) or not isinstance(meta.get("description"), str):
            raise SkillError(f"{source}: SKILL.md needs YAML frontmatter with 'name' and 'description'")
        dest = Path(dest_dir) / skill_name
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.rmtree(dest, ignore_errors=True)
        shutil.copytree(src, dest, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns(".git"))
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
    return f"installed skill {skill_name!r}"


def skill_tool(dirs_fn: Callable[[], list[tuple[str, Path]]]) -> Tool:
    """The ``skill`` tool: load a skill's instructions by name."""

    async def run(ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> ToolOutput:
        name = str(args.get("name", ""))
        try:
            return ToolOutput.text(load_body(name, dirs_fn()), f"skill {name}")
        except SkillError:
            names = ", ".join(s.name for s in discover(dirs_fn())) or "(none installed)"
            return ToolOutput.error(f"no skill named {name!r}; available: {names}")

    schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {"name": {"type": "string", "description": "Skill name from the Skills list."}}, "required": ["name"], "additionalProperties": False}
    return Tool("skill", "Load the full instructions of a skill listed in the system prompt.", schema, run, raw=True)


def save_skill_tool(dest_fn: Callable[[bool], Path]) -> Tool:
    """The ``save_skill`` tool: keep a reusable multi-step pattern as a skill."""

    async def run(ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> ToolOutput:
        project = bool(args.get("project", True))
        try:
            path = save(str(args.get("name", "")), str(args.get("description", "")), str(args.get("body", "")), dest_fn(project))
        except SkillError as exc:
            return ToolOutput.error(str(exc))
        return ToolOutput.text(f"saved skill at {path.as_posix()}; it is listed from the next session (or /reload)", f"saved skill {args.get('name')}")

    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "name": {"type": "string", "description": "Letters, digits, - and _."},
            "description": {"type": "string", "description": "One line: when to use it."},
            "body": {"type": "string", "description": "The reusable steps."},
            "project": {"type": "boolean", "description": "Project scope (default) or user scope.", "default": True},
        },
        "required": ["name", "description", "body"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }
    return Tool("save_skill", "Save a reusable multi-step pattern you just completed as a skill.", schema, run, raw=True, mutating=True)
```

```python
"""System prompt assembly and ``@path`` mentions."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import datetime
import platform
import shlex
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

from luna_core.memory import memory_files
from luna_core.messages import Image
from luna_core.skills import SkillInfo
from luna_core.workspace import OutsideWorkspace, Workspace

MAX_ATTACH = 100_000
MAX_MEMORY = 20_000
_IMAGES = {".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg", ".jpeg": "image/jpeg", ".gif": "image/gif", ".webp": "image/webp"}

DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
You are a coding agent working in the user's repository through file and shell tools.
Read before you write, make the smallest change that achieves the goal, run the
project's tests when they exist, and report plainly what you did and what failed.
Paths are relative to the project root; you cannot touch files outside it."""


def _read(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
    try:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except OSError:
        return ""
    return text if len(text) <= limit else text[:limit] + "\n… (truncated)"


def build_system_prompt(
    base: str,
    *,
    root: str | Path,
    skills: list[SkillInfo] | tuple = (),
    memory: list[str] | None = None,
    extra: str = "",
    now: datetime.date | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Assemble the system prompt (stable parts first for prompt caching)."""
    root = Path(root)
    today = (now or datetime.date.today()).isoformat()
    parts = [base.rstrip(), f"# Environment\n\nWorking directory: {root}\nPlatform: {platform.system()} {platform.machine()}\nDate: {today}"]
    agents = root / "AGENTS.md"
    if agents.is_file():
        parts.append(f"# Project instructions (AGENTS.md)\n\n{_read(agents, MAX_MEMORY)}")
    files = memory_files(root) if memory is None else memory
    if files:
        notes = "\n\n".join(f"## {rel}\n{_read(root / rel, MAX_MEMORY)}" for rel in files)
        parts.append(f"# Memory\n\n{notes}")
    if skills:
        index = "\n".join(f"- {s.name}: {s.description}" for s in skills)
        parts.append(f"# Skills\n\nLoad one with the `skill` tool before following it.\n{index}")
    if extra:
        parts.append(extra.strip())
    return "\n\n".join(parts) + "\n"


def expand_mentions(text: str, workspace: Workspace) -> tuple[str, list[Image]]:
    """Attach ``@path`` files and directories; image files become image blocks."""
    try:
        tokens = shlex.split(text)
    except ValueError:
        tokens = text.split()
    extra = ""
    images: list[Image] = []
    for token in tokens:
        if not token.startswith("@") or len(token) < 2:
            continue
        rel = token[1:]
        try:
            path = workspace.resolve(rel)
        except OutsideWorkspace:
            extra += f"\n(@{rel}: outside the project)"
            continue
        if path.is_dir():
            names = sorted(p.name + ("/" if p.is_dir() else "") for p in path.iterdir())
            extra += f"\n\n<dir {rel}>\n" + "\n".join(names) + "\n</dir>"
        elif path.is_file() and path.suffix.lower() in _IMAGES:
            images.append(Image(_IMAGES[path.suffix.lower()], base64.b64encode(path.read_bytes()).decode()))
            extra += f"\n<image: {rel}>"
        elif path.is_file():
            extra += f"\n\n<attached: {rel}>\n{_read(path, MAX_ATTACH)}\n</attached>"
        else:
            extra += f"\n(@{rel}: not found)"
    return text + extra, images


@dataclass
class PinnedFiles:
    """Files re-attached, fresh from disk, to every turn."""

    _paths: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)

    def add(self, *paths: str) -> None:
        """Pin paths."""
        self._paths.update(paths)

    def drop(self, *paths: str) -> None:
        """Unpin paths."""
        self._paths.difference_update(paths)

    @property
    def paths(self) -> list[str]:
        """Pinned paths, sorted."""
        return sorted(self._paths)


def render_pinned(pinned: PinnedFiles, root: str | Path) -> str:
    """Current content of every pinned file that exists."""
    out = ""
    for rel in pinned.paths:
        path = Path(root) / rel
        if path.is_file():
            out += f"\n\n<attached: {rel}>\n{_read(path, MAX_ATTACH)}\n</attached>"
    return out
```

### Task 21: Compaction

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/compaction.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_compaction.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Agent.request`, `Agent.model_info`, `Agent.hooks` (Task 19), `Session.entries/messages/append` (Task 17), `Compacted`, `Notice`.
- Produces:
  - `estimate_tokens(request: ModelRequest) -> int` (characters of system + messages + tool schemas ÷ 4; images count 1 500 tokens)
  - `Compactor(threshold=0.8, keep_turns=4, provider=None, model=None, max_summary_tokens=4000)` — `provider`/`model` default to the agent's (the app passes the `summarize` role of `ModelRoles`, Task 22, wired in Task 28)
    - `async maybe_compact(agent, session)` → yields events only when `estimate_tokens(agent.request(session)) >= threshold × context_window`
    - `async compact(agent, session, *, instructions="")` → `/compact`; yields `Compacted(summary, removed)` or a `Notice`
  - `SUMMARY_PROMPT`

Cut rule: keep the last `keep_turns` user-input messages and everything after the first of them; summarise everything visible before it (plus the previous summary). The cut always lands right before a user message, so a tool call is never separated from its result. Nothing older than the kept part → `Notice("info", "nothing to compact yet")`. A `pre_compact` hook block cancels with a notice. A provider failure → `Notice("warn", "compaction failed: …")`, history unchanged.

```python
"""Summarise old history when the context window fills up."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from dataclasses import dataclass

from luna_core.errors import ProviderError
from luna_core.events import Compacted, Notice
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, Text, ToolCall, ToolResult
from luna_core.providers.base import ModelRequest, Provider, StreamAccumulator

SUMMARY_PROMPT = """\
You compress a coding session so the work can continue without the full transcript.
Write a dense summary in the language of the conversation with these sections:
Goal; Decisions and constraints; Files touched (paths and what changed); Commands run
and their results; Open problems and next steps. Keep exact names, paths, error
messages and numbers. Do not invent anything."""
_IMAGE_TOKENS = 1500


def estimate_tokens(request: ModelRequest) -> int:
    """Rough prompt size: characters ÷ 4, plus a flat cost per image."""
    chars = len(request.system)
    images = 0
    for message in request.messages:
        for block in message.content:
            if isinstance(block, Text):
                chars += len(block.text)
            elif isinstance(block, Image):
                images += 1
            elif isinstance(block, ToolCall):
                chars += len(json.dumps(block.args)) + len(block.name)
            elif isinstance(block, ToolResult):
                chars += len(block.text)
                images += sum(1 for p in block.content if isinstance(p, Image))
    chars += sum(len(json.dumps(t.parameters)) + len(t.description) for t in request.tools)
    return chars // 4 + images * _IMAGE_TOKENS


def _render(messages: list[Message], previous: str | None) -> str:
    lines = [f"<previous summary>\n{previous}\n</previous summary>"] if previous else []
    for m in messages:
        if m.role == "user":
            lines.append(f"USER: {m.text}")
        elif m.role == "assistant":
            if m.text:
                lines.append(f"ASSISTANT: {m.text}")
            lines += [f"TOOL CALL {c.name}: {json.dumps(c.args, ensure_ascii=False)[:500]}" for c in m.tool_calls]
        else:
            lines += [f"TOOL RESULT {r.name}{' (error)' if r.is_error else ''}: {r.text[:2000]}" for r in m.results]
    return "\n\n".join(lines)


@dataclass
class Compactor:
    """Compaction policy; plug into ``Agent(compactor=...)``."""

    threshold: float = 0.8
    keep_turns: int = 4
    provider: Provider | None = None
    model: str | None = None
    max_summary_tokens: int = 4000

    async def maybe_compact(self, agent, session) -> AsyncIterator[object]:
        """Compact when the next request would exceed the threshold."""
        window = agent.model_info.context_window
        if estimate_tokens(agent.request(session)) < self.threshold * window:
            return
        async for event in self.compact(agent, session):
            yield event

    async def compact(self, agent, session, *, instructions: str = "") -> AsyncIterator[object]:
        """Summarise everything before the last ``keep_turns`` user messages."""
        entries = session.entries()
        user_positions = [i for i, e in enumerate(entries) if e.message.role == "user"]
        if len(user_positions) <= self.keep_turns:
            yield Notice("info", "nothing to compact yet")
            return
        first_kept = user_positions[-self.keep_turns] if self.keep_turns > 0 else len(entries)
        old = entries[:first_kept]
        if not old:
            yield Notice("info", "nothing to compact yet")
            return
        outcome = await agent.hooks.run("pre_compact", {"session_id": session.id, "messages": len(old)})
        for note in outcome.notices:
            yield Notice("info", note)
        if outcome.block:
            yield Notice("info", f"compaction skipped: {outcome.message or 'blocked by a hook'}")
            return
        previous = next((r.payload["summary"] for r in reversed(session.records()) if r.type == "compacted"), None)
        transcript = _render([e.message for e in old], previous)
        if instructions:
            transcript += f"\n\nFocus of the summary: {instructions}"
        provider = self.provider or agent.provider
        request = ModelRequest(
            model=self.model or agent.model,
            system=SUMMARY_PROMPT,
            messages=[Message.user(transcript)],
            max_tokens=self.max_summary_tokens,
        )
        acc = StreamAccumulator(provider.name)
        try:
            async for event in provider.stream(request):
                acc.feed(event)
        except ProviderError as exc:
            yield Notice("warn", f"compaction failed: {exc}")
            return
        summary = acc.message().text.strip()
        if not summary:
            yield Notice("warn", "compaction failed: the model returned an empty summary")
            return
        session.append("compacted", {"summary": summary, "upto_seq": old[-1].seq, "removed": len(old)})
        yield Compacted(summary, len(old))
```

`test_loop_compacts_automatically_at_the_threshold`: six 400-char turns ≈ 1 250 tokens > 0.8 × 1 000, so the first provider call is the summary ("SUMMARY") and the second answers the turn; the second request's first message carries the summary.

### Task 22: Model roles, image input, steering and `/btw`

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/roles.py`
- Modify: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/loop.py` (`Agent.__init__`, `Agent.request`, `Agent._turn`, new `Agent.side_question`)
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_roles.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `ModelRoles(fast=None, plan=None, vision=None, summarize=None)`; each value is a model id on the agent's provider (`str`) or a `(Provider, model)` pair for another provider.
    - `.get(role, provider, model) -> tuple[Provider, str]` (falls back to the given pair)
    - `.for_turn(provider, model, *, images: bool, plan: bool, overrides=None) -> tuple[Provider, str, str | None]` — `vision` when the turn has images and the catalogue says the default model has no vision; `plan` while plan mode is on; else the default; third item is the role used.
  - `Agent(..., roles: ModelRoles | None = None)`; `Agent.request(session, messages=None, *, model=None)`
  - `SideAnswer(text, usage: Usage, cost: float | None)`; `async Agent.side_question(session, text, *, images=()) -> SideAnswer` — one tool-less request over the current context plus the question; nothing is logged (backs `/btw`).
- Steering (`/steer` and typing while a turn runs) uses `Session.queue` from Task 17: queued text is delivered as a user message at the next step boundary — after the current tool batch, never in the middle of one.

```python
import pytest

from luna_core.events import NeedsApproval, Notice, TurnDone
from luna_core.loop import Agent
from luna_core.messages import Image, Message, ToolCall
from luna_core.policy import Decision, Policy
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider
from luna_core.roles import ModelRoles
from luna_core.session import EventLog, Session
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace

OVERRIDES = {"text-only": {"window": 100000, "vision": False}, "seeing": {"window": 100000, "vision": True}}


@pytest.fixture
def session(tmp_path):
    log = EventLog(tmp_path / "s.db")
    yield Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    log.close()


def test_role_resolution():
    main, other = FakeProvider([]), FakeProvider([])
    roles = ModelRoles(fast="small", vision=(other, "seeing"), plan="thinker")
    assert roles.get("fast", main, "big") == (main, "small")
    assert roles.get("summarize", main, "big") == (main, "big")
    assert roles.for_turn(main, "text-only", images=True, plan=False, overrides=OVERRIDES) == (other, "seeing", "vision")
    assert roles.for_turn(main, "seeing", images=True, plan=False, overrides=OVERRIDES) == (main, "seeing", None)
    assert roles.for_turn(main, "big", images=False, plan=True) == (main, "thinker", "plan")


async def test_image_turn_switches_to_the_vision_model(tmp_path, session):
    vision = FakeProvider([FakeProvider.text("a cat")])
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider([]), "text-only", tools=[], workspace=Workspace(tmp_path), roles=ModelRoles(vision=(vision, "seeing")), model_overrides=OVERRIDES)
    events = [e async for e in agent.run(session, "what is it?", images=[Image("image/png", "AAA")])]
    assert any(isinstance(e, Notice) and "seeing" in e.text for e in events) and isinstance(events[-1], TurnDone)
    sent = vision.requests[0]
    assert sent.model == "seeing" and isinstance(sent.messages[0].content[1], Image)


async def test_plan_mode_uses_the_plan_model(tmp_path, session):
    main = FakeProvider([FakeProvider.text("plan: step 1")])
    agent = Agent(main, "big", tools=[], workspace=Workspace(tmp_path), roles=ModelRoles(plan="thinker"), policy=Policy(workspace=Workspace(tmp_path), plan_mode=True))
    [e async for e in agent.run(session, "plan it")]
    assert main.requests[0].model == "thinker"


async def test_images_without_vision_warn(tmp_path, session):
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider([FakeProvider.text("?")]), "text-only", tools=[], workspace=Workspace(tmp_path), model_overrides=OVERRIDES)
    events = [e async for e in agent.run(session, "look", images=[Image("image/png", "AAA")])]
    assert any(isinstance(e, Notice) and e.level == "warn" and "images" in e.text for e in events)


async def test_side_question_is_not_logged(tmp_path, session):
    main = FakeProvider([FakeProvider.text("main answer"), FakeProvider.text("side answer")])
    agent = Agent(main, "big", workspace=Workspace(tmp_path))
    [e async for e in agent.run(session, "main question")]
    before = len(session.records())
    answer = await agent.side_question(session, "btw, what is 2+2?")
    assert answer.text == "side answer" and len(session.records()) == before
    sent = main.requests[1]
    assert sent.tools == [] and sent.messages[-1].text == "btw, what is 2+2?" and sent.messages[-2].text == "main answer"


async def test_steer_while_waiting_for_approval_arrives_after_the_tool_batch(tmp_path, session):
    main = FakeProvider([FakeProvider.call("execute", {"command": "echo hi"}, "c1"), FakeProvider.text("ok")])
    agent = Agent(main, "big", workspace=Workspace(tmp_path))
    async for event in agent.run(session, "go"):
        if isinstance(event, NeedsApproval):
            session.queue("use -n instead")
            session.decide("c1", Decision.approve())
    second = main.requests[1].messages
    assert second[-2].role == "tool" and second[-1] == Message.user("use -n instead")
    assert isinstance(second[-3].content[0], ToolCall)
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/roles.py`:

```python
"""Model roles: which model handles which kind of work."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Union

from luna_core import catalog
from luna_core.providers.base import Provider

RoleTarget = Union[str, tuple[Provider, str]]
ROLES = ("fast", "plan", "vision", "summarize")


@dataclass
class ModelRoles:
    """Optional per-role models; unset roles fall back to the default model."""

    fast: RoleTarget | None = None
    plan: RoleTarget | None = None
    vision: RoleTarget | None = None
    summarize: RoleTarget | None = None

    def get(self, role: str, provider: Provider, model: str) -> tuple[Provider, str]:
        """The provider and model for ``role``."""
        if role not in ROLES:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown role {role!r}; expected one of {ROLES}")
        target = getattr(self, role)
        if target is None:
            return provider, model
        if isinstance(target, tuple):
            return target
        return provider, target

    def for_turn(
        self, provider: Provider, model: str, *, images: bool, plan: bool, overrides: dict | None = None
    ) -> tuple[Provider, str, str | None]:
        """Pick the model for a turn: vision for images the default cannot see, plan in plan mode."""
        if images and self.vision is not None and not catalog.lookup(model, overrides).vision:
            return (*self.get("vision", provider, model), "vision")
        if plan and self.plan is not None:
            return (*self.get("plan", provider, model), "plan")
        return provider, model, None
```

   and inside the step loop use them: `acc = StreamAccumulator(provider.name)`, `provider.stream(self.request(session, model=model))`, `readable_error(exc, provider.name)`, and replace both `cost(total, self.model_info)` calls with `cost(total, info)`.

```python
    async def side_question(self, session: Session, text: str, *, images: tuple[Image, ...] | list[Image] = ()) -> SideAnswer:
        """Answer a question over the current context without tools and without logging it."""
        messages = [*session.messages(), Message("user", [Text(text), *images])]
        request = dataclasses.replace(self.request(session, messages), tools=[])
        acc = StreamAccumulator(self.provider.name)
        async for event in self.provider.stream(request):
            acc.feed(event)
        return SideAnswer(acc.message().text, acc.usage, cost(acc.usage, self.model_info))
```

### Task 23: Subagents — parallel, worktree isolation, approvals through the parent

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/subagents.py`, `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/tools/tasks.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_subagents.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Agent`, `PathLocks` (Task 19), `Policy.child(workspace)` (Task 16), `Session.adopt/release/notify`, `EventLog.children` (Task 17), `build_system_prompt` (Task 20), `ModelRoles` (Task 22), `_snapshot_tree` (Task 16, `luna_core.undo`).
- Produces:
  - `READ_ONLY`, `WRITE_TOOLS`, `VALID_TOOLS`, `SubagentConfigError(LunaCoreError)`
  - `SubagentSpec(name, description, prompt, tools=READ_ONLY, model=None, unsafe=False, isolation="auto", max_steps=30, max_cost=None)` with `.writes`
  - `BUILTIN_SUBAGENTS: dict[str, SubagentSpec]` — `researcher`, `reviewer` (read-only), `general-purpose` (all built-in file/shell tools + `write_todos`)
  - `load_subagents(files: list[Path], *, on_warn=None) -> dict[str, SubagentSpec]` (TOML `[subagent.<name>]`: `description`, `prompt`, `tools`, `model`, `unsafe`, `isolation`, `max_steps`, `max_cost`; mutating tools require `unsafe = true`)
  - `create_worktree(root, sub_id) -> tuple[Path, str, str]` (path, branch `luna/sub/<id>`, base commit of the current working tree); `finish_worktree(root, path, branch, base, message) -> tuple[str, str]` (diffstat, diff)
  - `SubagentResult(name, ok, text, error="", branch=None, base=None, diffstat="", session_id="")` with `.render() -> str`
  - `SubagentRunner(specs, *, max_parallel=4, model_for=None)`: `.describe() -> str`, `async .run(parent, session, name, task, *, isolation="auto", emit=None, cancel=None, turn_id=None) -> SubagentResult`
  - `task_tool(runner) -> Tool` — `task(description, subagent_type="general-purpose", background=False, isolation="auto")`; background runs go through `ctx.services["background"]` (Task 24)
- Rules: isolation `auto` = worktree when the subagent can write and the project is a git repo, else shared workspace; `worktree` without git → error result. In a shared workspace the child uses the parent's `PathLocks`, undo journal and turn (edits stay undoable, same-path writes are serialised); in a worktree the child's changes are committed on `luna/sub/<id>` and the parent gets the diffstat plus the command to apply them. Child approvals appear in the parent's stream as `NeedsApproval` and are answered with the parent session's `decide`. Child sessions are logged with `meta.kind = "subagent"` and count toward the parent's cost.

```python
import asyncio
import subprocess
import time

import pytest

from luna_core.events import NeedsApproval, Notice, TurnDone
from luna_core.loop import Agent
from luna_core.messages import Message, ToolCall
from luna_core.policy import Decision, Policy
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider
from luna_core.session import EventLog, Session
from luna_core.subagents import BUILTIN_SUBAGENTS, SubagentConfigError, SubagentRunner, SubagentSpec, load_subagents, task_tool
from luna_core.tools import builtin_tools
from luna_core.tools.base import tool
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace


class Routing:
    """Child provider that picks a script by a marker in the delegated task text."""

    name = "fake"

    def __init__(self, routes):
        self.routes = {k: FakeProvider(v) for k, v in routes.items()}

    async def stream(self, request):
        task = request.messages[0].text
        key = next(k for k in self.routes if k in task)
        async for ev in self.routes[key].stream(request):
            yield ev


def git(root, *args):
    subprocess.run(["git", "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t", *args], cwd=root, check=True, capture_output=True)


@pytest.fixture
def log(tmp_path):
    lg = EventLog(tmp_path / "db" / "s.db")
    yield lg
    lg.close()


def parent_agent(root, script, child_provider, *, tools=None, specs=None, yolo=True, max_parallel=4):
    runner = SubagentRunner(specs or dict(BUILTIN_SUBAGENTS), max_parallel=max_parallel, model_for=lambda spec: (child_provider, "child-model"))
    ws = Workspace(root)
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider(script), "parent-model", workspace=ws, tools=[*(tools or builtin_tools()), task_tool(runner)], policy=Policy(workspace=ws, yolo=yolo))
    return agent


def test_load_subagents_validation(tmp_path):
    good = tmp_path / "a.toml"
    good.write_text('[subagent.researcher]\ndescription = "mine"\nprompt = "p"\n[subagent.fixer]\ntools = ["read_file", "edit_file"]\nunsafe = true\nisolation = "none"\n')
    specs = load_subagents([good, tmp_path / "missing.toml"])
    assert specs["researcher"].description == "mine" and specs["fixer"].writes and specs["fixer"].isolation == "none"
    assert "general-purpose" in specs
    bad = tmp_path / "b.toml"
    bad.write_text('[subagent.x]\ntools = ["edit_file"]\n')
    with pytest.raises(SubagentConfigError, match="unsafe = true"):
        load_subagents([bad])
    bad.write_text('[subagent.x]\ntools = ["teleport"]\n')
    with pytest.raises(SubagentConfigError, match="unknown tools"):
        load_subagents([bad])


async def test_delegation_returns_the_child_answer(tmp_path, log):
    child = Routing({"bugs": [FakeProvider.text("found 3 bugs")]})
    agent = parent_agent(tmp_path, [FakeProvider.call("task", {"description": "look for bugs", "subagent_type": "researcher"}, "t1"), FakeProvider.text("ok")], child)
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    events = [e async for e in agent.run(session, "go")]
    assert isinstance(events[-1], TurnDone)
    assert "found 3 bugs" in agent.provider.requests[1].messages[-1].results[0].text
    kids = log.children(session.id)
    assert len(kids) == 1 and kids[0].meta["kind"] == "subagent" and kids[0].meta["agent"] == "researcher"


async def test_parallel_children_run_concurrently(tmp_path, log):
    both = asyncio.Event()
    count = {"n": 0}

    @tool()
    async def rendezvous() -> str:
        """Wait for the sibling."""
        count["n"] += 1
        if count["n"] == 2:
            both.set()
        await asyncio.wait_for(both.wait(), 3)
        return "met"

    specs = {"r": SubagentSpec("r", "rendezvous", "p", tools=("rendezvous",))}
    child = Routing({"A": [FakeProvider.call("rendezvous", {}), FakeProvider.text("A done")], "B": [FakeProvider.call("rendezvous", {}), FakeProvider.text("B done")]})
    two = Message.assistant([ToolCall("t1", "task", {"description": "A", "subagent_type": "r"}), ToolCall("t2", "task", {"description": "B", "subagent_type": "r"})])
    agent = parent_agent(tmp_path, [two, FakeProvider.text("ok")], child, tools=[rendezvous], specs=specs)

async def test_worktrees_keep_parallel_writers_apart(tmp_path, log):
    git(tmp_path, "init", "-q")

    (tmp_path / "wip.txt").write_text("uncommitted parent edit\n")
    git(tmp_path, "add", "README.md")
    git(tmp_path, "commit", "-qm", "init")

        {
            "first": [FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "notes.md", "content": "one\n"}), FakeProvider.text("wrote one")],
            "second": [FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "notes.md", "content": "two\n"}), FakeProvider.text("wrote two")],
        }
    )
```
