```python
    [e async for e in agent.run(session, "go")]
    r1, r2 = (r.text for r in session.messages()[2].results)
    assert "luna/sub/" in r1 and "notes.md" in r1 and "git apply" in r1 and "luna/sub/" in r2
    assert not (tmp_path / "notes.md").exists()
    branches = subprocess.run(["git", "branch", "--list", "luna/sub/*"], cwd=tmp_path, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()
    contents = sorted(subprocess.run(["git", "show", f"{b}:notes.md"], cwd=tmp_path, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout for b in branches)
    assert contents == ["one\n", "two\n"]

    assert seen_wip == "uncommitted parent edit\n"  # the child started from the parent's working tree
    assert not any((tmp_path / ".luna" / "worktrees").iterdir())


async def test_shared_workspace_serialises_writes_to_one_path(tmp_path, log):
    active = {"now": 0, "max": 0}

    @tool(mutating=True)
    async def slow_write(file_path: str) -> str:
        """Pretend to write slowly."""
        active["now"] += 1
        active["max"] = max(active["max"], active["now"])
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        active["now"] -= 1
        return "ok"

    specs = {"w": SubagentSpec("w", "writer", "p", tools=("slow_write",), unsafe=True, isolation="none")}
    child = Routing({"A": [FakeProvider.call("slow_write", {"file_path": "x"}), FakeProvider.text("A")], "B": [FakeProvider.call("slow_write", {"file_path": "x"}), FakeProvider.text("B")]})
    two = Message.assistant([ToolCall("t1", "task", {"description": "A", "subagent_type": "w"}), ToolCall("t2", "task", {"description": "B", "subagent_type": "w"})])
    agent = parent_agent(tmp_path, [two, FakeProvider.text("ok")], child, tools=[slow_write], specs=specs)
    [e async for e in agent.run(Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False), "go")]
    assert active["max"] == 1


async def test_child_approval_is_answered_through_the_parent(tmp_path, log):
    child = Routing({"run": [FakeProvider.call("execute", {"command": "echo child"}, "k1"), FakeProvider.text("ran it")]})
    specs = {"x": SubagentSpec("x", "runner", "p", tools=("execute",), unsafe=True, isolation="none")}
    agent = parent_agent(tmp_path, [FakeProvider.call("task", {"description": "run", "subagent_type": "x"}, "t1"), FakeProvider.text("ok")], child, specs=specs, yolo=False)
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    asked = []
    async for ev in agent.run(session, "go"):
        if isinstance(ev, NeedsApproval):
            asked.append(ev.request.call_id)
            session.decide(ev.request.call_id, Decision.approve())
    assert asked == ["k1"]
    assert "ran it" in session.messages()[2].results[0].text


async def test_parent_cancel_stops_the_child(tmp_path, log):
    child = Routing({"sleep": [FakeProvider.call("execute", {"command": "sleep 30"}, "k1")]})
    specs = {"x": SubagentSpec("x", "runner", "p", tools=("execute",), unsafe=True, isolation="none")}
    agent = parent_agent(tmp_path, [FakeProvider.call("task", {"description": "sleep", "subagent_type": "x"}, "t1")], child, specs=specs)
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    async for ev in agent.run(session, "go"):
        if isinstance(ev, Notice) and "execute" in ev.text:
            session.cancel_turn()
    assert time.monotonic() - t0 < 6


async def test_budget_is_checked_before_spawning(tmp_path, log):
    # Siblings started in one message can push the session over budget; the runner re-checks.
    runner = SubagentRunner(dict(BUILTIN_SUBAGENTS), model_for=lambda spec: (Routing({}), "c"))
    ws = Workspace(tmp_path)
    parent = Agent(FakeProvider([]), "m", workspace=ws, max_cost=0.0)
    result = await runner.run(parent, Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False), "researcher", "x")
    assert not result.ok and "budget reached" in result.error


async def test_worktree_isolation_without_git_is_an_error(tmp_path, log):
    agent = parent_agent(tmp_path, [FakeProvider.call("task", {"description": "x", "isolation": "worktree"}, "t1"), FakeProvider.text("ok")], Routing({}))
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    [e async for e in agent.run(session, "go")]
    result = session.messages()[2].results[0]
    assert result.is_error and "git repository" in result.text
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/subagents.py`:

```python
"""Subagents: delegated child runs, concurrently, optionally in git worktrees."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import os
import subprocess
import threading
import tomllib
import uuid
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

from luna_core.context import build_system_prompt
from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.events import Cancelled, NeedsApproval, Notice, ToolStarted, TurnError
from luna_core.loop import Agent
from luna_core.session import Session
from luna_core.undo import _snapshot_tree
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace

READ_ONLY = ("ls", "read_file", "glob", "grep")
WRITE_TOOLS = ("write_file", "edit_file", "delete", "execute")
VALID_TOOLS = frozenset((*READ_ONLY, *WRITE_TOOLS, "write_todos"))
ISOLATION = ("auto", "none", "worktree")
_IDENTITY = {"GIT_AUTHOR_NAME": "Luna", "GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL": "luna@localhost", "GIT_COMMITTER_NAME": "Luna", "GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL": "luna@localhost"}
_DIFF_LIMIT = 20_000
# git refuses concurrent worktree bookkeeping; parallel subagents take turns here.
_GIT_LOCK = threading.Lock()


class SubagentConfigError(LunaCoreError):
    """A subagent definition is invalid."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SubagentSpec:
    """A subagent definition."""

    name: str
    description: str
    prompt: str
    tools: tuple[str, ...] = READ_ONLY
    model: str | None = None
    unsafe: bool = False
    isolation: str = "auto"
    max_steps: int = 30
    max_cost: float | None = None

    @property
    def writes(self) -> bool:
        """True when it may change files or run commands."""
        return bool(set(self.tools) & set(WRITE_TOOLS)) or self.unsafe


BUILTIN_SUBAGENTS: dict[str, SubagentSpec] = {
    "researcher": SubagentSpec(
        "researcher",
        "Investigate the codebase, docs or a question and report back. Read-only.",
        "You explore and report. You never modify files or run mutating commands. End with a concise report: findings, evidence (paths and lines), open questions.",
    ),
    "reviewer": SubagentSpec(
        "reviewer",
        "Review a diff or files for bugs, risks and simplifications. Read-only.",
        "You review code critically and return concrete, prioritised findings with paths and lines.",
    ),
    "general-purpose": SubagentSpec(
        "general-purpose",
        "Do a self-contained multi-step task end to end; may edit files (in its own git worktree when possible).",
        "You complete the delegated task end to end, verify it (tests when they exist), and finish with a short report of what changed and what is left.",
        tools=(*READ_ONLY, *WRITE_TOOLS, "write_todos"),
        unsafe=True,
    ),
}


def load_subagents(files: list[Path], *, on_warn: Callable[[str], None] | None = None) -> dict[str, SubagentSpec]:
    """Built-in subagents overridden/extended by ``[subagent.<name>]`` tables in ``files``."""
    specs = dict(BUILTIN_SUBAGENTS)
    for path in files:
        try:
            with Path(path).open("rb") as fh:
                data = tomllib.load(fh)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            continue
        except (OSError, tomllib.TOMLDecodeError) as exc:
            raise SubagentConfigError(f"cannot read {path}: {exc}") from exc
        for name, cfg in (data.get("subagent") or {}).items():
            tools = tuple(cfg.get("tools", READ_ONLY))
            unknown = set(tools) - VALID_TOOLS
            if unknown:
                raise SubagentConfigError(f"subagent {name!r}: unknown tools {sorted(unknown)}; valid: {', '.join(sorted(VALID_TOOLS))}")
            mutating = set(tools) & set(WRITE_TOOLS)
            if mutating and cfg.get("unsafe") is not True:
                raise SubagentConfigError(
                    f"subagent {name!r} requests {sorted(mutating)}; add 'unsafe = true' to [subagent.{name}] to allow it to change the repository"
                )
            if mutating and on_warn:
                on_warn(f"subagent {name!r} may change files or run commands (rules, approvals and undo still apply)")
            isolation = cfg.get("isolation", "auto")
            if isolation not in ISOLATION:
                raise SubagentConfigError(f"subagent {name!r}: isolation must be one of {ISOLATION}")
            specs[name] = SubagentSpec(
                name,
                str(cfg.get("description", name)),
                str(cfg.get("prompt", f"You are the {name} subagent.")),
                tools,
                cfg.get("model"),
                bool(cfg.get("unsafe", False)),
                isolation,
                int(cfg.get("max_steps", 30)),
                float(cfg["max_cost"]) if "max_cost" in cfg else None,
            )
    return specs


def _git(root: Path, *args: str, env: dict | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, env={**os.environ, **(env or {})})


def _is_git(root: Path) -> bool:
    proc = _git(root, "rev-parse", "--is-inside-work-tree")
    return proc.returncode == 0 and proc.stdout.strip() == "true"


def create_worktree(root: Path, sub_id: str) -> tuple[Path, str, str]:
    """A worktree on a new branch, based on the current working tree (uncommitted edits included)."""
    with _GIT_LOCK:
        return _create_worktree(root, sub_id)


def _create_worktree(root: Path, sub_id: str) -> tuple[Path, str, str]:
    tree = _snapshot_tree(root)
    if tree is None:
        raise LunaCoreError("could not snapshot the working tree for the subagent")
    head = _git(root, "rev-parse", "--verify", "-q", "HEAD").stdout.strip()
    commit = _git(root, "commit-tree", tree, "-m", "luna: subagent base", *(["-p", head] if head else []), env=_IDENTITY)
    if commit.returncode != 0:
        raise LunaCoreError(f"git commit-tree failed: {commit.stderr.strip()}")
    base = commit.stdout.strip()
    branch = f"luna/sub/{sub_id}"
    path = root / ".luna" / "worktrees" / sub_id
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    added = _git(root, "worktree", "add", "-q", "-b", branch, str(path), base)
    if added.returncode != 0:
        raise LunaCoreError(f"git worktree add failed: {added.stderr.strip()}")
    return path, branch, base


def finish_worktree(root: Path, path: Path, branch: str, base: str, message: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Commit the child's changes on its branch, remove the worktree, return (diffstat, diff)."""
    with _GIT_LOCK:
        return _finish_worktree(root, path, branch, base, message)


def _finish_worktree(root: Path, path: Path, branch: str, base: str, message: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    _git(path, "add", "-A")
    if _git(path, "diff", "--cached", "--quiet").returncode != 0:
        _git(path, "commit", "-qm", message, env=_IDENTITY)
    stat = _git(root, "diff", "--stat", base, branch).stdout.strip()
    diff = _git(root, "diff", base, branch).stdout
    _git(root, "worktree", "remove", "--force", str(path))
    return stat, diff[:_DIFF_LIMIT]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SubagentResult:
    """Outcome of one delegated run."""

    name: str
    ok: bool
    text: str
    error: str = ""
    branch: str | None = None
    base: str | None = None
    diffstat: str = ""
    session_id: str = ""

    def render(self) -> str:
        """Text for the parent model."""
        head = self.text.strip() or "(the subagent gave no answer)"
        if not self.ok:
            head = f"subagent {self.name} failed: {self.error}\n\n{head}"
        if self.branch:
            head += (
                f"\n\n[changes are committed on branch {self.branch}; diff stat:\n{self.diffstat or 'no changes'}\n"
                f"apply them to the main working tree with: git diff {self.base} {self.branch} | git apply]"
            )
        return head


@dataclass
class SubagentRunner:
    """Runs subagents for the ``task`` tool."""

    specs: dict[str, SubagentSpec]
    max_parallel: int = 4
    model_for: Callable[[SubagentSpec], tuple[object, str]] | None = None
    _semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore | None = field(default=None, init=False, repr=False)

    def describe(self) -> str:
        """``- name: description`` lines for the tool description."""
        return "\n".join(f"- {s.name}: {s.description}" for s in self.specs.values())

    def _model(self, parent, spec: SubagentSpec) -> tuple[object, str]:
        if self.model_for is not None:
            return self.model_for(spec)
        if spec.model:
            return parent.provider, spec.model
        if getattr(parent, "roles", None) is not None:
            return parent.roles.get("fast", parent.provider, parent.model)
        return parent.provider, parent.model

    async def run(
        self,
        parent,
        session: Session,
        name: str,
        task: str,
        *,
        isolation: str = "auto",
        emit: Callable[[object], None] | None = None,
        cancel: asyncio.Event | None = None,
        turn_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> SubagentResult:
        """Run subagent ``name`` on ``task`` and return its result."""
        spec = self.specs.get(name)
        if spec is None:
            return SubagentResult(name, False, "", f"unknown subagent; available: {', '.join(self.specs)}")
        if isolation not in ISOLATION:
            return SubagentResult(name, False, "", f"isolation must be one of {ISOLATION}")
        emit = emit or (lambda event: None)
        if parent.max_cost is not None and session.cost() >= parent.max_cost:
            return SubagentResult(name, False, "", f"budget reached (max_cost=${parent.max_cost:g}); no new subagent was started")
        root = parent.workspace.root
        git = await asyncio.to_thread(_is_git, root)
        if isolation == "worktree" and not git:
            return SubagentResult(name, False, "", "worktree isolation needs a git repository")
        mode = isolation if isolation != "auto" else spec.isolation
        use_worktree = mode == "worktree" or (mode == "auto" and spec.writes and git)
        if self._semaphore is None:
            self._semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(self.max_parallel)
        async with self._semaphore:
            sub_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
            branch = base = None
            if use_worktree:
                path, branch, base = await asyncio.to_thread(create_worktree, root, sub_id)
                workspace = Workspace(path)
            else:
                workspace = parent.workspace
            services = dict(parent.services)
            if not use_worktree:
                services.update(undo_journal=session.undo_journal, undo_turn_id=turn_id)
            provider, model = self._model(parent, spec)
            child = Agent(
                provider,
                model,
                tools=[parent.tools[t] for t in spec.tools if t in parent.tools],
                system=build_system_prompt(spec.prompt, root=workspace.root, memory=[]),
                policy=parent.policy.child(workspace),
                workspace=workspace,
                sandbox=parent.sandbox,
                hooks=parent.hooks,
                max_steps=spec.max_steps,
                max_cost=spec.max_cost,
                locks=parent.locks,
                services=services,
                model_overrides=parent.model_overrides,
                name=spec.name,
            )
            child_id = session.log.create(str(workspace.root), title=f"{name}: {task[:60]}", parent_id=session.id, meta={"kind": "subagent", "agent": name})
            child_session = Session(session.log, child_id, workdir=str(workspace.root), undo=False)
            session.adopt(child_session)
            watcher = asyncio.create_task(self._watch(cancel, child_session)) if cancel else None
            ok, error = True, ""
            try:
                async for event in child.run(child_session, task):
                    if isinstance(event, NeedsApproval):
                        emit(event)
                    elif isinstance(event, ToolStarted):
                        emit(Notice("dim", f"{name} › {event.name} {event.preview}".rstrip()))
                    elif isinstance(event, TurnError):
                        ok, error = False, event.message
                    elif isinstance(event, Cancelled):
                        ok, error = False, "cancelled"
            finally:
                session.release(child_session)
                if watcher:
                    watcher.cancel()
            answers = [m for m in child_session.messages() if m.role == "assistant" and m.text]
            text = answers[-1].text if answers else ""
            diffstat = ""
            if use_worktree:
                diffstat, _diff = await asyncio.to_thread(finish_worktree, root, workspace.root, branch, base, f"luna subagent {name}: {task[:60]}")
            return SubagentResult(name, ok, text, error, branch, base, diffstat, child_id)

    @staticmethod
    async def _watch(cancel: asyncio.Event, child: Session) -> None:
        # Retry until the child's turn exists; the watcher is cancelled when the child ends.
        await cancel.wait()
        while not child.cancel_turn():
            await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
```

```python
"""``task``, ``task_status`` and ``task_cancel`` tools."""

from __future__ import annotations

from luna_core.messages import Text
from luna_core.subagents import ISOLATION, SubagentRunner
from luna_core.tools.base import Tool, ToolContext, ToolOutput


def task_tool(runner: SubagentRunner) -> Tool:
    """The ``task`` tool bound to ``runner``."""

    async def run(ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> ToolOutput:
        parent = ctx.services["agent"]
        session = ctx.services["session"]
        name = str(args.get("subagent_type") or "general-purpose")
        description = str(args.get("description", "")).strip()
        if not description:
            return ToolOutput.error("description is required")
        isolation = str(args.get("isolation") or "auto")
        if args.get("background"):
            background = ctx.services.get("background")
            if background is None:
                return ToolOutput.error("background tasks are not enabled in this host")
            handle = background.start(
                f"{name}: {description[:60]}",
                lambda: runner.run(parent, session, name, description, isolation=isolation, emit=session.notify),
                session=session,
            )
            return ToolOutput.text(f"started background task {handle}; its result arrives in a later message. Check it with task_status.", f"background {handle}")
        result = await runner.run(parent, session, name, description, isolation=isolation, emit=ctx.emit, cancel=ctx.cancel, turn_id=ctx.services.get("turn_id"))
        return ToolOutput((Text(result.render()),), not result.ok, f"{name}: {'done' if result.ok else 'failed'}")

    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "description": {"type": "string", "description": "The complete task for the subagent, with all context it needs."},
            "subagent_type": {"type": "string", "enum": list(runner.specs), "default": "general-purpose"},
            "background": {"type": "boolean", "default": False, "description": "Return at once; the result arrives later."},
            "isolation": {"type": "string", "enum": list(ISOLATION), "default": "auto", "description": "worktree: work on a separate git branch."},
        },
        "required": ["description"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }
    description = (
        "Delegate a self-contained task to a subagent. Several task calls in one message run in parallel.\n"
        f"Subagents:\n{runner.describe()}"
    )
    return Tool("task", description, schema, run, raw=True)
```

### Task 24: Background tasks and background sessions

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/background.py`
- Modify: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/tools/tasks.py` (add `task_status`, `task_cancel`)
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_background.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SubagentResult.render()` (Task 23), `Session.queue/notify/append` (Task 17), `to_wire`, `BackgroundDone`.
- Produces:
  - `TaskInfo(handle, label, session_id, status, started, finished=None, ok=None, summary="")` — status `queued | running | done | failed | cancelled`
  - `BackgroundTasks(max_parallel=4)`: `.start(label, factory, *, session) -> str`, `.get(handle)`, `.list(session_id=None) -> list[TaskInfo]`, `.cancel(handle) -> bool`, `async .wait(handle) -> TaskInfo`, `async .shutdown()`
  - On completion: log `background_done{handle, ok, summary}`, queue `"[background task <handle> · <label> · <status>]\n<summary>"` into the parent session (delivered at its next step), and `session.notify(BackgroundDone(...))`.
  - `task_status_tool()`, `task_cancel_tool()` — tools reading `ctx.services["background"]`; `task_status(handle="")` lists this session's tasks when `handle` is empty.
  - `RunInfo(session_id, label, status, started, finished=None, error="", cost=0.0, text="")` — status `queued | running | waiting | done | failed | cancelled`
  - `AgentManager(max_parallel=4, on_done=None, buffer=5000)`: `.start(agent, session, text, *, images=(), label="") -> RunInfo` (raises `LunaCoreError` if that session already runs), `.get(session_id)`, `.list()`, `.events(session_id, after=0) -> list[dict]` (wire dicts, each with an `index`), `.subscribe(session_id, fn) -> unsubscribe`, `.has_listeners(session_id) -> bool`, `.cancel(session_id) -> bool`, `async .wait(session_id) -> RunInfo`, `async .shutdown()`
- The manager owns the run: subscribers come and go (a TUI closing just unsubscribes); the run, its log and its completion callback do not depend on them.

```python
import asyncio

from luna_core.loop import Agent

from luna_core.workspace import Workspace


@pytest.fixture
def log(tmp_path):
    lg = EventLog(tmp_path / "db" / "s.db")
    yield lg
    lg.close()


async def test_background_task_result_is_queued_logged_and_notified(tmp_path, log):
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    seen = []
    session.subscribe(seen.append)
    tasks = BackgroundTasks()

    async def work():
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        return "all good"

    handle = tasks.start("check", work, session=session)
    assert tasks.get(handle).status in ("queued", "running")
    info = await tasks.wait(handle)
    assert info.status == "done" and info.ok and info.summary == "all good"
    assert session.drain_queue() == [f"[background task {handle} · check · done]\nall good"]
    assert any(r.type == "background_done" for r in session.records())
    assert seen == [BackgroundDone(handle, True, "all good")]


async def test_background_cancel_and_failure(tmp_path, log):
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    tasks = BackgroundTasks(max_parallel=1)

    async def forever():
        await asyncio.sleep(60)

    async def broken():
        raise RuntimeError("kaput")

    h1 = tasks.start("slow", forever, session=session)
    h2 = tasks.start("bad", broken, session=session)
    await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
    assert tasks.get(h2).status == "queued"  # max_parallel=1
    assert tasks.cancel(h1)
    assert (await tasks.wait(h1)).status == "cancelled"
    info = await tasks.wait(h2)
    assert info.status == "failed" and "kaput" in info.summary
    assert [t.handle for t in tasks.list(session.id)] == [h1, h2]

    runner = SubagentRunner(dict(BUILTIN_SUBAGENTS), model_for=lambda spec: (child, "c"))
    tasks = BackgroundTasks()
    ws = Workspace(tmp_path)
    script = [
        FakeProvider.call("task", {"description": "dig", "subagent_type": "researcher", "background": True}, "t1"),
        FakeProvider.call("task_status", {}, "s1"),
        FakeProvider.text("waiting"),
    ]
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider(script), "m", workspace=ws, policy=Policy(workspace=ws), tools=[*builtin_tools(), task_tool(runner), task_status_tool(), task_cancel_tool()], services={"background": tasks})
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    [e async for e in agent.run(session, "go")]
    results = {r.call_id: r for m in session.messages() if m.role == "tool" for r in m.results}
    handle = results["t1"].text.split("started background task ")[1].split(";")[0]
    assert handle in results["s1"].text
    await tasks.wait(handle)
    # Delivered either inside the running turn (next step boundary) or queued for the next one.
    delivered = session.drain_queue() + [m.text for m in session.messages() if m.role == "user"]
    assert any("background finding" in text for text in delivered)


async def test_background_session_survives_client_disconnect(tmp_path, log):
    gate = asyncio.Event()

    @tool()
    async def wait_gate() -> str:
        """Block until the test opens the gate."""
        await gate.wait()
        return "gate open"

    ws = Workspace(tmp_path)
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider([FakeProvider.call("wait_gate", {}), FakeProvider.text("finished the job")]), "m", workspace=ws, tools=[wait_gate])
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    done = []
    manager = AgentManager(on_done=done.append)
    info = manager.start(agent, session, "long job", label="job")
    assert info.status in ("queued", "running")
    with pytest.raises(LunaCoreError):
        manager.start(agent, session, "again")
    received = []
    unsubscribe = manager.subscribe(session.id, received.append)
    while not any(e["event"] == "tool_started" for e in received):
        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
    unsubscribe()  # the TUI closes
    gate.set()
    final = await manager.wait(session.id)
    assert final.status == "done" and "finished the job" in final.text
    assert done == [final]
    assert not any(e["event"] == "turn_done" for e in received)  # nobody was listening any more
    replay = manager.events(session.id)
    assert replay[-1]["event"] == "turn_done" and [e["index"] for e in replay] == list(range(len(replay)))
    assert [e["event"] for e in manager.events(session.id, after=len(replay) - 2)] == ["turn_done"]
    reopened = Session.open(log, session.id, undo=False)
    assert reopened.messages()[-1].text == "finished the job"


async def test_manager_waiting_status_and_cancel(tmp_path, log):
    ws = Workspace(tmp_path)
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider([FakeProvider.call("execute", {"command": "echo hi"}, "c1")]), "m", workspace=ws)
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    manager = AgentManager()
    manager.start(agent, session, "go")
    while manager.get(session.id).status != "waiting":
        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
    assert manager.cancel(session.id)
    assert (await manager.wait(session.id)).status == "cancelled"
    await manager.shutdown()
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/background.py`:

```python
"""Background work: subagent tasks inside a session, and whole detached sessions."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import time
import uuid
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass

from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.events import BackgroundDone, Cancelled, NeedsApproval, TextDelta, TurnDone, TurnError, TurnStarted, to_wire
from luna_core.session import Session


@dataclass
class TaskInfo:
    """A background task inside a session."""

    handle: str
    label: str
    session_id: str
    status: str
    started: float
    finished: float | None = None
    ok: bool | None = None
    summary: str = ""


class BackgroundTasks:
    """Runs ``task(background=true)`` work and delivers results to the parent session."""

    def __init__(self, max_parallel: int = 4) -> None:
        self._semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(max_parallel)
        self._infos: dict[str, TaskInfo] = {}
        self._tasks: dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}

    def start(self, label: str, factory: Callable[[], Awaitable[object]], *, session: Session) -> str:
        """Start ``factory()`` in the background; returns its handle."""
        handle = f"bg-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        self._infos[handle] = TaskInfo(handle, label, session.id, "queued", time.time())
        self._tasks[handle] = asyncio.create_task(self._run(handle, factory, session))
        return handle

    async def _run(self, handle: str, factory: Callable[[], Awaitable[object]], session: Session) -> None:
        info = self._infos[handle]
        try:
            async with self._semaphore:
                info.status = "running"
                result = await factory()
            info.ok = bool(getattr(result, "ok", True))
            info.summary = result.render() if hasattr(result, "render") else str(result)
            info.status = "done" if info.ok else "failed"
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            info.status, info.ok, info.summary = "cancelled", False, "cancelled"
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - reported to the parent, never raised
            info.status, info.ok, info.summary = "failed", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        finally:
            info.finished = time.time()
            session.append("background_done", {"handle": handle, "ok": bool(info.ok), "summary": info.summary})
            session.queue(f"[background task {handle} · {info.label} · {info.status}]\n{info.summary}")
            session.notify(BackgroundDone(handle, bool(info.ok), info.summary[:2000]))

    def get(self, handle: str) -> TaskInfo | None:
        """One task's info."""
        return self._infos.get(handle)

    def list(self, session_id: str | None = None) -> list[TaskInfo]:
        """Tasks (of one session), oldest first."""
        return [i for i in self._infos.values() if session_id is None or i.session_id == session_id]

    def cancel(self, handle: str) -> bool:
        """Cancel a running or queued task."""
        task = self._tasks.get(handle)
        if task is None or task.done():
            return False
        task.cancel()
        return True

    async def wait(self, handle: str) -> TaskInfo:
        """Wait until the task has finished."""
        task = self._tasks[handle]
        await asyncio.gather(task, return_exceptions=True)
        return self._infos[handle]

    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Cancel everything."""
        for task in self._tasks.values():
            task.cancel()
        await asyncio.gather(*self._tasks.values(), return_exceptions=True)


@dataclass
class RunInfo:
    """A detached session run."""

    session_id: str
    label: str
    status: str
    started: float
    finished: float | None = None
    error: str = ""
    cost: float = 0.0
    text: str = ""


class AgentManager:
    """Owns detached session runs (server side); clients subscribe and unsubscribe freely."""

    def __init__(self, max_parallel: int = 4, on_done: Callable[[RunInfo], None] | None = None, buffer: int = 5000) -> None:
        self._semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(max_parallel)
        self._on_done = on_done
        self._limit = buffer
        self._runs: dict[str, RunInfo] = {}
        self._tasks: dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}
        self._sessions: dict[str, Session] = {}
        self._events: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
        self._listeners: dict[str, list[Callable[[dict], None]]] = {}

    def start(self, agent, session: Session, text: str, *, images=(), label: str = "") -> RunInfo:
        """Run one turn of ``session`` detached from any client."""
        current = self._tasks.get(session.id)
        if (current and not current.done()) or session.busy:
            raise LunaCoreError(f"session {session.id} is already running")
        info = RunInfo(session.id, label or session.title or text[:60], "queued", time.time())
        self._runs[session.id] = info
        self._sessions[session.id] = session
        self._events[session.id] = []
        self._tasks[session.id] = asyncio.create_task(self._run(agent, session, text, list(images), info))
        return info

    def _publish(self, session_id: str, wire: dict) -> None:
        events = self._events[session_id]
        wire = {**wire, "index": len(events)}
        events.append(wire)
        if len(events) > self._limit:
            del events[: len(events) - self._limit]
        for fn in list(self._listeners.get(session_id, [])):
            try:
                fn(wire)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a broken client must not stop the run
                pass

    async def _run(self, agent, session: Session, text: str, images: list, info: RunInfo) -> None:
        try:
            async with self._semaphore:
                info.status = "running"
                async for event in agent.run(session, text, images=images):
                    if isinstance(event, NeedsApproval):
                        info.status = "waiting"
                    elif info.status == "waiting" and not session.pending_approvals:
                        info.status = "running"
                    if isinstance(event, TurnStarted):
                        info.text = ""
                    elif isinstance(event, TextDelta):
                        info.text += event.text
                    elif isinstance(event, TurnDone):
                        info.status = "done"
                    elif isinstance(event, TurnError):
                        info.status, info.error = "failed", event.message
                    elif isinstance(event, Cancelled):
                        info.status = "cancelled"
                    self._publish(session.id, to_wire(event))
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            info.status = "cancelled"
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - reported through RunInfo
            info.status, info.error = "failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        finally:
            info.finished = time.time()
            info.cost = session.cost()
            if self._on_done:
                self._on_done(info)
            session.notify(BackgroundDone(session.id, info.status == "done", info.text[-2000:] or info.error))

    def get(self, session_id: str) -> RunInfo | None:
        """Run info for a session."""
        return self._runs.get(session_id)

    def list(self) -> list[RunInfo]:
        """All runs, newest first."""
        return sorted(self._runs.values(), key=lambda r: r.started, reverse=True)

    def events(self, session_id: str, after: int = -1) -> list[dict]:
        """Buffered wire events with ``index > after`` (all by default)."""
        return [e for e in self._events.get(session_id, []) if e["index"] > after]

    def has_listeners(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
        """True while some client follows the run."""
        return bool(self._listeners.get(session_id))

    def subscribe(self, session_id: str, fn: Callable[[dict], None]) -> Callable[[], None]:
        """Receive live wire events; returns an unsubscribe callable."""
        self._listeners.setdefault(session_id, []).append(fn)

        def unsubscribe() -> None:
            listeners = self._listeners.get(session_id, [])
            if fn in listeners:
                listeners.remove(fn)

        return unsubscribe

    def cancel(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
        """Stop a run."""
        session = self._sessions.get(session_id)
        task = self._tasks.get(session_id)
        if session is None or task is None or task.done():
            return False
        if not session.cancel_turn():
            task.cancel()
        return True

    async def wait(self, session_id: str) -> RunInfo:
        """Wait for a run to finish."""
        await asyncio.gather(self._tasks[session_id], return_exceptions=True)
        return self._runs[session_id]

    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Cancel every run (server shutdown)."""
        for task in self._tasks.values():
            task.cancel()
        await asyncio.gather(*self._tasks.values(), return_exceptions=True)
```

```python
def task_status_tool() -> Tool:
    """``task_status``: list or inspect background tasks of this session."""

    async def run(ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> ToolOutput:
        background = ctx.services.get("background")
        if background is None:
            return ToolOutput.error("background tasks are not enabled in this host")
        handle = str(args.get("handle") or "")
        infos = [background.get(handle)] if handle else background.list(ctx.session_id)
        infos = [i for i in infos if i is not None]
        if not infos:
            return ToolOutput.text("no background tasks" if not handle else f"no task {handle}")
        lines = [f"{i.handle} · {i.label} · {i.status}" + (f"\n{i.summary[:1500]}" if i.summary else "") for i in infos]
        return ToolOutput.text("\n\n".join(lines), f"{len(infos)} tasks")

    schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {"handle": {"type": "string", "description": "Empty for all tasks."}}, "additionalProperties": False}
    return Tool("task_status", "Show background tasks and their results.", schema, run, raw=True)


def task_cancel_tool() -> Tool:
    """``task_cancel``: stop a background task."""

    async def run(ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> ToolOutput:
        background = ctx.services.get("background")
        if background is None:
            return ToolOutput.error("background tasks are not enabled in this host")
        handle = str(args.get("handle", ""))
        return ToolOutput.text(f"cancelled {handle}") if background.cancel(handle) else ToolOutput.error(f"{handle} is not running")

    schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {"handle": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["handle"], "additionalProperties": False}
    return Tool("task_cancel", "Cancel a running background task.", schema, run, raw=True)
```

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/mcp.py`, `packages/luna-core/tests/mcp_echo_server.py` (test fixture server)
- Modify: root `pyproject.toml` — the `dev` extra gains `"mcp>=1.12,<2"` and `"agent-client-protocol>=0.4"` so CI runs the extra-dependent tests; `packages/luna-core/pyproject.toml` extra becomes `mcp = ["mcp>=1.12,<2"]` (v2 replaces the client API and switches to `httpx2`; moving to v2 is its own future change)
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_mcp.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `INSTALL_HINT`, `MCPConfigError(LunaCoreError)`
  - `expand_env(value, environ)`, `load_config(files: list[Path], environ=None) -> dict[str, dict]` (Claude-style `{"mcpServers": {...}}`, later files win, `${VAR}` expanded), `add_server(path, name, spec) -> Path`, `remove_server(path, name) -> bool`, `transport_of(name, spec) -> "stdio" | "http" | "sse"`
  - `FileTokenStorage(path)` (OAuth tokens + client info as JSON, file mode 0600)
  - `MCPManager(servers, *, on_warn=print, connect_timeout=30.0, call_timeout=120.0, token_dir=None, open_browser=None)`: `async start() -> list[Tool]`, `async close()`, `.tools`, `.status() -> dict[str, str]`
- Tool mapping: name `<server>_<tool>` (sanitised to `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, 64 chars — same as the old `tool_name_prefix=True`), description and `inputSchema` passed through, text/image content mapped to `Text`/`Image`, other content as JSON text, `isError` → error result, a call timeout → error result. `mutating` is false only for tools annotated `readOnlyHint: true` (plan mode then still allows them).
- Each server lives in its own task (the SDK's transports must enter and exit their context managers in one task); a server that fails to start is reported with `on_warn` and skipped — a broken server never breaks the agent.
- HTTP servers with `"oauth": true` use the SDK's `OAuthClientProvider`: tokens in `token_dir/<server>.json`, the authorisation URL opened with `open_browser` (default `webbrowser.open`), the redirect caught by a one-shot local HTTP listener on `127.0.0.1:<free port>/callback`.

```python
from mcp.server.fastmcp import FastMCP

server = FastMCP("echo")


@server.tool(annotations={"readOnlyHint": True})
def add(a: int, b: int) -> str:
```

```python
import json
import stat
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

pytest.importorskip("mcp")

from luna_core.mcp import FileTokenStorage, MCPConfigError, MCPManager, add_server, load_config, remove_server, transport_of  # noqa: E402
from luna_core.messages import Image  # noqa: E402
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolContext  # noqa: E402

SERVER = str(Path(__file__).with_name("mcp_echo_server.py"))


def test_config_merge_env_and_edit(tmp_path):
    user, proj = tmp_path / "user.json", tmp_path / "proj.json"
    user.write_text(json.dumps({"mcpServers": {"a": {"command": "x"}, "b": {"command": "${TOOL}", "args": ["--k=${KEY}"]}}}))
    proj.write_text(json.dumps({"mcpServers": {"a": {"type": "http", "url": "https://h/mcp"}}}))
    cfg = load_config([user, proj, tmp_path / "missing.json"], environ={"TOOL": "npx", "KEY": "s3"})
    assert cfg["a"]["url"] == "https://h/mcp" and cfg["b"] == {"command": "npx", "args": ["--k=s3"]}
    add_server(proj, "c", {"command": "uvx", "args": ["thing"]})
    assert "c" in json.loads(proj.read_text())["mcpServers"]
    assert remove_server(proj, "c") and not remove_server(proj, "c")
    assert transport_of("a", cfg["a"]) == "http" and transport_of("b", cfg["b"]) == "stdio"
    with pytest.raises(MCPConfigError):
        transport_of("bad", {"type": "http"})


async def test_stdio_server_tools_round_trip():
    manager = MCPManager({"echo": {"command": sys.executable, "args": [SERVER]}}, on_warn=pytest.fail)
    tools = {t.name: t for t in await manager.start()}
    try:
        assert {"echo_add", "echo_fail", "echo_pixel"} <= set(tools)
        assert not tools["echo_add"].mutating and tools["echo_fail"].mutating
        assert tools["echo_add"].parameters["required"] == ["a", "b"]
        out = await tools["echo_add"].invoke(ToolContext(), {"a": 2, "b": 3})
        assert out.content[0].text == "5" and not out.is_error
        err = await tools["echo_fail"].invoke(ToolContext(), {"reason": "nope"})
        assert err.is_error and "nope" in err.content[0].text
        img = await tools["echo_pixel"].invoke(ToolContext(), {})
        assert isinstance(img.content[0], Image) and img.content[0].media_type == "image/png"
        assert manager.status() == {"echo": "connected"}
    finally:
        await manager.close()


async def test_broken_server_is_skipped_with_a_warning(tmp_path):
    warnings = []
    manager = MCPManager({"ghost": {"command": str(tmp_path / "does-not-exist")}}, on_warn=warnings.append, connect_timeout=5)
    assert await manager.start() == []
    assert "ghost" in warnings[0] and manager.status()["ghost"].startswith("failed")
    await manager.close()


async def test_token_storage_round_trip(tmp_path):
    from mcp.shared.auth import OAuthToken

    storage = FileTokenStorage(tmp_path / "tok" / "srv.json")
    assert await storage.get_tokens() is None
    await storage.set_tokens(OAuthToken(access_token="a", token_type="Bearer"))
    again = FileTokenStorage(tmp_path / "tok" / "srv.json")
    assert (await again.get_tokens()).access_token == "a"
    assert stat.S_IMODE((tmp_path / "tok" / "srv.json").stat().st_mode) == 0o600
```

```python
"""MCP client: tools from MCP servers as core tools (needs the ``mcp`` extra)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import os
import re
import webbrowser
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from contextlib import AsyncExitStack
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse

from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.messages import Image, Text
from luna_core.tools.base import Tool, ToolContext, ToolOutput

INSTALL_HINT = 'MCP support is not installed: pip install "luna-core[mcp]"'
_ENV = re.compile(r"\$\{([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\}")
_NAME = re.compile(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_-]")


class MCPConfigError(LunaCoreError):
    """An MCP server definition is invalid."""


def expand_env(value, environ: Mapping[str, str]):
    """Replace ``${VAR}`` in strings, recursively."""
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return _ENV.sub(lambda m: environ.get(m.group(1), m.group(0)), value)
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [expand_env(v, environ) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {k: expand_env(v, environ) for k, v in value.items()}
    return value


def _read(path: Path) -> dict:
    try:
        data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return {}
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise MCPConfigError(f"cannot read {path}: {exc}") from exc
    servers = data.get("mcpServers", {}) if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
    return servers if isinstance(servers, dict) else {}


def load_config(files: list[Path], environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> dict[str, dict]:
    """Merged ``mcpServers`` from ``files`` (later wins), ``${VAR}`` expanded."""
    env = os.environ if environ is None else environ
    merged: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for path in files:
        merged.update(_read(path))
    return {name: expand_env(spec, env) for name, spec in merged.items()}


def add_server(path: Path, name: str, spec: dict) -> Path:
    """Write ``mcpServers.<name>`` into ``path``."""
    path = Path(path)
    doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if path.exists() else {}
    doc.setdefault("mcpServers", {})[name] = spec
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def remove_server(path: Path, name: str) -> bool:
    """Delete ``mcpServers.<name>`` from ``path``."""
    path = Path(path)
    if not path.exists():
        return False
    doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if name not in doc.get("mcpServers", {}):
        return False
    del doc["mcpServers"][name]
    path.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return True


def transport_of(name: str, spec: dict) -> str:
    """``stdio``, ``http`` or ``sse`` for a server spec."""
    kind = spec.get("type") or spec.get("transport")
    if "command" in spec and kind in (None, "stdio"):
        return "stdio"
    if kind in ("http", "streamable_http", "streamable-http") and spec.get("url"):
        return "http"
    if kind == "sse" and spec.get("url"):
        return "sse"
    raise MCPConfigError(f"MCP server {name!r}: need a 'command' (stdio) or a 'url' with type 'http' or 'sse'")


class FileTokenStorage:
    """OAuth tokens and client registration for one server, as a private JSON file."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        self.path = Path(path)

    def _load(self) -> dict:
        try:
            return json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return {}

    def _save(self, data: dict) -> None:
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
        self.path.chmod(0o600)

    async def get_tokens(self):
        """Stored tokens or ``None``."""
        from mcp.shared.auth import OAuthToken

        raw = self._load().get("tokens")
        return OAuthToken.model_validate(raw) if raw else None

    async def set_tokens(self, tokens) -> None:
        """Store tokens."""
        data = self._load()
        data["tokens"] = tokens.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
        self._save(data)

    async def get_client_info(self):
        """Stored dynamic client registration or ``None``."""
        from mcp.shared.auth import OAuthClientInformationFull

        raw = self._load().get("client_info")
        return OAuthClientInformationFull.model_validate(raw) if raw else None

    async def set_client_info(self, client_info) -> None:
        """Store the client registration."""
        data = self._load()
        data["client_info"] = client_info.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
        self._save(data)


async def _callback_listener() -> tuple[str, asyncio.Future]:
    """A one-shot HTTP listener for the OAuth redirect; returns (redirect_uri, future of (code, state))."""
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    result: asyncio.Future = loop.create_future()

    async def handle(reader: asyncio.StreamReader, writer: asyncio.StreamWriter) -> None:
        request = (await reader.readline()).decode("latin-1")
        target = request.split(" ")[1] if " " in request else "/"
        query = parse_qs(urlparse(target).query)
        body = "Luna: authorisation finished, you can close this tab." if "code" in query else "Luna: authorisation failed."
        writer.write(f"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8\r\nContent-Length: {len(body.encode())}\r\n\r\n{body}".encode())
        await writer.drain()
        writer.close()
        if "code" in query and not result.done():
            result.set_result((query["code"][0], query.get("state", [None])[0]))

    server = await asyncio.start_server(handle, "127.0.0.1", 0)
    port = server.sockets[0].getsockname()[1]
    result.add_done_callback(lambda _f: server.close())
    return f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/callback", result


class MCPManager:
    """Connects to MCP servers and exposes their tools."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        servers: dict[str, dict],
        *,
        on_warn: Callable[[str], None] = print,
        connect_timeout: float = 30.0,
        call_timeout: float = 120.0,
        token_dir: Path | None = None,
        open_browser: Callable[[str], object] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.servers = dict(servers)
        self.on_warn = on_warn
        self.connect_timeout = connect_timeout
        self.call_timeout = call_timeout
        self.token_dir = Path(token_dir) if token_dir else Path.home() / ".config" / "luna" / "mcp-tokens"
        self.open_browser = open_browser or webbrowser.open
        self.tools: list[Tool] = []
        self._status: dict[str, str] = {}
        self._stops: dict[str, asyncio.Event] = {}
        self._tasks: dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}

    def status(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        """``connected`` or ``failed: …`` per server."""
        return dict(self._status)

    async def start(self) -> list[Tool]:
        """Connect to every server (concurrently) and collect their tools."""
        try:
            import mcp  # noqa: F401
        except ImportError:
            if self.servers:
                self.on_warn(INSTALL_HINT)
            return []
        results = await asyncio.gather(*(self._connect(name, spec) for name, spec in self.servers.items()))
        self.tools = [tool for tools in results for tool in tools]
        return self.tools

    async def _connect(self, name: str, spec: dict) -> list[Tool]:
        ready: asyncio.Future = asyncio.get_running_loop().create_future()
        stop = asyncio.Event()
        self._stops[name] = stop
        self._tasks[name] = asyncio.create_task(self._serve(name, spec, ready, stop))
        try:
            session, tools = await asyncio.wait_for(ready, self.connect_timeout)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - one bad server must not break the agent
            message = str(exc) or type(exc).__name__
            self._status[name] = f"failed: {message}"
            self.on_warn(f"MCP server {name!r} did not start: {message}")
            stop.set()
            return []
        self._status[name] = "connected"
        return [self._tool(name, session, t) for t in tools]

    async def _serve(self, name: str, spec: dict, ready: asyncio.Future, stop: asyncio.Event) -> None:
        from mcp import ClientSession, StdioServerParameters

        try:
            async with AsyncExitStack() as stack:
                kind = transport_of(name, spec)
                if kind == "stdio":
                    from mcp.client.stdio import stdio_client

                    env = {**os.environ, **spec["env"]} if spec.get("env") else None
                    params = StdioServerParameters(command=spec["command"], args=list(spec.get("args", [])), env=env, cwd=spec.get("cwd"))
                    read, write = await stack.enter_async_context(stdio_client(params))
                elif kind == "http":
                    from mcp.client.streamable_http import streamablehttp_client

                    auth = await self._oauth(name, spec) if spec.get("oauth") else None
                    read, write, _ = await stack.enter_async_context(streamablehttp_client(spec["url"], headers=spec.get("headers"), auth=auth))
                else:
                    from mcp.client.sse import sse_client

                    read, write = await stack.enter_async_context(sse_client(spec["url"], headers=spec.get("headers")))
                session = await stack.enter_async_context(ClientSession(read, write))
                await session.initialize()
                tools, cursor = [], None
                while True:
                    page = await session.list_tools(cursor=cursor)
                    tools.extend(page.tools)
                    cursor = getattr(page, "nextCursor", None)
                    if not cursor:
                        break
                ready.set_result((session, tools))
                await stop.wait()
        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - reported through ``ready``
            if not ready.done():
                ready.set_exception(exc if isinstance(exc, Exception) else RuntimeError(str(exc)))
            if isinstance(exc, asyncio.CancelledError):
                raise

    async def _oauth(self, name: str, spec: dict):
        from mcp.client.auth import OAuthClientProvider
        from mcp.shared.auth import OAuthClientMetadata
        from pydantic import AnyUrl

        redirect_uri, code_future = await _callback_listener()

        async def redirect(url: str) -> None:
            self.on_warn(f"MCP server {name!r} needs authorisation; opening {url}")
            self.open_browser(url)

        async def callback() -> tuple[str, str | None]:
            return await code_future

        metadata = OAuthClientMetadata(
            client_name="Luna",
            redirect_uris=[AnyUrl(redirect_uri)],
            grant_types=["authorization_code", "refresh_token"],
            response_types=["code"],
            scope=spec.get("scope"),
        )
        server_url = spec["url"].rsplit("/mcp", 1)[0] if spec["url"].endswith("/mcp") else spec["url"]
        return OAuthClientProvider(
            server_url=server_url,
            client_metadata=metadata,
            storage=FileTokenStorage(self.token_dir / f"{name}.json"),
            redirect_handler=redirect,
            callback_handler=callback,
        )

    def _tool(self, server: str, session, tool) -> Tool:
        timeout = self.call_timeout

        async def run(ctx: ToolContext, args: dict) -> ToolOutput:
            try:
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(session.call_tool(tool.name, arguments=args), timeout)
            except TimeoutError:
                return ToolOutput.error(f"{server}.{tool.name} timed out after {timeout:g}s")
            parts = []
            for item in result.content:
                kind = getattr(item, "type", "")
                if kind == "text":
                    parts.append(Text(item.text))
                elif kind == "image":
                    parts.append(Image(item.mimeType, item.data))
                else:
                    parts.append(Text(json.dumps(item.model_dump(mode="json"), ensure_ascii=False)[:5000]))
            if not parts and getattr(result, "structuredContent", None):
                parts.append(Text(json.dumps(result.structuredContent, ensure_ascii=False)))
            return ToolOutput(tuple(parts) or (Text("(no output)"),), bool(result.isError), f"{server}.{tool.name}")

        annotations = getattr(tool, "annotations", None)
        read_only = bool(annotations and getattr(annotations, "readOnlyHint", False))
        schema = tool.inputSchema or {"type": "object", "properties": {}}
        name = _NAME.sub("_", f"{server}_{tool.name}")[:64]
        return Tool(name, (tool.description or tool.name)[:1024], schema, run, raw=True, mutating=not read_only)

    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Disconnect every server."""
        for stop in self._stops.values():
            stop.set()
        tasks = list(self._tasks.values())
        done, pending = await asyncio.wait(tasks, timeout=5) if tasks else (set(), set())
        for task in pending:
            task.cancel()
        await asyncio.gather(*pending, return_exceptions=True)
```

### Task 26: Remote client and the wire protocol

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/protocol.py`, `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/client.py`, `docs/protocol.md`, `schema/events.schema.json`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_protocol.py`, `packages/luna-core/tests/test_client.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`protocol.py`): `PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1`, `EVENT_FIELDS: dict[str, dict[str, type | tuple]]` (required fields per wire event), `validate_event(event: dict) -> None` (raises `ValueError`), `json_schema() -> dict` (JSON Schema draft 2020-12 built from `EVENT_FIELDS`), `python -m luna_core.protocol` prints it.
- Produces (`client.py`): `ServerError(LunaCoreError)`, `LunaClient(base_url, token, *, client=None)`:
  - `async health() -> dict`
  - `async list_sessions(workdir) -> list[dict]`, `async create_session(workdir) -> str`, `async delete_session(session_id) -> None`
  - `async history(session_id, workdir) -> list[dict]`, `async state(session_id, workdir) -> dict`
  - `send(session_id, text, workdir, *, images=(), reload_after=False) -> AsyncIterator[dict]` (SSE; the stream stays open across approvals)
  - `async decide(session_id, call_id, decision: dict | Decision) -> None`, `async cancel(session_id) -> bool`
  - `async commands(workdir) -> list[dict]`, `async run_command(session_id, line, workdir) -> dict`
  - `async background(session_id, text, workdir) -> dict`, `async agents() -> list[dict]`, `attach(session_id, *, after=-1) -> AsyncIterator[dict]`
  - `async aclose()`
- HTTP API v1 (implemented by the app server in Task 30; written down in `docs/protocol.md`):

| Method | Path | Body / query | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/health` | — | `{"ok": true, "version": str, "protocol": 1}` |
| GET | `/sessions` | `?workdir=` | `{"sessions": [{"id", "title", "created", "updated", "group", "relative_time", "running", "background"}]}` |
| POST | `/sessions` | `{"workdir"}` | `{"id"}` |
| DELETE | `/sessions/{id}` | — | `{"deleted": bool}` |
| GET | `/sessions/{id}/messages` | `?workdir=` | `{"messages": [{"role": "user" \| "assistant", "content"} \| {"role": "tool", "name", "args_preview", "ok", "detail"}]}` |
| POST | `/sessions/{id}/messages` | `{"workdir", "content", "images"?: [{"media_type", "data"}], "reload_after"?}` | SSE of turn events until `turn_done` / `error` / `cancelled` |
| POST | `/sessions/{id}/approve` | `{"call_id", "decision"}` | `{"ok": true}` (the open turn stream continues) |
| POST | `/sessions/{id}/cancel` | — | `{"cancelled": bool}` |
| GET | `/sessions/{id}/state` | `?workdir=` | status-bar state |
| GET | `/commands` | `?workdir=` | `{"commands": [...]}` |
| POST | `/sessions/{id}/command` | `{"workdir", "line"}` | command result JSON |
| POST | `/sessions/{id}/background` | `{"workdir", "content"}` | run info |
| GET | `/agents` | — | `{"runs": [...], "tasks": [...]}` |
| GET | `/sessions/{id}/events` | `?after=` | SSE: buffered then live events of a background run |

  Every request carries `Authorization: Bearer <token>`; errors are `{"error": str}` with 4xx/5xx.

```python
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from luna_core.events import (
    ApprovalRequest,
    BackgroundDone,
    Cancelled,
    Compacted,
    NeedsApproval,
    Notice,
    ReloadRequested,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    TodosUpdated,
    ToolFinished,
    ToolStarted,
    TurnDone,
    TurnError,
    TurnStarted,
    UsageUpdate,
    to_wire,
)
from luna_core.protocol import EVENT_FIELDS, json_schema, validate_event
from luna_core.usage import Usage

SAMPLES = [
    TurnStarted("t1"),
    TextDelta("hi"),
    ThinkingDelta("hm"),
    ToolStarted("c1", "ls", {"path": "."}, "."),
    ToolFinished("c1", "ls", True, "3 entries", 0.1),
    NeedsApproval(ApprovalRequest("c2", "execute", {"command": "ls"}, "ls", "execute:ls *")),
    TodosUpdated([{"content": "x", "status": "pending"}]),
    Notice("info", "note"),
    UsageUpdate(Usage(1, 2)),
    Compacted("s", 3),
    BackgroundDone("h", True, "ok"),
    ReloadRequested(),
    TurnDone(Usage(1, 2), 0.01, "end"),
    TurnError("boom", "server"),
    Cancelled(),
]


def test_every_turn_event_validates():
    names = set()
    for event in SAMPLES:
        wire = to_wire(event)
        validate_event(wire)
        names.add(wire["event"])
    assert names == set(EVENT_FIELDS)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "bad",
    [{"text": "x"}, {"event": "nope"}, {"event": "text_delta"}, {"event": "text_delta", "text": 3}],
)
def test_invalid_events_are_rejected(bad):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_event(bad)


def test_schema_file_is_up_to_date():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
    on_disk = json.loads((root / "schema" / "events.schema.json").read_text())
    assert on_disk == json_schema()
    printed = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "luna_core.protocol"], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
    assert json.loads(printed) == on_disk
```

```python
import json

import httpx
import pytest

from luna_core.client import LunaClient, ServerError
from luna_core.policy import Decision


def sse(*events):
    return "".join(f"data: {json.dumps(e)}\n\n" for e in events).encode()


class Server:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []

    def __call__(self, request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        body = json.loads(request.content) if request.content else None
        self.calls.append((request.method, request.url.path, dict(request.url.params), body, request.headers.get("authorization")))
        path = request.url.path
        if path == "/sessions" and request.method == "POST":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "s1"})
        if path == "/sessions":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"sessions": [{"id": "s1", "title": "t"}]})
        if path == "/sessions/s1/messages" and request.method == "POST":
            return httpx.Response(200, headers={"content-type": "text/event-stream"}, content=sse({"event": "text_delta", "text": "hi"}, {"event": "turn_done", "usage": {}, "cost": None, "stop_reason": "end"}))
        if path == "/sessions/s1/approve":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"ok": True})
        if path == "/sessions/s1/cancel":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"cancelled": True})
        if path == "/sessions/s9/messages":
            return httpx.Response(404, json={"error": "no session s9"})
        if path == "/agents":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"runs": [{"session_id": "s1", "status": "running"}], "tasks": []})
        return httpx.Response(500, json={"error": "unexpected"})


@pytest.fixture
def pair():
    server = Server()
    client = LunaClient("http://luna", "tok", client=httpx.AsyncClient(base_url="http://luna", transport=httpx.MockTransport(server)))
    return server, client


async def test_session_calls_and_auth(pair):
    server, client = pair
    assert await client.create_session("/w") == "s1"
    assert (await client.list_sessions("/w"))[0]["id"] == "s1"
    assert server.calls[0][1:] == ("/sessions", {}, {"workdir": "/w"}, "Bearer tok")
    assert server.calls[1][2] == {"workdir": "/w"}


async def test_send_streams_events_and_images(pair):
    server, client = pair
    events = [e async for e in client.send("s1", "look", "/w", images=[{"media_type": "image/png", "data": "AAA"}])]
    assert [e["event"] for e in events] == ["text_delta", "turn_done"]
    assert server.calls[-1][3] == {"content": "look", "workdir": "/w", "images": [{"media_type": "image/png", "data": "AAA"}]}


async def test_decide_cancel_agents(pair):
    server, client = pair
    await client.decide("s1", "c1", Decision.edit({"command": "ls"}))
    assert server.calls[-1][3] == {"call_id": "c1", "decision": {"type": "edit", "args": {"command": "ls"}}}
    assert await client.cancel("s1") is True
    assert (await client.agents())["runs"][0]["status"] == "running"


async def test_errors_are_readable(pair):
    _server, client = pair
    with pytest.raises(ServerError, match="no session s9"):
        [e async for e in client.send("s9", "x", "/w")]
```

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/protocol.py`:

```python
"""The wire protocol of turn events: required fields, validation, JSON Schema."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1
_NUM = (int, float)
_OPT_NUM = (int, float, type(None))

EVENT_FIELDS: dict[str, dict[str, type | tuple]] = {
    "turn_started": {"turn_id": str},
    "text_delta": {"text": str},
    "thinking_delta": {"text": str},
    "tool_started": {"call_id": str, "name": str, "args": dict, "args_preview": str},
    "tool_finished": {"call_id": str, "name": str, "ok": bool, "detail": str, "duration": _NUM},
    "approval_needed": {"value": dict},
    "todos": {"todos": list},
    "notice": {"level": str, "text": str},
    "usage_delta": {"usage": dict},
    "compacted": {"summary": str, "removed": int},
    "background_done": {"handle": str, "ok": bool, "summary": str},
    "reload_requested": {},
    "turn_done": {"usage": dict, "cost": _OPT_NUM, "stop_reason": str},
    "error": {"message": str, "kind": str},
    "cancelled": {},
}
_JSON_TYPES = {str: "string", bool: "boolean", int: "integer", float: "number", dict: "object", list: "array", type(None): "null"}


def validate_event(event: dict) -> None:
    """Raise ``ValueError`` unless ``event`` is a well-formed wire event."""
    if not isinstance(event, dict) or not isinstance(event.get("event"), str):
        raise ValueError("a wire event is an object with a string 'event' field")
    fields = EVENT_FIELDS.get(event["event"])
    if fields is None:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown event {event['event']!r}")
    for name, kind in fields.items():
        if name not in event:
            raise ValueError(f"{event['event']}: missing field {name!r}")
        value = event[name]
        if isinstance(value, bool) and kind in (int, _NUM, _OPT_NUM):
            raise ValueError(f"{event['event']}.{name}: expected a number")
        if not isinstance(value, kind):
            raise ValueError(f"{event['event']}.{name}: expected {kind}, got {type(value).__name__}")
    if event["event"] == "approval_needed":
        requests = event["value"].get("action_requests")
        if not isinstance(requests, list) or not all(isinstance(r, dict) and "call_id" in r for r in requests):
            raise ValueError("approval_needed.value.action_requests must be a list of requests with call_id")


def _schema_type(kind) -> dict:
    kinds = kind if isinstance(kind, tuple) else (kind,)
    names = sorted({_JSON_TYPES[k] for k in kinds})
    if names == ["integer", "number"]:
        names = ["number"]
    if names == ["integer", "null", "number"]:
        names = ["null", "number"]
    return {"type": names[0] if len(names) == 1 else names}


def json_schema() -> dict:
    """JSON Schema (2020-12) for one wire event."""
    variants = []
    for name, fields in EVENT_FIELDS.items():
        props = {"event": {"const": name}, **{f: _schema_type(k) for f, k in fields.items()}}
        variants.append({"type": "object", "properties": props, "required": ["event", *fields], "additionalProperties": True})
    return {
        "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
        "$id": "https://github.com/Dushe4ka/luna-simple/schema/events.schema.json",
        "title": f"Luna turn event (protocol {PROTOCOL_VERSION})",
        "oneOf": variants,
    }


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(json.dumps(json_schema(), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
```

Generate the schema file once: `uv run python -m luna_core.protocol > schema/events.schema.json` (the test keeps it in sync).

`packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/client.py`:

```python
"""Async client for a running Luna server (``luna serve``)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

import httpx

from luna_core.errors import LunaCoreError
from luna_core.policy import Decision

_STREAM_TIMEOUT = httpx.Timeout(connect=10.0, read=None, write=30.0, pool=10.0)


class ServerError(LunaCoreError):
    """The server answered with an error or could not be reached."""


def _error(resp: httpx.Response) -> ServerError:
    try:
        message = resp.json().get("error") or resp.text
    except ValueError:
        message = resp.text or resp.reason_phrase
    return ServerError(f"HTTP {resp.status_code}: {message}")


class LunaClient:
    """REST + SSE client for the Luna HTTP API (protocol v1)."""

    def __init__(self, base_url: str, token: str, *, client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None) -> None:
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self._headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}
        self._http = client or httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=self.base_url, timeout=httpx.Timeout(30.0))

    async def _json(self, method: str, path: str, **kwargs) -> dict:
        try:
            resp = await self._http.request(method, path, headers=self._headers, **kwargs)
        except httpx.TransportError as exc:
            raise ServerError(f"cannot reach the Luna server: {type(exc).__name__}") from exc
        if resp.is_error:
            raise _error(resp)
        return resp.json()

    async def _stream(self, method: str, path: str, **kwargs) -> AsyncIterator[dict]:
        try:
            async with self._http.stream(method, path, headers=self._headers, timeout=_STREAM_TIMEOUT, **kwargs) as resp:
                if resp.is_error:
                    await resp.aread()
                    raise _error(resp)
                async for line in resp.aiter_lines():
                    if line.startswith("data:"):
                        yield json.loads(line[5:].strip())
        except httpx.TransportError as exc:
            raise ServerError(f"connection to the Luna server dropped: {type(exc).__name__}") from exc

    async def health(self) -> dict:
        """Server version and protocol."""
        return await self._json("GET", "/health")

    async def list_sessions(self, workdir: str) -> list[dict]:
        """Sessions of a project, newest first."""
        return (await self._json("GET", "/sessions", params={"workdir": workdir}))["sessions"]

    async def create_session(self, workdir: str) -> str:
        """Create a session; returns its id."""
        return (await self._json("POST", "/sessions", json={"workdir": workdir}))["id"]

    async def delete_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        """Delete a session."""
        await self._json("DELETE", f"/sessions/{session_id}")

    async def history(self, session_id: str, workdir: str) -> list[dict]:
        """Transcript entries, oldest first."""
        return (await self._json("GET", f"/sessions/{session_id}/messages", params={"workdir": workdir}))["messages"]

    async def state(self, session_id: str, workdir: str) -> dict:
        """Status-bar state of a session."""
        return await self._json("GET", f"/sessions/{session_id}/state", params={"workdir": workdir})

    def send(self, session_id: str, text: str, workdir: str, *, images: list[dict] | tuple = (), reload_after: bool = False) -> AsyncIterator[dict]:
        """Run a turn; yields wire events until the turn ends."""
        body: dict = {"content": text, "workdir": workdir}
        if images:
            body["images"] = list(images)
        if reload_after:
            body["reload_after"] = True
        return self._stream("POST", f"/sessions/{session_id}/messages", json=body)

    async def decide(self, session_id: str, call_id: str, decision: dict | Decision) -> None:
        """Answer an approval; the open turn stream continues."""
        wire = decision.to_dict() if isinstance(decision, Decision) else decision
        await self._json("POST", f"/sessions/{session_id}/approve", json={"call_id": call_id, "decision": wire})

    async def cancel(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
        """Cancel the running turn."""
        return bool((await self._json("POST", f"/sessions/{session_id}/cancel"))["cancelled"])

    async def commands(self, workdir: str) -> list[dict]:
        """Available slash commands."""
        return (await self._json("GET", "/commands", params={"workdir": workdir}))["commands"]

    async def run_command(self, session_id: str, line: str, workdir: str) -> dict:
        """Run a slash command."""
        return await self._json("POST", f"/sessions/{session_id}/command", json={"line": line, "workdir": workdir}, timeout=_STREAM_TIMEOUT)

    async def background(self, session_id: str, text: str, workdir: str) -> dict:
        """Run a turn detached from this client."""
        return await self._json("POST", f"/sessions/{session_id}/background", json={"content": text, "workdir": workdir})

    async def agents(self) -> dict:
        """Background runs and tasks."""
        return await self._json("GET", "/agents")

    def attach(self, session_id: str, *, after: int = -1) -> AsyncIterator[dict]:
        """Replay and follow a background run's events."""
        return self._stream("GET", f"/sessions/{session_id}/events", params={"after": after})

    async def aclose(self) -> None:
        """Close the HTTP client."""
        await self._http.aclose()
```

`docs/protocol.md` — write it with: purpose and versioning (`protocol` 1, additive changes keep the version, breaking ones bump it); authentication; the endpoint table above with one request/response example each; the SSE framing (`data: <json>\n\n`, no `event:` lines); the event catalogue — one subsection per `EVENT_FIELDS` entry with its fields, meaning and an example taken from `test_protocol.SAMPLES`; the approval flow (stream emits `approval_needed` → client POSTs `/approve` with `call_id` and a decision → the same stream continues); cancellation; background runs (`/background`, `/agents`, `/events?after=`); the decision format; a pointer to `schema/events.schema.json` and to `LunaClient`.

### Task 27: ACP agent for editors

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/acp.py`
- Test: `packages/luna-core/tests/test_acp.py`, `packages/luna-core/tests/acp_fake_agent.py` (fixture process)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Agent`, `Session`, `EventLog`, `Decision`, turn events.
- Produces:
  - `AcpBridge` (pure, no SDK import): `.updates(event) -> list[dict]` (ACP `session/update` payloads in wire form), `.permission(request: ApprovalRequest) -> tuple[dict, list[dict]]` (toolCall, options), `.decision(outcome: dict, request) -> Decision`, `.stop_reason(events) -> str`, `prompt_to_input(blocks: list[dict]) -> tuple[str, list[Image]]`
  - `LunaAcpAgent(make_agent: Callable[[str], Agent | Awaitable[Agent]], log: EventLog)` implementing the SDK's agent interface (`on_connect`, `initialize`, `new_session`, `load_session`, `prompt`, `cancel`)
  - `async run_acp(make_agent, log) -> None` (stdio)
- Mapping:

| Luna event | ACP `sessionUpdate` |
|---|---|
| `TextDelta` | `agent_message_chunk` `{content: {type: "text", text}}` |
| `ThinkingDelta` | `agent_thought_chunk` |
| `ToolStarted` | `tool_call` `{toolCallId, title: "<name> <preview>", kind, status: "in_progress", rawInput}` |
| `ToolFinished` | `tool_call_update` `{toolCallId, status: "completed" \| "failed", content: [{type: "content", content: {type: "text", text: detail}}]}` |
| `TodosUpdated` | `plan` `{entries: [{content, priority: "medium", status}]}` |
| `Notice` (warn/error) | `agent_message_chunk` with the text in italics |
| `NeedsApproval` | `session/request_permission` with options `allow_once`, `allow_always`, `reject_once` |
| `TurnDone` / `Cancelled` / `TurnError` | `PromptResponse.stopReason` `end_turn` (`max_tokens`, `refusal` passed through) / `cancelled` / `end_turn` after an error chunk |

  Tool kinds: `read_file`/`ls` → `read`, `write_file`/`edit_file` → `edit`, `delete` → `delete`, `glob`/`grep` → `search`, `execute` → `execute`, `task` → `think`, anything else → `other`.
