```python
"""An ACP agent process backed by FakeProvider (for test_acp)."""

import asyncio
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from luna_core.acp import run_acp
from luna_core.loop import Agent
from luna_core.policy import Policy
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider
from luna_core.session import EventLog
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace


def make_agent(cwd: str) -> Agent:
    script = [FakeProvider.call("execute", {"command": "echo hi"}, "c1"), FakeProvider.text("done: hi")]
    ws = Workspace(cwd)
    return Agent(FakeProvider(script), "fake", workspace=ws, policy=Policy(workspace=ws))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(run_acp(make_agent, EventLog(Path(sys.argv[1]) / "acp.db")))
```

`packages/luna-core/tests/test_acp.py`:

```python
import asyncio
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from luna_core.acp import AcpBridge, prompt_to_input
from luna_core.events import ApprovalRequest, Cancelled, Notice, TextDelta, ThinkingDelta, TodosUpdated, ToolFinished, ToolStarted, TurnDone
from luna_core.messages import Image
from luna_core.policy import Decision
from luna_core.usage import Usage

FIXTURE = str(Path(__file__).with_name("acp_fake_agent.py"))


def test_bridge_updates():
    b = AcpBridge()
    assert b.updates(TextDelta("hi")) == [{"sessionUpdate": "agent_message_chunk", "content": {"type": "text", "text": "hi"}}]
    assert b.updates(ThinkingDelta("hm"))[0]["sessionUpdate"] == "agent_thought_chunk"
    start = b.updates(ToolStarted("c1", "edit_file", {"file_path": "a.py"}, "a.py"))[0]
    assert start == {"sessionUpdate": "tool_call", "toolCallId": "c1", "title": "edit_file a.py", "kind": "edit", "status": "in_progress", "rawInput": {"file_path": "a.py"}}
    done = b.updates(ToolFinished("c1", "edit_file", False, "old_string not found", 0.1))[0]
    assert done["status"] == "failed" and done["content"][0]["content"]["text"] == "old_string not found"
    plan = b.updates(TodosUpdated([{"content": "x", "status": "in_progress"}]))[0]
    assert plan == {"sessionUpdate": "plan", "entries": [{"content": "x", "priority": "medium", "status": "in_progress"}]}
    assert b.updates(Notice("warn", "careful"))[0]["content"]["text"] == "\n_careful_\n"
    assert b.updates(Notice("dim", "quiet")) == []


def test_permission_round_trip():
    b = AcpBridge()
    req = ApprovalRequest("c2", "execute", {"command": "pytest"}, "pytest", "execute:pytest *")
    tool_call, options = b.permission(req)
    assert tool_call["toolCallId"] == "c2" and [o["optionId"] for o in options] == ["allow_once", "allow_always", "reject_once"]
    assert b.decision({"outcome": "selected", "optionId": "allow_once"}, req) == Decision.approve()
    assert b.decision({"outcome": "selected", "optionId": "allow_always"}, req) == Decision.approve(always="execute:pytest *")
    assert b.decision({"outcome": "selected", "optionId": "reject_once"}, req).type == "reject"
    assert b.decision({"outcome": "cancelled"}, req).type == "reject"


def test_stop_reasons_and_prompt_input():
    b = AcpBridge()
    assert b.stop_reason([TurnDone(Usage(), None, "end")]) == "end_turn"
    assert b.stop_reason([TurnDone(Usage(), None, "max_tokens")]) == "max_tokens"
    assert b.stop_reason([Cancelled()]) == "cancelled"
    text, images = prompt_to_input(
        [
            {"type": "text", "text": "fix"},
            {"type": "image", "mimeType": "image/png", "data": "AAA"},
            {"type": "resource", "resource": {"uri": "file:///a.py", "text": "x = 1"}},
            {"type": "resource_link", "uri": "file:///b.py", "name": "b.py"},
        ]
    )
    assert text.startswith("fix") and "<attached: file:///a.py>\nx = 1" in text and "file:///b.py" in text
    assert images == [Image("image/png", "AAA")]


async def test_acp_stdio_end_to_end(tmp_path):
    pytest.importorskip("acp")
    proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(sys.executable, FIXTURE, str(tmp_path), stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE, stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE)

    async def send(obj):
        proc.stdin.write((json.dumps(obj) + "\n").encode())
        await proc.stdin.drain()

    async def read():
        return json.loads(await asyncio.wait_for(proc.stdout.readline(), 10))

    await send({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "initialize", "params": {"protocolVersion": 1, "clientCapabilities": {}}})
    assert (await read())["result"]["protocolVersion"] == 1
    await send({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 2, "method": "session/new", "params": {"cwd": str(tmp_path), "mcpServers": []}})
    session_id = (await read())["result"]["sessionId"]
    await send({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 3, "method": "session/prompt", "params": {"sessionId": session_id, "prompt": [{"type": "text", "text": "run it"}]}})
    updates, final = [], None
    while final is None:
        msg = await read()
        if msg.get("method") == "session/request_permission":
            await send({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": msg["id"], "result": {"outcome": {"outcome": "selected", "optionId": "allow_once"}}})
        elif msg.get("method") == "session/update":
            updates.append(msg["params"]["update"])
        elif msg.get("id") == 3:
            final = msg
    kinds = [u["sessionUpdate"] for u in updates]
    assert "tool_call" in kinds and "tool_call_update" in kinds and "agent_message_chunk" in kinds
    assert final["result"]["stopReason"] == "end_turn"
    proc.stdin.close()
    proc.kill()
    await proc.wait()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/luna-core/src/luna_core/acp.py`:

```python
"""Agent Client Protocol (ACP) agent: Luna inside editors (needs the ``acp`` extra)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import inspect
from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

from luna_core import __version__
from luna_core.events import (
    ApprovalRequest,
    Cancelled,
    NeedsApproval,
    Notice,
    TextDelta,
    ThinkingDelta,
    TodosUpdated,
    ToolFinished,
    ToolStarted,
    TurnDone,
    TurnError,
)
from luna_core.messages import Image
from luna_core.policy import Decision
from luna_core.session import EventLog, Session

_KINDS = {"read_file": "read", "ls": "read", "write_file": "edit", "edit_file": "edit", "delete": "delete", "glob": "search", "grep": "search", "execute": "execute", "task": "think"}
_STOPS = {"end": "end_turn", "tool_use": "end_turn", "max_steps": "max_turn_requests", "max_tokens": "max_tokens", "refusal": "refusal"}


def _text(text: str) -> dict:
    return {"type": "text", "text": text}


def prompt_to_input(blocks: list[dict]) -> tuple[str, list[Image]]:
    """ACP prompt content blocks → user text and images."""
    parts: list[str] = []
    images: list[Image] = []
    for block in blocks:
        kind = block.get("type")
        if kind == "text":
            parts.append(block.get("text", ""))
        elif kind == "image":
            images.append(Image(block.get("mimeType", "image/png"), block.get("data", "")))
        elif kind == "resource":
            res = block.get("resource", {})
            parts.append(f"\n<attached: {res.get('uri', '')}>\n{res.get('text', '')}\n</attached>")
        elif kind == "resource_link":
            parts.append(f"\n(see {block.get('uri', '')})")
    return "".join(parts), images


class AcpBridge:
    """Pure mapping between Luna turn events and ACP payloads."""

    def updates(self, event: object) -> list[dict]:
        """``session/update`` payloads for one turn event."""
        if isinstance(event, TextDelta):
            return [{"sessionUpdate": "agent_message_chunk", "content": _text(event.text)}]
        if isinstance(event, ThinkingDelta):
            return [{"sessionUpdate": "agent_thought_chunk", "content": _text(event.text)}]
        if isinstance(event, ToolStarted):
            title = f"{event.name} {event.preview}".strip()
            return [{"sessionUpdate": "tool_call", "toolCallId": event.call_id, "title": title, "kind": _KINDS.get(event.name, "other"), "status": "in_progress", "rawInput": event.args}]
        if isinstance(event, ToolFinished):
            update = {"sessionUpdate": "tool_call_update", "toolCallId": event.call_id, "status": "completed" if event.ok else "failed"}
            if event.detail:
                update["content"] = [{"type": "content", "content": _text(event.detail)}]
            return [update]
        if isinstance(event, TodosUpdated):
            return [{"sessionUpdate": "plan", "entries": [{"content": t["content"], "priority": "medium", "status": t["status"]} for t in event.todos]}]
        if isinstance(event, Notice) and event.level in ("warn", "error"):
            return [{"sessionUpdate": "agent_message_chunk", "content": _text(f"\n_{event.text}_\n")}]
        if isinstance(event, TurnError):
            return [{"sessionUpdate": "agent_message_chunk", "content": _text(f"\n**Error:** {event.message}\n")}]
        return []

    def permission(self, request: ApprovalRequest) -> tuple[dict, list[dict]]:
        """The ``toolCall`` and ``options`` of a permission request."""
        tool_call = {"toolCallId": request.call_id, "title": f"{request.name} {request.description}".strip(), "kind": _KINDS.get(request.name, "other"), "status": "pending", "rawInput": request.args}
        options = [
            {"optionId": "allow_once", "name": "Allow", "kind": "allow_once"},
            {"optionId": "allow_always", "name": f"Always allow ({request.suggested_rule})", "kind": "allow_always"},
            {"optionId": "reject_once", "name": "Reject", "kind": "reject_once"},
        ]
        return tool_call, options

    def decision(self, outcome: dict, request: ApprovalRequest) -> Decision:
        """Translate the client's choice."""
        if outcome.get("outcome") != "selected":
            return Decision.reject("cancelled in the editor")
        option = outcome.get("optionId")
        if option == "allow_once":
            return Decision.approve()
        if option == "allow_always":
            return Decision.approve(always=request.suggested_rule)
        return Decision.reject("rejected in the editor")

    def stop_reason(self, events: list[object]) -> str:
        """ACP stop reason for a finished turn."""
        for event in reversed(events):
            if isinstance(event, Cancelled):
                return "cancelled"
            if isinstance(event, TurnDone):
                return _STOPS.get(event.stop_reason, "end_turn")
        return "end_turn"


def _dump(model: Any) -> dict:
    return model.model_dump(mode="json", by_alias=True, exclude_none=True) if hasattr(model, "model_dump") else dict(model)


class LunaAcpAgent:
    """The ACP agent: one Luna session per ACP session."""

    def __init__(self, make_agent: Callable[[str], Any], log: EventLog) -> None:
        self.make_agent = make_agent
        self.log = log
        self.bridge = AcpBridge()
        self.conn: Any = None
        self._sessions: dict[str, tuple[Any, Session]] = {}

    def on_connect(self, conn: Any) -> None:
        """Keep the client connection (SDK callback)."""
        self.conn = conn

    async def _agent_for(self, cwd: str):
        # ``make_agent`` may be sync or async (the Luna app builds agents asynchronously).
        agent = self.make_agent(cwd)
        return await agent if inspect.isawaitable(agent) else agent

    async def initialize(self, protocol_version: int, client_capabilities: Any = None, **kwargs: Any):
        """Handshake."""
        from acp import schema

        return schema.InitializeResponse.model_validate(
            {
                "protocolVersion": 1,
                "agentCapabilities": {"loadSession": True, "promptCapabilities": {"image": True, "embeddedContext": True}},
                "agentInfo": {"name": "luna", "title": "Luna", "version": __version__},
            }
        )

    async def new_session(self, cwd: str, mcp_servers: list | None = None, **kwargs: Any):
        """Create a Luna session in ``cwd``."""
        from acp import schema

        session = Session.create(self.log, cwd, meta={"kind": "acp"})
        self._sessions[session.id] = (await self._agent_for(cwd), session)
        return schema.NewSessionResponse.model_validate({"sessionId": session.id})

    async def load_session(self, cwd: str, session_id: str, mcp_servers: list | None = None, **kwargs: Any):
        """Reopen a session and replay its transcript as updates."""
        from acp import schema

        session = Session.open(self.log, session_id)
        self._sessions[session_id] = (await self._agent_for(cwd), session)
        for message in session.messages():
            if message.role in ("user", "assistant") and message.text:
                kind = "user_message_chunk" if message.role == "user" else "agent_message_chunk"
                await self._update(session_id, {"sessionUpdate": kind, "content": _text(message.text)})
        return schema.LoadSessionResponse.model_validate({})

    async def _update(self, session_id: str, update: dict) -> None:
        from acp import schema

        note = schema.SessionNotification.model_validate({"sessionId": session_id, "update": update})
        await self.conn.session_update(session_id=session_id, update=note.update)

    async def prompt(self, prompt: list, session_id: str, **kwargs: Any):
        """Run one turn and stream it to the editor."""
        from acp import schema

        agent, session = self._sessions[session_id]
        text, images = prompt_to_input([_dump(b) for b in prompt])
        seen: list[object] = []
        async for event in agent.run(session, text, images=images):
            seen.append(event)
            for update in self.bridge.updates(event):
                await self._update(session_id, update)
            if isinstance(event, NeedsApproval):
                asyncio.create_task(self._ask(session_id, session, event.request))
        return schema.PromptResponse.model_validate({"stopReason": self.bridge.stop_reason(seen)})

    async def _ask(self, session_id: str, session: Session, request: ApprovalRequest) -> None:
        from acp import schema

        tool_call, options = self.bridge.permission(request)
        params = schema.RequestPermissionRequest.model_validate({"sessionId": session_id, "toolCall": tool_call, "options": options})
        try:
            response = await self.conn.request_permission(session_id=session_id, tool_call=params.tool_call, options=params.options)
            outcome = _dump(response).get("outcome", {})
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a broken client answer rejects the call
            outcome = {"outcome": "cancelled"}
        if request.call_id in session.pending_approvals:
            session.decide(request.call_id, self.bridge.decision(outcome, request))

    async def cancel(self, session_id: str, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        """Cancel the running turn."""
        entry = self._sessions.get(session_id)
        if entry:
            entry[1].cancel_turn()


async def run_acp(make_agent: Callable[[str], Any], log: EventLog) -> None:
    """Serve ACP over stdio until the editor closes the pipe."""
    from acp import run_agent

    await run_agent(LunaAcpAgent(make_agent, log))
```

Version note: the method signatures follow `agent-client-protocol` ≥ 0.7 (snake_case keyword parameters, `on_connect`, `run_agent`). If the installed version names a parameter differently (e.g. `capabilities` instead of `client_capabilities`), keep `**kwargs` and rename the explicit parameter to match — record it as a Ruling. The pure `AcpBridge` tests do not depend on the SDK.

## Phase D — App cut-over

Phase D moves the app onto `luna_core` and removes the frameworks. Until Task 33 the app still *imports* deepagents in modules that are not yet ported; each task ports a slice and deletes what it replaced, and the full suite stays green at every commit. Test files that construct LangChain messages are ported in the task that owns their code, with this conversion table:

| Old (LangChain / LangGraph) | New (luna_core) |
|---|---|
| `fake_model(AIMessage(content="x"))` | `FakeProvider([FakeProvider.text("x")])` |
| `AIMessage(content="", tool_calls=[{"name": n, "args": a, "id": i}])` | `FakeProvider.call(n, a, i)` |
| `ToolMessage(content=c, tool_call_id=i, name=n)` | `ToolResult(i, (Text(c),), name=n)` |
| `HumanMessage(content=c)` | `Message.user(c)` |
| `agent.get_state(config).values["messages"]` | `session.messages()` |
| `agent.invoke(...)` / `agent.stream(...)` | `[e async for e in agent.run(session, text)]` |
| `Command(resume={"decisions": [d]})` | `session.decide(call_id, d)` |
| `thread_id` | `session.id` (the wire field keeps the name `thread_id` only where the TUI still uses it — Task 32 renames it to `id`) |

### Task 28: App providers, config and agent builder on the core

**Files:**
- Modify: `luna/config/providers.py` (rewrite), `luna/config/config.py` (new settings, custom providers as presets), `luna/extensions/extension_tools.py`, `luna/extensions/websearch.py`, `luna/extensions/lspnav.py`, `luna/extensions/subagents.py` (thin wrapper), `luna/extensions/mcp.py` (config helpers delegate to core), `luna/core/agent.py` (rewrite), `tests/conftest.py`
- Test: `tests/test_providers.py` (rewrite), `tests/test_agent.py` (rewrite), `tests/test_custom_providers.py`, `tests/test_subagents.py`, `tests/test_websearch.py`, `tests/test_config.py` (additions)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`luna.config.providers`): `LunaConfigError`, `ProviderSpec(key, api, default_model, env_var, base_url=None, options={})` (`.preset()`), `PROVIDERS`, `DEFAULT_PROVIDER = "anthropic"`, `merge_providers(custom)`, `build_provider(provider, model, *, registry=None) -> tuple[Provider, str]` (stored keys applied via `luna.config.credentials.apply_stored_key`; missing key → `LunaConfigError` naming `luna setup`, the env var and `--provider`).
- Produces (`LunaConfig` new fields): `reasoning: str | None` (`[agent] reasoning`), `max_cost: float | None` (`[agent] max_cost`), `max_parallel: int = 4` (`[agent] max_parallel`), `roles: dict[str, str]` (`[model] fast/plan/vision/summarize`; a value is `"model"` or `"provider:model"`; `fast_model` stays as an alias of `roles["fast"]`), `sandbox: str = "local"`, `sandbox_image: str = "python:3.12-slim"`, `sandbox_network: str = "none"` (`[sandbox] kind/image/network`), `hooks: list[dict]` (`[[hooks]]`). `model_kwargs` and `extra_model_kwargs` are removed (no LangChain kwargs any more); `[agent] extra` is ignored with a one-time warning from `load_config`'s caller.
- Produces (`luna.core.agent`): `EXTENSION_APPROVALS`, `AgentBundle(agent, mcp)` with `async aclose()`, `async build_agent(config, *, provider=None, on_warn=print, plan_flag=None, hooks=None, background=None) -> AgentBundle`, `async describe_capabilities(config) -> dict`, `skill_dirs(workdir) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]`.
- Extension tools become `luna_core` tools: `manage_mcp`, `manage_skills`, `web_search` (core `@tool`, blocking work in `asyncio.to_thread`); `remember`, `save_skill`, `skill` come from the core (Task 20). `manage_mcp` / `manage_skills` emit `ReloadRequested` through `ctx.emit` when they changed something (replaces the old "Run /reload" text sniffing). LSP tools keep their names and parameters.

```python
from luna_core.providers.openai_chat import OpenAIChat
```

```python
from pathlib import Path

from luna.config.config import LunaConfig
from luna.core.agent import EXTENSION_APPROVALS, build_agent
from luna_core.events import NeedsApproval, TurnDone
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider
from luna_core.session import EventLog, Session


async def test_agent_has_luna_tools_prompt_and_policy(tmp_path, fake_provider):
    (tmp_path / "AGENTS.md").write_text("Use uv.")
    bundle = await build_agent(LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), provider="anthropic", model="m"), provider=fake_provider())
    try:
        names = set(bundle.agent.tools)
        assert {"ls", "read_file", "write_file", "edit_file", "delete", "glob", "grep", "execute", "write_todos", "task", "task_status", "task_cancel", "remember", "save_skill", "skill", "manage_mcp", "manage_skills", "web_search"} <= names
        log = EventLog(tmp_path / "s.db")
        prompt = bundle.agent.system_prompt(Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False))
        assert "You are Luna" in prompt and "Use uv." in prompt
        assert {"manage_mcp", "manage_skills", "remember", "save_skill"} <= EXTENSION_APPROVALS
        assert bundle.agent.policy.workspace.root == Path(tmp_path).resolve()
    finally:
        await bundle.aclose()


async def test_write_asks_for_approval_unless_yolo(tmp_path):
    script = [FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "x"}), FakeProvider.text("done")]
    bundle = await build_agent(LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), model="m"), provider=FakeProvider(list(script)))
    log = EventLog(tmp_path / "s.db")
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    events = []
    async for ev in bundle.agent.run(session, "go"):
        events.append(ev)
        if isinstance(ev, NeedsApproval):
            session.decide(ev.request.call_id, {"type": "approve"})
    assert any(isinstance(e, NeedsApproval) for e in events) and (tmp_path / "a.txt").read_text() == "x"
    yolo = await build_agent(LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), model="m", yolo=True), provider=FakeProvider(list(script)))
    (tmp_path / "a.txt").unlink()
    events = [e async for e in yolo.agent.run(Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False), "go")]
    assert not any(isinstance(e, NeedsApproval) for e in events) and isinstance(events[-1], TurnDone)
    await bundle.aclose()
    await yolo.aclose()


async def test_plan_flag_blocks_writes(tmp_path):
    flag = {"on": True}
    script = [FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "x"}), FakeProvider.text("ok")]
    bundle = await build_agent(LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), model="m", yolo=True), provider=FakeProvider(script), plan_flag=lambda: flag["on"])
    log = EventLog(tmp_path / "s.db")
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    [e async for e in bundle.agent.run(session, "go")]
    assert not (tmp_path / "a.txt").exists() and "plan mode" in session.messages()[2].results[0].text
    await bundle.aclose()


async def test_roles_and_command_hooks_from_config(tmp_path):
    cfg = LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), model="big", roles={"fast": "small", "summarize": "small"}, hooks=[{"event": "pre_tool", "match": "execute", "command": "exit 2"}], yolo=True)
    script = [FakeProvider.call("execute", {"command": "echo hi"}), FakeProvider.text("ok")]
    bundle = await build_agent(cfg, provider=FakeProvider(script))
    assert bundle.agent.roles.fast == "small"
    log = EventLog(tmp_path / "s.db")
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    [e async for e in bundle.agent.run(session, "go")]
    assert session.messages()[2].results[0].is_error
    await bundle.aclose()
```

`luna/config/providers.py` (whole file):

```python
"""Provider registry for the app: luna-core presets plus user custom providers."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from luna_core.providers import PRESETS, Preset, resolve
from luna_core.providers.base import Provider


class LunaConfigError(Exception):
    """Raised for unrecoverable configuration problems (bad provider, missing key)."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderSpec:
    """A provider the app can use: wire API, default model, key variable, endpoint."""

    key: str
    api: str
    default_model: str
    env_var: str | None
    base_url: str | None = None
    options: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    def preset(self) -> Preset:
        """The core preset for this spec."""
        return Preset(self.key, self.api, self.default_model, self.env_var, self.base_url, dict(self.options))


PROVIDERS: dict[str, ProviderSpec] = {k: ProviderSpec(p.key, p.api, p.default_model, p.env_var, p.base_url, dict(p.options)) for k, p in PRESETS.items()}
DEFAULT_PROVIDER = "anthropic"


def merge_providers(custom: dict[str, ProviderSpec]) -> dict[str, ProviderSpec]:
    """Custom providers plus built-ins; a custom entry never shadows a built-in name."""
    merged = dict(custom)
    merged.update(PROVIDERS)
    return merged


def build_provider(provider: str, model: str | None, *, registry: dict[str, ProviderSpec] | None = None) -> tuple[Provider, str]:
    """The core adapter and model id for ``provider`` (stored keys applied)."""
    reg = PROVIDERS if registry is None else registry
    spec = reg.get(provider)
    if spec is None:
        raise LunaConfigError(f"Unknown provider {provider!r}. Choose one of: {', '.join(sorted(reg))}.")
    if spec.env_var and not os.environ.get(spec.env_var):
        from luna.config.credentials import apply_stored_key

        apply_stored_key(provider, spec.env_var)
    if spec.env_var and not os.environ.get(spec.env_var):
        raise LunaConfigError(
            f"No API key for {spec.key}. Run 'luna setup' to configure one, export {spec.env_var}, or pick another provider with --provider."
        )
    custom = {provider: spec.preset()} if provider not in PRESETS else None
    try:
        return resolve(provider, model=model, custom=custom)
    except Exception as exc:  # LunaCoreError from the core (e.g. YANDEX_FOLDER_ID)
        raise LunaConfigError(str(exc)) from exc
```

Check `luna/config/credentials.py`: `apply_stored_key` must set `os.environ[env_var]` from `get_api_key(provider)`; the stored-key test patches `get_api_key`, so `apply_stored_key` has to look it up through the module attribute (`credentials.get_api_key(...)`), which it already does if it calls the name at call time. If it imported the function into a local alias, change it to the module-level call.

`luna/extensions/websearch.py`: keep the three `_search_*` backends and `_format_results`; replace the LangChain tool with

```python
import asyncio

from luna_core.tools.base import tool


def _search(query: str, max_results: int) -> str:
    max_results = max(1, min(int(max_results), 10))
    errors: list[str] = []
    providers = (("tavily", _search_tavily), ("brave", _search_brave), ("duckduckgo", _search_ddgs))
    for name, backend in providers:
        try:
            results = backend(query, max_results)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - try the next backend instead of failing the turn
            errors.append(f"{name}: {exc}")
            continue
        if results is None:
            continue
        return _format_results(query, name, results)
    if errors:
        return "Web search failed on every configured backend: " + "; ".join(errors)
    return (
        "No web search backend is available: set TAVILY_API_KEY or BRAVE_API_KEY, "
        'or run `pip install ddgs` (or `pip install "luna-simple[websearch]"`) for a free, keyless fallback.'
    )


@tool()
async def web_search(query: str, max_results: int = 5) -> str:
    """Search the web: title, URL and snippet of the top results.

    Args:
        query: What to search for.
        max_results: 1-10 results.
    """
    return await asyncio.to_thread(_search, query, max_results)
```

(the comment about building `providers` per call stays — tests monkeypatch the backends).

`luna/extensions/extension_tools.py` (whole file):

```python
"""App tools the Luna agent uses to extend itself (approval-gated)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from luna_core.events import ReloadRequested
from luna_core.tools.base import ToolContext, ToolOutput, tool

from luna.config.providers import LunaConfigError
from luna.extensions import mcp, skills
from luna.extensions.registry import known_mcp, known_skills, resolve_mcp
from luna.extensions.websearch import web_search


@tool(mutating=True)
async def manage_mcp(
    ctx: ToolContext,
    action: Literal["add", "remove", "list"],
    name: str | None = None,
    command: str | None = None,
    command_args: list[str] | None = None,
) -> ToolOutput:
    """Add, remove or list MCP servers (add: a registry name, or command + command_args).

    Args:
        action: add, remove or list.
        name: Server name.
        command: Executable for a custom stdio server.
        command_args: Its arguments.
    """
    root = str(ctx.workspace.root)
    if action == "list":
        configured = ", ".join(mcp.load_mcp_config(root)) or "(none configured)"
        return ToolOutput.text(f"configured: {configured}\nknown: {', '.join(known_mcp())}")
    if not name:
        return ToolOutput.error("name is required for add/remove")
    if action == "remove":
        removed = await asyncio.to_thread(mcp.remove_server, name, workdir=root)
        if removed:
            ctx.emit(ReloadRequested())
        return ToolOutput.text(f"removed {name!r}" if removed else f"{name!r} was not configured")
    try:
        spec = {"command": command, "args": command_args or []} if command else resolve_mcp(name)
    except LunaConfigError as exc:
        return ToolOutput.error(str(exc))
    await asyncio.to_thread(mcp.add_server, name, spec, workdir=root)
    ctx.emit(ReloadRequested())
    return ToolOutput.text(f"added MCP server {name!r}; it is loaded automatically after this turn")


@tool(mutating=True)
async def manage_skills(ctx: ToolContext, action: Literal["add", "remove", "list"], name: str | None = None, source: str | None = None) -> ToolOutput:
    """Add, remove or list skills (add: a registry name, owner/repo or owner/repo/subdir).

    Args:
        action: add, remove or list.
        name: Skill name.
        source: Where to install it from.
    """
    root = str(ctx.workspace.root)
    if action == "list":
        installed = ", ".join(f"{s} ({scope})" for scope, s, _ in skills.list_skills(root))
        return ToolOutput.text(f"installed: {installed or '(none)'}\nknown: {', '.join(known_skills())}")
    if action == "remove":
        if name and await asyncio.to_thread(skills.remove, name, workdir=root):
            ctx.emit(ReloadRequested())
            return ToolOutput.text(f"removed {name!r}")
        return ToolOutput.error(f"{name!r} is not installed")
    target = source or name
    if not target:
        return ToolOutput.error("source (or name) is required for add")
    try:
        message = await asyncio.to_thread(skills.install, target, name=name if source else None, workdir=root)
    except LunaConfigError as exc:
        return ToolOutput.error(str(exc))
    ctx.emit(ReloadRequested())
    return ToolOutput.text(message)


EXTENSION_TOOLS = [manage_mcp, manage_skills, web_search]
EXTENSION_APPROVALS = frozenset({"manage_mcp", "manage_skills", "remember", "save_skill"})
```

`luna/extensions/skills.py`: keep `skills_dirs`, `existing_skill_dirs`, `install`, `remove`, `list_skills` (CLI and `/skills` use them) but implement them on `luna_core.skills`: `_frontmatter` → `luna_core.skills.parse_frontmatter(text)[0]`; `install(...)` → `luna_core.skills.install(source, dest, name=name, run=run, resolve=lambda n: resolve_skill(n, env=env))` with `SkillError` re-raised as `LunaConfigError`; `save(...)` → `luna_core.skills.save(...)`. The `install` status line keeps the suffix `". Run /reload to activate."` for the CLI.

`luna/extensions/mcp.py`: keep `mcp_files`, `load_mcp_config`, `add_server`, `remove_server`, `MCP_AVAILABLE` (now `importlib.util.find_spec("mcp") is not None`) as wrappers over `luna_core.mcp` (`load_config(mcp_files(workdir, env=env), environ)`, etc.); delete `to_connections` and `load_mcp_tools` (replaced by `MCPManager`). `luna mcp test <name>` (CLI) uses `MCPManager({name: spec}).start()` + `close()`.

`luna/extensions/subagents.py` (whole file):

```python
"""Subagent definitions for the app: user + project subagents.toml over the core built-ins."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from luna_core.subagents import SubagentConfigError, SubagentSpec, load_subagents as _load

from luna.config.config import config_dir
from luna.config.providers import LunaConfigError


def subagent_files(workdir: str = ".", *, env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> list[Path]:
    """User file first, then the project's ``.luna/subagents.toml``."""
    return [config_dir(env) / "subagents.toml", Path(workdir) / ".luna" / "subagents.toml"]


def load_subagents(workdir: str = ".", *, env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None, on_warn: Callable[[str], None] | None = None) -> dict[str, SubagentSpec]:
    """All subagents (raises ``LunaConfigError`` for an invalid definition)."""
    try:
        return _load(subagent_files(workdir, env=env), on_warn=on_warn)
    except SubagentConfigError as exc:
        raise LunaConfigError(str(exc)) from exc


def subagent_summaries(workdir: str = ".", *, env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """``(name, description)`` for every available subagent."""
    return [(s.name, s.description) for s in load_subagents(workdir, env=env).values()]
```

`luna/core/agent.py` (whole file):

```python
"""Build the Luna agent on luna-core from a resolved LunaConfig."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from luna_core import skills as core_skills
from luna_core.background import BackgroundTasks
from luna_core.compaction import Compactor
from luna_core.context import build_system_prompt
from luna_core.hooks import Hooks, load_command_hooks
from luna_core.loop import Agent
from luna_core.mcp import MCPManager
from luna_core.memory import remember
from luna_core.policy import DEFAULT_APPROVAL, Policy, Rules, append_rule
from luna_core.roles import ModelRoles
from luna_core.sandbox import DockerSandbox, LocalSandbox
from luna_core.subagents import SubagentRunner, task_tool
from luna_core.tools import builtin_tools
from luna_core.tools.tasks import task_cancel_tool, task_status_tool
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace

from luna.config.config import LunaConfig, config_dir
from luna.config.prompts import LUNA_SYSTEM_PROMPT
from luna.config.providers import build_provider, merge_providers
from luna.extensions import lspnav
from luna.extensions import mcp as mcp_mod
from luna.extensions.extension_tools import EXTENSION_APPROVALS, EXTENSION_TOOLS
from luna.extensions.subagents import load_subagents

__all__ = ["EXTENSION_APPROVALS", "AgentBundle", "build_agent", "describe_capabilities", "skill_dirs"]


@dataclass
class AgentBundle:
    """An agent plus the resources that must be closed with it."""

    agent: Agent
    mcp: MCPManager | None = None

    async def aclose(self) -> None:
        """Stop MCP servers and the sandbox."""
        if self.mcp is not None:
            await self.mcp.close()
        await self.agent.sandbox.close()


def skill_dirs(workdir: str | Path) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
    """User skills first, then the project's (a project skill overrides a user one)."""
    return [("user", config_dir() / "skills"), ("project", Path(workdir) / ".luna" / "skills")]


def _roles(config: LunaConfig, registry: dict, provider, model: str) -> ModelRoles:
    values: dict = {}
    for role, target in config.roles.items():
        name, sep, rest = target.partition(":")
        if sep and name in registry:
            values[role] = build_provider(name, rest or None, registry=registry)
        else:
            values[role] = target
    return ModelRoles(**values)


async def build_agent(
    config: LunaConfig,
    *,
    provider=None,
    on_warn: Callable[[str], None] = print,
    plan_flag: Callable[[], bool] | None = None,
    hooks: Hooks | None = None,
    background: BackgroundTasks | None = None,
) -> AgentBundle:
    """Build a Luna agent.

    ``provider`` injects an adapter (tests pass a ``FakeProvider``); the
    model then defaults to ``config.model or "fake-model"``.
    """
    workdir = Path(config.workdir).resolve()
    workspace = Workspace(workdir)
    registry = merge_providers(config.custom_providers)
    if provider is None:
        provider, model = build_provider(config.provider, config.model, registry=registry)
    else:
        model = config.model or "fake-model"
    if config.ignored_extra:
        on_warn("[agent] extra is no longer used (it held LangChain model kwargs); use temperature, max_tokens and reasoning")
    project_rules = workdir / ".luna" / "permissions.toml"
    policy = Policy(
        Rules.load([config_dir() / "config.toml", project_rules]),
        approval_tools=DEFAULT_APPROVAL | EXTENSION_APPROVALS,
        yolo=config.yolo,
        plan_mode=plan_flag or False,
        workspace=workspace,
        persist_rule=lambda rule: append_rule(project_rules, rule),
    )
    tools = [
        *builtin_tools(),
        remember,
        core_skills.skill_tool(lambda: skill_dirs(workdir)),
        core_skills.save_skill_tool(lambda project: skill_dirs(workdir)[1 if project else 0][1]),
        *EXTENSION_TOOLS,
    ]
    language = config.language or lspnav.detect_language(str(workdir))
    if language and lspnav.available():
        tools += lspnav.make_tools(str(workdir), language)
    elif language:
        on_warn(f"a '{language}' project was detected but the 'lsp' extra (multilspy) is not installed — goto_definition/find_references/hover/symbol_range are unavailable")
    servers = mcp_mod.load_mcp_config(str(workdir))
    manager = None
    if servers:
        manager = MCPManager(servers, on_warn=on_warn)
        tools += await manager.start()
    runner = SubagentRunner(load_subagents(str(workdir), on_warn=on_warn), max_parallel=config.max_parallel)
    tools += [task_tool(runner), task_status_tool(), task_cancel_tool()]
    all_hooks = Hooks()
    if hooks is not None:
        all_hooks.extend(hooks)
    all_hooks.extend(load_command_hooks(config.hooks, workdir))
    if config.sandbox == "docker":
        sandbox = DockerSandbox(workdir, image=config.sandbox_image, network=config.sandbox_network)
    else:
        sandbox = LocalSandbox()
    roles = _roles(config, registry, provider, model)
    summarize_provider, summarize_model = roles.get("summarize", provider, model)

    def system(session) -> str:
        return build_system_prompt(LUNA_SYSTEM_PROMPT, root=workdir, skills=core_skills.discover(skill_dirs(workdir)))

    agent = Agent(
        provider,
        model,
        tools=tools,
        system=system,
        policy=policy,
        workspace=workspace,
        sandbox=sandbox,
        hooks=all_hooks,
        max_tokens=config.max_tokens,
        temperature=config.temperature,
        reasoning=config.reasoning,
        max_cost=config.max_cost,
        compactor=Compactor(provider=summarize_provider, model=summarize_model),
        services={"background": background or BackgroundTasks(config.max_parallel)},
        model_overrides=config.pricing or None,
        roles=roles,
    )
    return AgentBundle(agent, manager)


async def describe_capabilities(config: LunaConfig) -> dict:
    """What ``build_agent`` would wire in right now (for ``/reload``)."""
    from luna.extensions import skills as skills_mod

    workdir = str(Path(config.workdir).resolve())
    return {
        "mcp": list(mcp_mod.load_mcp_config(workdir)),
        "skills": [name for _, name, _ in skills_mod.list_skills(workdir)],
        "subagents": list(load_subagents(workdir)),
    }
```

`config.pricing` uses the same per-model table shape as the core catalogue (`window`, `input`, `output`, `cache_read`, …): the app's old `models.toml` keys (`input`/`output` per million) already match; if a key differs (e.g. `context` instead of `window`), rename it in `_apply_toml` when reading `[model.pricing]` and note it in the changelog.

---

### Task 29: Turn pipeline as hooks; REPL and `-p` on the core

**Files:**
- Modify: `luna/turn/engine.py` (rewrite), `luna/core/session.py` (rewrite), `luna/core/session_state.py` (no usage table; state on the event log), `luna/repl/commands.py` (async dispatch over core sessions), `luna/ui/approve.py` (`from luna_core.policy import suggest_rule`; calls become `suggest_rule(action, args)`)
- Create: `luna/core/store.py`
- Delete: `luna/core/persistence.py`, `luna/core/turn_events.py`, `luna/core/toolguard.py`, `luna/turn/anchor.py`, `luna/turn/undo.py` (moved to the core), `luna/turn/memory.py` (core `memory`), `luna/turn/context.py` (core `context`; keep a 3-line re-export module only if non-test code still imports it — grep first)
- Test: `tests/test_engine.py` (new), `tests/test_repl_flow.py`, `tests/test_session.py`, `tests/test_end_to_end.py` (ported), delete `tests/test_turn_events.py`, `tests/test_persistence.py`, `tests/test_undo.py`, `tests/test_anchor.py` (their behaviour is covered by core tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`luna.core.store`): `db_path(env=None) -> Path` (`<config_dir>/sessions.db`), `open_log(env=None) -> EventLog` (one per process; on first open drops the legacy LangGraph/`luna_sessions` tables: `checkpoints`, `writes`, `checkpoint_blobs`, `checkpoint_writes`, `luna_sessions`), `make_title(text) -> str` (moved here).
- Produces (`luna.turn.engine`): `Notice` (unchanged), `MUTATING`, `delegate_line(line, names)`, `PreparedTurn(text, images, title_line)`, `prepare_turn(state, line, *, workdir, subagent_names) -> PreparedTurn`, `make_hooks(state_fn, cfg_fn) -> Hooks` (the format/diagnose/verify pipeline as `session_start`/`post_tool`/`turn_end` hooks), `format_and_diagnose(cfg, before)`, `verify_step(cfg)`, `fixup_prompt(cfg, tail)`.
- `SessionState` keeps `provider, model, plan, pinned, pending_diagnostics` and gains `dirty_before: list[str]`, `mutated: bool`, `fixup_turn: str | None`; the `usage` column goes (usage and cost come from `Session.usage()/cost()`); `SessionStateStore` keeps its table (`luna_session_state`) minus `usage`.
- Produces (`luna.core.session`): `async run_repl(bundle, *, console, input_fn, rebuild, log, session, config, plan_state) -> int`, `async run_once(bundle, prompt, *, console, log, session=None, cfg, input_fn=input, output_format="text") -> str`, `render_turn(agent, session, text, *, console, input_fn, images=()) -> TurnSummary` (the console renderer shared by both).

Pipeline semantics (unchanged for the user, now hooks): the turn's text is built by `prepare_turn` (carried-over diagnostics → pinned files → `@mentions`, images from mentions), `dirty_before` is captured before the turn; `post_tool` marks the turn mutating when one of `MUTATING` succeeded; `turn_end` on a mutating turn runs format + diagnose (notices; diagnostics carried to the next turn) and, with `verify_command`, the verify step: failure on the first `turn_end` of a turn → `HookResult(block=True, message=fixup_prompt)` (the core loop feeds it back, one fix-up), failure after the fix-up → notice "verify still failing after 1 retry"; success → notice "✓ verify ok".

```python
import subprocess

from luna.config.config import LunaConfig
from luna.core.session_state import SessionState
from luna.turn import engine
from luna_core.events import Notice, TurnDone
from luna_core.loop import Agent
from luna_core.policy import Policy
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider
from luna_core.session import EventLog, Session
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace


def git(root, *args):
    subprocess.run(["git", "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t", *args], cwd=root, check=True, capture_output=True)


def test_prepare_turn_builds_text_and_images(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "a.py").write_text("x = 1\n")
    state = SessionState("s", str(tmp_path))
    state.pending_diagnostics = "E1 bad"
    state.pinned.add("a.py")
    prepared = engine.prepare_turn(state, "@researcher look at @a.py", workdir=str(tmp_path), subagent_names={"researcher"})
    assert prepared.text.startswith("<diagnostics>\nE1 bad") and "<attached: a.py>" in prepared.text
    assert "Delegate this to the 'researcher' subagent" in prepared.text and state.pending_diagnostics == ""
    assert prepared.images == [] and prepared.title_line.startswith("@researcher")


async def test_verify_failure_gets_exactly_one_fixup_turn(tmp_path):
    git(tmp_path, "init", "-q")
    (tmp_path / "ok.flag").write_text("")
    git(tmp_path, "add", ".")
    git(tmp_path, "commit", "-qm", "init")
    cfg = LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), verify_command="test -f fixed.flag", format_command="", diagnose_command="")
    state = SessionState("s", str(tmp_path))
    hooks = engine.make_hooks(lambda: state, lambda: cfg)
    script = [
        FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "x.txt", "content": "1"}),
        FakeProvider.text("done"),
        FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "fixed.flag", "content": ""}),
        FakeProvider.text("fixed"),
    ]
    ws = Workspace(tmp_path)
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider(script), "m", workspace=ws, policy=Policy(workspace=ws, yolo=True), hooks=hooks)
    log = EventLog(tmp_path / ".luna" / "s.db")
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False)
    engine.before_turn(state)
    events = [e async for e in agent.run(session, "make x")]
    texts = [m.text for m in session.messages() if m.role == "user"]
    assert any("`test -f fixed.flag` failed" in t for t in texts)
    assert any(isinstance(e, Notice) and "verify ok" in e.text for e in events) and isinstance(events[-1], TurnDone)


async def test_verify_still_failing_is_a_notice_not_a_loop(tmp_path):
    cfg = LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), verify_command="false", format_command="", diagnose_command="")
    state = SessionState("s", str(tmp_path))
    script = [FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "x.txt", "content": "1"}), FakeProvider.text("done"), FakeProvider.text("tried")]
    ws = Workspace(tmp_path)
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider(script), "m", workspace=ws, policy=Policy(workspace=ws, yolo=True), hooks=engine.make_hooks(lambda: state, lambda: cfg))
    log = EventLog(tmp_path / "s.db")
    engine.before_turn(state)
    events = [e async for e in agent.run(Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False), "go")]
    assert any(isinstance(e, Notice) and "still failing" in e.text for e in events) and isinstance(events[-1], TurnDone)


async def test_read_only_turn_skips_the_pipeline(tmp_path):
    cfg = LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), verify_command="false")
    state = SessionState("s", str(tmp_path))
    ws = Workspace(tmp_path)
    agent = Agent(FakeProvider([FakeProvider.call("ls", {}), FakeProvider.text("ok")]), "m", workspace=ws, hooks=engine.make_hooks(lambda: state, lambda: cfg))
    log = EventLog(tmp_path / "s.db")
    engine.before_turn(state)
    events = [e async for e in agent.run(Session.create(log, str(tmp_path), undo=False), "look")]
    assert not any(isinstance(e, Notice) and "verify" in e.text for e in events)
```

`tests/test_repl_flow.py` (rewrite the existing scenarios on the core; keep their intent — streaming text, approve/reject/always prompts, `/command` dispatch, `-p` JSON output). Representative cases:

```python
import io
import json

from rich.console import Console

from luna.config.config import LunaConfig
from luna.core.agent import build_agent
from luna.core.session import run_once, run_repl
from luna.core.store import open_log
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider
from luna_core.session import Session


def console():
    return Console(file=io.StringIO(), force_terminal=False, width=120)


async def test_run_once_text_and_json(tmp_path, capsys):
    cfg = LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), model="m", format_command="", diagnose_command="")
    bundle = await build_agent(cfg, provider=FakeProvider([FakeProvider.text("hello"), FakeProvider.text("again")]))
    log = open_log()
    assert await run_once(bundle, "hi", console=console(), log=log, cfg=cfg) == "hello"
    await run_once(bundle, "hi", console=console(), log=log, cfg=cfg, output_format="json")
    payload = json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out.strip().splitlines()[-1])
    assert payload["text"] == "again" and payload["session_id"] and payload["usage"]["input"] == 10


async def test_repl_approval_reject_with_reason(tmp_path):
    cfg = LunaConfig(workdir=str(tmp_path), model="m", format_command="", diagnose_command="")
    script = [FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "x"}), FakeProvider.text("ok")]
    bundle = await build_agent(cfg, provider=FakeProvider(script))
    answers = iter(["write it", "n", "not now", "/exit"])
    log = open_log()
    session = Session.create(log, str(tmp_path))
    code = await run_repl(bundle, console=console(), input_fn=lambda prompt: next(answers), rebuild=None, log=log, session=session, config=cfg, plan_state=[False])
    assert code == 0 and not (tmp_path / "a.txt").exists()
    assert "rejected by the user: not now" in session.messages()[2].results[0].text
```

`luna/core/store.py`:

```python
"""The app's session store: the core event log in ``<config_dir>/sessions.db``."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from luna_core.session import EventLog

from luna.config.config import config_dir

_TITLE_MAX = 72
_WS = re.compile(r"\s+")
_LEGACY = ("checkpoints", "writes", "checkpoint_blobs", "checkpoint_writes", "checkpoint_migrations", "luna_sessions")
_LOGS: dict[str, EventLog] = {}


def db_path(env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> Path:
    """``<config_dir>/sessions.db`` (directory created)."""
    path = config_dir(env) / "sessions.db"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return path


def _drop_legacy(path: Path) -> None:
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as conn:
        for table in _LEGACY:
            conn.execute(f'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "{table}"')


def open_log(env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> EventLog:
    """The process-wide event log (legacy LangGraph tables are dropped on first open)."""
    path = db_path(env)
    key = str(path)
    if key not in _LOGS:
        _drop_legacy(path)
        _LOGS[key] = EventLog(path)
    return _LOGS[key]


def make_title(text: str) -> str:
    """Collapse whitespace and truncate for the session list."""
    clean = _WS.sub(" ", text).strip()
    return clean if len(clean) <= _TITLE_MAX else clean[:_TITLE_MAX] + "…"
```

`tests/conftest.py` additionally clears `luna.core.store._LOGS` in the `isolated_config_home` fixture (each test gets its own config home and so its own database).

`luna/turn/engine.py` (whole file):

```python
"""The app's turn pipeline: message preparation and the format/diagnose/verify hooks.

Streaming lives in the surfaces (REPL renderer, server SSE); everything
around a turn is here once and runs as luna-core hooks, so the REPL, the
server and background runs behave the same.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import re
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from luna_core.context import expand_mentions, render_pinned
from luna_core.hooks import HookResult, Hooks
from luna_core.messages import Image
from luna_core.workspace import Workspace

from luna.turn import diagnose, fmt, gitinfo
from luna.turn.verify import run_verify

#: Tools whose successful use marks a turn as mutating (format/diagnose/verify run after it).
MUTATING = frozenset({"write_file", "edit_file", "delete", "execute"})
AT_AGENT_RE = re.compile(r"^@([\w-]+)\s+(.+)$", re.DOTALL)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Notice:
    """One short status line: ``level`` is dim/info/ok/warn/error."""

    level: str
    text: str


@dataclass
class PreparedTurn:
    """What :func:`prepare_turn` hands to the surface."""

    text: str
    images: list[Image] = field(default_factory=list)
    title_line: str = ""


def delegate_line(line: str, subagent_names: set[str]) -> str:
    """Rewrite ``@name text`` into a delegation request when ``name`` is a subagent."""
    match = AT_AGENT_RE.match(line)
    if match and match.group(1) in subagent_names:
        return f"Delegate this to the '{match.group(1)}' subagent using the task tool: {match.group(2)}"
    return line


def prepare_turn(state, line: str, *, workdir: str, subagent_names: set[str]) -> PreparedTurn:
    """Build the user message: carried diagnostics, pinned files, ``@mentions``."""
    title = line
    line = delegate_line(line, subagent_names)
    diag = f"<diagnostics>\n{state.pending_diagnostics}\n</diagnostics>\n\n" if state.pending_diagnostics else ""
    state.pending_diagnostics = ""
    pinned = render_pinned(state.pinned, workdir)
    text, images = expand_mentions(line, Workspace(workdir))
    return PreparedTurn(diag + (pinned + "\n\n" if pinned else "") + text, images, title)


def before_turn(state) -> None:
    """Reset per-turn pipeline state and capture which files were already dirty."""
    state.mutated = False
    state.fixup_turn = None
    state.dirty_before = gitinfo.dirty_paths(state.workdir) if gitinfo.is_git_repo(state.workdir) else []


def format_and_diagnose(cfg, before: list[str] | None) -> tuple[list[Notice], str]:
    """Format, then diagnose, the files this turn changed. Returns (notices, diagnostics)."""
    notices: list[Notice] = []
    before_set = set(before) if before is not None else None
    is_repo = gitinfo.is_git_repo(cfg.workdir)

    def touched() -> list[str]:
        if not is_repo:
            return []
        current = gitinfo.dirty_paths(cfg.workdir)
        return current if before_set is None else [p for p in current if p not in before_set]

    changed = touched()
    if is_repo and before_set is not None and not changed:
        return notices, ""
    fmt_cmd = fmt.detect(cfg.workdir) if cfg.format_command == "auto" else cfg.format_command
    if fmt_cmd:
        formatted = fmt.run(fmt_cmd, cfg.workdir, changed)
        if formatted:
            notices.append(Notice("dim", f"⌁ formatted {len(formatted)} file(s)"))
    diag_cmd = diagnose.detect(cfg.workdir) if cfg.diagnose_command == "auto" else cfg.diagnose_command
    if not diag_cmd:
        return notices, ""
    changed = touched()
    if is_repo and before_set is not None and not changed:
        return notices, ""
    text = diagnose.run(diag_cmd, cfg.workdir, changed)
    if text:
        notices.append(Notice("dim", text))
    return notices, text


def verify_step(cfg) -> tuple[bool, str, list[Notice]]:
    """Run the verify command once: (ok, output tail, notices)."""
    ok, tail = run_verify(cfg.verify_command, cfg.workdir)
    if ok:
        return True, "", [Notice("ok", "✓ verify ok")]
    return False, tail, [Notice("warn", f"verify failed\n{tail}")]


def fixup_prompt(cfg, tail: str) -> str:
    """The message for the single automatic fix-up after a failed verify."""
    return f"The verify command `{cfg.verify_command}` failed. Output:\n{tail}\nFix it."


def make_hooks(state_fn: Callable[[], object], cfg_fn: Callable[[], object]) -> Hooks:
    """The format/diagnose/verify pipeline as luna-core hooks for one session."""
    hooks = Hooks()

    async def post_tool(payload: dict) -> HookResult | None:
        if payload.get("ok") and payload.get("tool") in MUTATING:
            state_fn().mutated = True
        return None

    async def turn_end(payload: dict) -> HookResult | None:
        state, cfg = state_fn(), cfg_fn()
        if not getattr(state, "mutated", False):
            return None
        state.mutated = False
        notices, diagnostics = await asyncio.to_thread(format_and_diagnose, cfg, state.dirty_before)
        state.pending_diagnostics = diagnostics
        texts = [n.text for n in notices]
        if not cfg.verify_command:
            return HookResult(notices=tuple(texts))
        ok, tail, verify_notices = await asyncio.to_thread(verify_step, cfg)
        if ok:
            return HookResult(notices=tuple(texts + [n.text for n in verify_notices]))
        if state.fixup_turn == payload.get("turn_id"):
            return HookResult(notices=tuple(texts + [f"⚠ verify still failing after 1 retry\n{tail}"]))
        state.fixup_turn = payload.get("turn_id")
        return HookResult(block=True, message=fixup_prompt(cfg, tail), notices=tuple(texts + [n.text for n in verify_notices]))

    hooks.add("post_tool", post_tool, timeout=10)
    hooks.add("turn_end", turn_end, timeout=900)
    return hooks
```

Hook notices arrive in the stream as `Notice("info", text)`; the levels of the old pipeline (`dim`/`ok`/`warn`) are kept by prefixing: change `Hooks.run` consumers? No — keep the core simple and let the app map: a notice text starting with `⌁` or a diagnostics block renders dim, `✓` renders ok, `⚠`/`verify failed` renders warn. Put that mapping in one helper `luna.turn.engine.notice_level(text) -> str` and use it in the REPL renderer and the server (it replaces the level only for `info` notices that came from hooks):

```python
def notice_level(text: str) -> str:
    """Level for a hook notice by its leading marker (the old pipeline's colours)."""
    if text.startswith("✓"):
        return "ok"
    if text.startswith(("⚠", "verify failed")):
        return "warn"
    if text.startswith("⌁") or "\n" in text:
        return "dim"
    return "info"
```

Also add `test_notice_level` to `tests/test_engine.py` with one assertion per marker.

`luna/core/session_state.py`: remove `usage` from the dataclass, the table schema and `load`/`save`; add the in-memory fields `dirty_before: list[str]`, `mutated: bool = False`, `fixup_turn: str | None = None` (not persisted). Existing databases: `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` keeps an old table with a `usage` column — the `INSERT` names its columns explicitly (`INSERT INTO luna_session_state(thread_id, workdir, provider, model, plan, pinned, pending_diagnostics) VALUES (...)`) so the extra column (with its default) is harmless.

`luna/core/session.py` (whole file):

```python
"""Console surfaces on luna-core: the line REPL and one-shot ``luna -p``."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import json
import os
import signal
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from rich.console import Console

from luna_core.events import (
    Cancelled,
    Compacted,
    NeedsApproval,
    Notice,
    ReloadRequested,
    TextDelta,
    TodosUpdated,
    ToolFinished,
    ToolStarted,
    TurnDone,
    TurnError,
)
from luna_core.policy import Decision
from luna_core.session import Session
from luna_core.usage import Usage

from luna.config.config import LunaConfig
from luna.core.session_state import SessionState, SessionStateStore
from luna.core.store import make_title
from luna.extensions.subagents import subagent_summaries
from luna.repl import usercmd
from luna.repl.commands import CommandContext, dispatch
from luna.turn import engine
from luna.ui.answer import AnswerRenderer
from luna.ui.approve import prompt_decision
from luna.ui.progress import ToolProgress
from luna.ui.theme import PALETTE
from luna.ui.turn import close_turn, open_turn

_NOTICE_STYLE = {"dim": "dim", "ok": "dim", "info": PALETTE["blue"], "warn": "yellow", "error": PALETTE["mauve"]}


@dataclass
class TurnSummary:
    """What a rendered turn produced."""

    text: str = ""
    usage: Usage = field(default_factory=Usage)
    cost: float | None = None
    tools: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    reload: bool = False
    error: str = ""
    cancelled: bool = False


def _request_dict(request) -> dict:
    return {"name": request.name, "action": request.name, "args": request.args, "description": request.description}


async def render_turn(agent, session: Session, text: str, *, console: Console, input_fn: Callable[[str], str], images=()) -> TurnSummary:
    """Run one turn, rendering it to ``console``; Ctrl-C cancels the turn."""
    summary = TurnSummary()
    parts: list[str] = []
    progress = ToolProgress(console)
    answer = AnswerRenderer(console)
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    with contextlib.suppress(NotImplementedError, RuntimeError):
        loop.add_signal_handler(signal.SIGINT, session.cancel_turn)
    open_turn(console)
    try:
        async for event in agent.run(session, text, images=images):
            if isinstance(event, TextDelta):
                parts.append(event.text)
                answer.append(event.text)
            elif isinstance(event, ToolStarted):
                answer.stop()
                progress.start(event.call_id, event.name, event.args)
            elif isinstance(event, ToolFinished):
                summary.tools.add(event.name)
                progress.finish(event.call_id, event.ok, event.detail)
            elif isinstance(event, NeedsApproval):
                progress.pause()
                console.print()
                raw = await asyncio.to_thread(prompt_decision, console, _request_dict(event.request), input_fn=input_fn)
                session.decide(event.request.call_id, Decision.from_dict(raw))
                progress.resume()
            elif isinstance(event, Notice):
                answer.stop()
                level = engine.notice_level(event.text) if event.level == "info" else event.level
                console.print(event.text, style=_NOTICE_STYLE.get(level, ""), markup=False)
            elif isinstance(event, TodosUpdated):
                answer.stop()
                marks = {"completed": "✓", "in_progress": "▸", "pending": "·"}
                console.print("\n".join(f"{marks[t['status']]} {t['content']}" for t in event.todos), style="dim", markup=False)
            elif isinstance(event, Compacted):
                console.print(f"compacted {event.removed} messages into a summary", style="dim")
            elif isinstance(event, ReloadRequested):
                summary.reload = True
            elif isinstance(event, TurnDone):
                summary.usage, summary.cost = event.usage, event.cost
            elif isinstance(event, TurnError):
                summary.error = event.message
                console.print(f"turn failed: {event.message}", style=PALETTE["mauve"], markup=False)
            elif isinstance(event, Cancelled):
                summary.cancelled = True
                console.print("turn cancelled", style=PALETTE["mauve"])
    finally:
        answer.stop()
        progress.close()
        with contextlib.suppress(NotImplementedError, RuntimeError):
            loop.remove_signal_handler(signal.SIGINT)
    close_turn(console)
    summary.text = "".join(parts).strip()
    return summary


async def run_once(bundle, prompt: str, *, console: Console, log, session: Session | None = None, cfg: LunaConfig, input_fn: Callable[[str], str] = input, output_format: str = "text") -> str:
    """Run a single prompt; returns the answer text (``""`` in json mode, which prints JSON)."""
    session = session or Session.create(log, str(bundle.agent.workspace.root), title=make_title(prompt))
    state = SessionState(session.id, session.workdir)
    names = {n for n, _ in subagent_summaries(session.workdir)}
    prepared = engine.prepare_turn(state, prompt, workdir=session.workdir, subagent_names=names)
    engine.before_turn(state)
    quiet = Console(file=open(os.devnull, "w")) if output_format == "json" else console  # noqa: SIM115 - lives for this call
    result = await render_turn(bundle.agent, session, prepared.text, console=quiet, input_fn=input_fn, images=prepared.images)
    if output_format == "json":
        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "text": result.text,
                    "tools_used": sorted(result.tools),
                    "usage": {"input": result.usage.input, "output": result.usage.output, "total": result.usage.total},
                    "cost_usd": result.cost,
                    "session_id": session.id,
                    "error": result.error or None,
                }
            )
        )
        return ""
    return result.text


async def run_repl(
    bundle,
    *,
    console: Console,
    input_fn: Callable[[str], str] = input,
    rebuild: Callable | None = None,
    log,
    session: Session,
    config: LunaConfig,
    plan_state: list | None = None,
    hooks_state: dict | None = None,
) -> int:
    """Interactive line REPL (non-TTY fallback). Returns an exit code.

    ``hooks_state`` is the dict the engine hooks of ``bundle`` read the
    session state from (``engine.make_hooks(lambda: hooks_state["state"], ...)``);
    the REPL keeps it pointing at the current session's state.
    """
    plan_state = plan_state if plan_state is not None else [False]
    store = SessionStateStore()
    state = store.load(session.id, session.workdir)
    hooks_state = hooks_state if hooks_state is not None else {}
    hooks_state["state"] = state
    console.print(f"[{PALETTE['peri']}]Luna is ready. Type /help for commands.[/]\n")
    ctx = CommandContext(console=console, config=config, bundle=bundle, rebuild=rebuild, session=session, log=log, state=state, input_fn=input_fn, user_commands=usercmd.load(session.workdir), plan_state=plan_state)
    names = {n for n, _ in subagent_summaries(session.workdir)}
    while True:
        try:
            line = (await asyncio.to_thread(input_fn, "luna (plan) › " if plan_state[0] else "luna › ")).strip()
        except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
            console.print()
            return 0
        if not line:
            continue
        if line.startswith("/"):
            res = await dispatch(line, ctx)
            if res.exit:
                return 0
            if res.prompt is None:
                bundle, session = ctx.bundle, ctx.session
                hooks_state["state"] = ctx.state
                continue
            line = res.prompt
        prepared = engine.prepare_turn(hooks_state["state"], line, workdir=session.workdir, subagent_names=names)
        engine.before_turn(hooks_state["state"])
        result = await render_turn(bundle.agent, session, prepared.text, console=console, input_fn=input_fn, images=prepared.images)
        store.save(hooks_state["state"])
        if result.usage.total:
            console.print(f"[dim]{result.usage.input} in · {result.usage.output} out" + (f" · ${result.cost:.4f}" if result.cost else "") + "[/]")
        if result.reload and rebuild is not None:
            try:
                await bundle.aclose()
                bundle = ctx.bundle = await rebuild()
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a bad config must not kill the session
                console.print(f"[{PALETTE['mauve']}]auto-reload failed: {exc}[/]")
            else:
                console.print(f"[{PALETTE['blue']}]auto-reloaded — new capabilities are live[/]")
```

The engine hooks are attached when the bundle is built: the caller creates `hooks_state = {}`, builds with `build_agent(config, hooks=engine.make_hooks(lambda: hooks_state["state"], lambda: config), ...)` and passes the same dict to `run_repl(..., hooks_state=hooks_state)` (the CLI does this in Task 33; the `test_repl_flow` cases build the bundle the same way when they exercise verify).

`luna/repl/commands.py`: `CommandContext` fields become `console, config, bundle, rebuild, session, log, state, input_fn, user_commands, plan_state`; `dispatch` becomes `async def dispatch(line, ctx) -> DispatchResult` and calls `await run_line(line, _ReplEnv(ctx))` (Task 31 makes `run_line` async; in this task wrap the still-sync call: `result = run_line(...)` and `if inspect.isawaitable(result): result = await result`). `_ReplEnv` exposes `session`, `agent` (= `ctx.bundle.agent`), `workdir`, `state`, `pinned` (= `ctx.state.pinned`), `config`, `get_plan/set_plan` (plan_state), `rebuild` (async: `ctx.bundle = await ctx.rebuild()`), `switch_model/switch_provider` (update `ctx.config` and rebuild, restoring on failure). `/new` creates `Session.create(ctx.log, workdir)` and sets `ctx.session`; `/resume` and `/sessions` list `ctx.log.list(workdir)`.

### Task 30: Server on the core — every turn runs in the AgentManager

**Files:**
- Modify (rewrite): `luna/server/runtime.py`, `luna/server/turns.py`, `luna/server/sessions.py`, `luna/server/app.py`, `luna/server/run.py` (factories), `luna/server/commands.py` (env over the new runtime; the command logic changes in Task 31)
- Delete: `luna/server/approvals.py` (approval is a JSON call now, in `turns.py`)
- Test: `tests/test_server_turns.py`, `tests/test_server_turn_flow.py`, `tests/test_server_approvals.py`, `tests/test_server_agent_registry.py`, `tests/test_server_sessions.py` (ported), `tests/test_server_background.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AgentManager`, `RunInfo` (Task 24), `to_wire` (Task 4), `build_agent`/`AgentBundle` (Task 28), `engine.prepare_turn/before_turn/make_hooks/notice_level` (Task 29), `open_log`, `make_title` (Task 29), protocol v1 (Task 26).
- Produces:
  - `SessionRuntime(state, session, *, store, build)`: `.id`, `.workdir`, `.session`, `.state`, `.lock`, `async .bundle() -> AgentBundle`, `.config()`, `async .rebuild()`, `async .switch_model(m)`, `async .switch_provider(p)`, `.set_plan(on)`, `.save()`, `.reload_after_turn`, `.reload_requested`
  - `RuntimeRegistry(build, *, log, manager, capacity=8)`: `async .get(session_id, workdir) -> SessionRuntime` (unknown id → session created with that id), `.peek(session_id)`, `async .drop(session_id)`, `.all()`, `async .aclose()`
  - `create_app(*, token, build, trust_check=None, log=None) -> Starlette` where `build: Callable[[SessionRuntime], Awaitable[AgentBundle]]`; `app.state.manager`, `app.state.runtimes`, `app.state.log`
  - `make_session_build(*, provider_factory=None)` in `luna/server/run.py` (tests pass `provider_factory=lambda runtime: FakeProvider([...])`)
- Endpoints: exactly the protocol v1 table (Task 26). History entries keep the TUI's shape: `{"role": "user" | "assistant", "content"}` and `{"role": "tool", "name", "args_preview", "ok", "detail"}`; session rows: `{"id", "title", "created", "updated", "group", "relative_time", "running", "background"}`.
- Every turn is started with `AgentManager.start`; `POST /messages` subscribes to it and streams until `turn_done`/`error`/`cancelled`, so a client that disconnects (TUI closed, network drop) only unsubscribes — the turn finishes, is logged, and its completion runs `after_turn` (state saved, auto-reload). `POST /background` starts the same way without a subscriber. `info`-level notices are re-levelled with `engine.notice_level` before they go out.

```python
"""Helpers for server tests: an app with scripted providers per session."""

import httpx

from luna.server.app import create_app
from luna.server.run import make_session_build
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider


def make_app(scripts: dict[str, list] | None = None, default: list | None = None):
    """scripts: session id -> FakeProvider script (default script otherwise)."""

    def provider_factory(runtime):
        return FakeProvider(list((scripts or {}).get(runtime.id, default or [FakeProvider.text("ok")])))

    return create_app(token="t", build=make_session_build(provider_factory=provider_factory), trust_check=lambda workdir: True)


def client(app):
    return httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://luna", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer t"})


async def sse(resp) -> list[dict]:
    import json

    out = []
    async for line in resp.aiter_lines():
        if line.startswith("data:"):
            out.append(json.loads(line[5:]))
    return out
```

```python
import asyncio

from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider
from server_helpers import client, make_app, sse


async def test_turn_streams_and_approval_is_a_json_call(tmp_path):
    app = make_app(default=[FakeProvider.call("write_file", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "x"}, "c1"), FakeProvider.text("done")])
    async with client(app) as c:
        sid = (await c.post("/sessions", json={"workdir": str(tmp_path)})).json()["id"]
        events = []
        async with c.stream("POST", f"/sessions/{sid}/messages", json={"workdir": str(tmp_path), "content": "write"}) as resp:
            async for line in resp.aiter_lines():
                if not line.startswith("data:"):
                    continue
                import json

                event = json.loads(line[5:])
                events.append(event)
                if event["event"] == "approval_needed":
                    call_id = event["value"]["action_requests"][0]["call_id"]
                    ok = await c.post(f"/sessions/{sid}/approve", json={"call_id": call_id, "decision": {"type": "approve"}})
                    assert ok.json() == {"ok": True}
        assert events[-1]["event"] == "turn_done" and (tmp_path / "a.txt").read_text() == "x"
        history = (await c.get(f"/sessions/{sid}/messages", params={"workdir": str(tmp_path)})).json()["messages"]
        assert [h["role"] for h in history] == ["user", "tool", "assistant"] and history[1]["name"] == "write_file"


async def test_background_session_survives_client_disconnect(tmp_path):
    app = make_app(default=[FakeProvider.call("execute", {"command": "sleep 0.3"}, "c1"), FakeProvider.text("finished")])
    async with client(app) as c:
        sid = (await c.post("/sessions", json={"workdir": str(tmp_path)})).json()["id"]
        runtime = await app.state.runtimes.get(sid, str(tmp_path))
        (await runtime.bundle()).agent.policy.yolo = True
        async with c.stream("POST", f"/sessions/{sid}/messages", json={"workdir": str(tmp_path), "content": "go"}) as resp:
            async for line in resp.aiter_lines():
                if "tool_started" in line:
                    break  # the TUI closes mid-turn
        info = await app.state.manager.wait(sid)
        assert info.status == "done"
        history = (await c.get(f"/sessions/{sid}/messages", params={"workdir": str(tmp_path)})).json()["messages"]
        assert history[-1] == {"role": "assistant", "content": "finished"}
        runs = (await c.get("/agents")).json()["runs"]
        assert runs[0]["session_id"] == sid and runs[0]["status"] == "done"


async def test_background_endpoint_attach_and_cancel(tmp_path):
    app = make_app(default=[FakeProvider.call("execute", {"command": "sleep 30"}, "c1")])
    async with client(app) as c:
        sid = (await c.post("/sessions", json={"workdir": str(tmp_path)})).json()["id"]
        runtime = await app.state.runtimes.get(sid, str(tmp_path))
        (await runtime.bundle()).agent.policy.yolo = True
        info = (await c.post(f"/sessions/{sid}/background", json={"workdir": str(tmp_path), "content": "long"})).json()
        assert info["status"] in ("queued", "running")
        busy = await c.post(f"/sessions/{sid}/messages", json={"workdir": str(tmp_path), "content": "again"})
        assert busy.status_code == 409
        while not any(e["event"] == "tool_started" for e in app.state.manager.events(sid)):
            await asyncio.sleep(0.02)
        assert (await c.post(f"/sessions/{sid}/cancel")).json() == {"cancelled": True}
        async with c.stream("GET", f"/sessions/{sid}/events", params={"after": -1}) as resp:
            events = await sse(resp)
        assert events[0]["event"] == "turn_started" and events[-1]["event"] == "cancelled"
        listed = (await c.get("/sessions", params={"workdir": str(tmp_path)})).json()["sessions"]
        assert listed[0]["id"] == sid and listed[0]["running"] is False


async def test_delete_session(tmp_path):
    app = make_app()
    async with client(app) as c:
        sid = (await c.post("/sessions", json={"workdir": str(tmp_path)})).json()["id"]
        assert (await c.delete(f"/sessions/{sid}")).json() == {"deleted": True}
        assert (await c.get("/sessions", params={"workdir": str(tmp_path)})).json()["sessions"] == []
```
