`luna/server/runtime.py` (whole file):

```python
"""Server-side state of each session: core session, app state, agent bundle."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from collections import OrderedDict
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from pathlib import Path

from luna_core.background import AgentManager, BackgroundTasks
from luna_core.session import EventLog, Session
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse

from luna.config.config import LunaConfig, load_config
from luna.config.providers import LunaConfigError
from luna.core.session_state import SessionState, SessionStateStore


class WorkdirMismatch(Exception):
    """A session id was used with a different project folder than its own."""


class SessionRuntime:
    """Live server-side state of one session."""

    def __init__(self, state: SessionState, session: Session, *, store: SessionStateStore, build: Callable[[SessionRuntime], Awaitable]) -> None:
        self.state = state
        self.session = session
        self._store = store
        self._build = build
        self._bundle = None
        self._bundle_lock = asyncio.Lock()
        self.lock = asyncio.Lock()
        self.background = BackgroundTasks()
        self.reload_after_turn = False
        self.reload_requested = False

    @property
    def id(self) -> str:
        """The session id."""
        return self.session.id

    @property
    def workdir(self) -> str:
        """The session's project folder (absolute)."""
        return self.state.workdir

    async def bundle(self):
        """The session's agent bundle, built on first use."""
        async with self._bundle_lock:
            if self._bundle is None:
                self._bundle = await self._build(self)
            return self._bundle

    def config(self) -> LunaConfig:
        """Project config with this session's provider/model applied."""
        try:
            cfg = load_config({"workdir": self.workdir}, cwd=self.workdir)
        except (LunaConfigError, OSError):
            cfg = LunaConfig(workdir=self.workdir)
        if self.state.provider is not None:
            cfg.provider, cfg.model = self.state.provider, self.state.model
        elif self.state.model is not None:
            cfg.model = self.state.model
        return cfg

    async def rebuild(self) -> None:
        """Rebuild the agent (raises on a bad config; the old one stays then)."""
        async with self._bundle_lock:
            new = await self._build(self)
            old, self._bundle = self._bundle, new
        if old is not None:
            await old.aclose()

    async def _switch(self, provider: str | None, model: str | None) -> None:
        previous = (self.state.provider, self.state.model)
        self.state.provider, self.state.model = provider, model
        try:
            await self.rebuild()
        except Exception:
            self.state.provider, self.state.model = previous
            raise
        self.save()

    async def switch_model(self, model: str) -> None:
        """Use ``model`` for this session only."""
        await self._switch(self.state.provider, model)

    async def switch_provider(self, provider: str) -> None:
        """Use ``provider`` (its default model) for this session only."""
        await self._switch(provider, None)

    def set_plan(self, on: bool) -> None:
        """Toggle plan mode (read by the policy on every tool call)."""
        self.state.plan = on
        self.save()

    def save(self) -> None:
        """Persist the app-level session state."""
        self._store.save(self.state)

    async def after_turn(self) -> None:
        """After a run finished: persist state and auto-reload when asked."""
        self.save()
        if self.reload_requested or self.reload_after_turn:
            self.reload_requested = self.reload_after_turn = False
            try:
                await self.rebuild()
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - reported as an out-of-turn notice
                from luna_core.events import Notice

                self.session.notify(Notice("error", f"auto-reload failed: {exc}"))

    async def aclose(self) -> None:
        """Close the agent's resources."""
        await self.background.shutdown()
        if self._bundle is not None:
            await self._bundle.aclose()


class RuntimeRegistry:
    """LRU of session runtimes; running sessions are never evicted."""

    def __init__(self, build: Callable[[SessionRuntime], Awaitable], *, log: EventLog, manager: AgentManager, capacity: int = 8) -> None:
        self._build = build
        self.log = log
        self.manager = manager
        self._capacity = capacity
        self._items: OrderedDict[str, SessionRuntime] = OrderedDict()
        self._store = SessionStateStore()

    def peek(self, session_id: str) -> SessionRuntime | None:
        """The runtime if loaded."""
        return self._items.get(session_id)

    def all(self) -> list[SessionRuntime]:
        """Loaded runtimes."""
        return list(self._items.values())

    def _running(self, runtime: SessionRuntime) -> bool:
        info = self.manager.get(runtime.id)
        return runtime.session.busy or (info is not None and info.status in ("queued", "running", "waiting"))

    async def get(self, session_id: str, workdir: str) -> SessionRuntime:
        """The runtime for ``session_id`` (session created when unknown)."""
        resolved = str(Path(workdir).resolve())
        runtime = self._items.get(session_id)
        if runtime is not None:
            if runtime.workdir != resolved:
                raise WorkdirMismatch(session_id)
            self._items.move_to_end(session_id)
            return runtime
        info = self.log.info(session_id)
        if info is None:
            self.log.create(resolved, session_id=session_id)
        elif info.workdir != resolved:
            raise WorkdirMismatch(session_id)
        state = self._store.load(session_id, resolved)
        runtime = SessionRuntime(state, Session.open(self.log, session_id), store=self._store, build=self._build)
        self._items[session_id] = runtime
        for key in list(self._items):
            if len(self._items) <= self._capacity:
                break
            candidate = self._items[key]
            if key != session_id and not self._running(candidate) and not candidate.lock.locked():
                del self._items[key]
                await candidate.aclose()
        return runtime

    async def drop(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        """Forget and close a runtime."""
        runtime = self._items.pop(session_id, None)
        if runtime is not None:
            await runtime.aclose()

    async def aclose(self) -> None:
        """Close every runtime."""
        for runtime in list(self._items.values()):
            await runtime.aclose()
        self._items.clear()


async def runtime_for(request: Request, session_id: str, workdir: str) -> SessionRuntime | JSONResponse:
    """The request's runtime, or a 400 when the folder does not match."""
    try:
        return await request.app.state.runtimes.get(session_id, workdir)
    except WorkdirMismatch:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "workdir_mismatch"}, status_code=400)
```

`luna/server/turns.py` (whole file):

```python
"""Turn endpoints: start runs in the AgentManager and stream their events."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json

from luna_core.messages import Image
from luna_core.policy import Decision
from sse_starlette.sse import EventSourceResponse
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse

from luna.config.providers import LunaConfigError
from luna.core.store import make_title
from luna.extensions.subagents import subagent_summaries
from luna.server.runtime import runtime_for
from luna.server.trust import trust_error
from luna.turn import engine

_FINAL = ("turn_done", "error", "cancelled")


def _relevel(event: dict) -> dict:
    if event.get("event") == "notice" and event.get("level") == "info":
        return {**event, "level": engine.notice_level(event["text"])}
    return event


def _sse(obj: dict) -> dict:
    return {"data": json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False)}


async def _start(request: Request, body: dict, session_id: str):
    """Common part of /messages and /background: returns (runtime, manager) or a JSONResponse."""
    if (error := trust_error(request, body.get("workdir"))) is not None:
        return error
    runtime = await runtime_for(request, session_id, body.get("workdir", "."))
    if isinstance(runtime, JSONResponse):
        return runtime
    manager = request.app.state.manager
    info = manager.get(session_id)
    if runtime.session.busy or (info is not None and info.status in ("queued", "running", "waiting")):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "session_busy"}, status_code=409)
    content = body["content"]
    try:
        names = {n for n, _ in subagent_summaries(runtime.workdir)}
    except LunaConfigError:
        names = set()
    prepared = engine.prepare_turn(runtime.state, content, workdir=runtime.workdir, subagent_names=names)
    engine.before_turn(runtime.state)
    images = prepared.images + [Image(i["media_type"], i["data"]) for i in body.get("images", [])]
    runtime.reload_after_turn = bool(body.get("reload_after"))
    if not runtime.session.title:
        runtime.session.log.set_title(session_id, make_title(prepared.title_line))
    bundle = await runtime.bundle()

    def watch(event: dict) -> None:
        if event.get("event") == "reload_requested":
            runtime.reload_requested = True

    runtime._unwatch = manager.subscribe(session_id, watch)
    info = manager.start(bundle.agent, runtime.session, prepared.text, images=images, label=make_title(prepared.title_line))
    return runtime, manager, info


async def post_message(request: Request):
    """Run a turn and stream it (the run continues if the client disconnects)."""
    session_id = request.path_params["session_id"]
    body = await request.json()
    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()
    # Subscribe before the run starts so no event is missed.
    unsubscribe = request.app.state.manager.subscribe(session_id, queue.put_nowait)
    started = await _start(request, body, session_id)
    if isinstance(started, JSONResponse):
        unsubscribe()
        return started

    async def stream():
        try:
            while True:
                event = _relevel(await queue.get())
                yield _sse(event)
                if event["event"] in _FINAL:
                    return
        finally:
            unsubscribe()

    return EventSourceResponse(stream())


async def post_background(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
    """Run a turn detached from any client."""
    session_id = request.path_params["session_id"]
    started = await _start(request, await request.json(), session_id)
    if isinstance(started, JSONResponse):
        return started
    _runtime, _manager, info = started
    return JSONResponse(info.__dict__)


async def get_events(request: Request):
    """Replay a run's buffered events, then follow it live."""
    session_id = request.path_params["session_id"]
    after = int(request.query_params.get("after", -1))
    manager = request.app.state.manager
    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()
    unsubscribe = manager.subscribe(session_id, queue.put_nowait)
    backlog = manager.events(session_id, after)
    info = manager.get(session_id)

    async def stream():
        try:
            last = after
            for event in backlog:
                last = event["index"]
                yield _sse(_relevel(event))
                if event["event"] in _FINAL:
                    return
            if info is None or info.status not in ("queued", "running", "waiting"):
                return
            while True:
                event = await queue.get()
                if event["index"] <= last:
                    continue
                yield _sse(_relevel(event))
                if event["event"] in _FINAL:
                    return
        finally:
            unsubscribe()

    return EventSourceResponse(stream())


async def post_approve(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
    """Answer a pending approval; the open stream continues."""
    session_id = request.path_params["session_id"]
    body = await request.json()
    runtime = request.app.state.runtimes.peek(session_id)
    if runtime is None:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "no running turn"}, status_code=404)
    try:
        runtime.session.decide(body["call_id"], Decision.from_dict(body["decision"]))
    except (KeyError, ValueError) as exc:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(exc)}, status_code=400)
    except Exception as exc:  # LunaCoreError: nothing pending for that call
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(exc)}, status_code=404)
    return JSONResponse({"ok": True})


async def post_cancel(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
    """Cancel the running turn."""
    return JSONResponse({"cancelled": request.app.state.manager.cancel(request.path_params["session_id"])})


def history_entries(messages) -> list[dict]:
    """The transcript the TUI replays: user/assistant text and one row per tool call."""
    from luna_core.tools.base import args_preview

    calls: dict[str, dict] = {}
    entries: list[dict] = []
    for message in messages:
        if message.role in ("user", "assistant"):
            if message.text:
                entries.append({"role": message.role, "content": message.text})
            for call in message.tool_calls:
                calls[call.id] = {"name": call.name, "args": call.args}
        else:
            for result in message.results:
                call = calls.get(result.call_id, {})
                detail = result.text.splitlines()[0][:120] if result.text else ""
                entries.append({"role": "tool", "name": result.name or call.get("name", ""), "args_preview": args_preview(call.get("args") or {}), "ok": not result.is_error, "detail": detail})
    return entries


async def get_history(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
    """The session's transcript, oldest first."""
    session_id = request.path_params["session_id"]
    workdir = request.query_params.get("workdir")
    if (error := trust_error(request, workdir)) is not None:
        return error
    runtime = await runtime_for(request, session_id, workdir or ".")
    if isinstance(runtime, JSONResponse):
        return runtime
    return JSONResponse({"messages": history_entries(runtime.session.messages())})


async def get_agents(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
    """Background runs and background tasks of loaded sessions."""
    runs = [r.__dict__ for r in request.app.state.manager.list()]
    tasks = [t.__dict__ for runtime in request.app.state.runtimes.all() for t in runtime.background.list()]
    return JSONResponse({"runs": runs, "tasks": tasks})
```

The `_unwatch` attribute set in `_start` is released in the manager's `on_done` (below). Keep `_start` returning the triple; `post_message` ignores it.

`luna/server/sessions.py`: keep `session_group` and `relative_time`; replace the two handlers:

```python
async def list_sessions(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
    """Sessions of a workdir, newest first (top-level sessions only)."""
    workdir = request.query_params.get("workdir")
    if (error := trust_error(request, workdir)) is not None:
        return error
    resolved = str(Path(workdir).resolve()) if workdir else None
    manager = request.app.state.manager
    rows = []
    for info in request.app.state.log.list(resolved):
        if info.parent_id is not None:
            continue
        run = manager.get(info.id)
        running = run is not None and run.status in ("queued", "running", "waiting")
        rows.append(
            {
                "id": info.id,
                "title": info.title or "(new session)",
                "created": info.created,
                "updated": info.updated,
                "group": session_group(info.updated),
                "relative_time": relative_time(info.updated),
                "running": running,
                "background": running and not manager.has_listeners(info.id),
            }
        )
    return JSONResponse({"sessions": rows})


async def create_session(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
    """Create an empty session in a workdir."""
    body = await request.json()
    if (error := trust_error(request, body.get("workdir"))) is not None:
        return error
    workdir = str(Path(body.get("workdir", ".")).resolve())
    return JSONResponse({"id": request.app.state.log.create(workdir)})


async def delete_session(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
    """Delete a session (refused while it runs)."""
    session_id = request.path_params["session_id"]
    run = request.app.state.manager.get(session_id)
    if run is not None and run.status in ("queued", "running", "waiting"):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "session_busy"}, status_code=409)
    await request.app.state.runtimes.drop(session_id)
    return JSONResponse({"deleted": request.app.state.log.delete(session_id)})
```

`luna/server/app.py` (routes and lifespan):

```python
def create_app(*, token: str, build, trust_check=None, log=None) -> Starlette:
    """Build the Starlette app over luna-core."""
    from luna_core.background import AgentManager

    from luna.core.store import open_log
    from luna.server.runtime import RuntimeRegistry

    log = log or open_log()

    async def on_done(info) -> None:
        runtime = app.state.runtimes.peek(info.session_id)
        if runtime is not None:
            unwatch = getattr(runtime, "_unwatch", None)
            if unwatch:
                unwatch()
            await runtime.after_turn()

    manager = AgentManager(on_done=lambda info: asyncio.get_running_loop().create_task(on_done(info)))

    @contextlib.asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app):
        yield
        await manager.shutdown()
        await app.state.runtimes.aclose()

    app = Starlette(
        routes=[
            Route("/health", _health),
            Route("/sessions", list_sessions, methods=["GET"]),
            Route("/sessions", create_session, methods=["POST"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}", delete_session, methods=["DELETE"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}/messages", get_history, methods=["GET"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}/messages", post_message, methods=["POST"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}/approve", post_approve, methods=["POST"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}/cancel", post_cancel, methods=["POST"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}/background", post_background, methods=["POST"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}/events", get_events, methods=["GET"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}/state", get_state, methods=["GET"]),
            Route("/sessions/{session_id}/command", post_command, methods=["POST"]),
            Route("/commands", get_commands, methods=["GET"]),
            Route("/agents", get_agents, methods=["GET"]),
        ],
        middleware=[Middleware(_AuthMiddleware, token=token)],
        lifespan=lifespan,
    )
    app.state.log = log
    app.state.manager = manager
    app.state.runtimes = RuntimeRegistry(build, log=log, manager=manager)
    app.state.trust_check = trust_check
    return app
```

`_health` returns `{"ok": True, "version": luna.__version__, "protocol": PROTOCOL_VERSION}`.

`luna/server/run.py`: delete `make_agent_factory` and `make_session_agent_factory`; add

```python
def make_session_build(*, provider_factory=None):
    """One agent bundle per session: its config, plan flag, pipeline hooks and background tasks."""

    async def build(runtime):
        from luna.core.agent import build_agent
        from luna.turn import engine

        provider = provider_factory(runtime) if provider_factory else None
        return await build_agent(
            runtime.config(),
            provider=provider,
            plan_flag=lambda: runtime.state.plan,
            hooks=engine.make_hooks(lambda: runtime.state, runtime.config),
            background=runtime.background,
        )

    return build
```

and `run_serve` passes `build=make_session_build()`.

`luna/server/commands.py`: `_ServerEnv` holds `runtime` and exposes `session = runtime.session`, `state = runtime.state`, `pinned = runtime.state.pinned`, `agent` (an awaitable getter is not allowed in a property — store the bundle: `post_command` does `bundle = await runtime.bundle()` and passes it to `_ServerEnv(runtime, bundle, manager)`), `async rebuild()`, `async switch_model()`, `async switch_provider()`, `manager`. `get_state` returns additionally `"undo_depth": runtime.session.undo_depth`, `"running": bool`, and builds `usage_summary` from `runtime.session.usage()` and `cost()`: `f"{u.input // 1000}k in · {u.output // 1000}k out" + (f" · ${cost:.2f}" if cost else "")`. Mutating commands hold `runtime.lock` **and** are refused with 409 while the session runs.

### Task 31: Commands on the core

**Files:**
- Modify: `luna/commands/registry.py` (`run_line` async), `luna/commands/base.py` (`CommandEnv`), `luna/commands/builtin.py`, `luna/repl/commands.py` (adapter), `luna/server/commands.py` (await)
- Test: `tests/test_commands.py` (ported + new cases), `tests/test_server_commands.py` (ported)

**Interfaces:**
- `run_line(line, env) -> Awaitable[CommandResult]`; handlers may be sync or async (`register` stores either; `run_line` awaits coroutines).
- `CommandEnv` gains `session` (core `Session`), `state` (`SessionState`), `bundle` (`AgentBundle`), `manager` (`AgentManager | None`); `rebuild`, `switch_model`, `switch_provider` become async; `thread_id`, `session_id`, `index` and `usage` are removed (use `session.id`, `session.usage()`).
- Changed commands: `/compact [focus]` → `Compactor.compact` (yields `Compacted` or a notice), `/undo` (`session.undo()` after a confirm naming the turn's first user line; `--yes` skips), `/redo`, `/diff` (`session.undo_journal.diff()`), `/usage` (`session.usage()`, `session.cost()`), `/tools` (the agent's real tool list, MCP included), `/agents` (subagents + background runs + background tasks).
- New commands: `/rewind [n]` (undo the last `n` turns, default 1, confirm), `/export [md|html|json] [path]` (default `md` to `.luna/exports/<session>-<time>.<ext>`), `/btw <question>` (`agent.side_question`, answer as text, not logged), `/steer <text>` (`session.queue(text)`; kind `read` so it works mid-turn), `/fork` (new session from this one; effect `{"session": new_id}` so the TUI switches to it), `/background <prompt>` (kind `ui` — the TUI sends it via `POST /background`).

`registry.py`: `run_line` becomes

```python
async def run_line(line: str, env) -> CommandResult:
    """Run one ``/command args`` line against ``env`` (sync or async handlers)."""
    name, _, arg = line.strip().partition(" ")
    arg = arg.strip()
    command = REGISTRY.get(name)
    if command is not None and command.run is not None:
        result = command.run(env, arg)
        return await result if inspect.isawaitable(result) else result
    bare = name[1:]
    if command is None and bare in (env.user_commands or {}):
        from luna.repl.usercmd import expand

        return CommandResult(prompt=expand(env.user_commands[bare], arg, env.workdir))
    return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("error", f"неизвестная команда {name} — /help")])
```

`builtin.py` — delete `TOOL_NAMES`, `peek_last`, `undo_last`, `forget_messages`, `compact_history`, `_message_count`; replace the handlers:

```python
@register("/tools", "list the agent's tools", "read")
def _tools(env, arg):
    return CommandResult(text=", ".join(sorted(env.agent.tools)))


@register("/agents", "subagents and background work", "read")
def _agents(env, arg):
    from luna.extensions.subagents import subagent_summaries

    try:
        lines = [f"- **{n}** — {d}" for n, d in subagent_summaries(env.workdir)]
    except LunaConfigError as exc:
        return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("error", str(exc))])
    runs = env.manager.list() if env.manager else []
    tasks = env.agent.services["background"].list(env.session.id)
    work = [f"- {r.session_id[:8]} · {r.label} · {r.status} · ${r.cost:.3f}" for r in runs]
    work += [f"- {t.handle} · {t.label} · {t.status}" for t in tasks]
    return CommandResult(text="**Subagents**\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n**Background**\n" + ("\n".join(work) or "(no background work)"))


@register("/usage", "show token usage and cost this session", "read")
def _usage(env, arg):
    usage, cost = env.session.usage(), env.session.cost()
    if not usage.total:
        return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("info", "no usage recorded yet")])
    lines = [f"in: {usage.input}  out: {usage.output}  cached: {usage.cache_read}  total: {usage.total}"]
    if cost:
        lines.append(f"cost: ${cost:.4f}")
    return CommandResult(text="\n".join(lines))


@register("/compact", "summarise old history to free context (/compact [focus])", "mutate")
async def _compact(env, arg):
    from luna_core.events import Compacted

    notices = []
    async for event in env.agent.compactor.compact(env.agent, env.session, instructions=arg):
        if isinstance(event, Compacted):
            notices.append(Notice("info", f"compacted {event.removed} messages into a summary"))
        else:
            notices.append(Notice(event.level, event.text))
    return CommandResult(notices=notices or [Notice("dim", "nothing to compact yet")])


@register("/diff", "show file changes made this session", "read")
def _diff(env, arg):
    journal = env.session.undo_journal
    text = journal.diff() if journal else ""
    fence = "`" * 3  # spelled out so this file's own Markdown fences stay intact
    return CommandResult(text=f"{fence}diff\n{text}\n{fence}") if text else CommandResult(notices=[Notice("dim", "no changes this session")])


def _last_user_line(session) -> str:
    entries = session.entries()
    turns = session.turn_ids()
    if not turns:
        return ""
    first = next((e.message.text for e in entries if e.turn_id == turns[-1] and e.message.role == "user"), "")
    return first.strip().splitlines()[0][:60] if first.strip() else ""


@register("/undo", "revert the last turn (files + conversation)", "mutate")
def _undo(env, arg):
    if not env.session.turn_ids():
        return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("dim", "nothing to undo")])
    if arg.strip() != "--yes":
        return CommandResult(confirm=Confirm(f"undo the last turn «{_last_user_line(env.session)}» (files + conversation)?", "/undo --yes", "undo cancelled"))
    result = env.session.undo()
    return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("info", f"undid the turn: {result.note}")], effects={"undo_depth": env.session.undo_depth})


@register("/redo", "re-apply the last undone turn", "mutate")
def _redo(env, arg):
    result = env.session.redo()
    if result is None:
        return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("dim", "nothing to redo")])
    return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("info", f"redo: {result.note}")], effects={"undo_depth": env.session.undo_depth})


@register("/rewind", "undo the last N turns (/rewind 3)", "mutate")
def _rewind(env, arg):
    parts = arg.split()
    yes = "--yes" in parts
    count = next((int(p) for p in parts if p.isdigit()), 1)
    available = len(env.session.turn_ids())
    if not available:
        return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("dim", "nothing to rewind")])
    count = min(count, available)
    if not yes:
        return CommandResult(confirm=Confirm(f"rewind the last {count} turn(s) (files + conversation)?", f"/rewind {count} --yes", "rewind cancelled"))
    for _ in range(count):
        env.session.undo()
    return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("info", f"rewound {count} turn(s)")], effects={"undo_depth": env.session.undo_depth})


@register("/export", "save the transcript (/export [md|html|json] [path])", "read")
def _export(env, arg):
    import time
    from pathlib import Path

    parts = arg.split()
    fmt = parts[0] if parts and parts[0] in ("md", "html", "json") else "md"
    rest = [p for p in parts if p not in ("md", "html", "json")]
    rel = rest[0] if rest else f".luna/exports/{env.session.id[:8]}-{time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}.{fmt}"
    target = env.agent.workspace.resolve(rel)
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_text(env.session.export(fmt), encoding="utf-8")
    shown = env.agent.workspace.rel(target)
    return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("info", f"exported to {shown}")], effects={"path": shown})


@register("/btw", "ask a side question without adding it to the conversation", "read")
async def _btw(env, arg):
    if not arg:
        return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("dim", "usage: /btw <question>")])
    answer = await env.agent.side_question(env.session, arg)
    return CommandResult(text=answer.text)


@register("/steer", "add guidance to the running turn (delivered after the current step)", "read")
def _steer(env, arg):
    if not arg:
        return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("dim", "usage: /steer <text>")])
    env.session.queue(arg)
    return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("dim", "queued for the next step")])


@register("/fork", "continue in a new session that copies this one", "mutate")
def _fork(env, arg):
    child = env.session.fork()
    return CommandResult(notices=[Notice("info", f"forked into {child.id[:8]}")], effects={"session": child.id})


register_ui("/background", "run a prompt in the background (/background <prompt>)")
```

`/model`, `/provider`, `/reload` become `async def` and `await env.switch_model(arg)` / `await env.switch_provider(arg)` / `await env.rebuild()`. `/verify` and `/diagnose` run their subprocess with `await asyncio.to_thread(...)`. `/init` unchanged. `/plan` unchanged.

`base.py`: update the `CommandEnv` protocol to the fields listed in Interfaces.

`luna/server/commands.py`: `result = await run_line(line, env)` (no threadpool); mutating kinds hold `runtime.lock` and get 409 while `runtime.session.busy`. `luna/repl/commands.py` already awaits since Task 29.

### Task 32: TUI on protocol v1 — cancel, images, approvals, undo depth, background work

**Files:**
- Modify: `luna/tui/chat.py`, `luna/tui/app.py`, `luna/tui/approval_modal.py`, `luna/tui/status_bar.py`, `luna/tui/sidebar_sessions.py`, `luna/tui/commands.py` (fallback list), `luna/tui/luna.tcss` (two rules)
- Delete: `luna/server/client.py` (the TUI uses `luna_core.client.LunaClient`), `luna/core/permissions.py` (its last user, the approval modal, moves to `luna_core.policy`), `tests/test_permissions.py` (covered by the core's `test_policy.py`)
- Test: `tests/test_tui_app.py`, `tests/test_tui_sidebars.py`, `tests/test_tui_chat.py` (ported to a fake `LunaClient`), `tests/test_tui_background.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LunaClient` methods (Task 26), protocol v1 events, `/state` fields `undo_depth` and `running` (Task 30), command effects `session`, `undo_depth` (Task 31).
- Behaviour:
  - Turn stream: one `client.send(...)` stream per turn; on `approval_needed` the modal opens and `client.decide(session, call_id, decision)` answers it while the same stream continues.
  - **Esc** (when the autocomplete is closed) and **Ctrl+C** cancel the running turn via `client.cancel`; when idle, Ctrl+C shows "Ctrl+D или /exit — выйти".
  - Events: `thinking_delta` keeps the "Luna думает" glyph and shows the last thought line dimmed under it; `todos` → a dim checklist notice (✓ ▸ ·); `compacted` → info notice "история сжата: N сообщений"; `cancelled` → dim notice "прервано"; `background_done` (out-of-turn, from polling) → toast.
  - Images: pasting a path of an existing image file into the input inserts `@<path relative to the project>`; `@image.png` in a message is attached server-side (Task 29's `prepare_turn`).
  - Approval modal: Approve / Always allow (uses the request's `suggested_rule`) / Edit (for `execute`: an input prefilled with the command → `{"type": "edit", "args": {...}}`) / Reject.
  - Status bar: `undo: N` (from `/state`), plan marker as before.
  - Background: **Ctrl+G** sends the current input as a background run (`client.background`) and clears the input; `/background <prompt>` does the same; the sidebar marks running sessions `●` and background-only ones `◌`; while any run is active the app polls `client.agents()` every 3 s and toasts "Фоновая сессия «…» завершена" / "… ошибка" on transitions, then refreshes the sidebar. A `session` effect (`/fork`) switches the pane to that session.
  - Field renames: rows use `id` (was `thread_id`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — the TUI tests use a fake client with the `LunaClient` method names. Shared fixture in `tests/tui_fakes.py`:

```python
"""A scripted stand-in for luna_core.client.LunaClient in TUI tests."""

import asyncio


class FakeLunaClient:
    def __init__(self, turns=None, sessions=None, agents=None):
        self.turns = list(turns or [])  # each: list of event dicts for one send()
        self.sessions = sessions or [{"id": "s1", "title": "first", "updated": 0, "group": "today", "relative_time": "сейчас", "running": False, "background": False}]
        self.agents_rows = agents or {"runs": [], "tasks": []}
        self.calls = []
        self.decisions = []
        self.cancelled = []
        self.background_calls = []

    async def list_sessions(self, workdir):
        return self.sessions

    async def create_session(self, workdir):
        return "s-new"

    async def history(self, session_id, workdir):
        return []

    async def commands(self, workdir):
        return [{"name": "/help", "help": "show this help", "kind": "ui"}, {"name": "/usage", "help": "usage", "kind": "read"}]

    async def state(self, session_id, workdir):
        return {"provider": "p", "model": "m", "plan": False, "pinned": [], "usage_summary": "", "undo_depth": 2, "running": False}

    async def send(self, session_id, text, workdir, *, images=(), reload_after=False):
        self.calls.append((session_id, text))
        for event in self.turns.pop(0) if self.turns else [{"event": "turn_done", "usage": {}, "cost": None, "stop_reason": "end"}]:
            if event.get("event") == "_pause":
                await asyncio.sleep(event["seconds"])
                continue
            yield event

    async def decide(self, session_id, call_id, decision):
        self.decisions.append((call_id, decision))

    async def cancel(self, session_id):
        self.cancelled.append(session_id)
        return True

    async def run_command(self, session_id, line, workdir):
        return {"text": "", "notices": [], "choice": None, "confirm": None, "prompt": None, "effects": {}}

    async def background(self, session_id, text, workdir):
        self.background_calls.append(text)
        return {"session_id": session_id, "status": "running"}

    async def agents(self):
        return self.agents_rows

    async def aclose(self):
        pass
```

- Add:

```python
    async def poll_agents(self) -> None:
        """Announce background runs that finished since the last poll."""
        try:
            rows = (await self.client.agents())["runs"]
        except ServerError:
            return
        changed = False
        for run in rows:
            before = self._runs.get(run["session_id"])
            self._runs[run["session_id"]] = run["status"]
            if before in ("queued", "running", "waiting") and run["status"] in ("done", "failed", "cancelled"):
                verb = {"done": "завершена", "failed": "ошибка", "cancelled": "отменена"}[run["status"]]
                self.notify(f"Фоновая сессия «{run['label']}» {verb}", severity="error" if run["status"] == "failed" else "information", markup=False)
                changed = True
        if changed:
            await self.query_one(SessionsSidebar).refresh_sessions()

    async def action_send_background(self) -> None:
        """Ctrl+G: run the typed prompt in the background in this session."""
        chat = self.query_one(ChatPane)
        chat_input = self.query_one("#chat-input")
        text = chat_input.value.strip()
        if not text:
            self.notify("Введите задачу, затем Ctrl+G.", severity="warning")
            return
        chat_input.value = ""
        await chat.send_background(text)
```

  and `_open_thread` is reused for the `session` effect (`ChatPane` posts `SwitchSession(id)`; the app handles it with `_open_thread`).

- New methods:

```python
    async def action_cancel_turn(self) -> None:
        """Esc / Ctrl+C: cancel the running turn."""
        if not self.busy:
            self.notify("Ctrl+D или /exit — выйти.", timeout=3)
            return
        try:
            await self.app.client.cancel(self._turn_session or self.thread_id)
        except ServerError as exc:
            self.notify(str(exc), severity="error", markup=False)

    async def send_background(self, text: str) -> None:
        """Start a background run of ``text`` in this session."""
        transcript = self.query_one("#transcript", VerticalScroll)
        try:
            await self.app.client.background(self.thread_id, text, self._workdir)
        except ServerError as exc:
            await transcript.mount(NoticeRow("error", str(exc)))
            return
        await transcript.mount(UserMessage(text))
        await transcript.mount(NoticeRow("dim", "отправлено в фон — результат придёт уведомлением (/agents)"))
        self.app.post_message(self.TurnFinished())

    def on_paste(self, event: events.Paste) -> None:
        """A pasted path of an image file becomes an ``@mention``."""
        from pathlib import Path

        raw = event.text.strip().strip("'\"")
        path = Path(raw).expanduser()
        if path.suffix.lower() in (".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".webp") and path.is_file():
            try:
                rel = path.resolve().relative_to(Path(self._workdir).resolve()).as_posix()
            except ValueError:
                self.notify("Картинка вне проекта — скопируйте её в папку проекта.", severity="warning")
                event.stop()
                return
            chat_input = self.query_one("#chat-input", Input)
            chat_input.insert_text_at_cursor(f"@{rel} ")
            event.stop()
```

- `_run_command`: `name == "/background"` → `await self.send_background(arg)`.
- `_render_command_result`: after effects, `if "session" in result.get("effects", {}): self.post_message(self.SwitchSession(result["effects"]["session"]))` (new `Message` class `SwitchSession(session_id)`).

```python
        thread_id = self.thread_id

        app = self.app

                async for evt in stream:

            except ServerError as exc:

        finally:
            self.busy = False
            self._turn_session = None
            await reply.stop()
            self.post_message(self.TurnFinished())
```

```python
import importlib.util
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
FORBIDDEN = re.compile(r"^\s*(from|import)\s+(deepagents|langchain\w*|langgraph\w*|litellm)\b", re.M)


def test_no_framework_imports_in_the_repo():
    offenders = [str(p.relative_to(ROOT)) for base in ("luna", "packages", "tests", "examples") for p in (ROOT / base).rglob("*.py") if FORBIDDEN.search(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore"))]
    assert offenders == []


def test_frameworks_are_not_installed():
    assert [name for name in ("deepagents", "langchain", "langchain_core", "langgraph") if importlib.util.find_spec(name)] == []


def test_importing_the_cli_loads_no_framework():
    code = "import sys, luna.cli; print(sorted(m for m in sys.modules if m.split('.')[0] in ('deepagents', 'langchain', 'langchain_core', 'langgraph')))"
    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
    assert out == "[]"
```

```python
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import time


def _time(args):
    runs = []
    for _ in range(5):
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, *args], capture_output=True, check=True)
        runs.append(time.perf_counter() - t0)
    return statistics.median(runs)


def test_version_is_fast():
    # Acceptance target is 150 ms on a developer laptop; CI machines are slower, so the
    # test guards against regressions (heavy imports creeping into the --version path).
    assert _time(["-m", "luna", "--version"]) < 0.5


def test_version_path_imports_nothing_heavy():
    code = "import sys; sys.argv=['luna','--version']; import luna.cli as c; c.main(['--version'])\nprint(sorted(m for m in sys.modules if m.split('.')[0] in ('textual','rich','httpx','luna_core','starlette')))"
    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1]
    assert out == "[]"
```

```python
def _run_sessions(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    import argparse
    from pathlib import Path

    from luna.core.store import open_log

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="luna sessions")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="cmd")
    sub.add_parser("list")
    rm = sub.add_parser("rm")
    rm.add_argument("session_id")
    prune = sub.add_parser("prune")
    prune.add_argument("--days", type=float, default=30)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    log = open_log()
    workdir = str(Path.cwd().resolve())
    if args.cmd in (None, "list"):
        for info in log.list(workdir):
            if info.parent_id is None:
                print(f"{info.id}  {info.title or '(new session)'}")
        return 0
    if args.cmd == "rm":
        if not log.delete(args.session_id):
            print(f"luna: no session {args.session_id}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        print(f"deleted {args.session_id}")
        return 0
    print(f"pruned {log.prune(args.days, workdir)} session(s)")
    return 0
```

- `_run_background(argv)`:

```python
def _run_run(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    import argparse
    from pathlib import Path

    from luna_core import client as core_client

    from luna.server import run as server_run

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="luna run")
    parser.add_argument("--background", action="store_true", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("prompt")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    workdir = str(Path.cwd().resolve())
    if not _trusted(workdir):
        print("luna: this folder is not trusted yet — open it once with `luna` and confirm", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    base_url, token = server_run.ensure_running()

    async def go() -> str:
        client = core_client.LunaClient(base_url, token)
        try:
            session_id = await client.create_session(workdir)
            await client.background(session_id, args.prompt, workdir)
            return session_id
        finally:
            await client.aclose()

    session_id = asyncio.run(go())
    print(f"session {session_id} started in the background; follow it with: luna --resume {session_id}")
    return 0
```

  (`ensure_running` returns `(base_url, token)` — if its current signature differs, adapt the call and keep the test's monkeypatch shape in sync; Ruling if changed.)
- `_run_acp(argv)`: `from luna.core.acp_app import run; return run(Path.cwd())` with `luna/core/acp_app.py`:

```python
"""``luna acp``: the Luna agent for ACP editors (Zed, JetBrains, …)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from pathlib import Path

from luna.config.config import load_config
from luna.core.agent import build_agent
from luna.core.session_state import SessionState
from luna.core.store import open_log
from luna.turn import engine


def run(cwd: Path) -> int:
    """Serve ACP over stdio until the editor disconnects."""
    try:
        from luna_core.acp import run_acp
    except ImportError:
        print('ACP support is not installed: pip install "luna-simple[acp]"')
        return 2

    async def make_agent(folder: str):
        config = load_config({"workdir": folder}, cwd=folder)
        state = SessionState("acp", folder)
        engine.before_turn(state)
        bundle = await build_agent(config, hooks=engine.make_hooks(lambda: state, lambda: config))
        return bundle.agent

    asyncio.run(run_acp(make_agent, open_log()))
    return 0
```

- The default path (`luna` / `-p` / non-TTY REPL) becomes:

```python
    from luna.core.agent import build_agent
    from luna.core.session import run_once, run_repl
    from luna.core.store import open_log
    from luna_core.session import Session

    log = open_log()
    workdir = str(Path(config.workdir).resolve())
    start = None
    if args.cont or args.resume:
        start = _resolve_resume(args, log, workdir, console, interactive)
        if start is None:
            return 2
    if interactive and not prompt:
        if start is None:
            start = log.create(workdir)
        return run_tui(config, workdir=workdir, thread_id=start)
    plan_state = [False]
    hooks_state: dict = {}

    async def rebuild():
        return await build_agent(config, on_warn=lambda m: console.print(f"[yellow]{m}[/]"), plan_flag=lambda: plan_state[0], hooks=engine.make_hooks(lambda: hooks_state["state"], lambda: config))

    async def go() -> int:
        bundle = await rebuild()
        session = Session.open(log, start) if start else Session.create(log, workdir)
        try:
            if prompt:
                hooks_state["state"] = SessionState(session.id, workdir)
                await run_once(bundle, prompt, console=console, log=log, session=session, cfg=config, output_format=args.output_format)
                return 0
            return await run_repl(bundle, console=console, rebuild=rebuild, log=log, session=session, config=config, plan_state=plan_state, hooks_state=hooks_state)
        finally:
            await bundle.aclose()

    try:
        return asyncio.run(go())
    except LunaConfigError as exc:
        print(f"luna: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
```

```python
        assert getattr(luna_core, name) is not None


def test_every_public_callable_has_a_docstring():
    missing = [n for n in luna_core.__all__ if callable(getattr(luna_core, n)) and not (getattr(luna_core, n).__doc__ or "").strip()]
    assert missing == []
```

`examples/minimal_agent.py`:

```python
"""Minimal embedded agent: one turn in a folder, printing the streamed answer."""

import argparse
import asyncio

from luna_core import Agent, EventLog, NeedsApproval, Session, TextDelta, ToolStarted, TurnDone, Workspace, providers
from luna_core.policy import Decision, Policy
from luna_core.providers.fake import FakeProvider


async def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--fake", action="store_true", help="offline: scripted model")
    parser.add_argument("--workdir", default=".")
    parser.add_argument("--provider", default="anthropic")
    parser.add_argument("prompt", nargs="?", default="List the files here and say what this project is.")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.fake:
        provider, model = FakeProvider([FakeProvider.call("ls", {"path": "."}), FakeProvider.text("This folder is a project.")]), "fake"
    else:
        provider, model = providers.resolve(args.provider)
    workspace = Workspace(args.workdir)
    agent = Agent(provider, model, workspace=workspace, policy=Policy(workspace=workspace))
    log = EventLog(f"{args.workdir}/.luna-example.db")
    session = Session.create(log, args.workdir)
    async for event in agent.run(session, args.prompt):
        if isinstance(event, TextDelta):
            print(event.text, end="", flush=True)
        elif isinstance(event, ToolStarted):
            print(f"\n[{event.name} {event.preview}]")
        elif isinstance(event, NeedsApproval):
            session.decide(event.request.call_id, Decision.reject("this example is read-only"))
        elif isinstance(event, TurnDone):
            print(f"\n\n{event.usage.total} tokens")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())
```

The other examples follow the same pattern:
- `custom_tool.py` — a `@tool` `word_count(file_path)` using `ctx.workspace.resolve`, added with `tools=[*builtin_tools(), word_count]`; the fake script calls it once.
- `custom_provider.py` — a class with `name` and `async def stream(request)` yielding `TextDelta` / `UsageReport` / `StopEvent` (an "echo" provider), used directly by `Agent`.
- `approvals_web.py` — a Starlette app: `POST /run` streams SSE with `to_wire`, `POST /approve` calls `session.decide`; `--self-test` drives it with `httpx.ASGITransport`.
- `remote_client.py` — `LunaClient` against `luna serve` (`--url`, `--token`); `--fake` starts `luna.server.app.create_app` with a fake provider in-process via `ASGITransport` and sends one message.
- `acp_agent.py` — `run_acp(make_agent, EventLog(...))`; `--self-test` performs the JSON-RPC exchange from `test_acp_stdio_end_to_end` against itself in a subprocess.

`docs/sdk/*.md`: one page each — what the concept is, a 10–30 line example, the API it uses (names exactly as exported), pitfalls (approvals must be answered or the turn waits; cancel with `session.cancel_turn()`; one turn per session at a time; `Workspace` path rules; subagent worktrees need git; background results arrive at the next step). `packages/luna-core/README.md`: install (`pip install luna-core`, extras), the minimal example, links to `docs/sdk` and `docs/protocol.md`.

`CHANGELOG.md` — `## 0.4.0 — luna-core` with: own runtime (no deepagents/LangChain/LangGraph), five native provider APIs (incl. GigaChat, Yandex AI Studio preset, native Gemini, OpenAI Responses), parallel and background agents (`task background=true`, `luna run --background`, `/agents`, Ctrl+G), worktree isolation, hooks (`[[hooks]]`), Docker sandbox (`[sandbox]`), ACP (`luna acp`), new commands (`/rewind /export /btw /steer /fork /background`), Esc cancels, image input, sessions start fresh (old history is not migrated), removed config `[agent] extra`, startup time numbers from the acceptance run.

`docs/superpowers/acceptance/luna-core.md` — the acceptance checklist with measured results (filled in during this step):

| Check | Target | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `luna --version` (median of 5) | < 150 ms | |
| TUI first frame | < 0.4 s | |
| Built-in tool schemas | ≤ 2 000 tokens | |
| `uv pip list` has no langchain/langgraph/deepagents | none | |
| "Find and fix a bug" — repo 1 (small Python CLI), DeepSeek | passes | |
| same, Anthropic | passes | |
| repo 2 (TypeScript web app), DeepSeek / Anthropic | passes | |
| repo 3 (this repository, a seeded failing test), DeepSeek / Anthropic | passes | |
| Esc cancels a running `execute` | process killed, next turn works | |
| TUI closed mid-turn, reopened | finished turn visible, notice shown | |
| Two parallel `general-purpose` subagents editing one file | separate branches, no conflict | |
| ACP in Zed (or JetBrains) | prompt, tool calls, permission dialog work | |

  "Find and fix a bug" procedure: copy the repo to a temp folder, break one function so one test fails, run `luna -p "the test suite fails; find and fix the bug" --output-format json`, then run the tests; pass = tests green and the diff touches only the broken function. Record tokens and cost per run. If a provider key is unavailable (the DeepSeek key returned 402 on 2026-10-08), record "blocked: <reason>" and do not mark the row passed.
